33: @Service
34: @ConditionalOnProperty(prefix = "talent-introduction.expert-discovery.openalex", name = ["enabled"], havingValue = "true")
35: class OpenAlexDataSource(
36:     @Qualifier("openAlexRestTemplate") private val restTemplate: RestTemplate,
37:     private val properties: OpenAlexProperties,
38:     private val europePmc: EuropePmcDataSource,
39:     private val pdfEmailExtractor: PdfEmailExtractor,
40:     private val unpaywallClient: UnpaywallClient,
41:     private val requestPolicy: OpenAlexRequestPolicy = OpenAlexRequestPolicy(properties)
42: ) : AcademicDataSource {
43: 
44:     private val log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(OpenAlexDataSource::class.java)
45: 
46:     override val sourceName = "OPENALEX"
47:     override val emailExtractionMethod = "FULLTEXT_XML"
48:     override val maxPapersPerSource: Int
49:         get() = properties.maxPapersPerSource
50: 
51:     override fun searchPapers(criteria: PaperSearchCriteria): PaperSearchResult {
52:         val filter = buildFilter(criteria)
53:         var url = "${properties.baseUrl}/works?filter=$filter&per_page=${criteria.pageSize}"
54:         if (criteria.cursor != null) url += "&cursor=${criteria.cursor}"
55:         else url += "&cursor=*"
56:         if (properties.politeEmail.isNotBlank()) url += "&mailto=${properties.politeEmail}"
57: 
58:         // I-1：关键词/语义搜索 10 credits，纯列表 1 credit —— 由构造方显式声明，再由 policy 校验目标路径。
59:         val operation = if (criteria.keywords.isNotEmpty()) Operation.SEARCH else Operation.LIST
60:         val response = try {
61:             if (properties.requestDelayMs > 0) Thread.sleep(properties.requestDelayMs)
62:             getJson(RequestKind.DISCOVERY, operation, url)
63:         } catch (e: Exception) {
64:             log.error("OpenAlex search failed: {}", e.message)
65:             throw e
66:         }
67:         return parseResponse(response)
68:     }
69: 
70:     /**
71:      * Every OpenAlex HTTP call passes through the shared [OpenAlexRequestPolicy]: the caller names its [RequestKind]
72:      * (purpose) and [Operation] (cost) explicitly, the request slot and its estimated cost are reserved before the
73:      * call, and the real quota headers reconcile the budget right after. A deferred budget raises
74:      * [OpenAlexBudgetDeferredException] instead of calling; a timeout marks the permit UNKNOWN (never refunded).
75:      * Every retry goes through this method again, so a retry reserves a second time (I-1/I-3).
76:      */
77:     private fun getJson(kind: RequestKind, operation: Operation, url: String): JsonNode? {
78:         val permit = requestPolicy.reserve(kind, operation, url)
79:         if (permit is Permit.Deferred) throw OpenAlexBudgetDeferredException(permit.reason, permit.retryAt)
80:         val permitId = (permit as Permit.Allowed).permitId
81:         val response = try {
82:             restTemplate.exchange(url, HttpMethod.GET, null, JsonNode::class.java)
83:         } catch (e: HttpStatusCodeException) {
84:             requestPolicy.recordResponse(permitId, e.responseHeaders ?: HttpHeaders())
85:             throw e
86:         } catch (e: Exception) {
87:             requestPolicy.recordTimeout(permitId)
88:             throw e
89:         }
90:         requestPolicy.recordResponse(permitId, response?.headers ?: HttpHeaders())
91:         return response?.body
92:     }
93: 
94:     /**
95:      * c10（I-1）：单篇论文的全文回退链。顺序固定为 PMC XML → 首选 OA PDF → 其他去重 OA PDF →
96:      * DOI→Unpaywall 开放位置；整篇共享一个 [FULLTEXT_PER_PAPER_DEADLINE_MS] 总时限，URL 侧最多
97:      * [MAX_FULLTEXT_ADDRESSES] 个地址（正文里说的「全文地址」就是 URL，PMC XML 按 ID 取，不算地址），
98:      * 同一 URL 只尝试一次，Unpaywall 只在前面都没取到内容时才问一次。
99:      * 任一地址取到内容（有邮箱或无邮箱）即停手：不再为同一篇发第二轮无效请求。
100:      */
101:     override fun extractAuthorEmails(paper: PaperMetadata): EmailExtractionOutcome =
102:         extractAuthorEmails(paper, Instant.now().plusMillis(FULLTEXT_PER_PAPER_DEADLINE_MS))
103: 
104:     /** 直接传 deadline 的入口：测试用它验证「共享总时限」而不是每个地址各给一份。 */
105:     internal fun extractAuthorEmails(paper: PaperMetadata, deadline: Instant): EmailExtractionOutcome {
106:         var requests = 0
107:         var lastFailure: EmailExtractionOutcome? = null
108: 
109:         if (paper.pmcId != null) {
110:             // R-4（V-4）：XML 阶段也在同一个共享总时限内 —— 时限已过就整段按 TIMEOUT 收口
111:             // （0 次请求），不再接着走 URL / Unpaywall。
112:             val europePmcOutcome = europePmc.extractAuthorEmails(paper, deadline)
113:             // I-2: PMC/JATS 是另一来源的提取结果，姓名/邮箱相似都不算证据 ——
114:             // 只有 ORCID 精确等值到一个唯一作者时才允许补接该作者的 OpenAlex ID。
115:             // I-1/I-2: 机构同理 —— JATS 没取到机构、且该唯一 ORCID 绑定到的作者身上恰有一个
116:             // 结构化 OpenAlex 机构时，才整条（名/类型/国家/来源）取自那个对象；两源矛盾保持 null。
117:             val xmlOutcome = europePmcOutcome.copy(
118:                 emails = europePmcOutcome.emails.map { email ->
119:                     val boundAuthorId = verifiedAuthorIdByOrcid(email, paper.authors)
120:                     val institution = if (boundAuthorId != null && email.institutionName == null) {
121:                         boundOpenAlexInstitution(email, paper.authors)
122:                     } else {
123:                         null
124:                     }
125:                     email.copy(
126:                         openAlexAuthorId = boundAuthorId,
127:                         institutionName = institution?.name ?: email.institutionName,
128:                         institutionCountry = institution?.country ?: email.institutionCountry,
129:                         institutionType = institution?.type ?: email.institutionType,
130:                         institutionSource = institution?.source ?: email.institutionSource
131:                     )
132:                 },
133:                 methodUsed = "FULLTEXT_XML"
134:             )
135:             requests += xmlOutcome.httpRequests
136:             if (xmlOutcome.resolvedFulltextObtained()) return xmlOutcome.copy(httpRequests = requests)
137:             // XML 没取到内容才继续回退；取到内容但没有邮箱按 I-3 算成功，不再重复下载。
138:             lastFailure = xmlOutcome
139:         }
140: 
141:         val attemptedUrls = LinkedHashSet<String>()
142:         val queue = ArrayDeque<String>()
143:         (listOfNotNull(paper.downloadUrl) + paper.candidateDownloadUrls)
144:             .mapNotNullTo(queue) { publicCandidateUrl(it) }
145:         var unpaywallConsulted = false
146: 
147:         while (attemptedUrls.size < MAX_FULLTEXT_ADDRESSES) {
148:             if (deadlineExpired(deadline)) {
149:                 // I-1：总时限是单篇自己的约束，不是来源耗尽 —— 没有拿到内容时单列 TIMEOUT。
150:                 if (lastFailure == null) lastFailure = timeoutOutcome()
151:                 break
152:             }
153:             if (queue.isEmpty()) {
154:                 if (unpaywallConsulted) break
155:                 unpaywallConsulted = true
156:                 val doi = paper.doi
157:                 if (doi != null && unpaywallClient.isConfigured()) {
158:                     // R-4（V-4）：Unpaywall 查询也是这一篇的全文阶段，同样受共享总时限约束；
159:                     // 时限已过就不再发这次查询，并按已有的 TIMEOUT 类别收口。
160:                     if (deadlineExpired(deadline)) {
161:                         if (lastFailure == null) lastFailure = timeoutOutcome()
162:                         break
163:                     }
164:                     requests++
165:                     unpaywallClient.findPdfUrls(doi, deadline).mapNotNullTo(queue) { publicCandidateUrl(it) }
166:                     // R-1（V-4）：查询被共享预算截断（客户端按剩余时间中止）且没拿到地址 → 按 TIMEOUT 收口，
167:                     // 不再对同一篇发起后续下载。
168:                     if (queue.isEmpty() && deadlineExpired(deadline)) {
169:                         if (lastFailure == null) lastFailure = timeoutOutcome()
170:                         break
171:                     }
172:                 }
173:                 if (queue.isEmpty()) break
174:             }
175:             val url = queue.removeFirst()
176:             if (!attemptedUrls.add(url)) continue
177:             // I-1 防御：计量主机绝不被下载（候选构建已过滤，这里再挡一次，未来调用方也不会误入）。
178:             if (OpenAlexMeteredDestinations.isMetered(url)) continue
179:             val outcome = pdfEmailExtractor.extract(url, paper.authors, sourceName, deadline) { }
180:             requests += outcome.httpRequests
181:             if (outcome.resolvedFulltextObtained()) return outcome.copy(httpRequests = requests)
182:             lastFailure = outcome
183:         }
184: 
185:         lastFailure?.let { return it.copy(httpRequests = requests) }
186:         // 一个地址都没有：保持改动前的语义（下游按 papersSkippedNoId 计数）。
187:         return EmailExtractionOutcome(emptyList(), emailExtractionMethod, "NO_PMC_ID")
188:     }
189: 
190:     private fun timeoutOutcome(): EmailExtractionOutcome = EmailExtractionOutcome(
191:         emptyList(), "PDF_PARSE", "PDF_DOWNLOAD_FAILED", httpRequests = 0,
192:         fulltextObtained = false, downloadFailureCategory = FULLTEXT_FAILURE_TIMEOUT
193:     )
194: 
195:     /**
196:      * I-1：公开全文候选 = 公开 http(s) 地址，且**不是** OpenAlex 计量主机。
197:      *
198:      * OpenAlex 的 Content API（`content.openalex.org`，100 credits/次）与 API 本体都不作为全文候选：
199:      * 本阶段拒绝新增计量下载，遇到这类地址就跳过、改试已有公开链接（出版社/PMC/仓库）。计量请求一律经
200:      * [OpenAlexRequestPolicy.reserve] 的显式操作预占，下载回调不是预占的替代品。
201:      */
202:     private fun publicCandidateUrl(raw: String?): String? =
203:         publicFulltextUrl(raw)?.takeIf { !OpenAlexMeteredDestinations.isMetered(it) }
204: 
205:     private fun deadlineExpired(deadline: Instant): Boolean = !Instant.now().isBefore(deadline)
206: 
207:     /**
208:      * I-2: 只有「提取出的邮箱带 ORCID，且该 ORCID 精确等值到唯一一个带作者 ID 的作者」才返回该 ID。
209:      * ORCID 缺失、不匹配，或同一 ORCID 对应多个不同作者 ID 时一律返回 null（不猜）。
210:      */
211:     private fun verifiedAuthorIdByOrcid(email: AuthorEmail, authors: List<PaperAuthor>): String? {
212:         val orcid = normalizeOrcid(email.orcidId) ?: return null
213:         return authors.mapNotNull { author ->
214:             if (normalizeOrcid(author.orcidId) != orcid) return@mapNotNull null
215:             normalizeOpenAlexAuthorId(author.openAlexAuthorId)
216:         }.distinct().singleOrNull()
217:     }
218: 
219:     /** I-1/I-2：同一 ORCID 唯一绑定到的作者身上那一个结构化 OpenAlex 机构（名/国家/类型同源）。 */
220:     private data class BoundInstitution(val name: String, val country: String?, val type: String?, val source: String)
221: 
222:     /**
223:      * I-1/I-2：只有「同一 ORCID 的作者们恰好给出**同一个**结构化 OpenAlex 机构」才返回它。
224:      * 缺来源标记、多个不同机构、或来源不是 OpenAlex 结构解析时一律 null（绝不合并半条记录）。
225:      */
226:     private fun boundOpenAlexInstitution(email: AuthorEmail, authors: List<PaperAuthor>): BoundInstitution? {
227:         val orcid = normalizeOrcid(email.orcidId) ?: return null
228:         return authors.filter { normalizeOrcid(it.orcidId) == orcid }
229:             .mapNotNull { author ->
230:                 val name = author.institutionName ?: return@mapNotNull null
231:                 val source = author.institutionSource ?: return@mapNotNull null
232:                 if (source != INSTITUTION_SOURCE_OPENALEX) return@mapNotNull null
233:                 BoundInstitution(name, author.institutionCountry, author.institutionType, source)
234:             }
235:             .distinct()
236:             .singleOrNull()
237:     }
238: 
239:     private fun normalizeOrcid(raw: String?): String? {
240:         val bare = raw?.trim()?.removePrefix("https://orcid.org/")?.removePrefix("http://orcid.org/")?.trim()
241:         return bare?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }?.lowercase()
242:     }
243: 
244:     private fun buildFilter(criteria: PaperSearchCriteria): String {
245:         val parts = mutableListOf<String>()
246:         if (criteria.openAccessOnly) parts += "is_oa:true"
247:         parts += "publication_year:${criteria.publicationYearFrom}-${criteria.publicationYearTo}"
248:         if (criteria.excludeCountries.isNotEmpty()) {
249:             for (country in criteria.excludeCountries) parts += "authorships.institutions.country_code:!$country"
250:         }
251:         if (criteria.keywords.isNotEmpty()) {
252:             parts += "title_and_abstract.search:${criteria.keywords.joinToString("|")}"
253:         }
254:         // I4-2: subjectScope == null 时返回空列表，parts 内容与改动前逐字相同（含参数顺序）。
255:         parts += SubjectScopeCatalog.openAlexFilterParts(criteria.subjectScope)
256:         return parts.joinToString(",")
257:     }
258: 
259:     private fun parseResponse(response: JsonNode?): PaperSearchResult {
260:         if (response == null) return PaperSearchResult(emptyList(), null, 0)
261:         val nextCursor = response.path("meta").path("next_cursor").asText(null)
262:         val totalResults = response.path("meta").path("count").asLong(0)
263:         val papers = response.path("results").mapNotNull { node -> parseWork(node) }
264:         return PaperSearchResult(papers, nextCursor, totalResults)
265:     }
266: 
267:     /**
268:      * I-1：单个 OpenAlex `work` 节点 → [PaperMetadata]（列表与精确单篇读取共用，作者解析规则只有这一份）。
269:      * 解析失败返回 null，绝不抛出（列表路径与精确路径都用同一种「无结果」表达）。
270:      */
271:     private fun parseWork(node: JsonNode): PaperMetadata? = try {
272:         val doi = node.path("doi").asText(null)?.removePrefix("https://doi.org/")
273:         val pmcId = extractPmcId(node)
274:         val pmid = node.path("ids").path("pmid")?.asText(null)
275:             ?.removePrefix("https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/")
276:         val pdfUrl = node.path("best_oa_location").path("pdf_url").asText(null)
277:         // c10（I-1）：locations 里的开放 PDF 是首选失效时的备用地址；只取 is_oa 的公开 http(s) 地址，
278:         // 去掉首选本身与重复项 —— 付费墙与非公开协议不成其为候选。
279:         val primaryPdfUrl = publicCandidateUrl(pdfUrl)
280:         val otherOaPdfUrls = node.path("locations")
281:             .filter { it.path("is_oa").asBoolean(false) }
282:             .mapNotNull { publicCandidateUrl(it.path("pdf_url").asText(null)) }
283:             .filter { it != primaryPdfUrl }
284:             .distinct()
285:         val authors = node.path("authorships").map { authorship ->
286:             val author = authorship.path("author")
287:             val orcid = author.path("orcid").asText(null)?.removePrefix("https://orcid.org/")
288:             val nameParts = splitDisplayName(author.path("display_name").asText(""))
289:             // I-1/I-3：展示机构只能是同一 authorship 里**唯一非空**的机构对象；
290:             // 多个不同机构一律 null（不任取第一项），affiliation 只是同一对象的原文线索。
291:             val institution = soleInstitution(authorship)
292:             val institutionName = institution?.path("display_name")?.asText(null)?.trim()?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }
293:             // I5a-3: 无 type/空串均产出 null。
294:             val institutionType = institution?.path("type")?.asText(null)?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }
295:             // I-2：国家取同一机构对象的 country_code，且必须能被地区表识别（未知 → null）。
296:             val institutionCountry = institution?.path("country_code")?.asText(null)?.trim()
297:                 ?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() && CountryContinentMapping.toRegion(it) != CountryContinentMapping.REGION_OTHER }
298:             PaperAuthor(
299:                 givenNames = nameParts.getOrNull(0), familyNames = nameParts.getOrNull(1),
300:                 orcidId = orcid, affiliation = institutionName,
301:                 isCorresponding = authorship.path("is_corresponding").asBoolean(false),
302:                 institutionType = institutionType,
303:                 openAlexAuthorId = normalizeOpenAlexAuthorId(author.path("id").asText(null)),
304:                 institutionName = institutionName,
305:                 institutionCountry = institutionCountry,
306:                 institutionSource = if (institutionName != null) INSTITUTION_SOURCE_OPENALEX else null
307:             )
308:         }
309:         PaperMetadata(pmcId = pmcId, pmid = pmid, doi = doi,
310:             title = node.path("title").asText(""), pubYear = node.path("publication_year").asInt(0),
311:             journal = node.path("primary_location").path("source").path("display_name").asText(null),
312:             authors = authors, source = sourceName, downloadUrl = pdfUrl,
313:             candidateDownloadUrls = otherOaPdfUrls)
314:     } catch (e: Exception) {
315:         log.debug("Failed to parse OpenAlex work: {}", e.message)
316:         null
317:     }
318: 
319:     /**
320:      * I-1（02）：精确单篇读取 —— `/works/doi:{doi}`。走既有 [OpenAlexRequestPolicy]（
321:      * `RequestKind.DISCOVERY` + `Operation.SINGLETON`，A10 起 `/works/{id}` 单实体为 0 credit）。
322:      * API 返回的 `doi` 与请求不一致时抛错（绝不做模糊后备）；查无此篇返回 null。
323:      */
324:     fun fetchWorkByDoi(doi: String): PaperMetadata? {
325:         val query = doi.trim().removePrefix("https://doi.org/").removePrefix("http://doi.org/").trim()
326:         if (query.isEmpty()) return null
327:         return fetchSingletonWork("doi:$query") { it.doi?.equals(query, ignoreCase = true) == true }
328:     }
329: 
330:     /**
331:      * I-1（02）：精确单篇读取 —— `/works/pmid:{pmid}`。语义与 [fetchWorkByDoi] 相同。
332:      */
333:     fun fetchWorkByPmid(pmid: String): PaperMetadata? {
334:         val query = pmid.trim().removePrefix("https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/").trim()
335:         if (query.isEmpty()) return null
336:         return fetchSingletonWork("pmid:$query") { it.pmid?.trim() == query }
337:     }
338: 
339:     /**
340:      * I-1：单实体读取的公共收口 —— 声明 `Operation.SINGLETON`，校验返回的来源 ID 与请求一致。
341:      * 404/空体视为「查无此篇」（null）；返回了别的来源 ID 抛错（调用方据此报 SOURCE_IDENTITY_CONFLICT）。
342:      */
343:     private fun fetchSingletonWork(locator: String, matches: (PaperMetadata) -> Boolean): PaperMetadata? {
344:         val url = "${properties.baseUrl}/works/$locator" + mailtoSuffix()
345:         val response = try {
346:             if (properties.requestDelayMs > 0) Thread.sleep(properties.requestDelayMs)
347:             getJson(RequestKind.DISCOVERY, Operation.SINGLETON, url) ?: return null
348:         } catch (e: OpenAlexBudgetDeferredException) {
349:             throw e
350:         } catch (e: HttpStatusCodeException) {
351:             val code = e.statusCode.value()
352:             if (code == 429 || code == 503) throw e
353:             if (code == 404) return null
354:             log.debug("OpenAlex single work read failed for {}: {} (HTTP {})", locator, e.message, code)
355:             throw e
356:         }
357:         val parsed = parseWork(response) ?: return null
358:         check(matches(parsed)) { "OpenAlex work read for $locator returned a different source id" }
359:         return parsed
360:     }
361: 
362:     /**
363:      * I-1：同一 authorship 里**唯一非空**的机构对象。
364:      * 有多个不同机构时返回 null（不任取第一项）；`display_name` 为空的对象不构成第二个机构，
365:      * 因此被忽略而不是被当成「唯一」的那个。
366:      */
367:     private fun soleInstitution(authorship: JsonNode): JsonNode? = authorship.path("institutions")
368:         .filter { it.path("display_name").asText(null)?.trim().orEmpty().isNotEmpty() }
369:         .singleOrNull()
370: 
371:     private fun extractPmcId(work: JsonNode): String? {
372:         val candidates = buildList {
373:             val id = work.path("ids").path("pmcid").asText(null)?.trim()
374:             if (id?.matches(Regex("PMC[0-9]+")) == true) add(id)
375:             else add(normalizePmcLocation(id))
376:             work.path("locations").forEach { add(normalizePmcLocation(it.path("landing_page_url").asText(null))) }
377:         }.filterNotNull().toSet()
378:         return candidates.singleOrNull()
379:     }
380: 
381:     private fun normalizePmcLocation(value: String?): String? {
382:         val candidate = value?.trim()?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() } ?: return null
383:         val uri = runCatching { URI(candidate) }.getOrNull() ?: return null
384:         if (uri.scheme !in setOf("http", "https")) return null
385:         val host = uri.host?.lowercase() ?: return null
386:         val path = uri.rawPath.orEmpty().trimEnd('/')
387:         val id = when {
388:             host in setOf("pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov", "www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov") &&
389:                 path.matches(Regex("/(?:pmc/)?articles/PMC[0-9]+")) -> path.substringAfterLast('/')
390:             host in setOf("europepmc.org", "www.europepmc.org") &&
391:                 path.matches(Regex("/(?:pmc/)?articles/PMC[0-9]+")) -> path.substringAfterLast('/')
392:             else -> null
393:         }
394:         return id
395:     }
396: 
397:     private fun splitDisplayName(name: String): List<String> {
398:         val commas = name.count { it == ',' }
399:         if (commas > 0) {
400:             if (commas != 1) return emptyList()
401:             val (family, given) = name.split(',', limit = 2)
402:             return if (family.isNotBlank() && given.isNotBlank()) listOf(given.trim(), family.trim()) else emptyList()
403:         }
404:         return name.split(" ", limit = 2)
405:     }
406: 
407:     /** Legacy entry point: the existing backfill callers are history enrichment (lowest priority). */
408:     fun enrichAuthor(openAlexAuthorId: String): AuthorEnrichment? =
409:         enrichAuthor(openAlexAuthorId, RequestKind.HISTORY_ENRICHMENT)
410: 
411:     fun enrichAuthor(openAlexAuthorId: String, kind: RequestKind): AuthorEnrichment? =
412:         (enrichAuthorOutcome(openAlexAuthorId, kind) as? EnrichmentOutcome.Success)?.data
413: 
414:     /**
415:      * I-3：单人作者补全的完整结果 —— 保留「基础事实有效、开关控制的最近论文/专利标题子请求失败」
416:      * 的细分（[EnrichmentOutcome.Success.titlesFailed]），附加数据可单独重试，不改变基础事实的可用性。
417:      * 404/网络错误与限流分开：前者 [EnrichmentOutcome.NotFound]/[EnrichmentOutcome.ApiError]，限流原样抛出。
418:      */
419:     private fun enrichAuthorOutcome(openAlexAuthorId: String, kind: RequestKind): EnrichmentOutcome {
420:         val url = "${properties.baseUrl}/authors/$openAlexAuthorId" +
421:             if (properties.politeEmail.isNotBlank()) "?mailto=${properties.politeEmail}" else ""
422:         return try {
423:             if (properties.requestDelayMs > 0) Thread.sleep(properties.requestDelayMs)
424:             val response = getJson(kind, Operation.SINGLETON, url) ?: return EnrichmentOutcome.NotFound
425:             enrichmentOutcome(response, kind)
426:         } catch (e: OpenAlexBudgetDeferredException) {
427:             throw e
428:         } catch (e: HttpStatusCodeException) {
429:             val code = e.statusCode.value()
430:             if (code == 429 || code == 503) throw e
431:             log.debug("OpenAlex author enrichment failed for {}: {} (HTTP {})", openAlexAuthorId, e.message, code)
432:             // V-3：404 是「查无此人」，其余 HTTP 失败可重试 —— 两者绝不混为一谈。
433:             if (code == 404) EnrichmentOutcome.NotFound else EnrichmentOutcome.ApiError("HTTP $code")
434:         } catch (e: Exception) {
435:             log.debug("OpenAlex author enrichment failed for {}: {}", openAlexAuthorId, e.message)
436:             EnrichmentOutcome.ApiError(e.message ?: "unknown error")
437:         }
438:     }
439: 
440:     /**
441:      * I-3：可单独重试的附加标题子请求（最近论文 / 专利共用一套开关与失败语义）。
442:      * - [titles] 为 null 且 [failed] = true：这次请求失败，可单独重试，绝不是「该作者没有作品」；
443:      * - [titles] 为空列表：请求成功但确实没有作品，不算失败；
444:      * - `NOT_REQUESTED`：按开关未请求（不计入失败）。
445:      * 限流（429/503）原样抛出，由批量调用方统一降级。
446:      */
447:     private fun fetchTitles(url: String, kind: RequestKind): TitlesFetch {
448:         val fullUrl = url + if (properties.politeEmail.isNotBlank()) "&mailto=${properties.politeEmail}" else ""
449:         return try {
450:             if (properties.requestDelayMs > 0) Thread.sleep(properties.requestDelayMs)
451:             val response = getJson(kind, Operation.LIST, fullUrl) ?: return TitlesFetch.failed()
452:             TitlesFetch.ok(
453:                 response.path("results")
454:                     .mapNotNull { it.path("title").asText(null)?.takeIf { title -> title.isNotBlank() } }
455:             )
456:         } catch (e: OpenAlexBudgetDeferredException) {
457:             throw e
458:         } catch (e: HttpStatusCodeException) {
459:             val code = e.statusCode.value()
460:             if (code == 429 || code == 503) throw e
461:             log.debug("OpenAlex titles fetch failed for {}: {} (HTTP {})", fullUrl, e.message, code)
462:             TitlesFetch.failed()
463:         } catch (e: Exception) {
464:             log.debug("OpenAlex titles fetch failed for {}: {}", fullUrl, e.message)
465:             TitlesFetch.failed()
466:         }
467:     }
468: 
469:     /** 最近论文标题：按发表年降序取前 [RECENT_TITLES_LIMIT] 条。 */
470:     private fun recentWorksUrl(worksUrl: String): String =
471:         "$worksUrl?sort=publication_year:desc&per_page=$RECENT_TITLES_LIMIT&select=title,publication_year"
472: 
473:     /** 专利标题：同样只取标题；专利开关默认关闭，本轮不接入专利数据。 */
474:     private fun patentWorksUrl(worksUrl: String): String =
475:         "$worksUrl?filter=type:patent&per_page=$RECENT_TITLES_LIMIT&select=title,publication_year"
476: 
477:     /** Legacy entry point: history enrichment (lowest priority). */
478:     fun enrichAuthorByOrcid(orcid: String): AuthorEnrichment? =
479:         enrichAuthorByOrcid(orcid, RequestKind.HISTORY_ENRICHMENT)
480: 
481:     /** 兼容包装：身份歧义与其余非成功结果一样返回 null（绝不任取一个作者）。 */
482:     fun enrichAuthorByOrcid(orcid: String, kind: RequestKind): AuthorEnrichment? {
483:         return when (val outcome = enrichAuthorByOrcidWithReason(orcid, kind)) {
484:             is EnrichmentOutcome.Success -> outcome.data
485:             else -> null
486:         }
487:     }
488: 
489:     /** Legacy entry point: history enrichment (lowest priority). */
490:     fun enrichAuthorByOrcidWithReason(orcid: String): EnrichmentOutcome =
491:         enrichAuthorByOrcidWithReason(orcid, RequestKind.HISTORY_ENRICHMENT)
492: 
493:     /**
494:      * I-1/I-2/I-3：单条 ORCID 查询 —— 与批量共用 [resolveOrcid] 的同一次响应完整性校验与身份分组，
495:      * 只有唯一作者身份才继续调用既有作者详情接口（详情请求的 404/限流口径不变）。
496:      */
497:     fun enrichAuthorByOrcidWithReason(orcid: String, kind: RequestKind): EnrichmentOutcome {
498:         val searchUrl = "${properties.baseUrl}/authors?filter=orcid:${canonicalOrcid(orcid)}" +
499:             "&per_page=$ORCID_PAGE_SIZE" + mailtoSuffix()
500:         return try {
501:             if (properties.requestDelayMs > 0) Thread.sleep(properties.requestDelayMs)
502:             val searchResponse = getJson(kind, Operation.LIST, searchUrl)
503:             when (val lookup = resolveOrcid(searchResponse, orcid)) {
504:                 // 唯一身份：仍走既有详情接口拿学术事实，不在列表节点上就地取数。
505:                 is OrcidLookup.Unique -> enrichAuthorOutcome(lookup.authorId, kind)
506:                 OrcidLookup.NotFound -> EnrichmentOutcome.NotFound
507:                 // I-3：同一 ORCID 对应多个作者 —— 绝不取第一条/最后一条，也不写任何学术事实。
508:                 OrcidLookup.Ambiguous -> EnrichmentOutcome.AmbiguousIdentity
509:                 is OrcidLookup.Invalid -> EnrichmentOutcome.ApiError(lookup.message)
510:             }
511:         } catch (e: OpenAlexBudgetDeferredException) {
512:             throw e
513:         } catch (e: HttpStatusCodeException) {
514:             val code = e.statusCode.value()
515:             if (code == 429 || code == 503) {
516:                 val retryAfter = e.responseHeaders?.getFirst("Retry-After")?.toLongOrNull()?.times(1000)
517:                 return EnrichmentOutcome.RateLimited(retryAfter)
518:             }
519:             log.debug("OpenAlex ORCID lookup failed for {}: {} (HTTP {})", orcid, e.message, code)
520:             EnrichmentOutcome.ApiError("HTTP $code: ${e.message}")
521:         } catch (e: Exception) {
522:             log.debug("OpenAlex ORCID lookup failed for {}: {}", orcid, e.message)
523:             EnrichmentOutcome.ApiError(e.message ?: "unknown error")
524:         }
525:     }
526: 
527:     /** Legacy entry point: history enrichment (lowest priority). */
528:     fun batchEnrichByOrcids(orcids: List<String>): Map<String, EnrichmentOutcome> =
529:         batchEnrichByOrcids(orcids, RequestKind.HISTORY_ENRICHMENT)
530: 
531:     /**
532:      * I-1/I-2/I-3：按 ORCID 批量查询 —— 与单条共用 [resolveOrcid] 的同一份完整性校验与身份判定。
533:      * 每个入参恰好一个结果：唯一作者 → 该节点的学术事实；0 个有效身份 → [EnrichmentOutcome.NotFound]；
534:      * 多个不同作者 ID → [EnrichmentOutcome.AmbiguousIdentity]（绝不任取一条）。
535:      * 请求按每页 [ORCID_PAGE_SIZE] 条发出；响应超过一页或有任何结构缺口时整体判
536:      * [ORCID_RESPONSE_INCOMPLETE]，不做无界翻页、也不降级成部分 Success/NotFound。
537:      */
538:     fun batchEnrichByOrcids(orcids: List<String>, kind: RequestKind): Map<String, EnrichmentOutcome> {
539:         if (orcids.isEmpty()) return emptyMap()
540:         val url = "${properties.baseUrl}/authors?filter=orcid:${orcids.joinToString("|") { canonicalOrcid(it) }}" +
541:             "&per_page=$ORCID_PAGE_SIZE" + mailtoSuffix()
542:         val response = try {
543:             getJson(kind, Operation.LIST, url)
544:         } catch (e: OpenAlexBudgetDeferredException) {
545:             throw e
546:         } catch (e: HttpStatusCodeException) {
547:             val code = e.statusCode.value()
548:             if (code == 429 || code == 503) {
549:                 val retryAfterHeader = e.responseHeaders?.getFirst("Retry-After")
550:                 log.warn(
551:                     "OpenAlex batch rate limited: status={}, Retry-After={}, body={}",
552:                     code, retryAfterHeader, e.responseBodyAsString.take(500)
553:                 )
554:                 return orcids.associateWith {
555:                     EnrichmentOutcome.RateLimited(retryAfterHeader?.toLongOrNull()?.times(1000))
556:                 }
557:             }
558:             return orcids.associateWith { EnrichmentOutcome.ApiError("HTTP $code") }
559:         } catch (e: Exception) {
560:             return orcids.associateWith { EnrichmentOutcome.ApiError(e.message ?: "unknown") }
561:         }
562: 
563:         val outcomes = LinkedHashMap<String, EnrichmentOutcome>()
564:         val lookups = HashMap<String, OrcidLookup>()
565:         val facts = HashMap<String, EnrichmentOutcome>()
566:         var rateLimited: EnrichmentOutcome.RateLimited? = null
567:         for (input in orcids) {
568:             val pending = rateLimited
569:             if (pending != null) {
570:                 // I-3：已经限流后不再为其余身份打接口，但每个入参仍必须有一个结果。
571:                 outcomes[input] = pending
572:                 continue
573:             }
574:             val key = canonicalOrcid(input)
575:             outcomes[input] = when (val lookup = lookups.getOrPut(key) { resolveOrcid(response, input) }) {
576:                 is OrcidLookup.Unique -> facts.getOrPut(key) {
577:                     try {
578:                         enrichmentOutcome(lookup.node, kind)
579:                     } catch (e: OpenAlexBudgetDeferredException) {
580:                         throw e
581:                     } catch (e: HttpStatusCodeException) {
582:                         // I-3：限流只影响开关控制的附加标题 —— 基础事实照常返回，其余身份标限流、不再打接口。
583:                         val code = e.statusCode.value()
584:                         if (code == 429 || code == 503) {
585:                             rateLimited = EnrichmentOutcome.RateLimited(
586:                                 e.responseHeaders?.getFirst("Retry-After")?.toLongOrNull()?.times(1000)
587:                             )
588:                         }
589:                         EnrichmentOutcome.Success(parseAuthorBase(lookup.node), titlesFailed = true)
590:                     }
591:                 }
592:                 OrcidLookup.NotFound -> EnrichmentOutcome.NotFound
593:                 OrcidLookup.Ambiguous -> EnrichmentOutcome.AmbiguousIdentity
594:                 is OrcidLookup.Invalid -> EnrichmentOutcome.ApiError(lookup.message)
595:             }
596:         }
597:         return outcomes
598:     }
599: 
600:     /** Legacy entry point: history enrichment (lowest priority). */
601:     fun batchEnrichByAuthorIds(authorIds: List<String>): Map<String, EnrichmentOutcome> =
602:         batchEnrichByAuthorIds(authorIds, RequestKind.HISTORY_ENRICHMENT)
603: 
604:     /**
605:      * I-1：按可信 OpenAlex 作者 ID 批量查询（`filter=openalex:A…|A…`），用于覆盖「有作者 ID 但没有 ORCID」的人。
606:      * 调用方保证每批 ≤100 个不同身份；结果对每个传入身份逐一给出（响应中缺失 = [EnrichmentOutcome.NotFound]）。
607:      * 响应里的 `id` 必须经 [normalizeOpenAlexAuthorId] 才是可信作者身份，其他形状绝不参与匹配。
608:      */
609:     fun batchEnrichByAuthorIds(authorIds: List<String>, kind: RequestKind): Map<String, EnrichmentOutcome> {
610:         if (authorIds.isEmpty()) return emptyMap()
611:         val url = "${properties.baseUrl}/authors?filter=openalex:${authorIds.joinToString("|")}" +
612:             "&per_page=${authorIds.size}" + mailtoSuffix()
613:         return batchEnrichIdentities(authorIds, url, kind) { node ->
614:             normalizeOpenAlexAuthorId(node.path("id").asText(null))
615:         }
616:     }
617: 
618:     private fun mailtoSuffix(): String =
619:         if (properties.politeEmail.isNotBlank()) "&mailto=${properties.politeEmail}" else ""
620: 
621:     /**
622:      * I-1/I-2：ORCID 列表响应 → 该 ORCID 的作者身份判定（单条与批量共用同一次分组，口径不漂移）。
623:      *
624:      * 先校验响应完整性：`results` 必须是数组，且 `meta.count` 必须是非负整数、等于返回数组长度；
625:      * 截断/缺 count/结构损坏一律 [OrcidLookup.Invalid]（绝不当成「唯一」或「未找到」）。
626:      * 再按规范化作者 ID 去重：0 个有效身份 → [OrcidLookup.NotFound]，恰好 1 个 → [OrcidLookup.Unique]，
627:      * 超过 1 个 → [OrcidLookup.Ambiguous]（响应顺序、相同姓名、引用量都不改变该判定）。
628:      * 关联节点缺可信作者 ID、或同一作者 ID 的多个节点给出不一致的学术事实 → [OrcidLookup.Invalid]。
629:      */
630:     private fun resolveOrcid(response: JsonNode?, orcid: String): OrcidLookup {
631:         val results = response?.path("results")?.takeIf { it.isArray }
632:             ?: return OrcidLookup.Invalid(ORCID_RESPONSE_INCOMPLETE)
633:         val count = response.path("meta").path("count")
634:         if (!count.isIntegralNumber || count.asLong() < 0 || count.asLong() != results.size().toLong()) {
635:             return OrcidLookup.Invalid(ORCID_RESPONSE_INCOMPLETE)
636:         }
637:         val target = canonicalOrcid(orcid)
638:         if (target.isEmpty()) return OrcidLookup.NotFound
639:         val related = results.filter { canonicalOrcid(it.path("orcid").asText(null)) == target }
640:         if (related.isEmpty()) return OrcidLookup.NotFound
641:         val authorIds = related.map { normalizeOpenAlexAuthorId(it.path("id").asText(null)) }
642:         if (authorIds.any { it == null }) {
643:             // 关联节点没有可信作者 ID：这次说不清是谁，也不能退回「查无此人」。
644:             return OrcidLookup.Invalid(ORCID_NODE_WITHOUT_AUTHOR_ID)
645:         }
646:         val identities = authorIds.filterNotNull().distinct()
647:         if (identities.size > 1) return OrcidLookup.Ambiguous
648:         if (related.map { parseAuthorBase(it) }.distinct().size > 1) {
649:             // 同一作者 ID 的多个节点给出冲突的学术事实：不靠响应顺序挑一份内容。
650:             return OrcidLookup.Invalid(ORCID_NODE_CONTENT_CONFLICT)
651:         }
652:         return OrcidLookup.Unique(identities.single(), related.first())
653:     }
654: 
655:     /** I-1/I-2：ORCID 响应的身份判定结果 —— 唯一身份 / 未找到 / 身份歧义 / 响应不可用（固定原因码）。 */
656:     private sealed class OrcidLookup {
657:         /** 唯一作者身份：[authorId] 供详情查询，[node] 供批量路径就地取学术事实。 */
658:         data class Unique(val authorId: String, val node: JsonNode) : OrcidLookup()
659:         object NotFound : OrcidLookup()
660:         object Ambiguous : OrcidLookup()
661:         data class Invalid(val message: String) : OrcidLookup()
662:     }
663: 
664:     /**
665:      * 单条/批量 ORCID 列表请求共用的批量查询：一次请求拿回多个作者，用 [identityOf] 把响应归因回传入身份。
666:      * 结果覆盖全部入参（缺失 = NotFound，绝不猜测归属）；限流（429/503）标
667:      * [EnrichmentOutcome.RateLimited]，其余 HTTP/网络失败标 [EnrichmentOutcome.ApiError]。
668:      *
669:      * 只服务「显式作者 ID」查询：A ID 本身就是被请求的身份，响应里非规范形状的节点一律不参与匹配
670:      * （`filter=openalex:A…` 的语义保持不变）。ORCID 身份有唯一性/完整性要求，走 [batchEnrichByOrcids]。
671:      */
672:     private fun batchEnrichIdentities(
673:         identities: List<String>,
674:         url: String,
675:         kind: RequestKind,
676:         identityOf: (JsonNode) -> String?
677:     ): Map<String, EnrichmentOutcome> {
678:         val response = try {
679:             getJson(kind, Operation.LIST, url)
680:         } catch (e: OpenAlexBudgetDeferredException) {
681:             throw e
682:         } catch (e: HttpStatusCodeException) {
683:             val code = e.statusCode.value()
684:             if (code == 429 || code == 503) {
685:                 val retryAfterHeader = e.responseHeaders?.getFirst("Retry-After")
686:                 log.warn(
687:                     "OpenAlex batch rate limited: status={}, Retry-After={}, body={}",
688:                     code, retryAfterHeader, e.responseBodyAsString.take(500)
689:                 )
690:                 return identities.associateWith {
691:                     EnrichmentOutcome.RateLimited(retryAfterHeader?.toLongOrNull()?.times(1000))
692:                 }
693:             }
694:             return identities.associateWith { EnrichmentOutcome.ApiError("HTTP $code") }
695:         } catch (e: Exception) {
696:             return identities.associateWith { EnrichmentOutcome.ApiError(e.message ?: "unknown") }
697:         }
698: 
699:         val foundEntries = mutableListOf<Pair<String, JsonNode>>()
700:         response?.path("results")?.forEach { node ->
701:             val identity = identityOf(node) ?: return@forEach
702:             foundEntries += identity to node
703:         }
704: 
705:         val results = mutableMapOf<String, EnrichmentOutcome>()
706:         for (identity in identities) {
707:             if (foundEntries.none { it.first == identity }) {
708:                 results[identity] = EnrichmentOutcome.NotFound
709:             }
710:         }
711: 
712:         for ((index, entry) in foundEntries.withIndex()) {
713:             val (identity, node) = entry
714:             try {
715:                 results[identity] = enrichmentOutcome(node, kind)
716:             } catch (e: OpenAlexBudgetDeferredException) {
717:                 throw e
718:             } catch (e: HttpStatusCodeException) {
719:                 // I-3：限流只影响开关控制的附加标题 —— 基础事实照常返回，其余身份标限流、不再打接口。
720:                 results[identity] = EnrichmentOutcome.Success(parseAuthorBase(node), titlesFailed = true)
721:                 val code = e.statusCode.value()
722:                 if (code != 429 && code != 503) continue
723:                 val rateLimited = EnrichmentOutcome.RateLimited(
724:                     e.responseHeaders?.getFirst("Retry-After")?.toLongOrNull()?.times(1000)
725:                 )
726:                 for (remaining in foundEntries.drop(index + 1)) {
727:                     if (remaining.first !in results) results[remaining.first] = rateLimited
728:                 }
729:                 break
730:             }
731:         }
732: 
733:         return identities.associateWith { results[it] ?: EnrichmentOutcome.NotFound }
734:     }
735: 
736:     /**
737:      * I-3：基础学术事实 —— hIndex / 引用数 / 论文数 / 研究方向 / 学科 / 最近发表年份。
738:      * 全部来自作者节点本身，与论文/专利开关无关，缺失一律为 null（null 不覆盖存量值）。
739:      */
740:     private fun parseAuthorBase(node: JsonNode): AuthorEnrichment {
741:         val topicsNode = node.path("topics").takeIf { it.isArray }
742:         val topTopics = topicsNode?.sortedByDescending { it.path("count").asInt(0) }?.take(5)
743:         val topics = topTopics?.mapNotNull { it.path("display_name").asText(null) }
744:         val researchFieldIds = topTopics?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }?.map { topic ->
745:             val fieldId = topic.path("field").path("id")
746:             val raw = if (fieldId.isTextual) fieldId.asText() else null
747:             raw?.let { FIELD_ID_PATTERN.matchEntire(it)?.groupValues?.get(1) }
748:                 ?.trimStart('0')?.ifEmpty { "0" }
749:         }?.takeIf { ids -> ids.all { it != null } }
750:             ?.filterNotNull()?.distinct()?.sortedWith(compareBy<String> { it.length }.thenBy { it })
751:         val disciplineCategory = resolveDisciplineCategory(topicsNode)
752:         // I5a-2/I5a-7: 取 last_known_institutions 第一项的 type（与 works 路径的署名机构不同源）；
753:         // I5a-3: 数组为空、无 type 键、type 为空串均产出 null。
754:         val institutionType = node.path("last_known_institutions").firstOrNull()
755:             ?.path("type")?.asText(null)?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }
756:         // I1-1/I1-2：取 works_count > 0 的最大 year；数组顺序不可依赖（CP-1 实测为升序）。
757:         // I1-3：无该键、空数组、或全部 works_count = 0 时为 null。
758:         val lastPublicationYear = node.path("counts_by_year")
759:             .filter { it.path("works_count").asInt(0) > 0 }
760:             .mapNotNull { it.path("year").let { y -> if (y.isInt) y.asInt() else null } }
761:             .maxOrNull()
762:         return AuthorEnrichment(
763:             hIndex = node.path("summary_stats").path("h_index").let { if (it.isInt) it.asInt() else null },
764:             citationCount = node.path("cited_by_count").let { if (it.isInt) it.asInt() else null },
765:             worksCount = node.path("works_count").let { if (it.isInt) it.asInt() else null },
766:             topics = topics,
767:             disciplineCategory = disciplineCategory,
768:             institutionType = institutionType,
769:             lastPublicationYear = lastPublicationYear,
770:             researchFieldIds = researchFieldIds
771:         )
772:     }
773: 
774:     /**
775:      * I-3：基础事实 + 开关控制的最近论文/专利标题。单人与批量共用同一套开关与失败语义：
776:      * 关闭的开关不发请求（[TitlesFetch.NOT_REQUESTED]），打开的开关失败只标 [ParsedAuthorEnrichment.titlesFailed]，
777:      * 绝不因此丢掉基础事实。
778:      */
779:     private fun parseAuthorEnrichment(node: JsonNode, kind: RequestKind): ParsedAuthorEnrichment {
780:         val base = parseAuthorBase(node)
781:         val worksUrl = node.path("works_api_url").asText(null)
782:         val recentWorks = if (worksUrl != null && properties.fetchWorksEnabled) {
783:             fetchTitles(recentWorksUrl(worksUrl), kind)
784:         } else {
785:             TitlesFetch.NOT_REQUESTED
786:         }
787:         val patents = if (worksUrl != null && properties.fetchPatentsEnabled) {
788:             fetchTitles(patentWorksUrl(worksUrl), kind)
789:         } else {
790:             TitlesFetch.NOT_REQUESTED
791:         }
792:         return ParsedAuthorEnrichment(
793:             data = base.copy(
794:                 recentWorkTitles = recentWorks.titles?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() },
795:                 patentTitles = patents.titles?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }
796:             ),
797:             titlesFailed = recentWorks.failed || patents.failed
798:         )
799:     }
800: 
801:     /** 基础事实 + 附加标题的成败标志（I-3：标题可单独重试，空结果不算失败）。 */
802:     private data class ParsedAuthorEnrichment(val data: AuthorEnrichment, val titlesFailed: Boolean)
803: 
804:     /** 作者节点 → 结果；限流（429/503）与额度延期照原样抛出，由调用方决定降级方式。 */
805:     private fun enrichmentOutcome(node: JsonNode, kind: RequestKind): EnrichmentOutcome {
806:         val parsed = parseAuthorEnrichment(node, kind)
807:         return EnrichmentOutcome.Success(parsed.data, parsed.titlesFailed)
808:     }
809: 
810:     private fun resolveDisciplineCategory(topicsNode: JsonNode?): String? {
811:         if (topicsNode == null) return null
812:         var stem = 0
813:         var humanities = 0
814:         for (topic in topicsNode) {
815:             when (topic.path("domain").path("display_name").asText(null)) {
816:                 "Physical Sciences", "Life Sciences", "Health Sciences" -> stem += topic.path("count").asInt(0)
817:                 "Social Sciences" -> humanities += topic.path("count").asInt(0)
818:             }
819:         }
820:         if (stem == 0 && humanities == 0) return null
821:         return if (stem >= humanities) "STEM" else "HUMANITIES"
822:     }
823: }
824: 
825: /**
826:  * I-1: OpenAlex 作者 ID 的规范形式是 `A` + 数字（API 返回 `https://openalex.org/A123…`）。
827:  * 其他形状的值不是可信作者身份：既不写 externalIds，也不参与任何主键/文档定位。
828:  */
829: internal fun normalizeOpenAlexAuthorId(raw: String?): String? {
830:     val bare = raw?.trim()?.removePrefix("https://openalex.org/")?.removePrefix("http://openalex.org/")?.trim()
831:     return bare?.takeIf { OPENALEX_AUTHOR_ID_PATTERN.matches(it) }
832: }
833: 
834: private val OPENALEX_AUTHOR_ID_PATTERN = Regex("A\\d+")
835: private val FIELD_ID_PATTERN = Regex("(?:https://openalex\\.org/fields/)?([0-9]+)")
836: 
837: /**
838:  * I-1/I-2：ORCID 的规范比较形式 —— 去掉首尾空白与 ORCID URL 前缀；缺失/空串规范化为空串
839:  * （空串不是有效身份，绝不用于身份匹配）。
840:  */
841: private fun canonicalOrcid(raw: String?): String =
842:     raw.orEmpty().trim().removePrefix("https://orcid.org/").removePrefix("http://orcid.org/").trim()
843: 
844: /** I-2：ORCID 列表查询的页大小 —— 与 `meta.count` 一起证明响应没有被截断。 */
845: private const val ORCID_PAGE_SIZE = 200
846: 
847: /** I-2：ORCID 响应不完整（截断、缺 `meta.count`、结构损坏）时的固定原因码。 */
848: private const val ORCID_RESPONSE_INCOMPLETE = "ORCID_RESPONSE_INCOMPLETE"
849: 
850: /** I-1：请求 ORCID 关联到的节点没有可信作者 ID 时的固定原因码。 */
851: private const val ORCID_NODE_WITHOUT_AUTHOR_ID = "ORCID_NODE_WITHOUT_AUTHOR_ID"
852: 
853: /** I-1：同一作者 ID 的多个节点学术事实冲突（不能靠顺序挑一份）时的固定原因码。 */
854: private const val ORCID_NODE_CONTENT_CONFLICT = "ORCID_NODE_CONTENT_CONFLICT"
855: 
856: sealed class EnrichmentOutcome {
857:     /**
858:      * 基础事实可用。[titlesFailed] = true 表示开关控制的最近论文/专利标题子请求失败（I-3：可单独重试），
859:      * 基础事实不受影响；空结果不算失败。
860:      */
861:     data class Success(val data: AuthorEnrichment, val titlesFailed: Boolean = false) : EnrichmentOutcome()
862:     object NotFound : EnrichmentOutcome()
863: 
864:     /**
865:      * I-3：同一 ORCID 在完整响应里对应多个不同作者 ID，本次无法唯一选择作者身份。
866:      * 它既不是成功也不是「查无此人」：不写任何学术事实，也不当作可无限即时重试的失败。
867:      */
868:     object AmbiguousIdentity : EnrichmentOutcome()
869:     data class ApiError(val message: String) : EnrichmentOutcome()
870:     data class RateLimited(val retryAfterMs: Long? = null) : EnrichmentOutcome()
871: }
872: 
873: /**
874:  * I-3：附加标题子请求的结果 —— 把「请求失败（可重试）」与「请求成功但没有作品」分开：
875:  * `titles = null, failed = true` 是失败；`titles = emptyList(), failed = false` 是真实的空结果；
876:  * `NOT_REQUESTED` 表示按开关未请求。
877:  */
878: private data class TitlesFetch(val titles: List<String>?, val failed: Boolean) {
879:     companion object {
880:         val NOT_REQUESTED = TitlesFetch(null, false)
881:         fun ok(titles: List<String>) = TitlesFetch(titles, false)
882:         fun failed() = TitlesFetch(null, true)
883:     }
884: }
885: 
886: /** I-3：最近论文/专利标题的取条数上界。 */
887: private const val RECENT_TITLES_LIMIT = 3
888: 
889: /**
890:  * c10（I-1）：单篇论文全部全文地址共享的总时限（与尝试上限是两个独立约束，超时按 TIMEOUT 上报，
891:  * 不冒充来源耗尽）。I-4：不随本次改动放大，仍按方案给定的 90 秒。
892:  */
893: internal const val FULLTEXT_PER_PAPER_DEADLINE_MS = 90_000L
894: 
895: /** c10（I-1）：单篇论文最多尝试的全文**地址（URL）**数 —— 第三个失败后不再访问第四个。 */
896: internal const val MAX_FULLTEXT_ADDRESSES = 3
897: 
898: data class AuthorEnrichment(
899:     val hIndex: Int?,
900:     val citationCount: Int?,
901:     val worksCount: Int?,
902:     val topics: List<String>? = null,
903:     val recentWorkTitles: List<String>? = null,
904:     val patentTitles: List<String>? = null,
905:     val disciplineCategory: String? = null,
906:     val institutionType: String? = null,
907:     val lastPublicationYear: Int? = null,
908:     val researchFieldIds: List<String>? = null
909: )

### src/main/kotlin/com/weibo/talentintroduction/discovery/service/OrcidDataSource.kt
1: package com.weibo.talentintroduction.discovery.service
2: 
3: import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode
4: import com.weibo.talentintroduction.config.OrcidProperties
5: import com.weibo.talentintroduction.discovery.domain.AuthorEmail
6: import com.weibo.talentintroduction.discovery.domain.EmailExtractionOutcome
7: import com.weibo.talentintroduction.discovery.domain.PaperMetadata
8: import com.weibo.talentintroduction.discovery.domain.PaperSearchCriteria
9: import com.weibo.talentintroduction.discovery.domain.PaperSearchResult
10: import com.weibo.talentintroduction.discovery.domain.SubjectScopeCatalog
11: import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory
12: import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.condition.ConditionalOnProperty
13: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service
14: import org.springframework.web.client.RestTemplate
15: import java.net.URI
16: import java.net.URLEncoder
17: 
18: @Service
19: @ConditionalOnProperty(prefix = "talent-introduction.expert-discovery.orcid", name = ["enabled"], havingValue = "true")
20: class OrcidDataSource(
21:     private val restTemplate: RestTemplate,
22:     private val properties: OrcidProperties
23: ) {
24: 
25:     private val log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(OrcidDataSource::class.java)
26: 
27:     val sourceName = "ORCID"
28:     val maxRecordsPerRun get() = properties.maxRecordsPerRun
29: 
30:     data class OrcidRecord(
31:         val orcidId: String,
32:         val givenNames: String?,
33:         val familyNames: String?,
34:         val emails: List<String>,
35:         val institutionName: String?,
36:         val country: String?
37:     )
38: 
39:     /**
40:      * I-2: 一次 ORCID 检索的结果。
41:      *
42:      * - [records] 只含带公开邮箱、可进入收录流程的记录（邮箱过滤保持改动前语义）。
43:      * - [rawCount] 是本次原始返回条数（**含无公开邮箱者**）：offset 只能按它推进，
44:      *   「一整页都没有公开邮箱」时也必须前进，否则会在原地反复首批。
45:      * - [nextCursor] 是下一次请求该用的分片游标；`null` 表示所有分片已遍历（可判穷尽）。
46:      */
47:     data class OrcidSearchPage(val records: List<OrcidRecord>, val nextCursor: String?, val rawCount: Int)
48: 
49:     /**
50:      * I-2: ORCID 分片游标 = 「主题种子分片 + offset」。手动关键词只有一个分片（topic 恒为 0），
51:      * 默认研发范围按目录主题种子逐片检索；分片主题必须随游标持久化，跨运行才知道该查哪一片。
52:      */
53:     data class OrcidCursor(val topic: Int, val offset: Int) {
54:         fun encode(): String = "$topic|$offset"
55: 
56:         companion object {
57:             /** 兼容 c2 写下的裸数字 offset（当时只有一个分片）；其余非法值按「无游标」处理。 */
58:             fun parse(value: String?): OrcidCursor? {
59:                 if (value.isNullOrBlank()) return null
60:                 val bare = value.toIntOrNull()
61:                 if (bare != null) return if (bare < 0) null else OrcidCursor(0, bare)
62:                 val parts = value.split('|')
63:                 if (parts.size != 2) return null
64:                 val topic = parts[0].toIntOrNull() ?: return null
65:                 val offset = parts[1].toIntOrNull() ?: return null
66:                 if (topic < 0 || offset < 0) return null
67:                 return OrcidCursor(topic, offset)
68:             }
69:         }
70:     }
71: 
72:     companion object {
73:         /** ORCID 公开检索的关键词字段名；主题种子只作检索约束，不参与国籍/机构判定（I-3）。 */
74:         private const val ORCID_KEYWORD_FIELD = "keyword"
75: 
76:         /**
77:          * I-2/I-4: ORCID Public API 检索窗口上限 —— 公开检索只可遍历 start 0..9999 共 10000 条
78:          * （docs: info.orcid.org「API Tutorial: Searching the ORCID registry」，Member API 不受此限）。
79:          * 达到边界即停止该分片并切下一分片，绝不无限递增 offset。
80:          */
81:         const val MAX_OFFSET = 9999
82:     }
83: 
84:     fun searchOrcidPage(criteria: PaperSearchCriteria): OrcidSearchPage {
85:         val shards = queryShards(criteria)
86:         if (shards.isEmpty()) {
87:             // I-2: 无关键词且无 scope 主题种子 = 明确跳过（不发请求，也不谎报穷尽/失败）。
88:             log.info("[ORCID] 无关键词且无学科范围主题种子，本次跳过检索")
89:             return OrcidSearchPage(emptyList(), null, 0)
90:         }
91: 
92:         val cursor = OrcidCursor.parse(criteria.cursor) ?: OrcidCursor(0, 0)
93:         val topic = cursor.topic.coerceIn(0, shards.size - 1)
94:         val query = shards[topic]
95:         val rows = minOf(criteria.pageSize, properties.maxRecordsPerRun).coerceAtLeast(1)
96: 
97:         val encodedQuery = URLEncoder.encode(query, "UTF-8")
98:         val url = "${properties.baseUrl}/expanded-search/?q=$encodedQuery&start=${cursor.offset}&rows=$rows"
99: 
100:         val response = try {
101:             if (properties.requestDelayMs > 0) Thread.sleep(properties.requestDelayMs)
102:             restTemplate.getForObject(URI.create(url), JsonNode::class.java)
103:         } catch (e: Exception) {
104:             log.error("ORCID search failed: {}", e.message)
105:             throw e
106:         }
107: 
108:         val rawCount = response?.path("expanded-result")?.size() ?: 0
109:         val nextOffset = cursor.offset + rawCount
110:         // I-2: 页空或原始返回不足一页即分片结束；I-4: offset 越过公开检索窗口即停止该分片。
111:         val shardFinished = rawCount < rows
112:         val windowLimit = !shardFinished && nextOffset > MAX_OFFSET
113:         val next = if (shardFinished || windowLimit) {
114:             if (topic + 1 < shards.size) OrcidCursor(topic + 1, 0) else null
115:         } else {
116:             OrcidCursor(topic, nextOffset)
117:         }
118:         if (windowLimit) {
119:             log.warn(
120:                 "ORCID 分片 topic={} 触达公开检索窗口 {}：本分片停止，未覆盖尾部 {} 条，改从下一分片继续",
121:                 topic, MAX_OFFSET, (MAX_OFFSET + 1 - nextOffset).coerceAtLeast(0)
122:             )
123:         }
124:         return OrcidSearchPage(parseOrcidRecords(response), next?.encode(), rawCount)
125:     }
126: 
127:     /**
128:      * I-2: 只关心记录集合、不推进分页的调用方（如按 orcid 反查邮箱）用这个视图；
129:      * 分页推进必须走 [searchOrcidPage]，否则拿不到 [OrcidSearchPage.rawCount] 与 [OrcidSearchPage.nextCursor]。
130:      */
131:     fun searchOrcidRecords(criteria: PaperSearchCriteria): List<OrcidRecord> = searchOrcidPage(criteria).records
132: 
133:     /**
134:      * I-2/I-3: 检索分片。
135:      * 操作端关键词永远优先（保持改动前的引号 AND 语义，只有一个分片）；关键词为空时用目录里的 scope
136:      * 主题种子，每个种子一片，检索 ORCID 的公开研究关键词字段，不把公司名或机构地址当国籍。
137:      */
138:     private fun queryShards(criteria: PaperSearchCriteria): List<String> = when {
139:         criteria.keywords.isNotEmpty() ->
140:             listOf(criteria.keywords.joinToString(" AND ") { "\"$it\"" })
141:         else ->
142:             SubjectScopeCatalog.orcidSeedKeywords(criteria.subjectScope)
143:                 .map { "$ORCID_KEYWORD_FIELD:\"$it\"" }
144:     }
145: 
146:     private fun parseOrcidRecords(response: JsonNode?): List<OrcidRecord> {
147:         if (response == null) return emptyList()
148: 
149:         val results = response.path("expanded-result")
150:         return (0 until results.size()).mapNotNull { i ->
151:             try {
152:                 val node = results[i]
153:                 val orcidId = node.path("orcid-id").asText(null) ?: return@mapNotNull null
154: 
155:                 val emails = mutableListOf<String>()
156:                 val emailNodes = node.path("email")
157:                 if (emailNodes.isArray) {
158:                     for (j in 0 until emailNodes.size()) {
159:                         val email = emailNodes[j].asText(null)
160:                         if (!email.isNullOrBlank()) emails.add(email)
161:                     }
162:                 }
163: 
164:                 if (emails.isEmpty()) null
165:                 else {
166:                     // I-1：`institution-name` 是**没有主次之分的集合**，只有恰好一家非空机构时才可展示；
167:                     // 零项或多家一律 null，绝不用 `firstOrNull()` 按数组顺序任选第一家当主机构。
168:                     val institutions = node.path("institution-name")
169:                         .mapNotNull { it.asText(null)?.trim()?.takeIf { name -> name.isNotEmpty() } }
170:                         .distinct()
171:                     OrcidRecord(
172:                         orcidId = orcidId,
173:                         givenNames = node.path("given-names").asText(null),
174:                         familyNames = node.path("family-names").asText(null),
175:                         emails = emails,
176:                         institutionName = institutions.singleOrNull(),
177:                         country = null
178:                     )
179:                 }
180:             } catch (e: Exception) {
181:                 log.debug("Failed to parse ORCID record: {}", e.message)
182:                 null
183:             }
184:         }
185:     }
186: 
187:     fun orcidRecordToAuthorEmails(record: OrcidRecord): List<AuthorEmail> {
188:         return record.emails.map { email ->
189:             AuthorEmail(
190:                 email = email,
191:                 givenNames = record.givenNames,
192:                 familyNames = record.familyNames,
193:                 isCorresponding = false,
194:                 affiliation = record.institutionName,
195:                 orcidId = record.orcidId,
196:                 identityEvidence = "ORCID_RECORD_SHA256:" + com.weibo.talentintroduction.expert.domain.DiscoveryIdentity.hash(
197:                     listOf(record.orcidId, record.givenNames, record.familyNames, email, record.institutionName).joinToString("\u0000"))
198:             )
199:         }
200:     }
201: }

### src/main/kotlin/com/weibo/talentintroduction/discovery/service/OrcidVerificationEvidenceClient.kt
1: package com.weibo.talentintroduction.discovery.service
2: 
3: import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode
4: import com.weibo.talentintroduction.config.DiscoveryVerificationProperties
5: import com.weibo.talentintroduction.discovery.domain.*
6: import com.weibo.talentintroduction.expert.domain.DiscoveryIdentity
7: import com.weibo.talentintroduction.expert.domain.ExpertProfile
8: import org.springframework.http.HttpMethod
9: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service
10: import org.springframework.web.client.RestTemplate
11: import java.net.URI
12: import java.time.Clock
13: import java.time.Instant
14: 
15: /** Constructed from the original profile, not from an Apollo hit or a same-name ORCID search. */
16: data class VerificationSnapshot(val identityHash: String, val factFingerprint: String, val profile: ExpertProfile) {
17:     init { verificationHash(identityHash); verificationHash(factFingerprint) }
18:     val identityValid: Boolean get() = profile.identityVerification?.status == "VERIFIED" && DiscoveryIdentity.allowed(profile)
19:     val fullName: String get() = listOfNotNull(profile.givenNames, profile.familyNames).joinToString(" ")
20:     val boundOrcid: String? get() {
21:         if (!identityValid) return null
22:         val proofId = normalizeOrcid(profile.identityVerification?.orcid) ?: return null
23:         val profileId = normalizeOrcid(profile.orcidId)
24:         val externalNode = profile.externalIds?.let {
25:             runCatching { com.fasterxml.jackson.module.kotlin.jacksonObjectMapper().readTree(it) }.getOrNull() ?: return null
26:         }
27:         val external = externalNode?.path("orcid")?.takeIf { !it.isMissingNode && !it.isNull }?.let {
28:             if (!it.isTextual) return null
29:             normalizeOrcid(it.asText()) ?: return null
30:         }
31:         if ((profileId != null && profileId != proofId) || (external != null && external != proofId)) return null
32:         return proofId
33:     }
34: }
35: 
36: fun normalizeOrcid(value: String?): String? {
37:     val id = value?.removePrefix("https://orcid.org/")?.removePrefix("http://orcid.org/") ?: return null
38:     if (!id.matches(Regex("[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{3}[0-9X]"))) return null
39:     val digits = id.replace("-", "")
40:     var total = 0
41:     digits.take(15).forEach { total = (total + (it - '0')) * 2 }
42:     val check = (12 - total % 11) % 11
43:     return id.takeIf { digits.last() == if (check == 10) 'X' else ('0'.code + check).toChar() }
44: }
45: 
46: data class OrcidEvidenceDocument(val endpoint: String, val body: JsonNode, val responseSha256: String, val fetchedAt: Instant)
47: data class OrcidEvidenceRead(val documents: List<OrcidEvidenceDocument>, val complete: Boolean,
48:     val reasonCodes: List<String>, val failure: VerificationHttpResult<Nothing>? = null)
49: 
50: /** Durable, bounded endpoint result. Missing public records are completed endpoints too. */
51: data class OrcidEndpointCompletion(val endpoint: String, val document: OrcidEvidenceDocument? = null)
52: 
53: @Service
54: class OrcidVerificationEvidenceClient(private val properties: DiscoveryVerificationProperties,
55:     http: RestTemplate, private val clock: Clock = Clock.systemUTC()) {
56:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired
57:     constructor(properties: DiscoveryVerificationProperties) : this(properties, verificationRestTemplate(properties), Clock.systemUTC())
58:     private val transport = VerificationHttp(http, clock)
59: 
60:     /** Existing callers retain aggregate semantics; durable callers acknowledge each response before another request. */
61:     fun read(snapshot: VerificationSnapshot, reserve: (String) -> RequestPermit?): OrcidEvidenceRead =
62:         read(snapshot, emptyList(), { _, _, _, _ -> }, reserve)
63: 
64:     fun read(snapshot: VerificationSnapshot, completed: List<OrcidEndpointCompletion>,
65:         onResponse: (String, RequestPermit, OrcidEvidenceDocument?, VerificationHttpResult<*>) -> Unit,
66:         reserve: (String) -> RequestPermit?): OrcidEvidenceRead {
67:         val orcid = snapshot.boundOrcid ?: return OrcidEvidenceRead(emptyList(), false, listOf("IDENTITY_EVIDENCE_MISSING"))
68:         val documents = ArrayList<OrcidEvidenceDocument>()
69:         val endpoints = java.util.ArrayDeque(listOf("person", "employments", "educations"))
70:         require(completed.map { it.endpoint }.distinct().size == completed.size)
71:         completed.forEach { require(allowedEndpoint(it.endpoint) && (it.document == null || it.document.endpoint == it.endpoint)) }
72:         val saved = completed.associateBy { it.endpoint }
73:         var requests = 0
74:         while (endpoints.isNotEmpty()) {
75:             val endpoint = endpoints.removeFirst()
76:             require(allowedEndpoint(endpoint))
77:             val cached = saved[endpoint]
78:             val doc: OrcidEvidenceDocument?
79:             if (cached != null) {
80:                 doc = cached.document
81:             } else {
82:                 if (requests + completed.size >= minOf(12, properties.maxRequestsPerItem))
83:                     return OrcidEvidenceRead(documents, false, listOf("EVIDENCE_INCOMPLETE"))
84:                 val path = "/$orcid/$endpoint"
85:                 val permit = reserve(path) ?: return OrcidEvidenceRead(documents, false, listOf("EVIDENCE_INCOMPLETE"))
86:                 val headers = mutableMapOf("Accept" to "application/json")
87:                 if (properties.orcidAccessToken.isNotBlank()) headers["Authorization"] = "Bearer ${properties.orcidAccessToken}"
88:                 requests++
89:                 val result = transport.request(URI(properties.orcidPublicBaseUrl.trimEnd('/') + path), HttpMethod.GET, headers, permit, VerificationProvider.ORCID)
90:                 doc = (result as? VerificationHttpResult.Success)?.let {
91:                     OrcidEvidenceDocument(endpoint, selectedFields(it.value), it.responseSha256, clock.instant())
92:                 }
93:                 // A failed acknowledgement throws out of this method: never issue the next HTTP request.
94:                 onResponse(endpoint, permit, doc, result)
95:                 when (result) {
96:                     is VerificationHttpResult.Success -> Unit
97:                     is VerificationHttpResult.PermanentFailure -> if (result.code != "EVIDENCE_MISSING")
98:                         return OrcidEvidenceRead(documents, false, listOf(result.code), result)
99:                     else -> return OrcidEvidenceRead(documents, false, listOf("INTERFACE_UNAVAILABLE"), result as VerificationHttpResult<Nothing>)
100:                 }
101:             }
102:             if (doc != null) {
103:                 documents += doc
104:                 if (endpoint == "employments" || endpoint == "educations") {
105:                     val kind = if (endpoint == "employments") "employment" else "education"
106:                     val groups = doc.body.path("affiliation-group")
107:                     if (!groups.isMissingNode && !groups.isArray) return OrcidEvidenceRead(documents, false, listOf("RESPONSE_INVALID"), VerificationHttpResult.PermanentFailure("RESPONSE_INVALID"))
108:                     groups.forEach { group -> group.path("summaries").forEach { wrapper ->
109:                         val summary = wrapper.path("$kind-summary")
110:                         val putCode = summary.path("put-code")
111:                         if (putCode.isIntegralNumber && putCode.canConvertToLong() && putCode.longValue() > 0 &&
112:                             (!summary.has("source") || !summary.has("start-date"))) {
113:                             val detail = "$kind/${putCode.longValue()}"
114:                             if (!endpoints.contains(detail) && documents.none { it.endpoint == detail }) endpoints.add(detail)
115:                         }
116:                     } }
117:                 }
118:             }
119:         }
120:         return OrcidEvidenceRead(documents, true, if (documents.isEmpty()) listOf("EVIDENCE_MISSING") else emptyList())
121:     }
122: 
123:     private val endpointPattern = Regex("person|employments|educations|employment/[0-9]+|education/[0-9]+")
124:     private fun allowedEndpoint(endpoint: String) = endpoint.matches(endpointPattern)
125:     private val retainedFields = setOf("name", "given-names", "family-name", "value", "other-names", "other-name", "content",
126:         "affiliation-group", "summaries", "employment-summary", "education-summary", "put-code", "source",
127:         "source-client-id", "source-orcid", "path", "visibility", "last-modified-date", "start-date", "end-date",
128:         "year", "month", "day", "organization", "role-title", "disambiguated-organization",
129:         "disambiguated-organization-identifier", "disambiguation-source")
130: 
131:     /** Retain parser inputs and detail cursor only, not biographies, emails, URLs or raw provider records. */
132:     private fun selectedFields(node: JsonNode): JsonNode {
133:         return when {
134:             node.isObject -> com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.node.JsonNodeFactory.instance.objectNode().also { result ->
135:                 node.fields().forEachRemaining { (key, value) -> if (key in retainedFields) result.set<JsonNode>(key, selectedFields(value)) }
136:             }
137:             node.isArray -> com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.node.JsonNodeFactory.instance.arrayNode().also { result -> node.forEach { result.add(selectedFields(it)) } }
138:             node.isTextual -> com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.node.TextNode(node.asText().take(500))
139:             else -> node.deepCopy()
140:         }
141:     }
142: }

### src/main/kotlin/com/weibo/talentintroduction/discovery/service/QualificationEvidenceParser.kt
1: package com.weibo.talentintroduction.discovery.service
2: 
3: import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode
4: import com.fasterxml.jackson.module.kotlin.jacksonObjectMapper
5: import com.weibo.talentintroduction.discovery.domain.*
6: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service
7: import java.time.Clock
8: import java.time.LocalDate
9: import java.time.ZoneOffset
10: 
11: /** Only these bounded field facts may be retained, never the original person/affiliation response. */
12: @Service
13: class QualificationEvidenceParser(private val clock: Clock = Clock.systemUTC()) {
14:     private val mapper = jacksonObjectMapper()
15:     fun parse(snapshot: VerificationSnapshot, read: OrcidEvidenceRead): List<VerificationEvidence> {
16:         val orcid = snapshot.boundOrcid ?: return emptyList()
17:         val facts = ArrayList<VerificationEvidence>()
18:         val today = LocalDate.now(clock)
19:         read.documents.forEach { document ->
20:             fun emit(field: String, value: String?, node: JsonNode, pointer: String, scope: TemporalScope = TemporalScope.UNKNOWN) {
21:                 if (value.isNullOrBlank() || value.length > 4096 || node.path("visibility").asText("PUBLIC") != "PUBLIC") return
22:                 val source = node.path("source")
23:                 val assertion = when {
24:                     source.path("source-client-id").path("path").asText().isNotBlank() -> AssertionSource.ORGANIZATION
25:                     source.path("source-orcid").path("path").asText() == orcid -> AssertionSource.SELF
26:                     else -> AssertionSource.UNKNOWN
27:                 }
28:                 val updated = node.path("last-modified-date").path("value").takeIf { it.isIntegralNumber && it.canConvertToLong() }
29:                     ?.longValue()?.let { runCatching { java.time.Instant.ofEpochMilli(it) }.getOrNull() }
30:                 facts += VerificationEvidence(snapshot.identityHash, snapshot.factFingerprint, "ORCID",
31:                     "https://pub.orcid.org/v3.0/$orcid/${document.endpoint}", document.fetchedAt, document.responseSha256,
32:                     field, value, assertion, scope, updated, pointer,
33:                     excerpt = mapper.writeValueAsString(mapOf("sourceOrcid" to source.path("source-orcid").path("path").asText(null),
34:                         "sourceClientId" to source.path("source-client-id").path("path").asText(null),
35:                         "visibility" to node.path("visibility").asText("PUBLIC"))).take(2048))
36:             }
37:             if (document.endpoint == "person") {
38:                 val name = document.body.path("name")
39:                 val fullName = listOf(name.path("given-names").path("value").asText(""), name.path("family-name").path("value").asText("")).filter { it.isNotBlank() }.joinToString(" ")
40:                 emit("name", fullName, name, "/name")
41:                 document.body.path("other-names").path("other-name").forEachIndexed { index, alias ->
42:                     emit("otherName", alias.path("content").asText(null), alias, "/other-names/other-name/$index/content")
43:                 }
44:             } else {
45:                 val employment = document.endpoint.startsWith("employment")
46:                 val kind = if (employment) "employment" else "education"
47:                 val nodes = ArrayList<Pair<String, JsonNode>>()
48:                 if (document.endpoint.contains('/')) nodes += "" to document.body
49:                 else document.body.path("affiliation-group").forEachIndexed { groupIndex, group ->
50:                     group.path("summaries").forEachIndexed { index, wrapper ->
51:                         nodes += "/affiliation-group/$groupIndex/summaries/$index/$kind-summary" to wrapper.path("$kind-summary")
52:                     }
53:                 }
54:                 nodes.forEach { (pointer, node) ->
55:                     if (!node.isObject || node.path("visibility").asText("PUBLIC") != "PUBLIC") return@forEach
56:                     val start = date(node.path("start-date"))
57:                     val endNode = node.path("end-date")
58:                     val end = date(endNode)
59:                     val scope = when {
60:                         end != null && !end.isAfter(today) -> TemporalScope.HISTORICAL
61:                         start != null && !start.isAfter(today) && (endNode.isMissingNode || endNode.isNull || (end != null && end.isAfter(today))) -> TemporalScope.CURRENT
62:                         else -> TemporalScope.UNKNOWN
63:                     }
64:                     val org = node.path("organization")
65:                     val role = node.path("role-title").asText("")
66:                     val value = mapper.writeValueAsString(linkedMapOf(
67:                         "orcid" to orcid,
68:                         "institution" to org.path("name").asText("").take(500),
69:                         "standardId" to org.path("disambiguated-organization").let { dis ->
70:                             val id = dis.path("disambiguated-organization-identifier").asText("")
71:                             val source = dis.path("disambiguation-source").asText("")
72:                             if (id.isNotBlank() && source.isNotBlank()) "$source:$id" else null
73:                         },
74:                         "role" to role.take(500),
75:                         "startDate" to start?.toString(), "endDate" to end?.toString(),
76:                         "completed" to (!employment && completedDoctorate(role))
77:                     ))
78:                     emit(kind, value, node, pointer, scope)
79:                 }
80:             }
81:         }
82:         return facts.distinctBy { listOf(it.field, it.value, it.assertionSource.name, it.temporalScope.name, it.sourceUrl.substringBeforeLast('/')) }
83:     }
84: 
85:     fun completedDoctorate(text: String): Boolean {
86:         val normalized = text.lowercase(java.util.Locale.ROOT)
87:         val doctorate = Regex("(?<![a-z])(?:ph\\.?\\s?d\\.?|dphil|doctorate|doctor of philosophy)(?![a-z])|博士").containsMatchIn(normalized)
88:         val student = Regex("student|candidate|pursuing|in progress|enrolled|在读|博士生|候选").containsMatchIn(normalized)
89:         val awarded = Regex("awarded|conferred|completed|graduated|degree obtained|已获|授予|毕业").containsMatchIn(normalized)
90:         val negated = Regex("\\b(?:not|never)\\s+(?:(?:yet|been|being|successfully|formally)\\s+)*(?:awarded|conferred|completed|graduated|obtained)\\b|\\b(?:uncompleted|incomplete|unawarded)\\b|(?:未|没有|尚未)(?:获|授予|完成|毕业)").containsMatchIn(normalized)
91:         return doctorate && awarded && !student && !negated
92:     }
93: 
94:     private fun date(node: JsonNode): LocalDate? = runCatching {
95:         val year = node.path("year").path("value").asText("").toInt()
96:         // A partial start date cannot establish current employment until its latest possible date.
97:         val month = node.path("month").path("value").asText("").toIntOrNull() ?: 12
98:         val day = node.path("day").path("value").asText("").toIntOrNull() ?: java.time.YearMonth.of(year, month).lengthOfMonth()
99:         LocalDate.of(year, month, day)
100:     }.getOrNull()
101: }

121:      * 否则「计划 30 秒后回访」在测试里无法证明。生产使用 [PolicyTimeSource.SYSTEM]（默认值），
122:      * 既有构造调用逐字兼容。

124:     private val timeSource: PolicyTimeSource = PolicyTimeSource.SYSTEM,

127:      * 不伪造结论、不晋升，按 [ADMISSION_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE] 计入过滤；生产恒由 Spring 注入。

132:      * 只按匹配 `_id` 补建补全任务，不写 proof；生产恒由 Spring 注入。

137:      * 原行为：只写 ES 事实、不建立人物关系；生产恒由 Spring 注入。

142: ) {
143:     private val log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(ExpertDiscoveryService::class.java)
144:     private val dateFormatter = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss")
145: 
146:     /** I-5：运营端展示用的时区（计划重试时间按北京时间显示，存储仍是 ISO-8601 UTC）。 */
147:     private val displayZone = ZoneId.of("Asia/Shanghai")
148: 
149:     /**
150:      * I-4（08）：最近一批自动/待补批次补全的逐源计数（入队/成功/待补/未匹配）。
151:      * 只是 `/enrich/stats` 的进程内观测值，任务生命周期的唯一事实仍是 expert_academic_enrichment_job。
152:      */
153:     private val lastEnrichmentBatch = java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference<AutoEnrichmentBatchResult?>(null)
154: 
155:     /**
156:      * 游标会在两次运行之间失效（例如 ES/搜索服务端 scroll context）因而不得持久化的来源。
157:      *
158:      * DP-4：CORE 已不在此集合内 —— c4 通过 [DiscoveryCheckpointCodec] 为它持久化稳定的 offset
159:      * envelope。该扩展点保留为显式接缝，且必须保持 CORE 不被重新加入。
160:      */
161:     private val nonPersistableCursorSources: Set<String> = emptySet()
162: 
163:     /**
164:      * I-2: 只读本次查询条件对应的 v2 key。旧条件写下的历史行（裸 source_name）不会被挪用，
165:      * 继续留在表中作备份；行存在但值不是本版本 envelope 时同样按「无检查点」处理。
166:      */
167:     private fun loadSourceCheckpoint(sourceName: String, criteria: PaperSearchCriteria): SourceCheckpoint {
168:         if (sourceName in nonPersistableCursorSources) return SourceCheckpoint.EMPTY
169:         val key = DiscoveryCheckpointCodec.sourceKey(sourceName, criteria)
170:         val stored = try {
171:             cursorRepository.findBySourceName(key)?.cursorValue
172:         } catch (e: Exception) {
173:             log.warn("Failed to load checkpoint for {} ({}): {}", sourceName, key, e.message)
174:             return SourceCheckpoint.EMPTY
175:         }
176:         if (stored == null) return SourceCheckpoint.EMPTY
177:         val decoded = DiscoveryCheckpointCodec.decode(stored)
178:         if (decoded == null) {
179:             log.warn(
180:                 "[{}] 检查点 {} 的值不是 {} envelope，按无检查点处理（旧值保留作备份）",
181:                 sourceName, key, DiscoveryCheckpointCodec.QUERY_VERSION
182:             )
183:             return SourceCheckpoint.EMPTY
184:         }
185:         return decoded
186:     }
187: 
188:     /**
189:      * I-1/I-2: 在安全边界写入检查点。[papersDelta] 是本次调用新增的处理量，累计进
190:      * `papers_processed_total`（该列只是计数，绝不用于反推恢复位置）。
191:      */
192:     private fun persistSourceCheckpoint(
193:         sourceName: String,
194:         criteria: PaperSearchCriteria,
195:         resumeCursor: String?,
196:         exhausted: Boolean,
197:         papersDelta: Int
198:     ) {
199:         if (sourceName in nonPersistableCursorSources) return
200:         val key = DiscoveryCheckpointCodec.sourceKey(sourceName, criteria)
201:         val cursorValue = DiscoveryCheckpointCodec.encode(resumeCursor, exhausted)
202:         try {
203:             val now = LocalDateTime.now()
204:             val existing = cursorRepository.findBySourceName(key)
205:             val entity = if (existing != null) {
206:                 existing.copy(
207:                     cursorValue = cursorValue,
208:                     papersProcessedTotal = existing.papersProcessedTotal + papersDelta,
209:                     lastRunAt = now,
210:                     updatedAt = now
211:                 )
212:             } else {
213:                 DiscoverySourceCursor(
214:                     sourceName = key,
215:                     cursorValue = cursorValue,
216:                     papersProcessedTotal = papersDelta.toLong(),
217:                     lastRunAt = now,
218:                     updatedAt = now
219:                 )
220:             }
221:             cursorRepository.save(entity)
222:             log.info(
223:                 "[{}] 检查点已保存: key={}, state={}, cursor={}, 本次新增处理 {}",
224:                 sourceName, key, if (exhausted) CheckpointState.EXHAUSTED else CheckpointState.ACTIVE,
225:                 resumeCursor?.take(30) ?: "null", papersDelta
226:             )
227:         } catch (e: Exception) {
228:             log.warn("Failed to save checkpoint for {} ({}): {}", sourceName, key, e.message)
229:         }
230:     }
231: 
232:     private fun recordTerminalSourceFailure(sourceStats: SourceStats, reason: String) {
233:         sourceStats.failureReasons.merge(reason, 1) { a, b -> a + b }
234:         sourceStats.sourceFailureCount++
235:     }
236: 
237:     /**
238:      * I-1（08）：去重结果必须携带**匹配文档的真实 `_id`** —— 重放/重复发现时要按它补建缺失的补全任务
239:      * （[DedupResult.Exists]），绝不用新论文派生的邮箱/姓名/主键去改写已入库专家身份。
240:      * [DedupResult.Exists.docId] 只在索引响应里确实带回 `_id` 时非空；读不到时只统计重复、不补建任务。
241:      */
242:     private sealed class DedupResult {
243:         /** RAW 已有该身份：`docId` = 匹配文档的真实 `_id`。 */
244:         data class Exists(val docId: String?, val source: com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode? = null) : DedupResult()
245: 
246:         object NotFound : DedupResult()
247:         object Error : DedupResult()
248:     }
249: 
250:     private fun snapshotErrors(stats: DiscoveryStats): List<String> {
251:         return stats.errors.asSequence().map { it.take(500) }.take(100).toList()
252:     }
253: 
254:     private fun snapshotFailureReasons(sourceStats: SourceStats): Map<String, Int> {
255:         return sourceStats.failureReasons.entries.sortedByDescending { it.value }.take(20)
256:             .associate { it.key to it.value }
257:     }
258: 
259:     private fun snapshotFilterReasons(sourceStats: SourceStats): Map<String, Int> {
260:         return sourceStats.filterReasons.entries.sortedByDescending { it.value }.take(20)
261:             .associate { it.key to it.value }
262:     }
263: 
264:     private fun snapshotRejectReasons(sourceStats: SourceStats): Map<String, Int> {
265:         val snapshot = HashMap(sourceStats.failureReasons)
266:         sourceStats.filterReasons.forEach { (reason, count) -> snapshot[reason] = count }
