267:         if (sourceStats.emailsRejected > 0) snapshot["EMAIL_INVALID"] = sourceStats.emailsRejected
268:         if (sourceStats.duplicates > 0) snapshot["DUPLICATE"] = sourceStats.duplicates
269:         if (sourceStats.dedupErrors > 0) snapshot["DEDUP_ERROR"] = sourceStats.dedupErrors
270:         if (sourceStats.rawWriteFailed > 0) snapshot["RAW_WRITE_FAILED"] = sourceStats.rawWriteFailed
271:         return snapshot
272:     }
273: 
274:     private fun computeBatchRejectReasons(before: Map<String, Int>, after: Map<String, Int>): Map<String, Int>? {
275:         val delta = mutableMapOf<String, Int>()
276:         for ((key, afterCount) in after) {
277:             val diff = afterCount - before.getOrDefault(key, 0)
278:             if (diff > 0) delta[key] = diff
279:         }
280:         return delta.ifEmpty { null }
281:     }
282: 
283:     private fun buildBySourceDetails(stats: DiscoveryStats): Map<String, Any> {
284:         val bySource = mutableMapOf<String, Any>()
285:         stats.bySource.forEach { (name, ss) ->
286:             bySource[name] = mapOf(
287:                 "extractionMethod" to ss.extractionMethod,
288:                 "papersSearched" to ss.papersSearched,
289:                 "papersSkippedNoId" to ss.papersSkippedNoId,
290:                 "fulltextAttempted" to ss.fulltextAttempted,
291:                 "fulltextObtained" to ss.fulltextObtained,
292:                 "pdfDownloadFailed" to ss.pdfDownloadFailed,
293:                 "pdfParseFailed" to ss.pdfParseFailed,
294:                 "noEmailInFulltext" to ss.noEmailInFulltext,
295:                 "authorsExtracted" to ss.authorsExtracted,
296:                 "emailsValid" to ss.emailsValid,
297:                 "emailsRejected" to ss.emailsRejected,
298:                 "duplicates" to ss.duplicates,
299:                 "dedupErrors" to ss.dedupErrors,
300:                 "indexed" to ss.indexed,
301:                 "rawWriteFailed" to ss.rawWriteFailed,
302:                 "promoted" to ss.promoted,
303:                 "promotionFailed" to ss.promotionFailed,
304:                 "filtered" to ss.filtered,
305:                 "filterReasons" to snapshotFilterReasons(ss),
306:                 "failureReasons" to snapshotFailureReasons(ss),
307:                 "elapsedMs" to ss.elapsedMs,
308:                 "apiRequests" to ss.apiRequests,
309:                 "pendingWork" to ss.pendingWork,
310:                 "sourceFailureCount" to ss.sourceFailureCount,
311:                 "stopReason" to (ss.stopReason ?: ""),
312:                 "runBudget" to ss.runBudget,
313:                 "unit" to ss.unit.name,
314:                 // I-5：唯一的重试观察对象（等待中的轮次/计划时间/原因），随 details_json/result_summary 序列化。
315:                 "retry" to (ss.retry?.let { retry ->
316:                     mapOf(
317:                         "round" to retry.round,
318:                         "maxRounds" to retry.maxRounds,
319:                         "nextRetryAt" to retry.nextRetryAt,
320:                         "reason" to retry.reason
321:                     )
322:                 } ?: emptyMap<String, Any?>())
323:             )
324:         }
325:         return bySource
326:     }
327: 
328:     private fun buildSummaryText(stats: DiscoveryStats, totalElapsed: Long, terminalStatus: String): String {
329:         val sourceSummaries = stats.bySource.map { (name, ss) ->
330:             "$name 收录 ${ss.indexed}/晋升 ${ss.promoted}"
331:         }.joinToString(", ")
332:         // I-4: 源终止失败与按专家计的失败分开说明，不把重试或整源失败算成失败专家数。
333:         val sourceFailureSegment = if (stats.sourceFailures > 0) {
334:             " | 源终止失败 ${stats.sourceFailures}/${stats.attemptedSources} 个来源(不计入专家失败)"
335:         } else {
336:             ""
337:         }
338:         val retrySegment = retryWaitSegment(stats)
339:         val headline = if (terminalStatus == SUMMARY_STATUS_RUNNING) {
340:             "发现任务进行中"
341:         } else {
342:             "发现任务完成[$terminalStatus]: 总耗时 ${totalElapsed}ms"
343:         }
344:         return "$headline | 各平台: $sourceSummaries | " +
345:             "合计: ${processedCountsSegment(stats)}, 收录 ${stats.indexed}, 晋升 ${stats.promoted}" +
346:             "$sourceFailureSegment$retrySegment"
347:     }
348: 
349:     /**
350:      * I-5：仍在等待延迟恢复的来源必须在 summaryText 里看得见（「不是穷尽，也不是失败」）。
351:      * 真正开始/取消/超时/结束时 [SourceRetryState.nextRetryAt] 被清空，这一段自然消失。
352:      */
353:     private fun retryWaitSegment(stats: DiscoveryStats): String {
354:         val waiting = stats.bySource.mapNotNull { (name, sourceStats) ->
355:             val retry = sourceStats.retry ?: return@mapNotNull null
356:             val nextRetryAt = retry.nextRetryAt ?: return@mapNotNull null
357:             "$name 等待恢复 ${retry.round}/${retry.maxRounds}（原因 ${retry.reason}），" +
358:                 "计划重试时间 ${displayTime(nextRetryAt)}（北京时间）"
359:         }
360:         return if (waiting.isEmpty()) "" else " | 待恢复: ${waiting.joinToString("; ")}"
361:     }
362: 
363:     /** I-5: 存储用 ISO-8601 UTC，展示转运营端时区（北京时间）。 */
364:     private fun displayTime(isoUtc: String): String =
365:         Instant.parse(isoUtc).atZone(displayZone).format(dateFormatter)
366: 
367:     /**
368:      * c9（I-1）：`papersSearched` 对 ORCID 的历史语义是「记录数」，不能与论文混称。这里按
369:      * [SourceUnit] 把两者分列；没有 ORCID 记录时输出与改动前逐字相同。
370:      */
371:     private fun processedCountsSegment(stats: DiscoveryStats): String {
372:         val paperTotal = stats.bySource.values.filter { it.unit == SourceUnit.PAPER }.sumOf { it.papersSearched }
373:         val recordTotal = stats.bySource.values.filter { it.unit == SourceUnit.RECORD }.sumOf { it.papersSearched }
374:         return if (recordTotal > 0) "论文 $paperTotal, ORCID 记录 $recordTotal" else "论文 $paperTotal"
375:     }
376: 
377:     private fun buildProgressDetails(stats: DiscoveryStats, sourceName: String? = null, method: String? = null): Map<String, Any> {
378:         val details = mutableMapOf<String, Any>(
379:             "indexed" to stats.indexed,
380:             "promoted" to stats.promoted,
381:             "bySource" to buildBySourceDetails(stats)
382:         )
383:         if (sourceName != null) details["currentSource"] = sourceName
384:         if (method != null) details["currentMethod"] = method
385:         // I-5：运行中的详情同样带 summaryText —— 等待提示必须随进度一起（并经晋升读投影）保留下来。
386:         // 终态更新会在其后覆盖为带真实终态与耗时的版本，不覆盖论文/收录/晋升信息。
387:         details["summaryText"] = buildSummaryText(stats, 0L, SUMMARY_STATUS_RUNNING)
388:         DiscoveryTrafficMeter.currentSession()?.let { details["traffic"] = it.snapshot() }
389:         return details
390:     }
391: 
392:     private fun resolveEnabledSources(criteria: PaperSearchCriteria): List<AcademicDataSource> {
393:         val sources = mutableListOf<AcademicDataSource>()
394:         fun add(provider: () -> AcademicDataSource?, name: String) {
395:             val src = provider()
396:             // I4-3: 学科范围排除是「本次不参与」的运行时过滤 —— 六行注册全部保留，
397:             // 其他 scope 或手动指定 sources 时仍可使用被排除源。
398:             if (src != null && (criteria.sources.isEmpty() || criteria.sources.contains(name))
399:                 && name !in SubjectScopeCatalog.excludedSources(criteria.subjectScope)) {
400:                 sources.add(src)
401:             }
402:         }
403:         // I4-4: EuropePmcDataSource 是七源中唯一无 @ConditionalOnProperty 的（裸 @Service），
404:         // 故必须在此显式读 enabled，否则 EUROPE_PMC_ENABLED=false 对定时发现（sources 为空）无效。
405:         if (europePmcProperties.enabled) add({ europePmc }, europePmc.sourceName)
406:         add({ pmcOaProvider.getIfAvailable() }, "PMC_OA")
407:         add({ openAlexProvider.getIfAvailable() }, "OPENALEX")
408:         add({ crossrefProvider.getIfAvailable() }, "CROSSREF")
409:         add({ coreProvider.getIfAvailable() }, "CORE")
410:         add({ arxivProvider.getIfAvailable() }, "ARXIV")
411:         return sources
412:     }
413: 
414:     /** Execution identity is supplied by the task owner, never by the mutable progress slot. */
415:     fun discover(
416:         criteria: PaperSearchCriteria,
417:         triggeredBy: String,
418:         executionId: Long?,
419:         includeRawScan: Boolean = discoveryProperties.includeRawScan
420:     ): DiscoveryResult {
421:         val stats = DiscoveryStats()
422:         val trafficSession = DiscoveryTrafficMeter.newSession()
423:         val execId = executionId
424:         val sources = resolveEnabledSources(criteria)
425:         // I-1（09）：启动校验 —— 全局 cap 必须覆盖各启用来源的基础份额，否则直接报配置错误，
426:         // 不静默饿死后来源，也不把 0 当成无限量。
427:         validateRunQuota(sources, criteria)
428:         // I-3（09）：运行级 deadline。请求前与每页内检查，到点按 TIME_BUDGET 停止并保留进入页的检查点。
429:         val deadline = timeSource.now().plus(discoveryProperties.timeBudget)
430:         val startTime = System.currentTimeMillis()
431:         // I-3: 结果与进度共用的终态；仅在正常路径赋值（异常路径由 catch 记录 FAILED 后重抛）。
432:         var terminalStatusOfRun = DiscoveryTerminalStatus.SUCCESS
433: 
434:         log.info("发现任务启动: 启用平台=${sources.map { it.sourceName }}, 关键词=${criteria.keywords}, " +
435:             "年份=${criteria.publicationYearFrom}-${criteria.publicationYearTo}, " +
436:             "全局限额: 论文 ${discoveryProperties.maxPapersPerRun} / 作者 ${discoveryProperties.maxAuthorsPerRun}, " +
437:             "时间预算 ${discoveryProperties.timeBudget}")
438:         val verification = verificationRegistration?.begin(execId, criteria) ?: DiscoveryVerificationExecution(execId)
439: 
440:         try {
441:             progressStore.update("EXPERT_DISCOVERY", TaskProgress(
442:                 taskType = "EXPERT_DISCOVERY", status = "RUNNING",
443:                 batchNumber = 0, processedCount = 0, totalCount = 0,
444:                 message = "正在加载数据源配置..."
445:             ), execId)
446: 
447:             if (includeRawScan) {
448:                 progressStore.update("EXPERT_DISCOVERY", TaskProgress(
449:                     taskType = "EXPERT_DISCOVERY", status = "RUNNING",
450:                     batchNumber = 0, processedCount = 0, totalCount = 0,
451:                     message = "正在扫描 RAW 索引并晋升..."
452:                 ), execId)
453:                 log.info("开始执行 RAW 晋升扫描与邮箱补全...")
454:                 runRawScan()
455:             }
456: 
457:             // I-1：本轮内存中的待恢复项（不落库、不起后台线程）；跨重启的续跑仍走既有持久化检查点。
458:             val deferredRecoveries = LinkedHashMap<String, DeferredSourceRecovery>()
459: 
460:             for ((index, source) in sources.withIndex()) {
461:                 if (progressStore.isCancelled("EXPERT_DISCOVERY")) break
462:                 stats.refreshGlobalCounts()
463:                 if (stats.totalAuthors >= discoveryProperties.maxAuthorsPerRun) break
464:                 if (stats.totalPapers >= discoveryProperties.maxPapersPerRun) break
465: 
466:                 // I-2: 先给每个后来源留一页基础份额，再按来源顺序分配本源的运行额度；
467:                 // 已穷尽/失效来源没用掉的份额自然留在全局剩余里给后来源复用。
468:                 val runQuota = allocateSourceQuota(sources, index, criteria.pageSize, papersUsedForGlobalCap(stats))
469:                 val outcome = DiscoveryTrafficMeter.measure(
470:                     trafficSession, source.sourceName, DiscoveryTrafficMeter.Category.METADATA
471:                 ) { discoverFromSource(source, criteria, stats, runQuota, deadline, verification) }
472:                 log.info("[{}] 本次运行结束: stopReason={}, exhausted={}, resumeCursor={}",
473:                     source.sourceName, outcome.stopReason, outcome.exhausted,
474:                     outcome.resumeCursor?.take(50) ?: "null")
475:                 // I-1：待恢复项只登记在本轮内存里，等所有来源（含 ORCID）停稳后才消费。
476:                 outcome.retry?.let { pending ->
477:                     val deferred = DeferredSourceRecovery(source, runQuota, pending)
478:                     deferredRecoveries[source.sourceName] = deferred
479:                     stats.refreshGlobalCounts()
480:                     writeRetryEvent(stats, deferred, retryWaitMessage(deferred), execId)
481:                 }
482:             }
483: 
484:             DiscoveryTrafficMeter.measure(trafficSession, "ORCID", DiscoveryTrafficMeter.Category.METADATA) {
485:                 discoverFromOrcid(criteria, stats, deadline, verification)
486:             }
487:             // I-1/I-2：其他来源已经耗过的等待时间就是等待时间，回访时不重复睡；这里也可能因取消/超时
488:             // 而一个请求都不发（保留游标并给真实停止原因）。
489:             consumeDeferredRecoveries(deferredRecoveries, criteria, stats, deadline, trafficSession, execId, verification)
490:             // I-5：本轮结束时清空「仍待执行」的计划时间；轮次与原因保留用于审计。
491:             clearRetryWait(stats)
492:             stats.refreshGlobalCounts()
493: 
494:             val totalElapsed = System.currentTimeMillis() - startTime
495:             val wasCancelled = progressStore.isCancelled("EXPERT_DISCOVERY")
496:             // I-3: 结果与进度共用同一个终态决策函数。
497:             terminalStatusOfRun = DiscoveryTerminalStatus.decide(
498:                 cancelled = wasCancelled,
499:                 attemptedSources = stats.attemptedSources,
500:                 failedSources = stats.failedSources,
501:                 pendingWork = stats.pendingSources > 0
502:             )
503:             val details = buildProgressDetails(stats).toMutableMap()
504:             details["traffic"] = trafficSession.snapshot()
505:             details["terminalStatus"] = terminalStatusOfRun
506:             details["summaryText"] = buildSummaryText(stats, totalElapsed, terminalStatusOfRun)
507: 
508:             if (wasCancelled) {
509:                 log.info("发现任务取消: 论文=${stats.totalPapers}, 收录=${stats.indexed}, 晋升=${stats.promoted}")
510:                 progressStore.update("EXPERT_DISCOVERY", TaskProgress(
511:                     taskType = "EXPERT_DISCOVERY",
512:                     status = DiscoveryTerminalStatus.toProgressStatus(terminalStatusOfRun),
513:                     batchNumber = -1, processedCount = stats.totalPapers.toLong(),
514:                     totalCount = stats.totalPapers.toLong(),
515:                     message = buildProgressMessage(terminalStatusOfRun, stats),
516:                     details = details, errors = snapshotErrors(stats)
517:                 ), execId)
518:                 return DiscoveryResult(triggeredBy, stats, wasCancelled = true,
519:                     summaryText = details["summaryText"] as String, traffic = trafficSession.snapshot())
520:             }
521: 
522:             val totalValidEmails = stats.bySource.values.sumOf { it.emailsValid }
523:             val sourceSummaries = stats.bySource.map { (name, ss) -> "$name 收录 ${ss.indexed}/晋升 ${ss.promoted}" }.joinToString(", ")
524:             log.info("发现任务完成[$terminalStatusOfRun]: 总耗时 ${totalElapsed}ms | 各平台: $sourceSummaries | " +
525:                 "合计: 论文 ${stats.totalPapers}, 作者候选 ${stats.totalAuthors}, " +
526:                 "邮箱有效 $totalValidEmails (无效 ${stats.emailRejected}), 收录 ${stats.indexed}, 晋升 ${stats.promoted}, " +
527:                 "源终止失败 ${stats.sourceFailures}, 待续跑来源 ${stats.pendingSources}")
528:             trafficSession.snapshot().let { traffic ->
529:                 log.info("发现任务下载量: 总计={} 字节, 元数据={} 字节, 全文={} 字节, 额外排空={} 字节, 超限下载={} 次/{} 字节, 来源={}, 站点={}",
530:                     traffic.totalBytes, traffic.metadataBytes, traffic.fulltextBytes,
531:                     traffic.discardedBytes, traffic.oversizedDownloads, traffic.oversizedBytes,
532:                     traffic.bySource, traffic.byHost)
533:             }
534: 
535:             progressStore.update("EXPERT_DISCOVERY", TaskProgress(
536:                 taskType = "EXPERT_DISCOVERY",
537:                 status = DiscoveryTerminalStatus.toProgressStatus(terminalStatusOfRun),
538:                 batchNumber = -1, processedCount = stats.totalPapers.toLong(),
539:                 totalCount = stats.totalPapers.toLong(),
540:                 message = buildProgressMessage(terminalStatusOfRun, stats),
541:                 details = details, errors = snapshotErrors(stats)
542:             ), execId)
543:         } catch (e: Exception) {
544:             stats.refreshGlobalCounts()
545:             // I-5：异常结束同样清空「仍待执行」的计划时间，避免页面一直显示已过期的等待。
546:             clearRetryWait(stats)
547:             val totalElapsed = System.currentTimeMillis() - startTime
548:             val details = buildProgressDetails(stats).toMutableMap()
549:             details["traffic"] = trafficSession.snapshot()
550:             details["summaryText"] = buildSummaryText(stats, totalElapsed, DiscoveryTerminalStatus.FAILED)
551:             progressStore.update("EXPERT_DISCOVERY", TaskProgress(
552:                 taskType = "EXPERT_DISCOVERY", status = "FAILED",
553:                 batchNumber = -1, processedCount = stats.totalPapers.toLong(), totalCount = 0,
554:                 message = "失败: ${e.message}",
555:                 details = details, errors = snapshotErrors(stats)
556:             ), execId)
557:             throw e
558:         } finally {
559:             verificationRegistration?.closeInput(verification)
560:         }
561:         stats.refreshGlobalCounts()
562:         val finalElapsed = System.currentTimeMillis() - startTime
563:         return DiscoveryResult(
564:             triggeredBy, stats,
565:             summaryText = buildSummaryText(stats, finalElapsed, terminalStatusOfRun),
566:             traffic = trafficSession.snapshot()
567:         )
568:     }
569: 
570:     /** I-3/I-4: 面向操作端的终态说明，源失败单独说明，不与专家级失败混在一起。 */
571:     private fun buildProgressMessage(terminalStatus: String, stats: DiscoveryStats): String {
572:         val sourceFailureSegment = if (stats.sourceFailures > 0) {
573:             "，源终止失败 ${stats.sourceFailures}/${stats.attemptedSources} 个来源"
574:         } else ""
575:         val pendingSegment = if (stats.pendingSources > 0) {
576:             "，待续跑来源 ${stats.pendingSources}"
577:         } else ""
578:         // c9（I-1）：论文与 ORCID 记录分列，不把记录数混称论文。
579:         val counts = processedCountsSegment(stats)
580:         return when (terminalStatus) {
581:             DiscoveryTerminalStatus.CANCELLED ->
582:                 "已取消: $counts, 收录 ${stats.indexed}, 晋升 ${stats.promoted}"
583:             DiscoveryTerminalStatus.FAILED ->
584:                 "失败: 全源搜索失败$sourceFailureSegment, $counts, 收录 ${stats.indexed}"
585:             DiscoveryTerminalStatus.PARTIAL_SUCCESS ->
586:                 "部分成功: $counts, 收录 ${stats.indexed}, 晋升 ${stats.promoted}" +
587:                     sourceFailureSegment + pendingSegment
588:             else ->
589:                 "完成: $counts, 收录 ${stats.indexed}, 晋升 ${stats.promoted}"
590:         }
591:     }
592: 
593:     /**
594:      * c9（I-1）：启动校验。三类错误都必须在**动手之前**以清晰配置错误暴露，而不是静默饿死后来源
595:      * 或把 0 解释成无限量：
596:      * 1. 全局论文上限 / 作者防护上限 / 时间预算必须为正数；
597:      * 2. 来源上限不得为负；
598:      * 3. 全局论文上限必须覆盖各启用来源的基础份额（每源至少一页，`min(pageSize, cap)`）。
599:      */
600:     private fun validateRunQuota(sources: List<AcademicDataSource>, criteria: PaperSearchCriteria) {
601:         val globalCap = discoveryProperties.maxPapersPerRun
602:         val authorCap = discoveryProperties.maxAuthorsPerRun
603:         val timeBudget = discoveryProperties.timeBudget
604:         require(globalCap > 0) {
605:             "深度发现配置错误：全局论文上限（EXPERT_DISCOVERY_MAX_PAPERS）必须为正数，当前为 $globalCap。" +
606:                 "0 表示配置错误而不是无限量。"
607:         }
608:         require(authorCap > 0) {
609:             "深度发现配置错误：作者防护上限（EXPERT_DISCOVERY_MAX_AUTHORS）必须为正数，当前为 $authorCap。" +
610:                 "0 表示配置错误而不是无限量。"
611:         }
612:         require(!timeBudget.isZero && !timeBudget.isNegative) {
613:             "深度发现配置错误：单次运行的时间预算（EXPERT_DISCOVERY_TIME_BUDGET）必须为正数，当前为 $timeBudget。" +
614:                 "0 表示配置错误而不是无限量。"
615:         }
616:         val negative = sources.filter { it.maxPapersPerSource < 0 }
617:         require(negative.isEmpty()) {
618:             "深度发现配置错误：来源上限不得为负：" +
619:                 negative.joinToString(", ") { "${it.sourceName}=${it.maxPapersPerSource}" }
620:         }
621:         val pageSize = pageSizeOf(criteria)
622:         val baseShares = sources.sumOf { minOf(pageSize, it.maxPapersPerSource) }
623:         require(globalCap >= baseShares) {
624:             "深度发现配置错误：全局论文上限 $globalCap 小于各启用来源的基础份额合计 $baseShares" +
625:                 "（每源至少一页 $pageSize：${sources.joinToString(", ") { "${it.sourceName}=${minOf(pageSize, it.maxPapersPerSource)}" }}）。" +
626:                 "请提高 EXPERT_DISCOVERY_MAX_PAPERS 或减少本次选择的来源。"
627:         }
628:     }
629: 
630:     /**
631:      * c9（I-2）：本源本次运行的额度 = `min(本源上限, 全局剩余 - 后来源保留份额)`。
632:      * 后来源保留份额只保底一页（`min(pageSize, cap)`），因此前来源有剩余额度时后来源在本源上限内
633:      * 仍可用掉它；反过来任何来源都不可能吃掉后来源的保底份额。返回值可能为 0（本源上限为 0），
634:      * 调用方按 0 额度运行 —— 绝不解释成无限量。
635:      */
636:     private fun allocateSourceQuota(
637:         sources: List<AcademicDataSource>,
638:         index: Int,
639:         pageSize: Int,
640:         papersUsed: Int
641:     ): Int {
642:         val page = pageSize.coerceAtLeast(1)
643:         val sourceCap = sources[index].maxPapersPerSource.coerceAtLeast(0)
644:         val globalRemaining = (discoveryProperties.maxPapersPerRun - papersUsed).coerceAtLeast(0)
645:         val reserveForLater = sources.drop(index + 1)
646:             .sumOf { minOf(page, it.maxPapersPerSource.coerceAtLeast(0)) }
647:         val available = (globalRemaining - reserveForLater).coerceAtLeast(0)
648:         return minOf(sourceCap, available)
649:     }
650: 
651:     private fun pageSizeOf(criteria: PaperSearchCriteria): Int = criteria.pageSize.coerceAtLeast(1)
652: 
653:     /**
654:      * c9（I-1）：计入全局论文上限的只有论文源。ORCID 走独立的记录限额与作者防护，
655:      * 不参与论文额度分配（避免它的记录数挤占论文份额）。
656:      */
657:     private fun papersUsedForGlobalCap(stats: DiscoveryStats): Int =
658:         stats.bySource.values.filter { it.unit == SourceUnit.PAPER }.sumOf { it.papersSearched }
659: 
660:     /** c9（I-3）：运行级时间预算是否已到点（与延迟恢复共用同一 clock，保证测试可驱动）。 */
661:     private fun timeBudgetReached(deadline: Instant): Boolean = !timeSource.now().isBefore(deadline)
662: 
663:     /**
664:      * I-1: 单来源运行。每一完整消费页后立即持久化 next cursor；首请求失败、部分页、取消与预算停止
665:      * 都保留进入该页的 cursor；异常与额度延期绝不产出 `exhausted = true`。
666:      *
667:      * c9（I-1/I-2）：[runQuota] 是本次运行分给本源的额度（已含后来源保留份额），它可能小于本源自身上限 ——
668:      * 此时到界按全局 cap 命名，而不是谎称本源上限用尽。
669:      * c9（I-3）：[deadline] 到点即按 `TIME_BUDGET` 停在进入页，不推进未消费的半页。
670:      */
671:     private fun discoverFromSource(
672:         source: AcademicDataSource,
673:         criteria: PaperSearchCriteria,
674:         stats: DiscoveryStats,
675:         runQuota: Int,
676:         deadline: Instant,
677:         verification: DiscoveryVerificationExecution,
678:         /** I-1/I-3：同轮延迟恢复的入口上下文；null = 首次进入本源（既有行为逐字保持）。 */
679:         resume: SourceRetryPending? = null
680:     ): SourceRunOutcome {
681:         val sourceStats = stats.getOrCreateSourceStats(source.sourceName, source.emailExtractionMethod)
682:         val sourceStartTime = System.currentTimeMillis()
683:         val execId = verification.executionId
684:         val sourceLimit = runQuota.coerceAtLeast(0)
685:         // 运行额度小于本源上限 = 本次是全局 cap 在约束本源，停止原因必须按全局 cap 命名。
686:         val quotaBoundByGlobalCap = sourceLimit < source.maxPapersPerSource
687:         sourceStats.runBudget = sourceLimit
688: 
689:         val checkpoint = loadSourceCheckpoint(source.sourceName, criteria)
690:         // I-2: 检查点是游标权威；EXHAUSTED 表示本次扫描周期从头重开，
691:         // 调用方显式给出的 cursor 只在本次运行起点生效（保持既有入口行为）。
692:         // 恢复回访用本轮内存里的确切页入口（[SourceRetryPending.resumeCursor]），不依赖再查库成功；
693:         // 其中的 null 是合法的「第一页」，绝不用检查点里的旧值替代。
694:         val enteringCursor: String? = if (resume != null) {
695:             resume.resumeCursor
696:         } else if (checkpoint.exhausted) {
697:             criteria.cursor
698:         } else {
699:             checkpoint.cursor ?: criteria.cursor
700:         }
701:         if (resume == null && enteringCursor != null) {
702:             log.info("[{}] 从上次检查点继续: {}", source.sourceName, enteringCursor.take(50))
703:         } else if (resume == null && checkpoint.exhausted) {
704:             log.info("[{}] 上次已穷尽，本次扫描周期从头重开", source.sourceName)
705:         }
706: 
707:         if (resume != null) {
708:             log.info(
709:                 "[{}] 延迟恢复回访 组 {}/{}: 原因={}, 入口游标={}, 已处理基线={}/{}",
710:                 source.sourceName, resume.round, resume.maxRounds, resume.reason,
711:                 enteringCursor?.take(50) ?: "null", resume.processedBaseline, sourceLimit
712:             )
713:         } else {
714:             log.info(
715:                 "[{}] 开始: 方式={}, 本源限额={}, 本次运行额度={}, 全局剩余={}",
716:                 source.sourceName, source.emailExtractionMethod, source.maxPapersPerSource, sourceLimit,
717:                 (discoveryProperties.maxPapersPerRun - papersUsedForGlobalCap(stats)).coerceAtLeast(0)
718:             )
719:         }
720: 
721:         val runCriteria = if (enteringCursor != null) criteria.copy(cursor = enteringCursor) else criteria
722:         var cursor: String? = enteringCursor
723:         // I-1: 可安全续跑的位置。进入某页失败时保持该页入口值，绝不用 null 覆盖已有进度。
724:         var resumeCursor: String? = enteringCursor
725:         // I-3: 恢复片段从上一片段的累计量起步 —— 既不重新获得完整额度，也不重复累计论文
726:         // （页面入口停止意味着此前处理量都已落盘，故两个基线同一个值）。
727:         var persistedPapers = resume?.processedBaseline ?: 0
728:         var batchNumber = 0
729:         var sourcePapersProcessed = resume?.processedBaseline ?: 0
730:         var consecutiveFailures = 0
731:         var openAlexPageAttempts = 0
732:         var circuitBreakerTripped = false
733:         var exhausted = false
734:         var stopReason = DiscoveryStopReason.EXHAUSTED
735:         // I-1: 已安排的延迟恢复组数（跨片段累计，不因成功一页而重置）。
736:         var recoveryGroupsScheduled = resume?.round ?: 0
737:         var retryPending: SourceRetryPending? = null
738: 
739:         /** 在页边界（或运行结束时）落盘检查点；delta 保证 papers_processed_total 不重复计数。 */
740:         fun persistCheckpoint(nextCursor: String?, reason: String, pageExhausted: Boolean) {
741:             val delta = sourceStats.papersSearched - persistedPapers
742:             persistSourceCheckpoint(source.sourceName, criteria, nextCursor, pageExhausted, delta)
743:             persistedPapers = sourceStats.papersSearched
744:             resumeCursor = nextCursor
745:             exhausted = pageExhausted
746:             stopReason = reason
747:         }
748: 
749:         while (true) {
750:             if (progressStore.isCancelled("EXPERT_DISCOVERY")) {
751:                 log.info("[{}] 已取消, 当前批次={}", source.sourceName, batchNumber)
752:                 stopReason = DiscoveryStopReason.CANCELLED
753:                 break
754:             }
755:             stats.refreshGlobalCounts()
756:             if (stats.totalPapers >= discoveryProperties.maxPapersPerRun) {
757:                 stopReason = DiscoveryStopReason.GLOBAL_PAPER_LIMIT
758:                 break
759:             }
760:             if (stats.totalAuthors >= discoveryProperties.maxAuthorsPerRun) {
761:                 stopReason = DiscoveryStopReason.GLOBAL_AUTHOR_LIMIT
762:                 break
763:             }
764:             if (sourcePapersProcessed >= sourceLimit) {
765:                 stopReason = if (quotaBoundByGlobalCap) {
766:                     DiscoveryStopReason.GLOBAL_PAPER_LIMIT
767:                 } else {
768:                     DiscoveryStopReason.SOURCE_LIMIT
769:                 }
770:                 break
771:             }
772:             // c9（I-3）：HTTP 请求前的 deadline 检查 —— 到点绝不发下一次请求。
773:             if (timeBudgetReached(deadline)) {
774:                 log.info("[{}] 时间预算到点，停止并发起无请求，保留进入页游标 {}", source.sourceName, resumeCursor?.take(50) ?: "null")
775:                 stopReason = DiscoveryStopReason.TIME_BUDGET
776:                 break
777:             }
778: 
779:             if (source is OpenAlexDataSource) openAlexPageAttempts++
780:             sourceStats.apiRequests++
781: 
782:             var batch: PaperSearchResult? = null
783:             var corePage: CoreDataSource.CoreSearchPage? = null
784:             try {
785:                 if (source is CoreDataSource) {
786:                     // I-1/I-4: CORE 走 offset 分片协议；分片触达供应商窗口必须显式记录，不能当成穷尽。
787:                     corePage = source.searchCorePage(runCriteria.copy(cursor = cursor))
788:                     batch = corePage?.result
789:                 } else {
790:                     batch = source.searchPapers(runCriteria.copy(cursor = cursor))
791:                 }
792:             } catch (e: OpenAlexBudgetDeferredException) {
793:                 // 额度延期不是搜索失败：保留进入该页的 cursor，也绝不置 exhausted。
794:                 log.warn("[{}] 额度延期至 {}，保留进入该页的游标 {}", source.sourceName, e.resetAt,
795:                     resumeCursor?.take(50) ?: "null")
796:                 stopReason = DiscoveryStopReason.BUDGET_DEFERRED
797:                 break
798:             } catch (e: HttpStatusCodeException) {
799:                 val code = e.statusCode.value()
800:                 if (source is OpenAlexDataSource && code == 429) {
801:                     // OpenAlex already records response cooling in its request policy; don't wait for Retry-After.
802:                     stopReason = DiscoveryStopReason.BUDGET_DEFERRED
803:                     break
804:                 }
805:                 val retryableOpenAlex = source is OpenAlexDataSource && code in OPENALEX_RETRYABLE_HTTP_STATUS
806:                 if (retryableOpenAlex && openAlexPageAttempts < OPENALEX_MAX_PAGE_ATTEMPTS) {
807:                     log.warn("[{}] 同页重试 {}/{}，异常类型={}", source.sourceName, openAlexPageAttempts,
808:                         OPENALEX_MAX_PAGE_ATTEMPTS, e.javaClass.simpleName)
809:                     val waitStop = waitForOpenAlexRetry(openAlexPageAttempts, deadline)
810:                     if (waitStop != null) {
811:                         stopReason = waitStop
812:                         break
813:                     }
814:                     continue
815:                 }
816:                 if (retryableOpenAlex) {
817:                     // I-1：短尝试用尽 —— 不立刻判来源失败，先安排有限延迟恢复（恢复组也有限）。
818:                     val deferred = planDeferredRecovery(
819:                         groupsScheduled = recoveryGroupsScheduled,
820:                         reason = retryReasonCode(e),
821:                         resumeCursor = resumeCursor,
822:                         processedBaseline = sourceStats.papersSearched
823:                     )
824:                     if (deferred != null) {
825:                         recoveryGroupsScheduled = deferred.round
826:                         retryPending = deferred
827:                         stopReason = DiscoveryStopReason.RETRY_WAIT
828:                         log.warn("[{}] 同页短尝试用尽，等待恢复 {}/{}（原因 {}，计划 {}）",
829:                             source.sourceName, deferred.round, deferred.maxRounds, deferred.reason,
830:                             deferred.nextRetryAt)
831:                         break
832:                     }
833:                 }
834:                 if (source !is OpenAlexDataSource && (code == 429 || code == 503)) {
835:                     consecutiveFailures++
836:                     sourceStats.failureReasons.merge("RATE_LIMITED", 1) { a, b -> a + b }
837:                     if (consecutiveFailures >= 5) {
838:                         circuitBreakerTripped = true
839:                         recordTerminalSourceFailure(sourceStats, "CIRCUIT_BREAKER")
840:                         log.warn("[{}] 连续 5 次限流/不可用，熔断", source.sourceName)
841:                         stopReason = DiscoveryStopReason.CIRCUIT_BREAKER
842:                         break
843:                     }
844:                     Thread.sleep(1000)
845:                     continue
846:                 }
847:                 recordTerminalSourceFailure(sourceStats, "SEARCH_FAILED")
848:                 log.error("[{}] 搜索失败: attempt={}/{}, 异常类型={}", source.sourceName,
849:                     if (source is OpenAlexDataSource) openAlexPageAttempts else 1,
850:                     if (source is OpenAlexDataSource) OPENALEX_MAX_PAGE_ATTEMPTS else 1,
851:                     e.javaClass.simpleName)
852:                 stopReason = DiscoveryStopReason.SEARCH_FAILED
853:                 break
854:             } catch (e: Exception) {
855:                 val retryableSearchFailure = source is OpenAlexDataSource && isRetryableOpenAlexFailure(e)
856:                 if (retryableSearchFailure && openAlexPageAttempts < OPENALEX_MAX_PAGE_ATTEMPTS) {
857:                     log.warn("[{}] 同页重试 {}/{}，异常类型={}", source.sourceName, openAlexPageAttempts,
858:                         OPENALEX_MAX_PAGE_ATTEMPTS, e.javaClass.simpleName)
859:                     val waitStop = waitForOpenAlexRetry(openAlexPageAttempts, deadline)
860:                     if (waitStop != null) {
861:                         stopReason = waitStop
862:                         break
863:                     }
864:                     continue
865:                 }
866:                 if (retryableSearchFailure) {
867:                     // I-1：短尝试用尽 —— 先按有限间隔安排恢复组；证书/协议/反序列化错误已在
868:                     // [isRetryableOpenAlexFailure] 里被排除，绝不进入这里。
869:                     val deferred = planDeferredRecovery(
870:                         groupsScheduled = recoveryGroupsScheduled,
871:                         reason = retryReasonCode(e),
872:                         resumeCursor = resumeCursor,
873:                         processedBaseline = sourceStats.papersSearched
874:                     )
875:                     if (deferred != null) {
876:                         recoveryGroupsScheduled = deferred.round
877:                         retryPending = deferred
878:                         stopReason = DiscoveryStopReason.RETRY_WAIT
879:                         log.warn("[{}] 同页短尝试用尽，等待恢复 {}/{}（原因 {}，计划 {}）",
880:                             source.sourceName, deferred.round, deferred.maxRounds, deferred.reason,
881:                             deferred.nextRetryAt)
882:                         break
883:                     }
884:                 }
885:                 recordTerminalSourceFailure(sourceStats, "SEARCH_FAILED")
886:                 log.error("[{}] 搜索失败: attempt={}/{}, 异常类型={}", source.sourceName,
887:                     if (source is OpenAlexDataSource) openAlexPageAttempts else 1,
888:                     if (source is OpenAlexDataSource) OPENALEX_MAX_PAGE_ATTEMPTS else 1,
889:                     e.javaClass.simpleName)
890:                 stopReason = DiscoveryStopReason.SEARCH_FAILED
891:                 break
892:             }
893: 
894:             openAlexPageAttempts = 0
895:             consecutiveFailures = 0
896: 
897:             if (corePage?.windowLimit == true) {
898:                 // I-4: 分片窗口边界是一次显式事件（不是失败、也不是穷尽）。游标已切到下一分片，
899:                 // 未覆盖尾部只记录不冒充覆盖；该分片不再发出任何 offset 请求。
900:                 sourceStats.failureReasons.merge(DiscoveryStopReason.WINDOW_LIMIT, 1) { a, b -> a + b }
901:                 log.warn("[{}] 分片触达供应商窗口，未覆盖尾部 {} 条（已切下一分片）",
902:                     source.sourceName, corePage.uncoveredTail)
903:             }
904: 
905:             if (batch == null || batch.papers.isEmpty()) {
906:                 val next = batch?.nextCursor
907:                 if (next == null) {
908:                     // V-2: 无记录且无 nextCursor 才判穷尽。
909:                     stopReason = DiscoveryStopReason.EXHAUSTED
910:                     exhausted = true
911:                     break
912:                 }
913:                 // V-2: 过滤后空页只要还有 nextCursor 就继续翻页。
914:                 cursor = next
915:                 persistCheckpoint(next, DiscoveryStopReason.EMPTY_PAGE, false)
916:                 continue
917:             }
918:             batchNumber++
919: 
920:             val papersBefore = sourceStats.papersSearched
921:             val indexedBefore = sourceStats.indexed
922:             val rawWriteFailedBefore = sourceStats.rawWriteFailed
923:             val enqueueFailedBefore = sourceStats.failureReasons[ENRICHMENT_ENQUEUE_FAILED] ?: 0
924:             val dedupErrorsBefore = sourceStats.dedupErrors
925:             val rejectReasonsBefore = snapshotRejectReasons(sourceStats)
926: 
927:             var limitReached = false
928:             // c9（I-3）：页内到点单独标记 —— 半页按「进入该页」落盘，且停止原因必须是 TIME_BUDGET。
929:             var timeBudgetExpired = false
930:             // c9（I-3）：到界的哪个约束自己命名（全局论文上限 / 作者防护 / 本源额度）。
931:             var limitReason: String? = null
932:             var consumedInBatch = 0
933:             // 01（I-3）：本页是否有补证未完成（部分/失败）；有则本页不推进、不记成功。
934:             var identityRepairFailedInPage = false
935:             // 05（I-3）：本页是否有可重试登记失败（STALE/FAILED）；有则本页不推进、不记成功。
936:             var personRegistrationFailedInPage = false
937: 
938:             val sampleContext = execId?.takeIf { it > 0 }?.let {
939:                 DiscoverySampleContext(it, source.sourceName, DiscoverySampleEntry.SYNC, batchNumber, null)
940:             }
941:             val extractions = parallelExtractOutcomes(batch.papers, source, sampleContext)
942:             for ((paper, extraction) in extractions) {
943:                 if (consumedInBatch % 10 == 0 && progressStore.isCancelled("EXPERT_DISCOVERY")) { limitReached = true; break }
944:                 if (timeBudgetReached(deadline)) { timeBudgetExpired = true; break }
945:                 stats.refreshGlobalCounts()
946:                 if (stats.totalPapers >= discoveryProperties.maxPapersPerRun) {
947:                     limitReached = true; limitReason = DiscoveryStopReason.GLOBAL_PAPER_LIMIT; break
948:                 }
949:                 if (stats.totalAuthors >= discoveryProperties.maxAuthorsPerRun) {
950:                     limitReached = true; limitReason = DiscoveryStopReason.GLOBAL_AUTHOR_LIMIT; break
951:                 }
952:                 if (sourcePapersProcessed >= sourceLimit) {
953:                     limitReached = true
954:                     limitReason = if (quotaBoundByGlobalCap) {
955:                         DiscoveryStopReason.GLOBAL_PAPER_LIMIT
956:                     } else {
957:                         DiscoveryStopReason.SOURCE_LIMIT
958:                     }
959:                     break
960:                 }
961:                 sourceStats.papersSearched++
962:                 sourcePapersProcessed++
963:                 consumedInBatch++
964:                 consumeOutcome(paper, extraction, source, stats, sourceStats, verification,
965:                     envelopeForPaper(source.sourceName, paper, Int.MAX_VALUE).itemKey).let {
966:                     if (it.identityRepairFailed) identityRepairFailedInPage = true
967:                     if (it.registrationFailed) personRegistrationFailedInPage = true
968:                 }
969:             }
970: 
971:             val rawWriteFailedInPage = sourceStats.rawWriteFailed - rawWriteFailedBefore
972:             val enqueueFailedInPage = (sourceStats.failureReasons[ENRICHMENT_ENQUEUE_FAILED] ?: 0) - enqueueFailedBefore
973:             // I-1: dedup lookup failures also make this page incomplete and replayable.
974:             val dedupErrorsInPage = sourceStats.dedupErrors - dedupErrorsBefore
975:             if (rawWriteFailedInPage > 0) {
976:                 log.warn("[{}] 批次 {} 内有 {} 篇 RAW 写入失败，保留进入该页的 cursor 以便重放",
977:                     source.sourceName, batchNumber, rawWriteFailedInPage)
978:             }
979:             if (enqueueFailedInPage > 0) {
980:                 log.warn("[{}] 批次 {} 内有 {} 条补全任务入队失败，保留进入该页的 cursor 以便重放补建",
981:                     source.sourceName, batchNumber, enqueueFailedInPage)
982:             }
983:             if (!limitReached && !timeBudgetExpired && rawWriteFailedInPage == 0 &&
984:                 enqueueFailedInPage == 0 && dedupErrorsInPage == 0 && !identityRepairFailedInPage &&
985:                 !personRegistrationFailedInPage) {
986:                 val nextCursor = batch.nextCursor
987:                 // 没有下一页即穷尽；完整消费的页在进入下一页前立即落盘。
988:                 persistCheckpoint(
989:                     nextCursor,
990:                     if (nextCursor == null) DiscoveryStopReason.EXHAUSTED else DiscoveryStopReason.PAGE_CONSUMED,
991:                     nextCursor == null
992:                 )
993:             }
994: 
995:             val batchProcessed = sourceStats.papersSearched - papersBefore
996:             val batchPassed = sourceStats.indexed - indexedBefore
997:             val batchRejected = batchProcessed - batchPassed
998:             val batchRejectReasons = computeBatchRejectReasons(
999:                 rejectReasonsBefore,
1000:                 snapshotRejectReasons(sourceStats)
1001:             )
1002: 
1003:             log.info("[{}] 批次 {}: 论文 +{} (累计 {}/{}), 获全文 {}, 抽到邮箱 {}, 有效 {}, 重复 {}, 收录 {}, 晋升 {}, 累计下载 {} 字节",
1004:                 source.sourceName, batchNumber, batchProcessed,
1005:                 sourceStats.papersSearched, sourceLimit,
1006:                 sourceStats.fulltextObtained, sourceStats.authorsExtracted,
1007:                 sourceStats.emailsValid, sourceStats.duplicates,
1008:                 sourceStats.indexed, sourceStats.promoted,
1009:                 DiscoveryTrafficMeter.currentSession()?.snapshot()?.totalBytes ?: 0)
1010: 
1011:             stats.refreshGlobalCounts()
1012:             val persistedBatchNumber = stats.nextBatchSeq()
1013:             progressStore.update("EXPERT_DISCOVERY", TaskProgress(
1014:                 taskType = "EXPERT_DISCOVERY", status = "RUNNING",
1015:                 batchNumber = persistedBatchNumber,
1016:                 processedCount = stats.totalPapers.toLong(),
1017:                 totalCount = discoveryProperties.maxPapersPerRun.toLong(),
1018:                 message = "[${source.sourceName}] 批次 $batchNumber: 论文 ${sourceStats.papersSearched}/$sourceLimit, 收录 ${sourceStats.indexed}, 晋升 ${sourceStats.promoted}",
1019:                 details = buildProgressDetails(stats, source.sourceName, source.emailExtractionMethod),
1020:                 errors = snapshotErrors(stats),
1021:                 batchProcessed = batchProcessed,
1022:                 batchPassed = batchPassed,
1023:                 batchRejected = batchRejected.coerceAtLeast(0),
1024:                 batchRejectReasons = batchRejectReasons
1025:             ), execId)
1026: 
1027:             if (progressStore.isCancelled("EXPERT_DISCOVERY")) {
1028:                 stopReason = DiscoveryStopReason.CANCELLED
1029:                 break
1030:             }
1031:             if (rawWriteFailedInPage > 0) {
1032:                 stopReason = DiscoveryStopReason.RAW_WRITE_INCOMPLETE
1033:                 break
1034:             }
1035:             if (enqueueFailedInPage > 0) {
1036:                 stopReason = DiscoveryStopReason.ENQUEUE_INCOMPLETE
1037:                 break
1038:             }
1039:             if (dedupErrorsInPage > 0) {
1040:                 stopReason = DiscoveryStopReason.DEDUP_INCOMPLETE
1041:                 break
1042:             }
1043:             if (identityRepairFailedInPage) {
1044:                 stopReason = DiscoveryStopReason.IDENTITY_REPAIR_INCOMPLETE
1045:                 break
1046:             }
1047:             if (personRegistrationFailedInPage) {
1048:                 stopReason = DiscoveryStopReason.PERSON_REGISTRATION_INCOMPLETE
1049:                 break
1050:             }
1051:             if (limitReached || circuitBreakerTripped || timeBudgetExpired) {
1052:                 stopReason = when {
1053:                     circuitBreakerTripped -> DiscoveryStopReason.CIRCUIT_BREAKER
1054:                     timeBudgetExpired -> DiscoveryStopReason.TIME_BUDGET
1055:                     limitReason != null -> limitReason!!
1056:                     else -> DiscoveryStopReason.PAGE_PARTIAL
1057:                 }
1058:                 break
1059:             }
1060:             if (exhausted) break
1061:             cursor = batch.nextCursor
1062:         }
1063: 
1064:         val elapsed = System.currentTimeMillis() - sourceStartTime
1065:         // I-3: 累加真实来源运行片段，不覆盖首段（恢复回访是同一来源的后续片段）。
1066:         sourceStats.elapsedMs += elapsed
1067:         // I-1: 运行结束时落盘终止状态；失败/部分页/取消都停在 resumeCursor（进入该页的位置）。
1068:         persistCheckpoint(resumeCursor, stopReason, exhausted)
1069:         sourceStats.pendingWork = !exhausted
1070:         sourceStats.stopReason = stopReason
1071:         // I-5: 唯一的重试观察对象。真正开始/取消/超时/结束时只清空 nextRetryAt，保留轮次与脱敏原因
1072:         // 用于审计；只有本轮新安排的恢复项才覆盖轮次与计划时间。无恢复历史的来源不产生观察对象。
1073:         sourceStats.retry = retryPending?.let { pending ->
1074:             SourceRetryState(
1075:                 round = pending.round,
1076:                 maxRounds = pending.maxRounds,
1077:                 nextRetryAt = pending.nextRetryAt.toString(),
1078:                 reason = pending.reason
1079:             )
1080:         } ?: sourceStats.retry?.copy(nextRetryAt = null)
1081: 
1082:         log.info("[{}] 完成: 耗时 ${elapsed}ms, API请求 ${sourceStats.apiRequests} 次 | " +
1083:             "漏斗: 搜索 ${sourceStats.papersSearched} → 尝试全文 ${sourceStats.fulltextAttempted} → 获全文 ${sourceStats.fulltextObtained}" +
1084:             " (PDF下载失败 ${sourceStats.pdfDownloadFailed}, 解析失败 ${sourceStats.pdfParseFailed})" +
1085:             " → 抽到邮箱 ${sourceStats.authorsExtracted} (无邮箱 ${sourceStats.noEmailInFulltext})" +
1086:             " → 有效 ${sourceStats.emailsValid} (无效 ${sourceStats.emailsRejected})" +
1087:             " → 去重后 ${sourceStats.indexed} (重复 ${sourceStats.duplicates})" +
1088:             " → 收录L3 ${sourceStats.indexed} → 晋升L2 ${sourceStats.promoted}" +
1089:             " (过滤（含身份未确认） ${sourceStats.filtered}, 过滤原因 ${sourceStats.filterReasons})" +
1090:             (if (sourceStats.failureReasons.isNotEmpty()) ", 失败原因 ${sourceStats.failureReasons}" else ""),
1091:             source.sourceName)
1092: 
1093:         return SourceRunOutcome(resumeCursor, exhausted, stopReason, retry = retryPending)
1094:     }
1095: 
1096:     /**
1097:      * I-1/I-3：本轮内存中的延迟恢复项。恢复不创建新的 [DiscoveryStats]、executionId 或 deadline，
1098:      * 因此这里只保存重新进入同一来源所需的最小上下文：本轮固定的 runBudget（绝不重新分配）与
1099:      * 待恢复信息（页入口游标、已处理基线、恢复组轮次/计划时刻/脱敏原因）。
1100:      */
1101:     private class DeferredSourceRecovery(
1102:         val source: AcademicDataSource,
1103:         val runBudget: Int,
1104:         var pending: SourceRetryPending
1105:     )
1106: 
1107:     /**
1108:      * I-1/I-2/I-7：消费本轮待恢复项。只有所有来源（含 ORCID）都停稳后才进入这里，因此长等待不会
1109:      * 饿死其他来源。每轮先等到该组的计划时刻（≤100ms 片段，取消优先于时间与额度），再按同一页
1110:      * 入口回访；未到期不发任何请求，到界则保留游标、清等待时间并给真实停止原因。
1111:      */
1112:     private fun consumeDeferredRecoveries(
1113:         deferred: LinkedHashMap<String, DeferredSourceRecovery>,
1114:         criteria: PaperSearchCriteria,
1115:         stats: DiscoveryStats,
1116:         deadline: Instant,
1117:         trafficSession: DiscoveryTrafficMeter.Session,
1118:         execId: Long?,
1119:         verification: DiscoveryVerificationExecution
1120:     ) {
1121:         if (deferred.isEmpty()) return
1122:         for (round in 1..discoveryProperties.openAlexSearchRecoveryDelays.size) {
1123:             val waiting = deferred.values.filter { it.pending.round == round }
1124:             if (waiting.isEmpty()) continue
1125:             val waitStop = awaitRetryAt(waiting.minOf { it.pending.nextRetryAt }, deadline)
1126:             if (waitStop != null) {
1127:                 // I-7：取消/时间预算一旦发生，之后不得再发出任何恢复请求。
1128:                 waiting.forEach { stopDeferredRecovery(it, waitStop, stats) }
1129:                 return
1130:             }
1131:             for (item in waiting) {
1132:                 stats.refreshGlobalCounts()
1133:                 val blocked = when {
1134:                     progressStore.isCancelled("EXPERT_DISCOVERY") -> DiscoveryStopReason.CANCELLED
1135:                     timeBudgetReached(deadline) -> DiscoveryStopReason.TIME_BUDGET
1136:                     stats.totalPapers >= discoveryProperties.maxPapersPerRun -> DiscoveryStopReason.GLOBAL_PAPER_LIMIT
1137:                     stats.totalAuthors >= discoveryProperties.maxAuthorsPerRun -> DiscoveryStopReason.GLOBAL_AUTHOR_LIMIT
1138:                     else -> null
1139:                 }
1140:                 if (blocked != null) {
1141:                     stopDeferredRecovery(item, blocked, stats)
1142:                     log.info("[{}] 恢复回访未开始: 停止原因={}, 保留游标 {}",
1143:                         item.source.sourceName, blocked, item.pending.resumeCursor?.take(50) ?: "null")
1144:                     continue
1145:                 }
1146:                 beginRetryAttempt(stats, item, execId)
1147:                 val outcome = DiscoveryTrafficMeter.measure(
1148:                     trafficSession, item.source.sourceName, DiscoveryTrafficMeter.Category.METADATA
1149:                 ) {
1150:                     discoverFromSource(item.source, criteria, stats, item.runBudget, deadline, verification, resume = item.pending)
1151:                 }
1152:                 log.info("[{}] 恢复回访结束: stopReason={}, exhausted={}, resumeCursor={}",
1153:                     item.source.sourceName, outcome.stopReason, outcome.exhausted,
1154:                     outcome.resumeCursor?.take(50) ?: "null")
1155:                 stats.refreshGlobalCounts()
1156:                 val next = outcome.retry
1157:                 if (next == null) {
1158:                     deferred.remove(item.source.sourceName)
1159:                     writeRetryEvent(stats, item,
1160:                         "[${item.source.sourceName}] 恢复结束: 停止原因 ${outcome.stopReason}", execId)
1161:                 } else {
1162:                     item.pending = next
1163:                     writeRetryEvent(stats, item, retryWaitMessage(item), execId)
1164:                 }
1165:             }
1166:         }
1167:     }
1168: 
1169:     /**
1170:      * I-7：等到 [target]（检查粒度 ≤100ms，可中断）。返回非 null 表示等待被取消/时间预算打断，
1171:      * 调用方必须停止一切恢复请求；取消优先于时间。绝不一次睡满整个退避。
1172:      */
1173:     private fun awaitRetryAt(target: Instant, deadline: Instant): String? {
1174:         while (timeSource.now().isBefore(target)) {
1175:             if (progressStore.isCancelled("EXPERT_DISCOVERY")) return DiscoveryStopReason.CANCELLED
1176:             if (timeBudgetReached(deadline)) return DiscoveryStopReason.TIME_BUDGET
1177:             val remaining = Duration.between(timeSource.now(), target).toMillis().coerceAtLeast(1L)
1178:             try {
1179:                 timeSource.sleep(minOf(remaining, RETRY_WAIT_SLICE_MS))
1180:             } catch (_: InterruptedException) {
1181:                 Thread.currentThread().interrupt()
1182:                 return DiscoveryStopReason.CANCELLED
1183:             }
1184:         }
1185:         return when {
1186:             progressStore.isCancelled("EXPERT_DISCOVERY") -> DiscoveryStopReason.CANCELLED
1187:             timeBudgetReached(deadline) -> DiscoveryStopReason.TIME_BUDGET
1188:             else -> null
1189:         }
1190:     }
1191: 
1192:     /**
1193:      * I-5：开始一次真正的恢复回访 —— 清空 nextRetryAt（保留轮次/原因），并写一次带
1194:      * expectedExecutionId 的进度事件（等待期间不按 100ms 落日志）。
1195:      */
1196:     private fun beginRetryAttempt(stats: DiscoveryStats, item: DeferredSourceRecovery, execId: Long?) {
1197:         val sourceStats = stats.bySource[item.source.sourceName]
1198:         val retry = sourceStats?.retry
1199:         if (sourceStats != null && retry != null) sourceStats.retry = retry.copy(nextRetryAt = null)
1200:         writeRetryEvent(stats, item, "[${item.source.sourceName}] 开始恢复回访 " +
1201:             "${item.pending.round}/${item.pending.maxRounds}（原因 ${item.pending.reason}）", execId)
1202:     }
1203: 
1204:     /**
1205:      * I-4/I-5：到界时保留「未穷尽、可续跑」，清空计划时间并给真实停止原因；
1206:      * 不因先前的短暂失败记 SEARCH_FAILED，也不新增 sourceFailureCount。
1207:      */
1208:     private fun stopDeferredRecovery(item: DeferredSourceRecovery, reason: String, stats: DiscoveryStats) {
1209:         val sourceStats = stats.bySource[item.source.sourceName] ?: return
1210:         sourceStats.stopReason = reason
1211:         sourceStats.pendingWork = true
1212:         val retry = sourceStats.retry
1213:         if (retry != null) sourceStats.retry = retry.copy(nextRetryAt = null)
1214:     }
1215: 
1216:     /** I-5：运行结束（正常或异常）时清空所有「仍待执行」的计划时间，避免页面一直显示已过期的等待。 */
1217:     private fun clearRetryWait(stats: DiscoveryStats) {
1218:         stats.bySource.values.forEach { sourceStats ->
1219:             val retry = sourceStats.retry ?: return@forEach
1220:             if (retry.nextRetryAt != null) sourceStats.retry = retry.copy(nextRetryAt = null)
1221:         }
1222:     }
1223: 
1224:     /**
1225:      * I-5：写一次重试事件（安排等待 / 回访开始 / 恢复结束）。用 [RETRY_EVENT_BATCH_NUMBER] 作为
1226:      * 非批次日志号：`batchOnly=true` 只聚合 batchNumber>0 的日志，因此既有按批聚合规则逐字不变。
1227:      */
1228:     private fun writeRetryEvent(
1229:         stats: DiscoveryStats,
1230:         item: DeferredSourceRecovery,
1231:         message: String,
1232:         execId: Long?
1233:     ) {
1234:         progressStore.update("EXPERT_DISCOVERY", TaskProgress(
1235:             taskType = "EXPERT_DISCOVERY", status = "RUNNING",
1236:             batchNumber = RETRY_EVENT_BATCH_NUMBER,
1237:             processedCount = stats.totalPapers.toLong(),
1238:             totalCount = discoveryProperties.maxPapersPerRun.toLong(),
1239:             message = message,
1240:             details = buildProgressDetails(stats, item.source.sourceName, item.source.emailExtractionMethod),
1241:             errors = snapshotErrors(stats)
1242:         ), execId)
1243:     }
1244: 
1245:     /** I-5：等待恢复的运营端文案（轮次、计划重试时间按北京时间显示）。 */
1246:     private fun retryWaitMessage(item: DeferredSourceRecovery): String {
1247:         val pending = item.pending
1248:         return "[${item.source.sourceName}] 搜索${retryReasonLabel(pending.reason)}，等待恢复 " +
1249:             "${pending.round}/${pending.maxRounds}，计划重试时间 ${displayTime(pending.nextRetryAt.toString())}" +
1250:             "（北京时间）；先处理其他来源"
1251:     }
1252: 
1253:     private fun retryReasonLabel(reason: String): String = when {
1254:         reason == "TIMEOUT" -> "超时"
1255:         reason.startsWith("HTTP_") -> "服务不可用（$reason）"
1256:         else -> "连接中断"
1257:     }
1258: 
1259:     /**
1260:      * I-1：短尝试用尽后的延迟恢复决定。返回 null 表示不进入恢复（恢复列表为空或恢复组已用尽），
1261:      * 调用方按既有逻辑记一次终止性 SEARCH_FAILED。恢复组额度不因成功一页而重置：调用方把
1262:      * [groupsScheduled] 一直带在本次运行的局部状态里。
1263:      */
1264:     private fun planDeferredRecovery(
1265:         groupsScheduled: Int,
1266:         reason: String,
1267:         resumeCursor: String?,
1268:         processedBaseline: Int
1269:     ): SourceRetryPending? {
1270:         val delays = discoveryProperties.openAlexSearchRecoveryDelays
1271:         if (groupsScheduled >= delays.size) return null
1272:         val round = groupsScheduled + 1
1273:         return SourceRetryPending(
1274:             round = round,
1275:             maxRounds = delays.size,
1276:             nextRetryAt = timeSource.now().plus(delays[round - 1]),
1277:             reason = reason,
1278:             resumeCursor = resumeCursor,
1279:             processedBaseline = processedBaseline
1280:         )
1281:     }
1282: 
1283:     /**
1284:      * I-1：恢复原因只允许脱敏码，绝不携带 URL、查询串或密钥。调用方已确认错误在可重试白名单内
1285:      * （或属于 500/502/503/504）。
1286:      */
1287:     private fun retryReasonCode(error: Throwable?): String {
1288:         if (error is HttpStatusCodeException) return "HTTP_${error.statusCode.value()}"
1289:         val causes = generateSequence(error) { it.cause }.toList()
1290:         if (causes.any { it is SSLHandshakeException }) return "REMOTE_TLS_HANDSHAKE"
1291:         if (causes.any { it is SocketTimeoutException || it.javaClass.simpleName == "ConnectTimeoutException" }) {
1292:             return "TIMEOUT"
1293:         }
1294:         return "NETWORK_IO"
1295:     }
1296: 
1297:     /**
1298:      * I-1/I-2: ORCID 分页只在完整消费一页后推进游标；部分页、取消与失败保留进入该页的 offset。
1299:      * 推进量由 [OrcidDataSource.searchOrcidPage] 按原始返回条数算出，因此「一整页都没有公开邮箱」
1300:      * 也会前进而不是原地打转；只有所有主题分片遍历完（`nextCursor == null`）才判穷尽，
1301:      * EXHAUSTED 允许下一个扫描周期重开并靠去重避免重复收录。
1302:      */
1303:     private fun discoverFromOrcid(criteria: PaperSearchCriteria, stats: DiscoveryStats, deadline: Instant,
1304:                                   verification: DiscoveryVerificationExecution): SourceRunOutcome? {
1305:         val orcid = orcidProvider.getIfAvailable() ?: return null
1306:         if (criteria.sources.isNotEmpty() && !criteria.sources.contains(orcid.sourceName)) return null
1307: 
1308:         val sourceStats = stats.getOrCreateSourceStats(orcid.sourceName, "API_FIELD")
1309:         val execId = verification.executionId
1310:         val sourceStartTime = System.currentTimeMillis()
1311: 
1312:         val checkpoint = loadSourceCheckpoint(orcid.sourceName, criteria)
1313:         val enteringCursor: String? = if (checkpoint.exhausted) {
1314:             criteria.cursor
1315:         } else {
1316:             checkpoint.cursor ?: criteria.cursor
1317:         }
1318:         if (enteringCursor != null) {
1319:             log.info("[{}] 从上次检查点继续: offset={}", orcid.sourceName, enteringCursor)
1320:         } else if (checkpoint.exhausted) {
1321:             log.info("[{}] 上次已穷尽，本次扫描周期从头重开", orcid.sourceName)
1322:         }
1323: 
1324:         log.info("[{}] 开始: 方式=API_FIELD", orcid.sourceName)
1325: 
1326:         val orcidLimit = orcid.maxRecordsPerRun
1327:         // c9（I-2）：ORCID 的计量单位是「记录」，限额独立于论文全局 cap，只受作者总数防护约束。
1328:         sourceStats.unit = SourceUnit.RECORD
1329:         sourceStats.runBudget = orcidLimit
1330:         var cursor: String? = enteringCursor ?: "0"
1331:         var resumeCursor: String? = enteringCursor
1332:         var persistedRecords = 0
1333:         var batchNumber = 0
1334:         var recordsProcessed = 0
1335:         var exhausted = false
1336:         var stopReason = DiscoveryStopReason.EXHAUSTED
1337: 
1338:         fun persistCheckpoint(nextOffset: String?, reason: String, pageExhausted: Boolean) {
1339:             val delta = sourceStats.papersSearched - persistedRecords
1340:             persistSourceCheckpoint(orcid.sourceName, criteria, nextOffset, pageExhausted, delta)
1341:             persistedRecords = sourceStats.papersSearched
1342:             resumeCursor = nextOffset
1343:             exhausted = pageExhausted
1344:             stopReason = reason
1345:         }
1346: 
1347:         while (true) {
1348:             if (progressStore.isCancelled("EXPERT_DISCOVERY")) {
1349:                 log.info("[{}] 已取消", orcid.sourceName)
1350:                 stopReason = DiscoveryStopReason.CANCELLED
1351:                 break
1352:             }
1353:             stats.refreshGlobalCounts()
1354:             if (stats.totalAuthors >= discoveryProperties.maxAuthorsPerRun) {
1355:                 stopReason = DiscoveryStopReason.GLOBAL_AUTHOR_LIMIT
1356:                 break
1357:             }
1358:             if (recordsProcessed >= orcidLimit) {
1359:                 stopReason = DiscoveryStopReason.SOURCE_LIMIT
1360:                 break
1361:             }
1362:             // c9（I-3）：请求前 deadline 检查 —— 时间预算到点绝不发下一次 ORCID 请求。
1363:             if (timeBudgetReached(deadline)) {
1364:                 log.info("[{}] 时间预算到点，保留进入页 offset {}", orcid.sourceName, resumeCursor ?: "null")
1365:                 stopReason = DiscoveryStopReason.TIME_BUDGET
1366:                 break
1367:             }
1368: 
1369:             sourceStats.apiRequests++
1370: 
1371:             val page = try {
1372:                 orcid.searchOrcidPage(criteria.copy(cursor = cursor))
1373:             } catch (e: Exception) {
1374:                 recordTerminalSourceFailure(sourceStats, "SEARCH_FAILED")
1375:                 log.error("[{}] 搜索失败: {}", orcid.sourceName, e.message)
1376:                 stopReason = DiscoveryStopReason.SEARCH_FAILED
1377:                 break
1378:             }
1379:             val records = page.records
1380:             if (records.isEmpty()) {
1381:                 val next = page.nextCursor
1382:                 if (next == null) {
1383:                     // I-2/V-2: 只有「原始返回为 0 且没有下一分片」才算穷尽；
1384:                     // 一整页都没有公开邮箱时 nextCursor 仍会前进，必须继续翻页而不是停在原地。
1385:                     stopReason = DiscoveryStopReason.EXHAUSTED
1386:                     exhausted = true
1387:                     break
1388:                 }
1389:                 // I-2: 无公开邮箱的整页按原始条数推进 offset/分片后继续。
1390:                 cursor = next
1391:                 persistCheckpoint(next, DiscoveryStopReason.EMPTY_PAGE, false)
1392:                 continue
1393:             }
1394:             batchNumber++
1395:             val indexedBefore = sourceStats.indexed
1396:             val recordsProcessedBeforeBatch = recordsProcessed
1397:             val rawWriteFailedBefore = sourceStats.rawWriteFailed
1398:             val enqueueFailedBefore = sourceStats.failureReasons[ENRICHMENT_ENQUEUE_FAILED] ?: 0
1399:             val dedupErrorsBefore = sourceStats.dedupErrors
1400:             val rejectReasonsBefore = snapshotRejectReasons(sourceStats)
1401: 
1402:             // c9（I-3）：页内到点单独标记 —— 部分页按「进入该页」落盘，停止原因按 TIME_BUDGET 命名。
1403:             var timeBudgetExpired = false
1404:             var limitReason: String? = null
1405:             // 01（I-3）：本页是否有补证未完成（部分/失败）；有则本页不推进、不记成功。
1406:             var identityRepairFailedInPage = false
1407:             // 05（I-3）：本页是否有可重试登记失败（STALE/FAILED）；有则本页不推进、不记成功。
1408:             var personRegistrationFailedInPage = false
1409:             for (record in records) {
1410:                 if (progressStore.isCancelled("EXPERT_DISCOVERY")) break
1411:                 if (recordsProcessed >= orcidLimit) {
1412:                     limitReason = DiscoveryStopReason.SOURCE_LIMIT
1413:                     break
1414:                 }
1415:                 if (timeBudgetReached(deadline)) { timeBudgetExpired = true; break }
1416:                 stats.refreshGlobalCounts()
1417:                 if (stats.totalAuthors >= discoveryProperties.maxAuthorsPerRun) {
1418:                     limitReason = DiscoveryStopReason.GLOBAL_AUTHOR_LIMIT
1419:                     break
1420:                 }
1421:                 sourceStats.papersSearched++
1422:                 sourceStats.fulltextObtained++
1423:                 recordsProcessed++
1424: 
1425:                 val authorEmails = orcid.orcidRecordToAuthorEmails(record)
1426: 
1427:                 for (authorEmail in authorEmails) {
1428:                     stats.refreshGlobalCounts()
1429:                     if (stats.totalAuthors >= discoveryProperties.maxAuthorsPerRun) {
1430:                         limitReason = DiscoveryStopReason.GLOBAL_AUTHOR_LIMIT
1431:                         break
1432:                     }
1433:                     sourceStats.authorsExtracted++
1434: 
1435:                     val identityReject = identityRejection(authorEmail)
1436:                     if (identityReject != null) {
1437:                         sourceStats.filterReasons.merge(identityReject, 1) { a, b -> a + b }
1438:                         sourceStats.filtered++
1439:                         continue
1440:                     }
1441:                     val emailResult = emailValidationService.validate(authorEmail.email)
1442:                     if (!emailResult.valid) { sourceStats.emailsRejected++; continue }
1443:                     sourceStats.emailsValid++
1444: 
1445:                     // I-1（08）：与论文路径对称 —— 重复命中只按匹配文档真实 `_id` 补建缺失任务。
1446:                     val duplicate = when (val emailDedup = existsInRawIndexByEmail(authorEmail.email)) {
1447:                         is DedupResult.Exists -> emailDedup
1448:                         DedupResult.Error -> { sourceStats.dedupErrors++; continue }
1449:                         DedupResult.NotFound -> null
1450:                     }
1451:                     if (duplicate != null) {
1452:                         sourceStats.duplicates++
1453:                         val disposition = recordIdentityDuplicate(duplicate, authorEmail, sourceStats, orcid.sourceName, execId,
1454:                             record.orcidId)
1455:                         if (disposition.retryable) identityRepairFailedInPage = true
1456:                         if (disposition.person?.retryable == true) personRegistrationFailedInPage = true
1457:                         continue
1458:                     }
1459: 
1460:                     val profile = buildOrcidProfile(record, authorEmail, emailResult.level)
1461:                     val esDocId = ExpertIdGenerator.generate(null, DiscoveryIdentity.normalizedEmail(authorEmail.email))
1462:                     val eligibility = eligibilityService.evaluateEligibility(profile)
1463:                     val filterResult = if (eligibility.eligible) "PASSED" else "REJECTED"
1464:                     val rejectReasons = if (eligibility.eligible) emptyList() else eligibility.rejectReasons
1465: 
1466:                     val profileMap = toIndexMap(profile, null, esDocId, filterResult, rejectReasons)
1467:                     val registration = try {
1468:                         verificationRegistration?.reserve(verification, esDocId, profile, orcid.sourceName, record.orcidId)
1469:                             ?: DiscoveryRegistration()
1470:                     } catch (e: DiscoveryRegistrationException) {
1471:                         sourceStats.failureReasons.merge("VERIFICATION_REGISTRATION_FAILED", 1, Int::plus)
1472:                         personRegistrationFailedInPage = true
1473:                         continue
1474:                     }
1475:                     if (registration.protected) continue
1476:                     if (!expertIndexWriterService.indexToRaw(esDocId, profileMap)) { sourceStats.rawWriteFailed++; continue }
1477:                     sourceStats.indexed++
1478:                     // I-1（08）：RAW 落库成功后才入队；入队失败不推进本页。
1479:                     enqueueEnrichmentJob(esDocId, orcid.sourceName, execId, sourceStats)
1480:                     try {
1481:                         verificationRegistration?.activate(registration)
1482:                     } catch (e: DiscoveryRegistrationException) {
1483:                         sourceStats.failureReasons.merge("VERIFICATION_REGISTRATION_FAILED", 1, Int::plus)
1484:                         personRegistrationFailedInPage = true
1485:                         continue
1486:                     }
1487: 
1488:                     // 04（I-1/I-2）：统一准入在 RAW 成功后决定晋升，不再由旧 eligibility 单独决定。
1489:                     if (registration.item == null) admitDiscoveryExpert(esDocId, profile, eligibility, profileMap, sourceStats)
1490: 
1491:                     // 05（I-1/I-2）：ES 事实持久化后统一登记人物关系；登记失败按可重试语义传播。
1492:                     val person = registerPerson(orcid.sourceName, esDocId, sourceStats)
1493:                     if (person?.retryable == true) personRegistrationFailedInPage = true
1494:                 }
1495:                 if (limitReason != null) break
1496:             }
1497: 
1498:             val batchProcessed = recordsProcessed - recordsProcessedBeforeBatch
1499:             val batchPassed = sourceStats.indexed - indexedBefore
1500:             val batchRejected = batchProcessed - batchPassed
1501:             val batchRejectReasons = computeBatchRejectReasons(
1502:                 rejectReasonsBefore,
1503:                 snapshotRejectReasons(sourceStats)
1504:             )
1505: 
1506:             stats.refreshGlobalCounts()
1507:             val persistedBatchNumber = stats.nextBatchSeq()
1508:             progressStore.update("EXPERT_DISCOVERY", TaskProgress(
1509:                 taskType = "EXPERT_DISCOVERY", status = "RUNNING",
1510:                 batchNumber = persistedBatchNumber,
1511:                 processedCount = recordsProcessed.toLong(),
1512:                 totalCount = orcidLimit.toLong(),
1513:                 message = "[${orcid.sourceName}] 批次 $batchNumber: 记录 $recordsProcessed/$orcidLimit, 收录 ${sourceStats.indexed}, 晋升 ${sourceStats.promoted}",
1514:                 details = buildProgressDetails(stats, orcid.sourceName, "API_FIELD"),
1515:                 errors = snapshotErrors(stats),
1516:                 batchProcessed = batchProcessed,
1517:                 batchPassed = batchPassed,
1518:                 batchRejected = batchRejected.coerceAtLeast(0),
1519:                 batchRejectReasons = batchRejectReasons
1520:             ), execId)
1521: 
1522:             val pageFullyConsumed = recordsProcessed - recordsProcessedBeforeBatch == records.size
1523:             val rawWriteFailedInPage = sourceStats.rawWriteFailed - rawWriteFailedBefore
1524:             val enqueueFailedInPage = (sourceStats.failureReasons[ENRICHMENT_ENQUEUE_FAILED] ?: 0) - enqueueFailedBefore
1525:             val dedupErrorsInPage = sourceStats.dedupErrors - dedupErrorsBefore
1526:             if (rawWriteFailedInPage > 0) {
1527:                 log.warn("[{}] 批次 {} 内有 {} 条记录 RAW 写入失败，保留进入该页的 offset 以便重放",
1528:                     orcid.sourceName, batchNumber, rawWriteFailedInPage)
1529:             }
1530:             if (enqueueFailedInPage > 0) {
1531:                 log.warn("[{}] 批次 {} 内有 {} 条补全任务入队失败，保留进入该页的 offset 以便重放补建",
1532:                     orcid.sourceName, batchNumber, enqueueFailedInPage)
1533:             }
1534:             if (pageFullyConsumed && limitReason == null && !timeBudgetExpired && rawWriteFailedInPage == 0 &&
1535:                 enqueueFailedInPage == 0 && dedupErrorsInPage == 0 && !identityRepairFailedInPage &&
1536:                 !personRegistrationFailedInPage) {
1537:                 // I-2: 推进量由数据源按原始返回条数算好（不受邮箱过滤影响），这里只搬运它的游标。
1538:                 cursor = page.nextCursor
1539:                 persistCheckpoint(
1540:                     cursor,
1541:                     if (cursor == null) DiscoveryStopReason.EXHAUSTED else DiscoveryStopReason.PAGE_CONSUMED,
1542:                     cursor == null
1543:                 )
1544:                 if (exhausted) break
1545:             } else {
1546:                 // I-1: partial, interrupted, or failed pages retain their entering offset.
1547:                 persistCheckpoint(
1548:                     resumeCursor,
1549:                     when {
1550:                         progressStore.isCancelled("EXPERT_DISCOVERY") -> DiscoveryStopReason.CANCELLED
1551:                         rawWriteFailedInPage > 0 -> DiscoveryStopReason.RAW_WRITE_INCOMPLETE
1552:                         enqueueFailedInPage > 0 -> DiscoveryStopReason.ENQUEUE_INCOMPLETE
1553:                         dedupErrorsInPage > 0 -> DiscoveryStopReason.DEDUP_INCOMPLETE
1554:                         identityRepairFailedInPage -> DiscoveryStopReason.IDENTITY_REPAIR_INCOMPLETE
1555:                         personRegistrationFailedInPage -> DiscoveryStopReason.PERSON_REGISTRATION_INCOMPLETE
1556:                         timeBudgetExpired -> DiscoveryStopReason.TIME_BUDGET
1557:                         limitReason != null -> limitReason!!
1558:                         else -> DiscoveryStopReason.PAGE_PARTIAL
1559:                     },
1560:                     false
1561:                 )
1562:                 break
1563:             }
1564:         }
1565: 
1566:         val elapsed = System.currentTimeMillis() - sourceStartTime
1567:         sourceStats.elapsedMs = elapsed
1568:         persistCheckpoint(resumeCursor, stopReason, exhausted)
1569:         sourceStats.pendingWork = !exhausted
1570:         sourceStats.stopReason = stopReason
1571: 
1572:         log.info("[{}] 完成: 耗时 ${elapsed}ms | " +
1573:             "漏斗: 记录 ${recordsProcessed} → 邮箱 ${sourceStats.authorsExtracted}" +
1574:             " → 有效 ${sourceStats.emailsValid} (无效 ${sourceStats.emailsRejected})" +
1575:             " → 去重后 ${sourceStats.indexed} (重复 ${sourceStats.duplicates})" +
1576:             " → 收录L3 ${sourceStats.indexed} → 晋升L2 ${sourceStats.promoted}" +
1577:             " (过滤（含身份未确认） ${sourceStats.filtered}, 过滤原因 ${sourceStats.filterReasons})" +
1578:             (if (sourceStats.failureReasons.isNotEmpty()) ", 失败原因 ${sourceStats.failureReasons}" else ""),
1579:             orcid.sourceName)
1580: 
1581:         return SourceRunOutcome(resumeCursor, exhausted, stopReason)
1582:     }
1583: 
1584:     /** I-1/I-2（01b）：ORCID 的 `institution-name` 只表达来源关联，不证明当前雇主/职位，也不是国籍证据。 */
1585:     private fun buildOrcidProfile(record: OrcidDataSource.OrcidRecord, authorEmail: AuthorEmail, emailVerifiedLevel: Int): ExpertProfile {
1586:         return ExpertProfile(
1587:             orcidId = record.orcidId,
1588:             email = DiscoveryIdentity.normalizedEmail(authorEmail.email),
1589:             givenNames = record.givenNames,
1590:             familyNames = record.familyNames,
1591:             country = null,
1592:             keyword = null, employment = null,
1593:             institution = record.institutionName, lastPublicationYear = null,
1594:             emailSource = "ORCID_PUBLIC", emailVerifiedLevel = emailVerifiedLevel, dataSource = "ORCID",
1595:             externalIds = objectMapper.writeValueAsString(mapOf("orcid" to authorEmail.orcidId)),
1596:             identityVerification = proofFor(authorEmail)
1597:         // 02（I-1/I-2）：ORCID 证据只能基于 01b 的「唯一机构」判定（机构为 null 时本函数原样返回）。
1598:         ).withInstitutionEvidence(DiscoveryIdentity.EVIDENCE_SOURCE_ORCID)
1599:     }
1600: 
1601:     private data class PaperExtraction(
1602:         val outcome: EmailExtractionOutcome?,
1603:         val extractionError: String?
1604:     )
1605: 
1606:     private fun extractOutcome(
1607:         paper: PaperMetadata,
1608:         source: AcademicDataSource,
1609:         sampleContext: DiscoverySampleContext?
1610:     ): PaperExtraction {
1611:         return try {
1612:             val outcome = if (source.sourceName == "OPENALEX" && sampleContext?.valid() == true && sampleCapture != null) {
1613:                 sampleCapture.capture(sampleContext, paper) { source.extractAuthorEmails(paper) }
1614:             } else source.extractAuthorEmails(paper)
1615:             PaperExtraction(outcome, null)
1616:         } catch (e: Exception) {
1617:             PaperExtraction(null, e.message ?: e.javaClass.simpleName)
1618:         }
1619:     }
1620: 
1621:     /** I-7（c2）：RAW 晋升扫描 + 邮箱补全的**唯一**入口（旧 `discover(includeRawScan=true)` 与队列窗口共用同一门禁）。 */
1622:     fun runRawScan(backfillLimit: Int = 100) {
1623:         try {
1624:             revalidationService.promoteEligibleRawExperts()
1625:         } catch (e: Exception) {
1626:             log.warn("Failed to run RAW promotion scan during discovery", e)
1627:         }
1628:         try {
1629:             backfillRawEmailsAndPromote(backfillLimit)
1630:         } catch (e: Exception) {
1631:             log.warn("Failed to run RAW email backfill during discovery", e)
1632:         }
1633:     }
1634: 
1635:     /** I-4（c2）：消费一条工作时**唯一**的身份构造差异点 —— 论文走 [buildProfile]+论文主键，
1636:      * ORCID 记录走 [buildOrcidProfile]+ORCID 主键。门禁（邮箱校验/身份去重/资格/RAW 写/晋升/补全入队）
1637:      * 只有一份实现，论文路径与队列路径共用，绝不复制。
1638:      */
1639:     private fun interface ConsumeIdentityFactory {
1640:         fun create(authorEmail: AuthorEmail, emailVerifiedLevel: Int): ConsumeIdentity
1641:     }
1642: 
1643:     private data class ConsumeIdentity(
1644:         val profile: ExpertProfile,
1645:         val esDocId: String,
1646:         /** `toIndexMap` 的论文上下文；ORCID 记录为 null（与旧 ORCID 路径逐字一致）。 */
1647:         val paper: PaperMetadata?
1648:     )
1649: 
1650:     private fun paperIdentityFactory(paper: PaperMetadata): ConsumeIdentityFactory =
1651:         ConsumeIdentityFactory { authorEmail, emailVerifiedLevel ->
1652:             ConsumeIdentity(
1653:                 profile = buildProfile(paper, authorEmail, emailVerifiedLevel),
1654:                 esDocId = ExpertIdGenerator.generate(null, DiscoveryIdentity.normalizedEmail(authorEmail.email)),
1655:                 paper = paper
1656:             )
1657:         }
1658: 
1659:     private fun orcidIdentityFactory(record: OrcidDataSource.OrcidRecord): ConsumeIdentityFactory =
1660:         ConsumeIdentityFactory { authorEmail, emailVerifiedLevel ->
1661:             ConsumeIdentity(
1662:                 profile = buildOrcidProfile(record, authorEmail, emailVerifiedLevel),
1663:                 esDocId = ExpertIdGenerator.generate(null, DiscoveryIdentity.normalizedEmail(authorEmail.email)),
1664:                 paper = null
1665:             )
1666:         }
1667: 
1668:     private fun parallelExtractOutcomes(
1669:         papers: List<PaperMetadata>,
1670:         source: AcademicDataSource,
1671:         sampleContext: DiscoverySampleContext?
1672:     ): List<Pair<PaperMetadata, PaperExtraction>> {
1673:         if (papers.isEmpty()) return emptyList()
1674:         val trafficSession = DiscoveryTrafficMeter.currentSession()
1675:         fun extract(paper: PaperMetadata): PaperExtraction = if (trafficSession == null) {
1676:             extractOutcome(paper, source, sampleContext)
1677:         } else {
1678:             DiscoveryTrafficMeter.measure(trafficSession, source.sourceName, DiscoveryTrafficMeter.Category.FULLTEXT) {
1679:                 extractOutcome(paper, source, sampleContext)
1680:             }
1681:         }
1682:         if (discoveryProperties.fetchConcurrency <= 1) {
1683:             return papers.map { it to extract(it) }
1684:         }
1685:         val futures = papers.map { paper ->
1686:             paper to CompletableFuture.supplyAsync({ extract(paper) }, discoveryFetchExecutor)
1687:         }
1688:         return futures.map { (paper, future) -> paper to future.join() }
1689:     }
1690: 
1691:     private fun consumeOutcome(
1692:         paper: PaperMetadata,
1693:         extraction: PaperExtraction,
1694:         source: AcademicDataSource,
1695:         stats: DiscoveryStats,
1696:         sourceStats: SourceStats,
1697:         verification: DiscoveryVerificationExecution,
1698:         sourceItemKey: String
1699:     ): ConsumeOutcomeResult {
1700:         if (extraction.extractionError != null) {
1701:             stats.errors += "[${source.sourceName}] 提取失败: ${extraction.extractionError}"
1702:             sourceStats.failureReasons.merge("EXTRACTION_EXCEPTION", 1) { a, b -> a + b }
1703:             return ConsumeOutcomeResult(extractionFailed = true, failureReasons = mapOf("EXTRACTION_EXCEPTION" to 1))
1704:         }
1705:         val outcome = extraction.outcome!!
1706:         return consumeOutcomeInternal(
1707:             authorEmails = outcome.emails,
1708:             failureReason = outcome.failureReason,
1709:             downloadFailureCategory = outcome.downloadFailureCategory,
1710:             contentObtained = outcome.resolvedFulltextObtained(),
1711:             httpRequests = outcome.httpRequests,
1712:             sourceName = source.sourceName,
1713:             stats = stats,
1714:             sourceStats = sourceStats,
1715:             verification = verification,
1716:             sourceItemKey = sourceItemKey,
1717:             identityFactory = paperIdentityFactory(paper)
1718:         )
1719:     }
1720: 
1721:     /**
1722:      * I-4：消费（写入/晋升/补全入队）的唯一门禁实现，旧流程与队列共同调用。
1723:      *
1724:      * 返回值把旧流程里被吞掉的**可重试写失败**显式表达出来（RAW 写失败、补全入队失败、去重查询失败），
1725:      * 队列据此不把 job 置为成功；旧流程 [consumeOutcome] 忽略返回值，统计语义逐字不变。
1726:      */
1727:     private fun consumeOutcomeInternal(
1728:         authorEmails: List<AuthorEmail>,
