1729:         failureReason: String?,
1730:         downloadFailureCategory: String?,
1731:         contentObtained: Boolean,
1732:         httpRequests: Int,
1733:         sourceName: String,
1734:         stats: DiscoveryStats,
1735:         sourceStats: SourceStats,
1736:         verification: DiscoveryVerificationExecution,
1737:         sourceItemKey: String,
1738:         identityFactory: ConsumeIdentityFactory
1739:     ): ConsumeOutcomeResult {
1740:         val executionId = verification.executionId
1741:         sourceStats.fulltextAttempted++
1742:         sourceStats.apiRequests += httpRequests
1743: 
1744:         if (failureReason != null) {
1745:             sourceStats.failureReasons.merge(failureReason, 1) { a, b -> a + b }
1746:             if (failureReason == "PDF_DOWNLOAD_FAILED") sourceStats.pdfDownloadFailed++
1747:             if (failureReason == "PDF_PARSE_FAILED") sourceStats.pdfParseFailed++
1748:         }
1749:         // c10（I-3）：下载失败按低基数类别分桶（HTTP_403/404/429/5XX/4XX、TLS_ERROR、TIMEOUT、
1750:         // INVALID_CONTENT、NETWORK_ERROR），随 failureReasons 进入任务 details_json。
1751:         downloadFailureCategory?.let { category ->
1752:             sourceStats.failureReasons.merge(category, 1) { a, b -> a + b }
1753:         }
1754: 
1755:         if (authorEmails.isEmpty()) {
1756:             if (failureReason == "NO_PMC_ID" || failureReason == "NO_DOI") {
1757:                 sourceStats.papersSkippedNoId++
1758:             } else if (contentObtained) {
1759:                 // PDF/XML 取到内容但没有邮箱：内容获取成功、无邮箱单列。HTML 也计入这里，
1760:                 // 但取到的 HTML 不保证就是论文全文，故以 NO_EMAIL_IN_HTML 单列原因保留区分度。
1761:                 sourceStats.noEmailInFulltext++
1762:                 sourceStats.fulltextObtained++
1763:             } else {
1764:                 // PDF_DOWNLOAD_FAILED, PDF_PARSE_FAILED, NO_FULLTEXT, 超时/超限等 —— fulltext not obtained
1765:             }
1766:             return ConsumeOutcomeResult(
1767:                 fulltextAttempted = true,
1768:                 fulltextObtained = contentObtained,
1769:                 noEligibleEmail = true,
1770:                 failureReasons = snapshotFailureReasons(sourceStats)
1771:             )
1772:         }
1773: 
1774:         sourceStats.fulltextObtained++
1775: 
1776:         var emailsValid = 0
1777:         var indexed = 0
1778:         var duplicates = 0
1779:         var promoted = 0
1780:         var rawWriteFailed = false
1781:         var enqueueFailed = false
1782:         var dedupFailed = false
1783:         var identityRepairFailed = false
1784:         var verificationFailed = false
1785:         val personCounts = PersonRegistrationCounts()
1786:         val enqueueFailedBefore = sourceStats.failureReasons[ENRICHMENT_ENQUEUE_FAILED] ?: 0
1787: 
1788:         for (authorEmail in authorEmails) {
1789:             stats.refreshGlobalCounts()
1790:             if (stats.totalAuthors >= discoveryProperties.maxAuthorsPerRun) break
1791:             sourceStats.authorsExtracted++
1792: 
1793:             val identityReject = identityRejection(authorEmail)
1794:             if (identityReject != null) {
1795:                 sourceStats.filterReasons.merge(identityReject, 1) { a, b -> a + b }
1796:                 sourceStats.filtered++
1797:                 continue
1798:             }
1799:             val emailResult = emailValidationService.validate(authorEmail.email)
1800:             if (!emailResult.valid) { sourceStats.emailsRejected++; continue }
1801:             sourceStats.emailsValid++
1802:             emailsValid++
1803: 
1804:             // I-1（08）：重复命中时只按匹配文档的真实 `_id` 补建缺失任务，不重写整份专家、不算新增。
1805:             val duplicate = when (val emailDedup = existsInRawIndexByEmail(authorEmail.email)) {
1806:                 is DedupResult.Exists -> emailDedup
1807:                 DedupResult.Error -> { sourceStats.dedupErrors++; dedupFailed = true; continue }
1808:                 DedupResult.NotFound -> null
1809:             }
1810:             if (duplicate != null) {
1811:                 sourceStats.duplicates++
1812:                 duplicates++
1813:                 val disposition = recordIdentityDuplicate(duplicate, authorEmail, sourceStats, sourceName, executionId, sourceItemKey)
1814:                 if (disposition.retryable) identityRepairFailed = true
1815:                 personCounts.fold(disposition.person)
1816:                 continue
1817:             }
1818: 
1819:             val identity = identityFactory.create(authorEmail, emailResult.level)
1820:             val eligibility = eligibilityService.evaluateEligibility(identity.profile)
1821:             val filterResult = if (eligibility.eligible) "PASSED" else "REJECTED"
1822:             val rejectReasons = if (eligibility.eligible) emptyList() else eligibility.rejectReasons
1823: 
1824:             val profileMap = toIndexMap(identity.profile, identity.paper, identity.esDocId, filterResult, rejectReasons)
1825:             val registration = try {
1826:                 verificationRegistration?.reserve(verification, identity.esDocId, identity.profile, sourceName, sourceItemKey)
1827:                     ?: DiscoveryRegistration()
1828:             } catch (e: DiscoveryRegistrationException) {
1829:                 sourceStats.failureReasons.merge("VERIFICATION_REGISTRATION_FAILED", 1, Int::plus)
1830:                 verificationFailed = true
1831:                 continue
1832:             }
1833:             if (registration.protected) continue
1834:             if (!expertIndexWriterService.indexToRaw(identity.esDocId, profileMap)) {
1835:                 sourceStats.rawWriteFailed++
1836:                 rawWriteFailed = true
1837:                 continue
1838:             }
1839:             sourceStats.indexed++
1840:             indexed++
1841:             // I-1（08）：RAW 落库成功后才入队；入队失败不推进本页（见 enqueueEnrichmentJob）。
1842:             enqueueEnrichmentJob(identity.esDocId, sourceName, executionId, sourceStats)
1843:             try {
1844:                 verificationRegistration?.activate(registration)
1845:             } catch (e: DiscoveryRegistrationException) {
1846:                 sourceStats.failureReasons.merge("VERIFICATION_REGISTRATION_FAILED", 1, Int::plus)
1847:                 verificationFailed = true
1848:                 continue
1849:             }
1850: 
1851:             // 04（I-1/I-2）：统一准入在 RAW 成功后决定晋升。
1852:             if (registration.item == null && admitDiscoveryExpert(identity.esDocId, identity.profile, eligibility, profileMap, sourceStats) ==
1853:                 AdmissionDisposition.PROMOTED
1854:             ) {
1855:                 promoted++
1856:             }
1857: 
1858:             // 05（I-1/I-2）：ES 事实已持久化后统一登记人物关系（同 ORCID 的后续邮箱挂接同一人物）。
1859:             personCounts.fold(registerPerson(sourceName, identity.esDocId, sourceStats))
1860:         }
1861: 
1862:         enqueueFailed = (sourceStats.failureReasons[ENRICHMENT_ENQUEUE_FAILED] ?: 0) > enqueueFailedBefore
1863:         return ConsumeOutcomeResult(
1864:             fulltextAttempted = true,
1865:             fulltextObtained = true,
1866:             noEligibleEmail = false,
1867:             emailsValid = emailsValid,
1868:             indexedExperts = indexed,
1869:             duplicateExperts = duplicates,
1870:             promoted = promoted,
1871:             rawWriteFailed = rawWriteFailed,
1872:             enqueueFailed = enqueueFailed,
1873:             dedupFailed = dedupFailed,
1874:             identityRepairFailed = identityRepairFailed || verificationFailed,
1875:             personCreated = personCounts.created,
1876:             personAttached = personCounts.attached,
1877:             personNeedsReview = personCounts.needsReview,
1878:             personScopeConflict = personCounts.scopeConflict,
1879:             registrationFailed = personCounts.failed,
1880:             failureReasons = snapshotRejectReasons(sourceStats)
1881:         )
1882:     }
1883: 
1884: 
1885:     // ------------------------------------------------------------------
1886:     // c2（I-1/I-2/I-4/I-6）：持久化队列的采集 / 抽取 / 消费接缝
1887:     //
1888:     // 这三个方法把「取一页」「抽一条」「消费一条」变成可以分开调用、分开持久化的接缝：
1889:     // 取数与处理因此彻底解耦（R-1），而门禁（邮箱校验 / 身份去重 / 资格 / RAW 写 / 晋升 /
1890:     // 补全入队）仍然只有 [consumeOutcomeInternal] 一份实现。
1891:     // ------------------------------------------------------------------
1892: 
1893:     /**
1894:      * I-1/I-6：本查询下**实际参与**的来源名（已启用 + 未被学科范围排除）。
1895:      * 队列 API 只接受这里返回过的名字，绝不接受任意 source 字符串。
1896:      */
1897:     fun queueSourceNames(criteria: PaperSearchCriteria): List<String> {
1898:         val normalized = queueCriteria(criteria)
1899:         return resolveEnabledSources(normalized).map { it.sourceName } + orcidQueueSourceNames(normalized)
1900:     }
1901: 
1902:     /**
1903:      * I-1/I-6：ORCID **不在** [resolveEnabledSources] 的论文来源注册表里（它走独立的分页协议），
1904:      * 因此队列必须显式按同一套规则纳入它：显式指定来源时必须被点名，且不得被学科范围排除。
1905:      */
1906:     private fun orcidQueueSourceNames(criteria: PaperSearchCriteria): List<String> {
1907:         val orcid = orcidProvider.getIfAvailable() ?: return emptyList()
1908:         if (criteria.sources.isNotEmpty() && !criteria.sources.contains(orcid.sourceName)) return emptyList()
1909:         if (orcid.sourceName in SubjectScopeCatalog.excludedSources(criteria.subjectScope)) return emptyList()
1910:         return listOf(orcid.sourceName)
1911:     }
1912: 
1913:     /** I-1/I-6：来源名是否是**已校验**的 ORCID（它不在论文来源注册表里，协议也不同）。 */
1914:     private fun isQueueOrcidSource(criteria: PaperSearchCriteria, sourceName: String): Boolean =
1915:         orcidQueueSourceNames(queueCriteria(criteria)).contains(sourceName)
1916: 
1917:     /**
1918:      * I-1：队列查询的**唯一**规范化入口 —— 固定 `scope=RND_TARGET`（与旧定时运行同一学科范围）、
1919:      * 清空临时游标。所有 hash / 取数 / 抽取 / 消费都从这里出发，因此 stream 身份与实际查询永不脱节。
1920:      */
1921:     private fun queueCriteria(criteria: PaperSearchCriteria): PaperSearchCriteria =
1922:         criteria.copy(subjectScope = SubjectScopeCatalog.RND_TARGET, cursor = null)
1923: 
1924:     /**
1925:      * I-1：某一来源的规范化 query_hash —— 覆盖关键词、机构、国家排除、年份、OA、scope、页大小
1926:      * 与**本源自己的名字**，与其他 sources 的排列/省略方式无关（sources 恒归一为 `[sourceName]`）。
1927:      * 同一个来源在不同书写顺序/是否显式列出的条件下得到同一个 hash，因此 stream 身份稳定。
1928:      */
1929:     fun queueQueryHash(sourceName: String, criteria: PaperSearchCriteria): String {
1930:         require(sourceName.isNotBlank()) { "队列来源名不得为空" }
1931:         val canonical = DiscoveryCheckpointCodec.sourceCanonicalCriteria(sourceName,
1932:             queueCriteria(criteria).copy(sources = listOf(sourceName))
1933:         )
1934:         return sha256Hex(canonical)
1935:     }
1936: 
1937:     /**
1938:      * I-1：旧 v2 消费游标只用于**首次种子**，且只在完整条件可确认匹配时给出。
1939:      *
1940:      * 候选是恰好两种旧书写方式：显式单源（`sources=[source]`）与旧定时运行的「全部启用来源」
1941:      * （`sources=[]`）—— 数据源的查询构造不读 `criteria.sources`，所以这两种书写对**本源**等价。
1942:      *
1943:      * - 两条都找不到 → null（无法确认 → 从头保守重放）；
1944:      * - 任一条是 `EXHAUSTED`（没有游标）→ null：不能把「旧流程已翻到底」当成新流的穷尽，
1945:      *   否则会静默漏采；
1946:      * - 两条都存在且游标不同 → null：**禁止挑最大游标**，从头保守重放；
1947:      * - 结论唯一时才返回该游标。
1948:      */
1949:     fun queueLegacySeedCursor(sourceName: String, criteria: PaperSearchCriteria): String? {
1950:         if (sourceName.isBlank()) return null
1951:         val normalized = queueCriteria(criteria)
1952:         val forms = listOf(
1953:             normalized.copy(sources = listOf(sourceName)),
1954:             normalized.copy(sources = emptyList())
1955:         )
1956:         val checkpoints = forms.mapNotNull { form ->
1957:             val key = DiscoveryCheckpointCodec.sourceKey(sourceName, form)
1958:             val stored = try {
1959:                 cursorRepository.findBySourceName(key)?.cursorValue
1960:             } catch (e: Exception) {
1961:                 log.warn("[{}] 读取旧检查点 {} 失败: {}", sourceName, key, e.message)
1962:                 null
1963:             } ?: return@mapNotNull null
1964:             DiscoveryCheckpointCodec.decode(stored) ?: return@mapNotNull null
1965:         }
1966:         if (checkpoints.isEmpty()) return null
1967:         if (checkpoints.any { it.exhausted }) return null
1968:         val cursors = checkpoints.map { it.cursor }.distinct()
1969:         if (cursors.size != 1) return null
1970:         return cursors.first()
1971:     }
1972: 
1973:     /**
1974:      * I-1/I-2/I-6：队列的**单页**采集。每轮每源只取一页（[PIPELINE_PAGE_SIZE] 篇 / 100 条记录），
1975:      * 不使用 `maxPapersPerSource`/`maxPapersPerRun` 的当日上限（那是旧单次模式的语义）。
1976:      *
1977:      * 额度延期（OpenAlex）以 [QueuedSourcePage.deferredUntil] 表达，绝不是失败、也绝不置穷尽；
1978:      * 取数异常以 [QueuedSourcePage.errorReason] 表达，调用方按来源级错误安排下次尝试。
1979:      */
1980:     fun collectQueuePage(
1981:         sourceName: String,
1982:         criteria: PaperSearchCriteria,
1983:         cursor: String?,
1984:         metadataMaxBytes: Int
1985:     ): QueuedSourcePage {
1986:         val orcidSource = if (isQueueOrcidSource(criteria, sourceName)) orcidProvider.getIfAvailable() else null
1987:         val paperSource = if (orcidSource == null) {
1988:             resolveEnabledSources(queueCriteria(criteria)).firstOrNull { it.sourceName == sourceName }
1989:         } else {
1990:             null
1991:         }
1992:         if (orcidSource == null && paperSource == null) {
1993:             return QueuedSourcePage(
1994:                 sourceName = sourceName, unit = QueueItemUnit.PAPER, items = emptyList(),
1995:                 nextCursor = cursor, exhausted = false, errorReason = QUEUE_SOURCE_NOT_ENABLED
1996:             )
1997:         }
1998:         val unit = if (orcidSource != null) QueueItemUnit.RECORD else QueueItemUnit.PAPER
1999:         val pageCriteria = queueCriteria(criteria).copy(cursor = cursor, pageSize = PIPELINE_PAGE_SIZE)
2000:         return try {
2001:             when {
2002:                 orcidSource != null -> {
2003:                     val page = orcidSource.searchOrcidPage(pageCriteria)
2004:                     QueuedSourcePage(
2005:                         sourceName = sourceName, unit = unit,
2006:                         items = page.records.map { record ->
2007:                             envelopeForOrcid(sourceName, record, metadataMaxBytes)
2008:                         },
2009:                         nextCursor = page.nextCursor,
2010:                         exhausted = page.nextCursor == null
2011:                     )
2012:                 }
2013:                 paperSource is CoreDataSource -> {
2014:                     val page = paperSource.searchCorePage(pageCriteria)
2015:                     QueuedSourcePage(
2016:                         sourceName = sourceName, unit = unit,
2017:                         items = page.result.papers.map { paper ->
2018:                             envelopeForPaper(sourceName, paper, metadataMaxBytes)
2019:                         },
2020:                         nextCursor = page.result.nextCursor,
2021:                         exhausted = page.result.nextCursor == null,
2022:                         windowLimit = page.windowLimit
2023:                     )
2024:                 }
2025:                 else -> {
2026:                     val page = requireNotNull(paperSource).searchPapers(pageCriteria)
2027:                     QueuedSourcePage(
2028:                         sourceName = sourceName, unit = unit,
2029:                         items = page.papers.map { paper -> envelopeForPaper(sourceName, paper, metadataMaxBytes) },
2030:                         nextCursor = page.nextCursor,
2031:                         exhausted = page.nextCursor == null
2032:                     )
2033:                 }
2034:             }
2035:         } catch (e: OpenAlexBudgetDeferredException) {
2036:             log.warn("[{}] 队列采集额度延期至 {}：{}", sourceName, e.retryAt, e.reason)
2037:             QueuedSourcePage(
2038:                 sourceName = sourceName, unit = unit, items = emptyList(),
2039:                 nextCursor = cursor, exhausted = false,
2040:                 deferredUntil = e.retryAt, deferredReason = e.reason.name
2041:             )
2042:         } catch (e: Exception) {
2043:             log.warn("[{}] 队列采集单页失败: {}", sourceName, e.message)
2044:             QueuedSourcePage(
2045:                 sourceName = sourceName, unit = unit, items = emptyList(),
2046:                 nextCursor = cursor, exhausted = false, errorReason = QUEUE_SEARCH_FAILED
2047:             )
2048:         }
2049:     }
2050: 
2051:     /**
2052:      * I-4/I-6：在**队列提取作用域**内抽取一条工作。
2053:      *
2054:      * - 作用域由本方法进入/退出（finally），因此每目标域的并发许可只对本队列提取生效；
2055:      * - 域名许可不可得 → [QueuedItemExtraction.HostBusy]（外层延期，不消耗 attempts）；
2056:      * - 适配器抛异常 → [QueuedItemExtraction.Failed]（可重试，受 5 次上限约束）；
2057:      * - 抽取成功（含「确实没有邮箱」）→ [QueuedItemExtraction.Extracted]，结果**可持久化**，
2058:      *   后续重试直接消费、不再下载；
2059:      * - 结果超过 `extractionMaxBytes` → [QueuedItemExtraction.TooLarge]（FAILED/EXTRACTION_TOO_LARGE）。
2060:      */
2061:     fun extractQueuedItem(
2062:         envelope: QueuedItemEnvelope,
2063:         criteria: PaperSearchCriteria,
2064:         perHostConcurrency: Int,
2065:         extractionMaxBytes: Int,
2066:         sampleContext: DiscoverySampleContext? = null
2067:     ): QueuedItemExtraction {
2068:         if (envelope.payloadVersion != QUEUED_PAYLOAD_VERSION_V1) {
2069:             return QueuedItemExtraction.Failed(QUEUE_UNKNOWN_PAYLOAD_VERSION, retryable = false)
2070:         }
2071:         val orcidSource = if (isQueueOrcidSource(criteria, envelope.sourceName)) {
2072:             orcidProvider.getIfAvailable()
2073:         } else {
2074:             null
2075:         }
2076:         val paperSource = if (orcidSource == null) {
2077:             resolveEnabledSources(queueCriteria(criteria)).firstOrNull { it.sourceName == envelope.sourceName }
2078:         } else {
2079:             null
2080:         }
2081:         if (orcidSource == null && paperSource == null) {
2082:             return QueuedItemExtraction.Failed(QUEUE_SOURCE_NOT_ENABLED, retryable = false)
2083:         }
2084:         val deadline = Instant.now().plusMillis(FulltextRequestGate.PAPER_DEADLINE_MS)
2085:         return FulltextRequestGate.inQueueExtractionScope(perHostConcurrency, deadline) {
2086:             try {
2087:                 val outcome = when (envelope.unit) {
2088:                     QueueItemUnit.RECORD -> {
2089:                         val orcid = orcidSource
2090:                             ?: return@inQueueExtractionScope QueuedItemExtraction.Failed(
2091:                                 QUEUE_SOURCE_NOT_ENABLED, retryable = false
2092:                             )
2093:                         EmailExtractionOutcome(
2094:                             emails = orcid.orcidRecordToAuthorEmails(readOrcidRecord(envelope.payloadJson)),
2095:                             methodUsed = "API_FIELD"
2096:                         )
2097:                     }
2098:                     else -> {
2099:                         val source = requireNotNull(paperSource)
2100:                         val paper = readPaper(envelope.payloadJson)
2101:                         if (envelope.unit == QueueItemUnit.PAPER && source.sourceName == "OPENALEX" &&
2102:                             sampleContext?.valid() == true && sampleCapture != null) {
2103:                             sampleCapture.capture(sampleContext, paper) { source.extractAuthorEmails(paper) }
2104:                         } else source.extractAuthorEmails(paper)
2105:                     }
2106:                 }
2107:                 if (FulltextRequestGate.hostBusyInScope()) {
2108:                     QueuedItemExtraction.HostBusy
2109:                 } else {
2110:                     val json = objectMapper.writeValueAsString(outcome.copy(identityRuleVersion = DiscoveryIdentity.EXTRACTION_VERSION))
2111:                     if (utf8Bytes(json) > extractionMaxBytes) {
2112:                         QueuedItemExtraction.TooLarge
2113:                     } else {
2114:                         QueuedItemExtraction.Extracted(
2115:                             extractionJson = json,
2116:                             emailsEmpty = outcome.emails.isEmpty(),
2117:                             httpRequests = outcome.httpRequests,
2118:                             contentObtained = outcome.resolvedFulltextObtained(),
2119:                             failureReason = outcome.failureReason
2120:                         )
2121:                     }
2122:                 }
2123:             } catch (e: FulltextRequestGate.HostBusyException) {
2124:                 QueuedItemExtraction.HostBusy
2125:             } catch (e: Exception) {
2126:                 QueuedItemExtraction.Failed(e.message ?: e.javaClass.simpleName, retryable = true)
2127:             }
2128:         }
2129:     }
2130: 
2131:     /**
2132:      * I-4：幂等消费已保存的抽取结果 —— 专家写入仍走原来的门禁与补全入队，
2133:      * 只有必要写入全部成功（或明确「无合格邮箱」）才返回 `succeeded = true`。
2134:      *
2135:      * 计数与门禁使用的 `stats` 是**每次调用的临时对象**：它是门禁的输入（作者防护等），
2136:      * 不是队列的权威计数（I-8 的计数在 pipeline/stream 行上按终态 CAS 累加）。
2137:      */
2138:     fun consumeQueuedItem(
2139:         envelope: QueuedItemEnvelope,
2140:         extractionJson: String,
2141:         verification: DiscoveryVerificationExecution
2142:     ): QueuedItemConsumption {
2143:         if (envelope.payloadVersion != QUEUED_PAYLOAD_VERSION_V1) {
2144:             return QueuedItemConsumption(unrecoverableReason = QUEUE_UNKNOWN_PAYLOAD_VERSION)
2145:         }
2146:         val extraction = try {
2147:             objectMapper.readValue(extractionJson, EmailExtractionOutcome::class.java)
2148:         } catch (e: Exception) {
2149:             return QueuedItemConsumption(unrecoverableReason = QUEUE_EXTRACTION_UNREADABLE)
2150:         }
2151:         if (extraction.identityRuleVersion != DiscoveryIdentity.EXTRACTION_VERSION) {
2152:             return QueuedItemConsumption(unrecoverableReason = "IDENTITY_EXTRACTION_VERSION_UNSUPPORTED")
2153:         }
2154:         val identityFactory = try {
2155:             when (envelope.unit) {
2156:                 QueueItemUnit.RECORD -> orcidIdentityFactory(readOrcidRecord(envelope.payloadJson))
2157:                 else -> paperIdentityFactory(readPaper(envelope.payloadJson))
2158:             }
2159:         } catch (e: Exception) {
2160:             return QueuedItemConsumption(unrecoverableReason = QUEUE_PAYLOAD_UNREADABLE)
2161:         }
2162:         val stats = DiscoveryStats()
2163:         val sourceStats = stats.getOrCreateSourceStats(envelope.sourceName, "PIPELINE")
2164:         val result = consumeOutcomeInternal(
2165:             authorEmails = extraction.emails,
2166:             failureReason = extraction.failureReason,
2167:             downloadFailureCategory = extraction.downloadFailureCategory,
2168:             contentObtained = extraction.resolvedFulltextObtained(),
2169:             httpRequests = extraction.httpRequests,
2170:             sourceName = envelope.sourceName,
2171:             stats = stats,
2172:             sourceStats = sourceStats,
2173:             verification = verification,
2174:             sourceItemKey = envelope.itemKey,
2175:             identityFactory = identityFactory
2176:         )
2177:         return QueuedItemConsumption(
2178:             indexedExperts = result.indexedExperts,
2179:             duplicateExperts = result.duplicateExperts,
2180:             promoted = result.promoted,
2181:             emailsValid = result.emailsValid,
2182:             rawWriteFailed = result.rawWriteFailed,
2183:             enqueueFailed = result.enqueueFailed,
2184:             dedupFailed = result.dedupFailed,
2185:             identityRepairFailed = result.identityRepairFailed,
2186:             personCreated = result.personCreated,
2187:             personAttached = result.personAttached,
2188:             personNeedsReview = result.personNeedsReview,
2189:             personScopeConflict = result.personScopeConflict,
2190:             registrationFailed = result.registrationFailed,
2191:             failureReasons = result.failureReasons
2192:         )
2193:     }
2194: 
2195:     /** I-2：论文条目的身份 + 版本化负载（全文只在**没有可重取地址**时才进负载）。 */
2196:     private fun envelopeForPaper(
2197:         sourceName: String,
2198:         paper: PaperMetadata,
2199:         metadataMaxBytes: Int
2200:     ): QueuedItemEnvelope {
2201:         val publiclyDownloadable = !paper.downloadUrl.isNullOrBlank() || paper.candidateDownloadUrls.isNotEmpty()
2202:         val embeddedFullText = if (publiclyDownloadable) null else paper.fullText
2203:         val stored = paper.copy(fullText = embeddedFullText)
2204:         val payloadJson = objectMapper.writeValueAsString(stored)
2205:         val bytes = utf8Bytes(payloadJson)
2206:         val (itemKey, quality) = paperIdentity(paper, payloadJson)
2207:         return QueuedItemEnvelope(
2208:             sourceName = sourceName,
2209:             itemKey = itemKey,
2210:             identityQuality = quality,
2211:             unit = QueueItemUnit.PAPER,
2212:             payloadVersion = QUEUED_PAYLOAD_VERSION_V1,
2213:             payloadJson = payloadJson,
2214:             payloadBytes = bytes,
2215:             publiclyDownloadable = publiclyDownloadable || !embeddedFullText.isNullOrBlank(),
2216:             rejectReason = if (bytes > metadataMaxBytes) QUEUE_PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE else null
2217:         )
2218:     }
2219: 
2220:     /** I-2：ORCID 条目的身份 + 版本化负载；键就是完整 ORCID，不做哈希。 */
2221:     private fun envelopeForOrcid(
2222:         sourceName: String,
2223:         record: OrcidDataSource.OrcidRecord,
2224:         metadataMaxBytes: Int
2225:     ): QueuedItemEnvelope {
2226:         val payloadJson = objectMapper.writeValueAsString(record)
2227:         val bytes = utf8Bytes(payloadJson)
2228:         val orcid = record.orcidId.trim()
2229:         return QueuedItemEnvelope(
2230:             sourceName = sourceName,
2231:             itemKey = orcid.ifBlank { "RECORD:${sha256Hex(payloadJson)}" },
2232:             identityQuality = if (orcid.isBlank()) QueueIdentityQuality.PAYLOAD_HASH else QueueIdentityQuality.ORCID,
2233:             unit = QueueItemUnit.RECORD,
2234:             payloadVersion = QUEUED_PAYLOAD_VERSION_V1,
2235:             payloadJson = payloadJson,
2236:             payloadBytes = bytes,
2237:             publiclyDownloadable = true,
2238:             rejectReason = when {
2239:                 orcid.isBlank() -> QUEUE_UNIDENTIFIABLE
2240:                 bytes > metadataMaxBytes -> QUEUE_PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE
2241:                 else -> null
2242:             }
2243:         )
2244:     }
2245: 
2246:     /**
2247:      * I-2：论文工作身份 —— 优先「类型前缀 + 规范化标识的 SHA-256」（DOI/PMCID/PMID），
2248:      * 没有可靠标识时退化为**规范序列化元数据**的 SHA-256 并标记 `PAYLOAD_HASH`。
2249:      * 绝不用标题做模糊合并（标题相似的两篇不同论文必须两条）。
2250:      */
2251:     private fun paperIdentity(paper: PaperMetadata, payloadJson: String): Pair<String, String> {
2252:         normalizedDoi(paper.doi)?.let {
2253:             return "${QueueIdentityQuality.DOI}:${sha256Hex(it)}" to QueueIdentityQuality.DOI
2254:         }
2255:         normalizedPmcid(paper.pmcId)?.let {
2256:             return "${QueueIdentityQuality.PMCID}:${sha256Hex(it)}" to QueueIdentityQuality.PMCID
2257:         }
2258:         normalizedPmid(paper.pmid)?.let {
2259:             return "${QueueIdentityQuality.PMID}:${sha256Hex(it)}" to QueueIdentityQuality.PMID
2260:         }
2261:         return "${QueueIdentityQuality.PAYLOAD_HASH}:${sha256Hex(payloadJson)}" to QueueIdentityQuality.PAYLOAD_HASH
2262:     }
2263: 
2264:     private fun normalizedDoi(raw: String?): String? {
2265:         val value = raw?.trim()?.lowercase(Locale.ROOT) ?: return null
2266:         if (value.isEmpty()) return null
2267:         return value
2268:             .removePrefix("https://doi.org/")
2269:             .removePrefix("http://doi.org/")
2270:             .removePrefix("https://dx.doi.org/")
2271:             .removePrefix("doi:")
2272:             .trim()
2273:             .ifEmpty { null }
2274:     }
2275: 
2276:     private fun normalizedPmcid(raw: String?): String? {
2277:         val value = raw?.trim()?.uppercase(Locale.ROOT) ?: return null
2278:         if (value.isEmpty()) return null
2279:         return if (value.startsWith("PMC")) value else "PMC$value"
2280:     }
2281: 
2282:     private fun normalizedPmid(raw: String?): String? {
2283:         val digits = raw?.trim()?.filter { it.isDigit() } ?: return null
2284:         return digits.ifEmpty { null }
2285:     }
2286: 
2287:     private fun readPaper(payloadJson: String): PaperMetadata =
2288:         objectMapper.readValue(payloadJson, PaperMetadata::class.java)
2289: 
2290:     private fun readOrcidRecord(payloadJson: String): OrcidDataSource.OrcidRecord =
2291:         objectMapper.readValue(payloadJson, OrcidDataSource.OrcidRecord::class.java)
2292: 
2293:     private fun sha256Hex(value: String): String {
2294:         val digest = java.security.MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256").digest(value.toByteArray(Charsets.UTF_8))
2295:         val builder = StringBuilder(digest.size * 2)
2296:         for (byte in digest) {
2297:             builder.append(HEX_DIGITS[(byte.toInt() shr 4) and 0x0F])
2298:             builder.append(HEX_DIGITS[byte.toInt() and 0x0F])
2299:         }
2300:         return builder.toString()
2301:     }
2302: 
2303:     private fun utf8Bytes(value: String): Long = value.toByteArray(Charsets.UTF_8).size.toLong()
2304: 
2305:     // ------------------------------------------------------------------
2306:     // I-1（08）：RAW 先落地再入队
2307:     // ------------------------------------------------------------------
2308: 
2309:     /**
2310:      * 新增专家（RAW 已落库）的补全入队。数据库错误**不吞掉**：计入
2311:      * [ENRICHMENT_ENQUEUE_FAILED]，让当前页按 [DiscoveryStopReason.ENQUEUE_INCOMPLETE] 保留重放
2312:      * （重放时该专家已是重复命中，会由 [ensureEnrichmentJob] 补建缺失任务），
2313:      * 既不假装任务已建、也不推进检查点。
2314:      */
2315:     private fun enqueueEnrichmentJob(docId: String, sourceName: String, executionId: Long?, sourceStats: SourceStats) {
2316:         try {
2317:             enrichmentJobService.enqueue(docId, sourceName, executionId)
2318:         } catch (e: Exception) {
2319:             log.warn("Failed to enqueue academic enrichment job for {} (source={}): {}", docId, sourceName, e.message)
2320:             sourceStats.failureReasons.merge(ENRICHMENT_ENQUEUE_FAILED, 1) { a, b -> a + b }
2321:         }
2322:     }
2323: 
2324:     /**
2325:      * 重放/重复发现的补建入口：只幂等入队（07 的存储按 `UNIQUE(expert_doc_id)` 合并，
2326:      * 可靠身份变更时重开 `UNMATCHED`），绝不重新写整份专家文档。
2327:      */
2328:     private fun ensureEnrichmentJob(docId: String, sourceName: String, executionId: Long?, sourceStats: SourceStats) {
2329:         enqueueEnrichmentJob(docId, sourceName, executionId, sourceStats)
2330:     }
2331: 
2332:     private fun identityRejection(author: AuthorEmail): String? = when {
2333:         author.givenNames.isNullOrBlank() || author.familyNames.isNullOrBlank() -> "IDENTITY_UNRESOLVED"
2334:         else -> null
2335:     }
2336: 
2337:     private fun proofFor(author: AuthorEmail) = author.identityEvidence?.takeIf { identityRejection(author) == null && DiscoveryIdentity.validEvidence(it) }?.let {
2338:         DiscoveryIdentity.verified(author.email, author.givenNames, author.familyNames, it, author.orcidId, author.openAlexAuthorId)
2339:     }
2340: 
2341:     /**
2342:      * 01/05（I-1/I-2/I-3）：重复命中的处置。[DuplicateDisposition.retryable] 为 true 表示本轮必须
2343:      * 保留可重试错误（部分/失败补证或可重试登记失败），消费入口据此不推进当前页、不把 job 记成功。
2344:      * [DuplicateDisposition.person] 是本出口触发的人物登记刷新结果（未触发时为 null）。
2345:      * 仍只计 duplicate、不计 indexed。
2346:      */
2347:     private fun recordIdentityDuplicate(existing: DedupResult.Exists, incoming: AuthorEmail, stats: SourceStats,
2348:         sourceName: String, executionId: Long?, sourceItemKey: String): DuplicateDisposition {
2349:         if (existing.docId != null) {
2350:             try {
2351:                 verificationRegistration?.recoverDuplicate(existing.docId, sourceName, sourceItemKey)
2352:             } catch (e: DiscoveryRegistrationException) {
2353:                 stats.failureReasons.merge("VERIFICATION_REGISTRATION_FAILED", 1, Int::plus)
2354:                 return DuplicateDisposition(retryable = true, person = null)
2355:             }
2356:         }
2357:         val source = existing.source
2358:         val proof = source?.let { DiscoveryIdentity.read(it.path("identityVerification")) }
2359:         val externalIds = source?.path("externalIds")?.let {
2360:             if (it.isTextual) try { objectMapper.readTree(it.asText()) } catch (_: Exception) { null } else it
2361:         }
2362:         fun conflictingId(stored: String?, incomingId: String?) =
2363:             !stored.isNullOrBlank() && !incomingId.isNullOrBlank() && stored != incomingId
2364:         val mismatch = source != null && (source.path("givenNames").asText(null) != incoming.givenNames ||
2365:             source.path("familyNames").asText(null) != incoming.familyNames ||
2366:             DiscoveryIdentity.normalizedEmail(source.path("email").asText(null)) != DiscoveryIdentity.normalizedEmail(incoming.email) ||
2367:             (proof?.orcid != null && incoming.orcidId != null && proof.orcid != incoming.orcidId) ||
2368:             (proof?.openAlexAuthorId != null && incoming.openAlexAuthorId != null && proof.openAlexAuthorId != incoming.openAlexAuthorId) ||
2369:             conflictingId(externalIds?.path("orcid")?.asText(null), incoming.orcidId) ||
2370:             conflictingId(externalIds?.path("openAlexAuthorId")?.asText(null), incoming.openAlexAuthorId))
2371:         // A matching retry can recover a missing job without rewriting the profile. The enrichment
2372:         // worker independently requires bound author IDs; no proof means no academic author query.
2373:         if (mismatch || source == null || !DiscoveryIdentity.isDiscoverySource(source)) {
2374:             stats.failureReasons.merge(if (mismatch) "IDENTITY_EXISTING_CONFLICT" else "IDENTITY_EXISTING_UNVERIFIED", 1) { a, b -> a + b }
2375:             return DuplicateDisposition(retryable = false, person = null)
2376:         }
2377:         val docId = existing.docId
2378:         // 01（I-1）：只有带完整姓名与有效证据的合法 incoming 才进入补证；否则沿用原入队行为。
2379:         val repair = if (docId != null && proofFor(incoming) != null) {
2380:             identityRepairService?.repairDuplicateIdentity(docId, incoming)
2381:         } else {
2382:             null
2383:         }
2384:         if (repair != null && docId != null) {
2385:             stats.failureReasons.merge("IDENTITY_REPAIR_${repair.outcome.name}", 1) { a, b -> a + b }
2386:             return when (repair.outcome) {
2387:                 // 补证成功或本就已有 proof：入队并沿用既有准入/重验接缝；登记由补证内统一刷新。
2388:                 IdentityRepairOutcome.REPAIRED, IdentityRepairOutcome.ALREADY_VERIFIED -> {
2389:                     ensureEnrichmentJob(docId, sourceName, executionId, stats)
2390:                     DuplicateDisposition(retryable = false, person = repair.personRegistration)
2391:                 }
2392:                 // 缺姓名/冲突：独立原因、零写入，交给 02 定向恢复；不补建学术任务、不登记关系。
2393:                 IdentityRepairOutcome.MISSING_NAME_NEEDS_RECOVERY, IdentityRepairOutcome.CONFLICT ->
2394:                     DuplicateDisposition(retryable = false, person = null)
2395:                 // 部分/整体失败：保留可重试错误，绝不推进为修复成功，也不先行入队/登记。
2396:                 IdentityRepairOutcome.PARTIAL, IdentityRepairOutcome.FAILED ->
2397:                     DuplicateDisposition(retryable = true, person = null)
2398:             }
2399:         }
2400:         // 05（I-1）：无补证可走的重复命中（已有可信 discovered 事实但 incoming 无凭证）——仍按当前
2401:         // 持久化事实登记一次，确保重复出口归人；登记失败按可重试语义传播。
2402:         if (docId == null) return DuplicateDisposition(retryable = false, person = null)
2403:         ensureEnrichmentJob(docId, sourceName, executionId, stats)
2404:         val person = registerPerson(sourceName, docId, stats)
2405:         return DuplicateDisposition(retryable = person?.retryable == true, person = person)
2406:     }
2407: 
2408:     /** 05（I-1/I-3）：重复出口的处置结果（补证可重试标记 + 人物登记刷新结果）。 */
2409:     private data class DuplicateDisposition(val retryable: Boolean, val person: DiscoveryPersonRefreshResult?)
2410: 
2411:     /**
2412:      * 05（I-1/I-3）：一次消费/一页内的人物登记计数口径（新增人物、追加邮箱、需复核、范围冲突、登记失败）。
2413:      * 单位相互独立：新增人物 ≠ 追加邮箱 ≠ 重复事实；[failed] 表示存在可重试登记失败。
2414:      */
2415:     private class PersonRegistrationCounts {
2416:         var created = 0
2417:         var attached = 0
2418:         var needsReview = 0
2419:         var scopeConflict = 0
2420:         var failed = false
2421: 
2422:         fun fold(result: DiscoveryPersonRefreshResult?) {
2423:             when (result?.status) {
2424:                 DiscoveryPersonRefreshStatus.CREATED -> created++
2425:                 DiscoveryPersonRefreshStatus.ATTACHED -> attached++
2426:                 DiscoveryPersonRefreshStatus.NEEDS_REVIEW -> needsReview++
2427:                 DiscoveryPersonRefreshStatus.SCOPE_CONFLICT -> scopeConflict++
2428:                 DiscoveryPersonRefreshStatus.STALE, DiscoveryPersonRefreshStatus.FAILED -> failed = true
2429:                 else -> {}
2430:             }
2431:         }
2432:     }
2433: 
2434:     /**
2435:      * 05（I-1/I-3）：统一人物登记调用点。接缝为空时直接返回 null（历史行为）。异常按可重试失败
2436:      * 处理；需复核/范围冲突/登记失败原因进入 failureReasons 供页面传播，绝不吞掉可重试信号。
2437:      */
2438:     private fun registerPerson(source: String, docId: String, stats: SourceStats): DiscoveryPersonRefreshResult? {
2439:         val registrar = personRegistrationService ?: return null
2440:         val result = try {
2441:             registrar.refresh(source, docId)
2442:         } catch (e: Exception) {
2443:             log.warn("人物登记刷新异常 docId={}: {}", docId, e.message)
2444:             DiscoveryPersonRefreshResult(DiscoveryPersonRefreshStatus.FAILED, "REGISTRATION_EXCEPTION")
2445:         }
2446:         when (result.status) {
2447:             DiscoveryPersonRefreshStatus.NEEDS_REVIEW ->
2448:                 stats.failureReasons.merge("PERSON_NEEDS_REVIEW", 1) { a, b -> a + b }
2449:             DiscoveryPersonRefreshStatus.SCOPE_CONFLICT ->
2450:                 stats.failureReasons.merge("PERSON_SCOPE_CONFLICT", 1) { a, b -> a + b }
2451:             DiscoveryPersonRefreshStatus.STALE, DiscoveryPersonRefreshStatus.FAILED ->
2452:                 stats.failureReasons.merge("PERSON_REGISTRATION_FAILED", 1) { a, b -> a + b }
2453:             else -> {}
2454:         }
2455:         return result
2456:     }
2457: 
2458:     private fun buildProfile(paper: PaperMetadata, authorEmail: AuthorEmail, emailVerifiedLevel: Int): ExpertProfile {
2459:         return ExpertProfile(
2460:             orcidId = authorEmail.orcidId ?: "",
2461:             email = DiscoveryIdentity.normalizedEmail(authorEmail.email),
2462:             givenNames = authorEmail.givenNames, familyNames = authorEmail.familyNames,
2463:             // I-1/I-2：机构与国籍只来自与该作者唯一绑定的结构机构；原文署名只是提取线索，
2464:             // 既不回填展示字段，也不按逗号末段猜国籍、更不是当前任职。
2465:             country = authorEmail.institutionCountry,
2466:             keyword = null, employment = null, institution = authorEmail.institutionName,
2467:             lastPublicationYear = paper.pubYear, emailSource = "PAPER_FULLTEXT",
2468:             emailVerifiedLevel = emailVerifiedLevel, dataSource = paper.source,
2469:             externalIds = buildExternalIds(paper, authorEmail),
2470:             institutionType = authorEmail.institutionType,
2471:             identityVerification = proofFor(authorEmail)
2472:         // 02（I-1/I-2）：证据在原始 AuthorEmail 尚在场时签发；toIndexMap 只负责写入，不重算。
2473:         ).withInstitutionEvidence(authorEmail.institutionSource)
2474:     }
2475: 
2476:     /**
2477:      * 02（I-1/I-2）：机构来源证据的唯一签发调用点。来源种类只认 01 的内部 `institutionSource`
2478:      * （JATS/OPENALEX）或 ORCID 路径的显式 `ORCID`；其它来源（SBIR/CORE/旧 `dataSource`）一律不签发。
2479:      * 不满足签发条件时**原样返回**（不写这个键，绝不写 false/UNVERIFIED）。
2480:      */
2481:     private fun ExpertProfile.withInstitutionEvidence(source: String?): ExpertProfile {
2482:         val evidence = source?.let { DiscoveryIdentity.institutionEvidence(this, it) } ?: return this
2483:         return copy(institutionEvidence = evidence)
2484:     }

2486:     private fun toIndexMap(profile: ExpertProfile, paper: PaperMetadata?, esDocId: String,
2487:                            filterResult: String, rejectReasons: List<String>): Map<String, Any?> {
2488:         val now = LocalDateTime.now().format(dateFormatter)
2489:         val doc = mutableMapOf<String, Any?>(
2490:             "orcidId" to esDocId, "email" to profile.email,
2491:             "givenNames" to profile.givenNames, "familyNames" to profile.familyNames,
2492:             "country" to profile.country, "keyword" to profile.keyword,
2493:             "employment" to profile.employment, "institution" to profile.institution,
2494:             "institutionType" to profile.institutionType,
2495:             "lastPublicationYear" to profile.lastPublicationYear,
2496:             "emailSource" to profile.emailSource, "emailVerifiedLevel" to profile.emailVerifiedLevel,
2497:             "dataSource" to profile.dataSource,
2498:             "identityVerification" to profile.identityVerification,
2499:             "externalIds" to profile.externalIds?.let { objectMapper.readValue(it, Map::class.java) },
2500:             "discoveredAt" to now, "updatedAt" to now,
2501:             "filterResult" to filterResult,
2502:             "expertClassification" to expertIndexWriterService.classificationNode(expertClassificationService.classify(profile)),
2503:             "filterRejectReason" to rejectReasons.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }?.joinToString("; "),
2504:             "tags" to listOf("discovered")
2505:         )
2506:         // 02（I-1）：无证据就不写这个键 —— 绝不写 null / false / UNVERIFIED。
2507:         profile.institutionEvidence?.let { doc["institutionEvidence"] = it }
2508:         return doc
2509:     }

2511:     /** 04（I-1/I-2）：RAW 落库成功后一次准入的处置结果。 */
2512:     private enum class AdmissionDisposition { PROMOTED, PROMOTION_FAILED, FILTERED }
2513: 
2514:     /**
2515:      * 04（I-1/I-2）：两个发现收录分支在 RAW 成功之后的**统一准入**。
2516:      *
2517:      * 由 01 的 [DiscoveryAdmissionPolicy]（经 [DiscoveryReviewService.recordAutomatic]）持久
2518:      * `AUTO_PASSED`/`NEEDS_REVIEW`/`LEGACY_APPROVED` 并决定晋升；人工结论优先。准入通过后经共享
2519:      * 投影方法按真实 `_id` create 候选（已存在/已进有效层不覆盖）。投影失败计入
2520:      * [SourceStats.promotionFailed]，绝不伪装成功。
2521:      */
2522:     private fun admitDiscoveryExpert(
2523:         docId: String,
2524:         profile: ExpertProfile,
2525:         eligibility: EligibilityResult,
2526:         rawSource: Map<String, Any?>,
2527:         sourceStats: SourceStats
2528:     ): AdmissionDisposition {
2529:         val review = discoveryReviewService
2530:         if (review == null) {
2531:             sourceStats.filtered++
2532:             sourceStats.filterReasons.merge(ADMISSION_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE, 1) { a, b -> a + b }
2533:             return AdmissionDisposition.FILTERED
2534:         }
2535:         val admission = review.recordAutomatic(docId, profile, eligibility)
2536:         if (!admission.admitted) {
2537:             sourceStats.filtered++
2538:             val reasons = admission.reasons.map { it.code }.ifEmpty { eligibility.rejectReasons }
2539:             for (reason in reasons) sourceStats.filterReasons.merge(reason, 1) { a, b -> a + b }
2540:             return AdmissionDisposition.FILTERED
2541:         }
2542:         val sync = review.projectApprovedCandidate(docId, rawSource, admission.decision)
2543:         return if (sync.ok) {
2544:             sourceStats.promoted++
2545:             AdmissionDisposition.PROMOTED
2546:         } else {
2547:             sourceStats.promotionFailed++
2548:             AdmissionDisposition.PROMOTION_FAILED
2549:         }
2550:     }
2551: 
2552:     fun getEnrichmentStats(): EnrichmentStats {
2553:         val cutoff = LocalDateTime.now().minusDays(30).format(dateFormatter)
2554:         val total = expertSearchService.countExperts(ExpertIndexLevel.CANDIDATE)
2555:         val pending = expertSearchService.countExperts(ExpertIndexLevel.CANDIDATE, buildEnrichmentFilters(cutoff))
2556:         val enrichedRecently = expertSearchService.countExperts(
2557:             ExpertIndexLevel.CANDIDATE,
2558:             listOf(mapOf("range" to mapOf("enrichedAt" to mapOf("gte" to cutoff))))
2559:         )
2560:         val institutionTypePending = expertSearchService.countExperts(
2561:             ExpertIndexLevel.CANDIDATE, buildInstitutionTypeBackfillFilters()
2562:         )
2563:         val lastPublicationYearPending = expertSearchService.countExperts(
2564:             ExpertIndexLevel.CANDIDATE, buildLastPublicationYearBackfillFilters()
2565:         )
2566:         return EnrichmentStats(
2567:             pending, enrichedRecently, total, institutionTypePending, lastPublicationYearPending,
2568:             autoEnrichment = lastEnrichmentBatch.get()
2569:         )
2570:     }
2571: 
2572:     private fun buildEnrichmentFilters(cutoff: String): List<Map<String, Any>> {
2573:         return listOf(
2574:             mapOf(
2575:                 "bool" to mapOf(
2576:                     "should" to listOf(
2577:                         mapOf("bool" to mapOf("must_not" to listOf(mapOf("exists" to mapOf("field" to "enrichedAt"))))),
2578:                         mapOf("range" to mapOf("enrichedAt" to mapOf("lt" to cutoff))),
2579:                         mapOf(
2580:                             "bool" to mapOf(
2581:                                 "must" to listOf(
2582:                                     mapOf("exists" to mapOf("field" to "enrichedAt")),
2583:                                     mapOf("exists" to mapOf("field" to "researchFields"))
2584:                                 ),
2585:                                 "must_not" to listOf(
2586:                                     mapOf("exists" to mapOf("field" to "disciplineCategory"))
2587:                                 )
2588:                             )
2589:                         )
2590:                     ),
2591:                     "minimum_should_match" to 1,
2592:                     "must_not" to listOf(
2593:                         mapOf("prefix" to mapOf("orcidId" to "EMAIL-"))
2594:                     )
2595:                 )
2596:             )
2597:         )
2598:     }

2600:     /** I5a2-1/I5a2-3：补采只针对 OpenAlex 认得（有 enrichedAt）且尚无 institutionType 的人。 */
2601:     private fun buildInstitutionTypeBackfillFilters(): List<Map<String, Any>> = listOf(
2602:         mapOf("bool" to mapOf(
2603:             "must" to listOf(mapOf("exists" to mapOf("field" to "enrichedAt"))),
2604:             "must_not" to listOf(
2605:                 mapOf("exists" to mapOf("field" to "institutionType")),
2606:                 mapOf("prefix" to mapOf("orcidId" to "EMAIL-"))
2607:             )
2608:         ))
2609:     )
2610: 
2611:     /** I1-5：只针对 OpenAlex 认得（有 enrichedAt）且尚无发表年份的人。 */
2612:     private fun buildLastPublicationYearBackfillFilters(): List<Map<String, Any>> = listOf(
2613:         mapOf("bool" to mapOf(
2614:             "must" to listOf(mapOf("exists" to mapOf("field" to "enrichedAt"))),
2615:             "must_not" to listOf(
2616:                 mapOf("exists" to mapOf("field" to "lastPublicationYear")),
2617:                 mapOf("prefix" to mapOf("orcidId" to "EMAIL-"))
2618:             )
2619:         ))
2620:     )
