2622:     private fun sleepInterruptible(taskType: String, ms: Long): Boolean {
2623:         if (ms <= 0) return progressStore.isCancelled(taskType)
2624:         var remaining = ms
2625:         while (remaining > 0) {
2626:             if (progressStore.isCancelled(taskType)) return true
2627:             val slice = minOf(remaining, 1000L)
2628:             Thread.sleep(slice)
2629:             remaining -= slice
2630:         }
2631:         return progressStore.isCancelled(taskType)
2632:     }
2633:     private fun waitForOpenAlexRetry(attempt: Int, deadline: Instant): String? {
2634:         var remaining = attempt * 1000L + ThreadLocalRandom.current().nextLong(201L)
2635:         while (remaining > 0L) {
2636:             if (progressStore.isCancelled("EXPERT_DISCOVERY")) return DiscoveryStopReason.CANCELLED
2637:             if (timeBudgetReached(deadline)) return DiscoveryStopReason.TIME_BUDGET
2638:             val slice = minOf(remaining, RETRY_WAIT_SLICE_MS)
2639:             try {
2640:                 // I-1：短等待与延迟恢复共用同一等待接缝（生产=Thread.sleep，测试=fake clock）。
2641:                 timeSource.sleep(slice)
2642:             } catch (_: InterruptedException) {
2643:                 Thread.currentThread().interrupt()
2644:                 return DiscoveryStopReason.CANCELLED
2645:             }
2646:             remaining -= slice
2647:         }
2648:         return when {
2649:             progressStore.isCancelled("EXPERT_DISCOVERY") -> DiscoveryStopReason.CANCELLED
2650:             timeBudgetReached(deadline) -> DiscoveryStopReason.TIME_BUDGET
2651:             else -> null
2652:         }
2653:     }
2654: 
2655:     private fun isRetryableOpenAlexFailure(error: Throwable): Boolean {
2656:         val causes = generateSequence(error) { it.cause }.toList()
2657:         if (causes.any {
2658:                 it is CertificateException || it is SSLPeerUnverifiedException || it is SSLProtocolException ||
2659:                     it is com.fasterxml.jackson.core.JsonProcessingException ||
2660:                     it is org.springframework.http.converter.HttpMessageNotReadableException
2661:             }) return false
2662:         val handshake = causes.filterIsInstance<SSLHandshakeException>().firstOrNull()
2663:         if (handshake != null) {
2664:             return handshake.message?.lowercase(Locale.ROOT)?.contains("remote host terminated the handshake") == true
2665:         }
2666:         return causes.any { cause ->
2667:             when (cause) {
2668:                 is SocketTimeoutException, is ConnectException, is EOFException -> true
2669:                 is SocketException -> cause.message?.lowercase(Locale.ROOT)?.let {
2670:                     it.contains("reset") || it.contains("aborted") || it.contains("broken pipe")
2671:                 } == true
2672:                 is IOException -> cause.javaClass.simpleName == "ConnectTimeoutException" ||
2673:                     cause.message?.lowercase(Locale.ROOT)?.let {
2674:                         it.contains("connection reset") || it.contains("unexpected end of file") ||
2675:                             it.contains("connection closed")
2676:                     } == true
2677:                 else -> false
2678:             }
2679:         }
2680:     }
2681: 
2682:     private fun computeEnrichmentBackoffMs(consecutiveRateLimits: Int, retryAfterMs: Long?): Long {
2683:         val exponential = 2000L * (1L shl (consecutiveRateLimits - 1).coerceAtMost(20))
2684:         return (retryAfterMs ?: exponential).coerceAtMost(openAlexProperties.enrichmentMaxBackoffMs)
2685:     }
2686: 
2687:     fun enrichExistingExperts(scope: EnrichmentScope = EnrichmentScope.DEFAULT): EnrichmentResult {
2688:         // I-3/I-4（08）：新增的人工待补重试入口；旧三种 scope 的过滤条件与语义完全不变。
2689:         if (scope == EnrichmentScope.DISCOVERY_PENDING) return enrichDiscoveryPendingJobs()
2690: 
2691:         val taskType = EXPERT_ENRICHMENT_TASK_TYPE
2692:         val execId = progressStore.getCurrentExecutionId(taskType)
2693:         val cutoff = LocalDateTime.now().minusDays(30).format(dateFormatter)
2694:         val filters = when (scope) {
2695:             EnrichmentScope.DEFAULT -> buildEnrichmentFilters(cutoff)
2696:             EnrichmentScope.INSTITUTION_TYPE_BACKFILL -> buildInstitutionTypeBackfillFilters()
2697:             EnrichmentScope.LAST_PUBLICATION_YEAR_BACKFILL -> buildLastPublicationYearBackfillFilters()
2698:             // DISCOVERY_PENDING 不走 CANDIDATE 过滤扫描（已在上方提前返回），这里只是穷尽性占位。
2699:             EnrichmentScope.DISCOVERY_PENDING -> emptyList()
2700:         }
2701:         val pendingCount = expertSearchService.countExperts(ExpertIndexLevel.CANDIDATE, filters)
2702:         val rateLimitMode = openAlexProperties.enrichmentRateLimitMode.uppercase(Locale.ROOT)
2703:         progressStore.update(taskType, TaskProgress(
2704:             taskType = taskType, status = "RUNNING",
2705:             batchNumber = 0, processedCount = 0, totalCount = pendingCount, message = "初始化中..."
2706:         ), execId)
2707: 
2708:         val openAlex = openAlexProvider.getIfAvailable()
2709:         if (openAlex == null) {
2710:             progressStore.update(taskType, TaskProgress(
2711:                 taskType = taskType, status = "COMPLETED",
2712:                 batchNumber = -1, processedCount = 0, totalCount = pendingCount,
2713:                 message = "OpenAlex 未启用，跳过补充"
2714:             ), execId)
2715:             return EnrichmentResult(0, 0)
2716:         }
2717: 
2718:         var enriched = 0
2719:         var failed = 0
2720:         var scanned = 0
2721:         var rateLimitWaits = 0
2722:         val failureReasons = mutableMapOf<String, Int>()
2723:         var consecutiveRateLimits = 0
2724:         var circuitBreakerTripped = false
2725:         // I-5：额度延期是与限流/失败都不同的停止原因，单独记录、单独终态。
2726:         var budgetDeferred = false
2727: 
2728:         try {
2729:             var batchNumber = 0
2730:             expertSearchService.searchAfterExpertsFiltered(ExpertIndexLevel.CANDIDATE, filters) { batch ->
2731:                 if (circuitBreakerTripped || budgetDeferred || progressStore.isCancelled(taskType)) {
2732:                     log.info("Enrichment task cancelled, budget deferred or circuit breaker tripped at batch {}", batchNumber)
2733:                     return@searchAfterExpertsFiltered false
2734:                 }
2735:                 batchNumber++
2736:                 val enrichedBefore = enriched
2737:                 val failedBefore = failed
2738:                 val failureReasonsBefore = HashMap(failureReasons)
2739:                 scanned += batch.size
2740: 
2741:                 for (chunk in batch.chunked(enrichmentBatchSize())) {
2742:                     if (circuitBreakerTripped || budgetDeferred || progressStore.isCancelled(taskType)) break
2743: 
2744:                     // 只重试真正可重试（限流）的身份：已成功写过的层不会被再次触碰。
2745:                     var retryChunk = chunk
2746: 
2747:                     while (retryChunk.isNotEmpty()) {
2748:                         if (circuitBreakerTripped || budgetDeferred || progressStore.isCancelled(taskType)) break
2749: 
2750:                         if (openAlexProperties.enrichmentDelayMs > 0) {
2751:                             if (sleepInterruptible(taskType, openAlexProperties.enrichmentDelayMs)) break
2752:                         }
2753: 
2754:                         val outcomes = enrichProfiles(retryChunk, RequestKind.HISTORY_ENRICHMENT)
2755:                         val retryable = mutableListOf<ExpertProfile>()
2756:                         var firstRetryAfterMs: Long? = null
2757: 
2758:                         for (profile in retryChunk) {
2759:                             val outcome = outcomes[enrichmentDocId(profile)] ?: ProfileEnrichmentOutcome.NotFound
2760:                             val layers = when (outcome) {
2761:                                 is ProfileEnrichmentOutcome.Success -> outcome.layers
2762:                                 is ProfileEnrichmentOutcome.Partial -> outcome.layers
2763:                                 else -> null
2764:                             }
2765:                             if (layers != null) {
2766:                                 // I-2：只要有现存层写入失败就不能算成功；RAW-only 成功必须算成功。
2767:                                 when {
2768:                                     layers.hasFailedLayer() -> {
2769:                                         failed++
2770:                                         failureReasons.merge("ES_UPDATE_FAILED", 1) { a, b -> a + b }
2771:                                     }
2772:                                     layers.updatedAnyLayer() -> enriched++
2773:                                     else -> {
2774:                                         failed++
2775:                                         failureReasons.merge("NO_TARGET_LAYER", 1) { a, b -> a + b }
2776:                                     }
2777:                                 }
2778:                                 continue
2779:                             }
2780:                             when (outcome) {
2781:                                 is ProfileEnrichmentOutcome.NotFound -> {
2782:                                     failed++
2783:                                     failureReasons.merge("ORCID_NOT_IN_OPENALEX", 1) { a, b -> a + b }
2784:                                 }
2785:                                 is ProfileEnrichmentOutcome.NoId -> {
2786:                                     failed++
2787:                                     failureReasons.merge("NO_TRUSTED_IDENTITY", 1) { a, b -> a + b }
2788:                                 }
2789:                                 is ProfileEnrichmentOutcome.AmbiguousIdentity -> {
2790:                                     // I-3：身份歧义是「本次没能唯一确定是谁」，不计成功、不改动任何学术字段。
2791:                                     failed++
2792:                                     failureReasons.merge("AUTHOR_IDENTITY_AMBIGUOUS", 1) { a, b -> a + b }
2793:                                 }
2794:                                 is ProfileEnrichmentOutcome.RetryableError -> {
2795:                                     if (outcome.rateLimited) {
2796:                                         retryable += profile
2797:                                         if (firstRetryAfterMs == null) firstRetryAfterMs = outcome.retryAfterMs
2798:                                     } else {
2799:                                         failed++
2800:                                         failureReasons.merge("OPENALEX_API_ERROR", 1) { a, b -> a + b }
2801:                                     }
2802:                                 }
2803:                                 is ProfileEnrichmentOutcome.Deferred -> budgetDeferred = true
2804:                                 // Success/Partial 已在上面的 layers 分支处理。
2805:                                 else -> Unit
2806:                             }
2807:                         }
2808: 
2809:                         // 额度延期：剩余身份一律标 Deferred，不当作失败、也不再打接口。
2810:                         if (budgetDeferred) break
2811:                         if (retryable.isEmpty()) {
2812:                             consecutiveRateLimits = 0
2813:                             break
2814:                         }
2815: 
2816:                         consecutiveRateLimits++
2817:                         rateLimitWaits++
2818:                         if (rateLimitMode == "ABORT" && consecutiveRateLimits >= 5) {
2819:                             failureReasons["CIRCUIT_BREAKER"] = 1
2820:                             circuitBreakerTripped = true
2821:                             log.warn("Enrichment: 连续 {} 次限流，熔断退出 (ABORT 模式)", consecutiveRateLimits)
2822:                             break
2823:                         }
2824: 
2825:                         val backoffMs = computeEnrichmentBackoffMs(consecutiveRateLimits, firstRetryAfterMs)
2826:                         val processed = enriched + failed
2827:                         log.info("Enrichment: 限流退避 {}ms (第 {} 次)", backoffMs, consecutiveRateLimits)
2828:                         progressStore.update(taskType, TaskProgress(
2829:                             taskType = taskType, status = "RUNNING",
2830:                             batchNumber = batchNumber,
2831:                             processedCount = processed.toLong(),
2832:                             totalCount = pendingCount,
2833:                             message = "限流退避中 ${backoffMs / 1000}s（第 $rateLimitWaits 次），已处理 $processed/$pendingCount，成功 $enriched，失败 $failed",
2834:                             details = mapOf(
2835:                                 "enriched" to enriched,
2836:                                 "failed" to failed,
2837:                                 "scanned" to scanned,
2838:                                 "failureReasons" to HashMap(failureReasons),
2839:                                 "rateLimitWaits" to rateLimitWaits,
2840:                                 "currentBackoffMs" to backoffMs,
2841:                                 "mode" to rateLimitMode
2842:                             )
2843:                         ), execId)
2844: 
2845:                         if (sleepInterruptible(taskType, backoffMs)) break
2846:                         retryChunk = retryable
2847:                     }
2848:                 }
2849: 
2850:                 val processed = enriched + failed
2851:                 val batchPassed = enriched - enrichedBefore
2852:                 val batchRejected = failed - failedBefore
2853:                 progressStore.update(taskType, TaskProgress(
2854:                     taskType = taskType, status = "RUNNING",
2855:                     batchNumber = batchNumber,
2856:                     processedCount = processed.toLong(),
2857:                     totalCount = pendingCount,
2858:                     message = "批次 $batchNumber: 已处理 $processed/$pendingCount, 成功 $enriched, 失败 $failed",
2859:                     details = mapOf(
2860:                         "enriched" to enriched,
2861:                         "failed" to failed,
2862:                         "scanned" to scanned,
2863:                         "failureReasons" to HashMap(failureReasons),
2864:                         "rateLimitWaits" to rateLimitWaits,
2865:                         "mode" to rateLimitMode
2866:                     ),
2867:                     batchProcessed = batch.size,
2868:                     batchPassed = batchPassed.coerceAtLeast(0),
2869:                     batchRejected = batchRejected.coerceAtLeast(0),
2870:                     batchRejectReasons = computeBatchRejectReasons(failureReasonsBefore, failureReasons)
2871:                 ), execId)
2872:                 !progressStore.isCancelled(taskType) && !circuitBreakerTripped && !budgetDeferred
2873:             }
2874: 
2875:             if (progressStore.isCancelled(taskType)) {
2876:                 val processed = enriched + failed
2877:                 log.info("Enrichment cancelled: enriched={}, failed={}, scanned={}", enriched, failed, scanned)
2878:                 progressStore.update(taskType, TaskProgress(
2879:                     taskType = taskType, status = "CANCELLED",
2880:                     batchNumber = -1, processedCount = processed.toLong(), totalCount = pendingCount,
2881:                     message = "已暂停: 成功 $enriched, 失败 $failed",
2882:                     details = mapOf(
2883:                         "enriched" to enriched,
2884:                         "failed" to failed,
2885:                         "scanned" to scanned,
2886:                         "failureReasons" to HashMap(failureReasons),
2887:                         "rateLimitWaits" to rateLimitWaits,
2888:                         "mode" to rateLimitMode
2889:                     )
2890:                 ), execId)
2891:                 return EnrichmentResult(enriched, failed, HashMap(failureReasons), wasCancelled = true)
2892:             }
2893: 
2894:             if (budgetDeferred) {
2895:                 val processed = enriched + failed
2896:                 failureReasons["BUDGET_DEFERRED"] = 1
2897:                 log.info(
2898:                     "Enrichment stopped by OpenAlex daily budget: enriched={}, failed={}, scanned={}",
2899:                     enriched, failed, scanned
2900:                 )
2901:                 progressStore.update(taskType, TaskProgress(
2902:                     taskType = taskType, status = "PARTIAL_SUCCESS",
2903:                     batchNumber = -1, processedCount = processed.toLong(), totalCount = pendingCount,
2904:                     message = "OpenAlex 日额度延期，本轮暂停（剩余专家可续跑）: 成功 $enriched, 失败 $failed",
2905:                     details = mapOf(
2906:                         "enriched" to enriched,
2907:                         "failed" to failed,
2908:                         "scanned" to scanned,
2909:                         "failureReasons" to HashMap(failureReasons),
2910:                         "rateLimitWaits" to rateLimitWaits,
2911:                         "mode" to rateLimitMode,
2912:                         "budgetDeferred" to true
2913:                     )
2914:                 ), execId)
2915:                 return EnrichmentResult(enriched, failed, HashMap(failureReasons), budgetDeferred = true)
2916:             }
2917: 
2918:             if (circuitBreakerTripped) {
2919:                 val processed = enriched + failed
2920:                 log.warn(
2921:                     "Enrichment circuit breaker tripped: enriched={}, failed={}, scanned={}, failureReasons={}",
2922:                     enriched, failed, scanned, failureReasons
2923:                 )
2924:                 progressStore.update(taskType, TaskProgress(
2925:                     taskType = taskType, status = "FAILED",
2926:                     batchNumber = -1, processedCount = processed.toLong(), totalCount = pendingCount,
2927:                     message = "连续限流熔断退出 (ABORT 模式): 成功 $enriched, 失败 $failed",
2928:                     details = mapOf(
2929:                         "enriched" to enriched,
2930:                         "failed" to failed,
2931:                         "scanned" to scanned,
2932:                         "failureReasons" to HashMap(failureReasons),
2933:                         "rateLimitWaits" to rateLimitWaits,
2934:                         "mode" to rateLimitMode
2935:                     )
2936:                 ), execId)
2937:                 return EnrichmentResult(
2938:                     enriched, failed, HashMap(failureReasons), circuitBreakerTripped = true
2939:                 )
2940:             }
2941: 
2942:             val processed = enriched + failed
2943:             progressStore.update(taskType, TaskProgress(
2944:                 taskType = taskType, status = "COMPLETED",
2945:                 batchNumber = -1, processedCount = processed.toLong(), totalCount = pendingCount,
2946:                 message = "完成: 成功 $enriched, 失败 $failed",
2947:                 details = mapOf(
2948:                     "enriched" to enriched,
2949:                     "failed" to failed,
2950:                     "scanned" to scanned,
2951:                     "failureReasons" to HashMap(failureReasons),
2952:                     "rateLimitWaits" to rateLimitWaits,
2953:                     "mode" to rateLimitMode
2954:                 )
2955:             ), execId)
2956:         } catch (e: Exception) {
2957:             progressStore.update(taskType, TaskProgress(
2958:                 taskType = taskType, status = "FAILED",
2959:                 batchNumber = -1, processedCount = (enriched + failed).toLong(), totalCount = pendingCount,
2960:                 message = "失败: ${e.message}",
2961:                 details = mapOf(
2962:                     "enriched" to enriched,
2963:                     "failed" to failed,
2964:                     "failureReasons" to HashMap(failureReasons),
2965:                     "rateLimitWaits" to rateLimitWaits,
2966:                     "mode" to rateLimitMode
2967:                 )
2968:             ), execId)
2969:             throw e
2970:         }
2971:         log.info("Enrichment complete: enriched={}, failed={}, scanned={}, failureReasons={}", enriched, failed, scanned, failureReasons)
2972:         return EnrichmentResult(enriched, failed, HashMap(failureReasons))
2973:     }
2974: 
2975:     // ------------------------------------------------------------------
2976:     // I-2/I-3/I-4（08）：补全队列的批次核心（worker 与人工 DISCOVERY_PENDING 共用同一实现）
2977:     // ------------------------------------------------------------------
2978: 
2979:     /**
2980:      * I-3/I-4（08）：人工 DISCOVERY_PENDING —— 显式重试自动补全队列（PENDING、到点的 RETRY_WAIT、
2981:      * 租约已过期的 RUNNING）。复用与 worker 相同的「领取 + 批次核心」（06 核心补全 + 逐人终态 +
2982:      * RAW-only 定向复评），请求口径按人工历史回填取 [RequestKind.HISTORY_ENRICHMENT]；
2983:      * 不触碰旧三种 scope 的过滤条件，也不会伪造成功/失败计数。
2984:      */
2985:     private fun enrichDiscoveryPendingJobs(): EnrichmentResult {
2986:         val taskType = EXPERT_ENRICHMENT_TASK_TYPE
2987:         val jobs = claimDueEnrichmentJobs(discoveryProperties.autoEnrichmentBatchSize)
2988:         if (jobs.isEmpty()) {
2989:             val reason = if (openAlexProvider.getIfAvailable() == null) "OpenAlex 未启用" else "没有到期任务"
2990:             progressStore.update(taskType, TaskProgress(
2991:                 taskType = taskType, status = "COMPLETED", batchNumber = -1,
2992:                 processedCount = 0, totalCount = 0, message = "待补重试：$reason，本轮未领取任何任务"
2993:             ), progressStore.getCurrentExecutionId(taskType))
2994:             return EnrichmentResult(0, 0)
2995:         }
2996:         val batch = processClaimedEnrichmentJobBatch(jobs, RequestKind.HISTORY_ENRICHMENT, taskType)
2997:         val failureReasons = LinkedHashMap<String, Int>()
2998:         if (batch.unmatched > 0) failureReasons["ENRICHMENT_UNMATCHED"] = batch.unmatched
2999:         if (batch.pending > 0) failureReasons["ENRICHMENT_RETRY_WAIT"] = batch.pending
3000:         if (batch.failed > 0) failureReasons["ENRICHMENT_FAILED"] = batch.failed
3001:         return EnrichmentResult(
3002:             enriched = batch.succeeded,
3003:             failed = batch.failed + batch.unmatched,
3004:             failureReasons = failureReasons,
3005:             wasCancelled = batch.cancelled,
3006:             budgetDeferred = batch.budgetDeferred
3007:         )
3008:     }
3009: 
3010:     /**
3011:      * R-3（V-3）：只读的「有没有到期任务」探针 —— 不领取、不改任何列、不写进度日志。
3012:      *
3013:      * worker 每 30 秒检查一次；如果空转也先拿任务锁（`tryStartWithToken` 会落一条
3014:      * `execution_id` 为负的孤儿进度行），清理内存后就会留下一次「中断/失败」的假生命周期证据。
3015:      * 用本探针在拿锁前短路，空转不再产生任何任务记录/进度行。
3016:      *
3017:      * 到期判定谓词与 [claimDueEnrichmentJobs] 完全一致（同一份 `findDueCandidates`），只读一次；
3018:      * 真正的领取仍在拿锁后按同一谓词重新求值，所以这里只决定「要不要开始」，
3019:      * 不影响互斥、尾批、租约恢复与崩溃后重领。
3020:      */
3021:     fun hasDueEnrichmentJobs(): Boolean {
3022:         if (openAlexProvider.getIfAvailable() == null) return false
3023:         return try {
3024:             enrichmentJobRepository.findDueCandidates(1, LocalDateTime.now()).isNotEmpty()
3025:         } catch (e: Exception) {
3026:             // 探针失败按「有活」处理：宁可多拿一次锁空跑一批，也不能让到期任务被探针饿死。
3027:             log.warn("补全到期探针失败，按存在到期任务处理: {}", e.message)
3028:             true
3029:         }
3030:     }
3031: 
3032:     /**
3033:      * I-2（08）：领取至多 [limit] 条**到期**任务（硬上界 [MAX_ENRICHMENT_IDENTITIES_PER_BATCH]：
3034:      * 100 只是一批，不是每日总量），不处理、不写任务终态。不足上限也照常返回，尾批不等待。
3035:      *
3036:      * OpenAlex 未启用时一条都不领（保持任务原状态、不消耗重试预算），也绝不写进度日志 ——
3037:      * worker 每 30 秒检查一次，空闲/未启用的一次检查不应在任务记录与进度日志里留下噪音。
3038:      */
3039:     fun claimDueEnrichmentJobs(limit: Int): List<ExpertAcademicEnrichmentJob> {
3040:         if (openAlexProvider.getIfAvailable() == null) {
3041:             log.info("OpenAlex 未启用，本轮不领取补全任务")
3042:             return emptyList()
3043:         }
3044:         // I-2：到期判定与租约写入用同一套朴素本地时钟（与 07 的 next_attempt_at 口径一致）。
3045:         return enrichmentJobService.claimDue(
3046:             limit.coerceIn(1, MAX_ENRICHMENT_IDENTITIES_PER_BATCH),
3047:             LocalDateTime.now()
3048:         )
3049:     }
3050: 
3051:     /**
3052:      * I-2/I-3/I-4（08）：处理一批已领取的补全任务 —— 复用 06 的 [enrichProfiles] 按真实 `_id` 补全、
3053:      * 逐人经 07 的 CAS 写终态，并对**成功且 RAW-only** 的专家做定向复评；批次逐源计数写进既有进度 details。
3054:      *
3055:      * - 跨进程互斥靠 07 的租约（完成必须匹配 token，旧 token 不写任何列）；进程内互斥由调用方
3056:      *   （worker / 人工入口）用 [TaskProgressStore.tryStartWithToken] 的同一把锁保证。
3057:      * - RAW 文档整体读不到（索引/Mapping/ES 异常）时不写任何任务终态：任务保持 `RUNNING`，
3058:      *   租约到期后可重领，基础设施故障不烧掉任务的重试预算。
3059:      * - 逐个任务完成前检查取消，取消后剩余任务保持租约未完成（保存未完成状态）。
3060:      * - [taskType] 只决定进度日志归属；`task_execution` 记录由调用方按自己的 triggerType 写入。
3061:      */
3062:     fun processClaimedEnrichmentJobBatch(
3063:         jobs: List<ExpertAcademicEnrichmentJob>,
3064:         requestKind: RequestKind,
3065:         taskType: String = EXPERT_ENRICHMENT_TASK_TYPE
3066:     ): AutoEnrichmentBatchResult {
3067:         val claimed = jobs.filter { it.id != null && it.leaseToken != null }
3068:         if (claimed.isEmpty()) return AutoEnrichmentBatchResult()
3069: 
3070:         val profiles = expertSearchService.findByDocumentIds(ExpertIndexLevel.RAW, claimed.map { it.expertDocId })
3071:         if (profiles.isEmpty()) {
3072:             throw IllegalStateException(
3073:                 "RAW 文档读取失败：${claimed.size} 条补全任务无法定位专家文档，本轮不写任务终态"
3074:             )
3075:         }
3076:         val profilesByDocId = profiles.associateBy { enrichmentDocId(it) }
3077:         val work = claimed.map { job -> job to profilesByDocId[job.expertDocId] }
3078:         val outcomes = enrichProfiles(work.mapNotNull { (_, profile) -> profile }, requestKind)
3079: 
3080:         val counters = BatchCounters()
3081:         val bySource = LinkedHashMap<String, SourceBucket>()
3082:         var deferredUntil: String? = null
3083:         var cancelled = false
3084: 
3085:         for ((job, profile) in work) {
3086:             if (progressStore.isCancelled(taskType)) {
3087:                 log.info("补全批次已取消，剩余 {} 条任务保持租约未完成", work.size - counters.enqueued)
3088:                 cancelled = true
3089:                 break
3090:             }
3091:             val bucket = bySource.getOrPut(job.source) { SourceBucket() }
3092:             bucket.enqueued++
3093:             counters.enqueued++
3094:             // 文档读不到（ES 查无此 `_id`）：算可重试失败，绝不伪造成功；故障尝试用尽后由 07 记为 FAILED。
3095:             val initial = profile?.let { outcomes[enrichmentDocId(it)] } ?: ProfileEnrichmentOutcome.RetryableError()
3096:             val outcome = if (initial is ProfileEnrichmentOutcome.Success && isRawOnly(initial.layers) &&
3097:                 profile != null && !DiscoveryIdentity.isDiscovery(profile)) {
3098:                 counters.revalidated++
3099:                 if (revalidateRawOnlySuccess(job.expertDocId)) counters.promoted++
3100:                 initial
3101:             } else initial
3102:             if (initial is ProfileEnrichmentOutcome.Success && profile != null &&
3103:                 DiscoveryIdentity.isDiscovery(profile)) {
3104:                 counters.revalidated++
3105:                 if (initial.promoted && outcome is ProfileEnrichmentOutcome.Success) counters.promoted++
3106:             }
3107:             if (outcome is ProfileEnrichmentOutcome.Deferred && deferredUntil == null) {
3108:                 deferredUntil = outcome.resetAt.toString()
3109:             }
3110:             if (!enrichmentJobService.complete(job.id!!, job.leaseToken!!, outcome)) {
3111:                 // 租约已被其他 worker 拿走或行不再是 RUNNING：不写任何列，也不计入结果桶。
3112:                 log.warn("补全任务 {} 未写终态：租约已失效或行已非 RUNNING", job.expertDocId)
3113:                 counters.claimLost++
3114:                 continue
3115:             }
3116:             when (classifyBatchOutcome(outcome, job.attempts)) {
3117:                 BatchOutcomeBucket.SUCCEEDED -> { counters.succeeded++; bucket.succeeded++ }
3118:                 BatchOutcomeBucket.PENDING -> { counters.pending++; bucket.pending++ }
3119:                 BatchOutcomeBucket.UNMATCHED -> { counters.unmatched++; bucket.unmatched++ }
3120:                 BatchOutcomeBucket.FAILED -> { counters.failed++; bucket.failed++ }
3121:             }
3122:         }
3123: 
3124:         val result = AutoEnrichmentBatchResult(
3125:             claimed = counters.enqueued,
3126:             succeeded = counters.succeeded,
3127:             pending = counters.pending,
3128:             unmatched = counters.unmatched,
3129:             failed = counters.failed,
3130:             claimLost = counters.claimLost,
3131:             revalidated = counters.revalidated,
3132:             promoted = counters.promoted,
3133:             bySource = bySource.mapValues { (_, bucket) -> bucket.toCounts() },
3134:             budgetDeferred = deferredUntil != null,
3135:             deferredUntil = deferredUntil,
3136:             cancelled = cancelled
3137:         )
3138:         recordEnrichmentBatch(taskType, result)
3139:         return result
3140:     }
3141: 
3142:     /**
3143:      * Legacy non-discovery RAW-only promotion after enrichment. Discovery records instead use
3144:      * targeted revalidation inside enrichProfiles, before their job may become SUCCEEDED.
3145:      */
3146:     private fun isRawOnly(layers: LayerUpdateResult): Boolean =
3147:         layers.updatedAnyLayer() &&
3148:             layers.candidate == LayerUpdateStatus.ABSENT &&
3149:             layers.application == LayerUpdateStatus.ABSENT
3150: 
3151:     /**
3152:      * Legacy RAW-only revalidation retains its historical best-effort semantics.
3153:      * Discovery revalidation errors are retryable outcomes and cannot report success.
3154:      */
3155:     private fun revalidateRawOnlySuccess(docId: String): Boolean = try {
3156:         revalidationService.revalidateEnrichedRaw(docId) == PromotionOutcome.Promoted
3157:     } catch (e: Exception) {
3158:         log.warn("补全后定向复评失败 {}: {}", docId, e.message)
3159:         false
3160:     }
3161: 
3162:     /** I-4（08）：把一批的逐源计数写进既有进度 details（不加列、不改前端），并留存供 `/enrich/stats` 读取。 */
3163:     private fun recordEnrichmentBatch(taskType: String, result: AutoEnrichmentBatchResult) {
3164:         lastEnrichmentBatch.set(result)
3165:         progressStore.update(taskType, TaskProgress(
3166:             taskType = taskType,
3167:             status = if (result.cancelled) "CANCELLED" else "COMPLETED",
3168:             batchNumber = -1,
3169:             processedCount = (result.succeeded + result.pending + result.unmatched + result.failed).toLong(),
3170:             totalCount = result.claimed.toLong(),
3171:             message = buildEnrichmentBatchMessage(result),
3172:             details = result.toDetails()
3173:         ), progressStore.getCurrentExecutionId(taskType))
3174:     }
3175: 
3176:     private fun buildEnrichmentBatchMessage(result: AutoEnrichmentBatchResult): String {
3177:         val deferred = result.deferredUntil?.let { "，额度延期至 $it" } ?: ""
3178:         return "补全批次: 领取 ${result.claimed}, 成功 ${result.succeeded}, 待补 ${result.pending}, " +
3179:             "未匹配 ${result.unmatched}, 失败 ${result.failed}$deferred"
3180:     }
3181: 
3182:     /**
3183:      * I-4（08）：把 06 的逐人结果投射成批次结果桶，口径与 07 的状态机一致：
3184:      * `Success → SUCCEEDED`；`NotFound`/`NoId → UNMATCHED`；`Partial` 与网络/5xx 故障各消耗一次故障尝试，
3185:      * 达到 [ExpertAcademicEnrichmentJobService.MAX_FAILURE_ATTEMPTS] 即为 `FAILED`，否则 `PENDING`（待重试）；
3186:      * 额度延期与限流不消耗故障尝试。这里只做计数投射，状态本身由 07 写入。
3187:      */
3188:     private fun classifyBatchOutcome(outcome: ProfileEnrichmentOutcome, currentAttempts: Int): BatchOutcomeBucket =
3189:         when (outcome) {
3190:             is ProfileEnrichmentOutcome.Success -> BatchOutcomeBucket.SUCCEEDED
3191:             is ProfileEnrichmentOutcome.Deferred -> BatchOutcomeBucket.PENDING
3192:             ProfileEnrichmentOutcome.NotFound, ProfileEnrichmentOutcome.NoId -> BatchOutcomeBucket.UNMATCHED
3193:             // I-3：身份歧义与「查无此人」同属未匹配 —— 不写事实、不计成功、也不消耗故障尝试。
3194:             ProfileEnrichmentOutcome.AmbiguousIdentity -> BatchOutcomeBucket.UNMATCHED
3195:             is ProfileEnrichmentOutcome.Partial ->
3196:                 if (exhaustsFailureBudget(currentAttempts)) BatchOutcomeBucket.FAILED else BatchOutcomeBucket.PENDING
3197:             is ProfileEnrichmentOutcome.RetryableError -> when {
3198:                 outcome.rateLimited -> BatchOutcomeBucket.PENDING
3199:                 exhaustsFailureBudget(currentAttempts) -> BatchOutcomeBucket.FAILED
3200:                 else -> BatchOutcomeBucket.PENDING
3201:             }
3202:         }
3203: 
3204:     /** 07 的故障尝试上限对齐：本次完成会把故障尝试 +1，达到上限即 `FAILED`。 */
3205:     private fun exhaustsFailureBudget(currentAttempts: Int): Boolean =
3206:         currentAttempts + 1 >= ExpertAcademicEnrichmentJobService.MAX_FAILURE_ATTEMPTS
3207: 
3208:     /** 批次内累加器（只在本方法的作用域内使用，不进任何持久化契约）。 */
3209:     private class BatchCounters {
3210:         var enqueued = 0
3211:         var succeeded = 0
3212:         var pending = 0
3213:         var unmatched = 0
3214:         var failed = 0
3215:         var claimLost = 0
3216:         var revalidated = 0
3217:         var promoted = 0
3218:     }
3219: 
3220:     private class SourceBucket {
3221:         var enqueued = 0
3222:         var succeeded = 0
3223:         var pending = 0
3224:         var unmatched = 0
3225:         var failed = 0
3226: 
3227:         fun toCounts() = AutoEnrichmentSourceCounts(enqueued, succeeded, pending, unmatched, failed)
3228:     }
3229: 
3230:     private enum class BatchOutcomeBucket { SUCCEEDED, PENDING, UNMATCHED, FAILED }
3231: 
3232:     /**
3233:      * I-2：学术字段的唯一写入点。按真实 `_id` 对每个已存在层做局部 `_update`：404 跳过、非 404 失败可按层重试；
3234:      * 只写非 null 事实（null 绝不覆盖已有值）并重算分类；不触碰姓名/邮箱/署名机构/运营状态，
3235:      * 也绝不创建缺失层（尤其不创建 APPLICATION）。返回逐层结果而不是单一布尔。
3236:      */
3237:     private fun updateExpertAcademicFields(profile: ExpertProfile, enrichment: AuthorEnrichment): LayerUpdateResult {
3238:         val docId = enrichmentDocId(profile)
3239:         val now = LocalDateTime.now().format(dateFormatter)
3240:         val doc = mutableMapOf<String, Any?>(
3241:             // V-1/I-3：补全只写学术事实与 enrichedAt，不推进根级 `updatedAt`（那是发现/运营写入的语义）。
3242:             "enrichedAt" to now,
3243:             "enrichmentSource" to "OPENALEX"
3244:         )
3245:         // I-2：null 事实不写入（否则 _update 会用 null 擦掉已有指标）。
3246:         enrichment.hIndex?.let { doc["hIndex"] = it }
3247:         enrichment.citationCount?.let { doc["citationCount"] = it }
3248:         enrichment.worksCount?.let { doc["worksCount"] = it }
3249:         enrichment.topics?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }?.let { doc["researchFields"] = it.joinToString(", ") }
3250:         enrichment.researchFieldIds?.let { doc["researchFieldIds"] = it }
3251:         enrichment.recentWorkTitles?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }?.let { doc["recentWorkTitles"] = it }
3252:         enrichment.patentTitles?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }?.let { doc["patentTitles"] = it }
3253:         enrichment.disciplineCategory?.let { doc["disciplineCategory"] = it }
3254:         // I-3：`enrichment.institutionType` 取自作者 `last_known_institutions[0]`，与文档里显示的
3255:         // 机构（论文 authorship / ORCID 的 institution-name）**不是同一个来源机构对象** ——
3256:         // 绝不跨源覆盖展示机构的类型，因此这个键根本不出现在补全写入体里。
3257:         // I1-3: null 时不写入该键，避免覆盖发现时的真实值；I1-4: 非 null 时无条件覆盖。
3258:         enrichment.lastPublicationYear?.let { doc["lastPublicationYear"] = it }
3259:         val enrichedProfile = profile.copy(
3260:             hIndex = enrichment.hIndex ?: profile.hIndex,
3261:             citationCount = enrichment.citationCount ?: profile.citationCount,
3262:             worksCount = enrichment.worksCount ?: profile.worksCount,
3263:             researchFields = enrichment.topics?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }?.joinToString(", ")
3264:                 ?: profile.researchFields,
3265:             researchFieldIds = enrichment.researchFieldIds ?: profile.researchFieldIds,
3266:             recentWorkTitles = enrichment.recentWorkTitles?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }
3267:                 ?: profile.recentWorkTitles,
3268:             patentTitles = enrichment.patentTitles?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() } ?: profile.patentTitles,
3269:             disciplineCategory = enrichment.disciplineCategory ?: profile.disciplineCategory,
3270:             lastPublicationYear = enrichment.lastPublicationYear ?: profile.lastPublicationYear
3271:         )
3272:         doc["expertClassification"] = expertClassificationService.classify(enrichedProfile)
3273:         val updateBody: Map<String, Any> = if (DiscoveryIdentity.isDiscovery(profile)) {
3274:             mapOf("script" to mapOf("lang" to "painless", "source" to
3275:                 "if (ctx._source.identityVerification != params.identity || ctx._source.email != params.email || ctx._source.givenNames != params.given || ctx._source.familyNames != params.family || ctx._source.externalIds != params.externalIds) { ctx.op = 'none'; } else { for (entry in params.doc.entrySet()) { ctx._source[entry.getKey()] = entry.getValue(); } }",
3276:                 "params" to mapOf("identity" to profile.identityVerification, "email" to profile.email,
3277:                     "given" to profile.givenNames, "family" to profile.familyNames,
3278:                     "externalIds" to profile.externalIds?.let { objectMapper.readValue(it, Map::class.java) }, "doc" to doc)))
3279:         } else mapOf("doc" to doc)
3280:         return LayerUpdateResult(
3281:             raw = updateAcademicFieldsInLayer(ExpertIndexLevel.RAW, docId, updateBody),
3282:             candidate = updateAcademicFieldsInLayer(ExpertIndexLevel.CANDIDATE, docId, updateBody),
3283:             application = updateAcademicFieldsInLayer(ExpertIndexLevel.APPLICATION, docId, updateBody)
3284:         )
3285:     }
3286: 
3287:     /**
3288:      * I-2：单层局部更新。HEAD 404 = 该层本来就没有这份文档（跳过，绝不创建）；其余 HEAD 失败与
3289:      * `_update` 失败都标 [LayerUpdateStatus.FAILED] 以便按层重试（成功层在重试时不会被再次破坏）。
3290:      */
3291:     private fun updateAcademicFieldsInLayer(
3292:         level: ExpertIndexLevel,
3293:         docId: String,
3294:         updateBody: Map<String, Any?>
3295:     ): LayerUpdateStatus {
3296:         val index = expertIndexService.indexName(level)
3297:         try {
3298:             restTemplate.exchange(
3299:                 "${esProperties.baseUrl}/$index/_doc/$docId", HttpMethod.HEAD, HttpEntity(null, esHeaders()),
3300:                 Void::class.java
3301:             )
3302:         } catch (e: HttpClientErrorException) {
3303:             if (e.statusCode == HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND) return LayerUpdateStatus.ABSENT
3304:             log.warn("Failed to check academic field target for {} in index {}: {}", docId, level, e.message)
3305:             return LayerUpdateStatus.FAILED
3306:         } catch (e: Exception) {
3307:             log.warn("Failed to check academic field target for {} in index {}: {}", docId, level, e.message)
3308:             return LayerUpdateStatus.FAILED
3309:         }
3310:         return try {
3311:             val response = restTemplate.exchange(
3312:                 "${esProperties.baseUrl}/$index/_update/$docId", HttpMethod.POST,
3313:                 HttpEntity(updateBody, esHeaders()), com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode::class.java
3314:             )
3315:             if (response.body?.path("result")?.asText() == "noop") LayerUpdateStatus.FAILED else LayerUpdateStatus.UPDATED
3316:         } catch (e: Exception) {
3317:             log.warn("Failed to update academic fields for {} in index {}: {}", docId, level, e.message)
3318:             LayerUpdateStatus.FAILED
3319:         }
3320:     }
3321: 
3322:     /** I-1：定位文档一律用真实 `_id`；缺失时退回既有口径 `orcidId`，API ID 绝不参与定位。 */
3323:     private fun enrichmentDocId(profile: ExpertProfile): String = profile.esDocId ?: profile.orcidId
3324: 
3325:     /** I-1：每批最多 100 个不同身份（OpenAlex 的 OR 过滤上限），配置更小时按配置。 */
3326:     private fun enrichmentBatchSize(): Int =
3327:         openAlexProperties.enrichmentBatchSize.coerceIn(1, MAX_ENRICHMENT_IDENTITIES_PER_BATCH)
3328: 
3329:     /**
3330:      * I-1：可信作者身份只来自 `externalIds.openAlexAuthorId`，并且只接受 `A` + 数字的规范形状。
3331:      * 任何其他形状（含 `EMAIL-*`、W 前缀、空串）都当作「没有作者 ID」，绝不参与查询或文档定位。
3332:      */
3333:     private fun trustedOpenAlexAuthorId(profile: ExpertProfile): String? {
3334:         if (DiscoveryIdentity.isDiscovery(profile)) {
3335:             return if (DiscoveryIdentity.allowed(profile)) normalizeOpenAlexAuthorId(profile.identityVerification?.openAlexAuthorId) else null
3336:         }
3337:         val externalIds = profile.externalIds ?: return null
3338:         val parsed = try {
3339:             objectMapper.readValue(externalIds, Map::class.java)
3340:         } catch (e: Exception) {
3341:             return null
3342:         }
3343:         return normalizeOpenAlexAuthorId(parsed["openAlexAuthorId"] as? String)
3344:     }
3345: 
3346:     /** I-1：有效 ORCID = 非空且不是 `EMAIL-*` 主键；`EMAIL-*` 绝不能作为 `filter=orcid:` 的值。 */
3347:     private fun trustedOrcid(profile: ExpertProfile): String? {
3348:         if (DiscoveryIdentity.isDiscovery(profile)) {
3349:             return profile.identityVerification?.orcid?.takeIf {
3350:                 DiscoveryIdentity.allowed(profile) && it.matches(Regex("\\d{4}-\\d{4}-\\d{4}-\\d{3}[0-9X]"))
3351:             }
3352:         }
3353:         return profile.orcidId.trim().takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() && !it.startsWith(EMAIL_PRIMARY_KEY_PREFIX) }
3354:     }
3355: 
3356:     /**
3357:      * I-1/I-3：定向补全共享核心 —— 同一批量核心处理原始库/候选库/申请库中的指定专家。
3358:      * - 每批最多 [MAX_ENRICHMENT_IDENTITIES_PER_BATCH] 个不同身份；先用可信 `externalIds.openAlexAuthorId`，
3359:      *   缺失时用有效 ORCID；两者都没有 = [ProfileEnrichmentOutcome.NoId]，不发作者查询；
3360:      * - `EMAIL-*` 主键绝不当 ORCID 传出去，API ID/ORCID 也绝不替代真实 `_id` 定位文档；
3361:      * - 结果以真实 `esDocId` 为键，逐人一个结果；
3362:      * - 同一 ORCID 在完整响应里对应多个作者 ID 时是 [ProfileEnrichmentOutcome.AmbiguousIdentity]：
3363:      *   该身份分组的全部文档都不写学术事实、不触发再核验、也不请求标题（I-3）；
3364:      * - 额度延期是 [ProfileEnrichmentOutcome.Deferred]（未发请求、不消耗尝试次数），限流/网络失败才是
3365:      *   [ProfileEnrichmentOutcome.RetryableError]；
3366:      * - 基础事实写入成功、但开关控制的最近论文/专利标题子请求失败时是 [ProfileEnrichmentOutcome.Partial]。
3367:      */
3368:     @JvmOverloads
3369:     fun enrichProfiles(
3370:         profiles: List<ExpertProfile>,
3371:         requestKind: RequestKind = RequestKind.HISTORY_ENRICHMENT
3372:     ): Map<String, ProfileEnrichmentOutcome> {
3373:         val outcomes = LinkedHashMap<String, ProfileEnrichmentOutcome>()
3374:         if (profiles.isEmpty()) return outcomes
3375: 
3376:         val openAlex = openAlexProvider.getIfAvailable()
3377:         if (openAlex == null) {
3378:             // 未启用 OpenAlex：本次无法补全，可等启用后重试（绝不是「查无此人」或「已完成」）。
3379:             profiles.forEach { outcomes[enrichmentDocId(it)] = ProfileEnrichmentOutcome.RetryableError() }
3380:             return outcomes
3381:         }
3382: 
3383:         val byAuthorId = LinkedHashMap<String, MutableList<ExpertProfile>>()
3384:         val byOrcid = LinkedHashMap<String, MutableList<ExpertProfile>>()
3385:         for (profile in profiles) {
3386:             val authorId = trustedOpenAlexAuthorId(profile)
3387:             val orcid = trustedOrcid(profile)
3388:             when {
3389:                 authorId != null -> byAuthorId.getOrPut(authorId) { mutableListOf() }.add(profile)
3390:                 orcid != null -> byOrcid.getOrPut(orcid) { mutableListOf() }.add(profile)
3391:                 else -> outcomes[enrichmentDocId(profile)] = ProfileEnrichmentOutcome.NoId
3392:             }
3393:         }
3394: 
3395:         var deferredResetAt = enrichIdentityGroups(byAuthorId, outcomes, requestKind) { ids, kind ->
3396:             openAlex.batchEnrichByAuthorIds(ids, kind)
3397:         }
3398:         if (deferredResetAt == null) {
3399:             deferredResetAt = enrichIdentityGroups(byOrcid, outcomes, requestKind) { ids, kind ->
3400:                 openAlex.batchEnrichByOrcids(ids, kind)
3401:             }
3402:         }
3403:         if (deferredResetAt != null) {
3404:             // I-5：额度耗尽后不再发请求，本轮没得出结论的身份一律标 Deferred（可续跑、不算失败）。
3405:             for (profile in profiles) {
3406:                 val docId = enrichmentDocId(profile)
3407:                 if (!outcomes.containsKey(docId)) outcomes[docId] = ProfileEnrichmentOutcome.Deferred(deferredResetAt)
3408:             }
3409:         }
3410:         return outcomes
3411:     }
3412: 
3413:     /**
3414:      * 按身份分批查询并写回。[groups] 的键是不同身份，值是该身份对应的全部文档。
3415:      * 返回非 null 表示额度已耗尽：调用方停止后续查询并把剩余身份标 Deferred。
3416:      */
3417:     private fun enrichIdentityGroups(
3418:         groups: Map<String, MutableList<ExpertProfile>>,
3419:         outcomes: MutableMap<String, ProfileEnrichmentOutcome>,
3420:         requestKind: RequestKind,
3421:         query: (List<String>, RequestKind) -> Map<String, EnrichmentOutcome>
3422:     ): Instant? {
3423:         for (chunk in groups.keys.toList().chunked(enrichmentBatchSize())) {
3424:             val lookup = try {
3425:                 query(chunk, requestKind)
3426:             } catch (e: OpenAlexBudgetDeferredException) {
3427:                 log.info("OpenAlex budget deferred until {}: {} identities not attempted", e.resetAt, chunk.size)
3428:                 return e.resetAt
3429:             }
3430:             for (identity in chunk) {
3431:                 val profiles = groups[identity].orEmpty()
3432:                 when (val found = lookup[identity] ?: EnrichmentOutcome.NotFound) {
3433:                     is EnrichmentOutcome.Success -> {
3434:                         for (profile in profiles) {
3435:                             val layers = updateExpertAcademicFields(profile, found.data)
3436:                             val docId = enrichmentDocId(profile)
3437:                             if (layers.hasFailedLayer() || found.titlesFailed) {
3438:                                 outcomes[docId] = ProfileEnrichmentOutcome.Partial(layers, recentWorksFailed = found.titlesFailed)
3439:                             } else if (DiscoveryIdentity.isDiscovery(profile)) {
3440:                                 val result = try {
3441:                                     revalidationService.revalidateDiscovery(docId)
3442:                                 } catch (e: Exception) {
3443:                                     log.warn("Discovery admission retry required for {}: {}", docId, e.message)
3444:                                     PromotionOutcome.WriteFailed
3445:                                 }
3446:                                 outcomes[docId] = when (result) {
3447:                                     PromotionOutcome.Promoted -> ProfileEnrichmentOutcome.Success(layers, promoted = true)
3448:                                     PromotionOutcome.AlreadyPresent, is PromotionOutcome.Rejected ->
3449:                                         ProfileEnrichmentOutcome.Success(layers)
3450:                                     else -> ProfileEnrichmentOutcome.RetryableError()
3451:                                 }
3452:                             } else outcomes[docId] = ProfileEnrichmentOutcome.Success(layers)
3453:                         }
3454:                     }
3455:                     is EnrichmentOutcome.NotFound ->
3456:                         profiles.forEach { outcomes[enrichmentDocId(it)] = ProfileEnrichmentOutcome.NotFound }
3457:                     is EnrichmentOutcome.AmbiguousIdentity ->
3458:                         // I-3：同一 ORCID 对应多个作者 —— 该分组的**全部**文档都不写学术事实、不触发再核验，
3459:                         // 也不请求最近论文/标题（跳过 updateExpertAcademicFields 与 revalidationService）。
3460:                         profiles.forEach {
3461:                             outcomes[enrichmentDocId(it)] = ProfileEnrichmentOutcome.AmbiguousIdentity
3462:                         }
3463:                     is EnrichmentOutcome.ApiError ->
3464:                         profiles.forEach {
3465:                             outcomes[enrichmentDocId(it)] = ProfileEnrichmentOutcome.RetryableError()
3466:                         }
3467:                     is EnrichmentOutcome.RateLimited ->
3468:                         profiles.forEach {
3469:                             outcomes[enrichmentDocId(it)] = ProfileEnrichmentOutcome.RetryableError(
3470:                                 retryAfterMs = found.retryAfterMs, rateLimited = true
3471:                             )
3472:                         }
3473:                 }
3474:             }
3475:         }
3476:         return null
3477:     }
3478: 
3479:     /** I-1（08）：ORCID 就是该文档的真实 `_id`（HEAD 已按它命中），补建任务直接用这个值。 */
3480:     private fun existsInRawIndexByOrcid(orcid: String): DedupResult {
3481:         val url = "${esProperties.baseUrl}/${expertIndexService.indexName(ExpertIndexLevel.RAW)}/_doc/$orcid"
3482:         return try {
3483:             restTemplate.exchange(url, HttpMethod.HEAD, HttpEntity(null, esHeaders()), Void::class.java)
3484:             DedupResult.Exists(orcid)
3485:         } catch (e: HttpClientErrorException) {
3486:             if (e.statusCode == HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND) DedupResult.NotFound else DedupResult.Error
3487:         } catch (e: Exception) { DedupResult.Error }
3488:     }
3489: 
3490:     /**
3491:      * I-1（08）：命中时取回匹配文档的真实 `_id`（`size=1` + 不取 `_source`，命中判据仍是 `total`）。
3492:      * 只做去重读取，绝不按新论文的身份改写已入库文档；`_id` 读不到时按 [DedupResult.Exists.docId] 为空处理。
3493:      */
3494:     private fun existsInRawIndexByEmail(email: String): DedupResult {
3495:         val indices = ExpertIndexLevel.values().joinToString(",") { expertIndexService.indexName(it) }
3496:         val url = "${esProperties.baseUrl}/$indices/_search?ignore_unavailable=true"
3497:         val query = mapOf(
3498:             "query" to mapOf("term" to mapOf("email" to DiscoveryIdentity.normalizedEmail(email))),
3499:             "size" to 1,
3500:             "_source" to listOf("email", "givenNames", "familyNames", "identityVerification", "externalIds", "emailSource", "tags")
3501:         )
3502:         return try {
3503:             val response = restTemplate.exchange(url, HttpMethod.POST, HttpEntity(query, esHeaders()),
3504:                 com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode::class.java).body
3505:             val hits = response?.path("hits")
3506:             val total = hits?.path("total")?.path("value")?.asInt(0) ?: 0
3507:             if (total > 0) {
3508:                 DedupResult.Exists(hits?.path("hits")?.firstOrNull()?.path("_id")?.asText()?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }, hits?.path("hits")?.firstOrNull()?.path("_source"))
3509:             } else {
3510:                 DedupResult.NotFound
3511:             }
3512:         } catch (e: HttpClientErrorException) {
3513:             if (e.statusCode == HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND) DedupResult.NotFound else DedupResult.Error
3514:         } catch (e: Exception) { DedupResult.Error }
3515:     }
3516: 
3517:     private fun buildExternalIds(paper: PaperMetadata, authorEmail: AuthorEmail): String? {
3518:         val ids = mutableMapOf<String, String>()
3519:         paper.pmcId?.let { ids["pmcId"] = it }
3520:         paper.doi?.let { ids["doi"] = it }
3521:         paper.pmid?.let { ids["pmid"] = it }
3522:         authorEmail.orcidId?.let { ids["orcid"] = it }
3523:         // I-1/I-3: 作者 ID 只是 externalIds 的一个子键（合并写入，不覆盖其他导入 ID），
3524:         // 也绝不参与 ES _id：无 ORCID 的专家主键仍是 EMAIL-*。
3525:         normalizeOpenAlexAuthorId(authorEmail.openAlexAuthorId)?.let { ids["openAlexAuthorId"] = it }
3526:         return if (ids.isEmpty()) null else objectMapper.writeValueAsString(ids)
3527:     }
3528: 
3529:     private fun esHeaders(): HttpHeaders = HttpHeaders().apply {
3530:         contentType = MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON
3531:         val raw = "${esProperties.username}:${esProperties.password}"
3532:         set(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, "Basic ${Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(raw.toByteArray(Charsets.UTF_8))}")
3533:     }
3534: 
3535:     private fun tryGetEmailFromOrcid(orcidId: String): List<String> {
3536:         val orcid = orcidProvider.getIfAvailable() ?: return emptyList()
3537:         try {
3538:             val criteria = PaperSearchCriteria(
3539:                 keywords = listOf("orcid:$orcidId"),
3540:                 pageSize = 5
3541:             )
3542:             val records = orcid.searchOrcidRecords(criteria)
3543:             val normalizedTarget = orcidId.removePrefix("https://orcid.org/").trim()
3544:             val matched = records.firstOrNull {
3545:                 it.orcidId?.removePrefix("https://orcid.org/")?.trim().equals(normalizedTarget, ignoreCase = true)
3546:             }
3547:             return matched?.emails.orEmpty()
3548:         } catch (e: Exception) {
3549:             log.warn("Failed to get email from ORCID for ID {}: {}", orcidId, e.message)
3550:             return emptyList()
3551:         }
3552:     }
3553: 
3554:     private fun updateRawDocumentEmail(orcidId: String, email: String): Boolean {
3555:         val rawIndex = expertIndexService.indexName(ExpertIndexLevel.RAW)
3556:         val now = LocalDateTime.now().format(dateFormatter)
3557:         val updateBody = mapOf(
3558:             "doc" to mapOf(
3559:                 "email" to email,
3560:                 "updatedAt" to now
3561:             )
3562:         )
3563:         val updateUrl = "${esProperties.baseUrl}/$rawIndex/_update/$orcidId"
3564:         return try {
3565:             restTemplate.exchange(updateUrl, HttpMethod.POST, HttpEntity(updateBody, esHeaders()),
3566:                 com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode::class.java)
3567:             true
3568:         } catch (e: Exception) {
3569:             log.warn("Failed to update email for {} in RAW index: {}", orcidId, e.message)
3570:             false
3571:         }
3572:     }
3573: 
3574:     private fun promoteRawToCandidateWithEmail(profile: ExpertProfile): Boolean {
3575:         val candidateIndex = expertIndexService.indexName(ExpertIndexLevel.CANDIDATE)
3576:         try {
3577:             restTemplate.exchange(
3578:                 "${esProperties.baseUrl}/$candidateIndex/_doc/${profile.orcidId}",
3579:                 HttpMethod.HEAD,
3580:                 HttpEntity(null, esHeaders()),
3581:                 Void::class.java
3582:             )
3583:             log.debug("CANDIDATE already exists for {}, skip promotion", profile.orcidId)
3584:             return false
3585:         } catch (e: HttpClientErrorException) {
3586:             if (e.statusCode != HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND) {
3587:                 log.warn("Failed to check CANDIDATE existence for {}: {}", profile.orcidId, e.message)
3588:                 return false
3589:             }
3590:         } catch (e: Exception) {
3591:             log.warn("Failed to check CANDIDATE existence for {}: {}", profile.orcidId, e.message)
3592:             return false
3593:         }
3594: 
3595:         val rawIndex = expertIndexService.indexName(ExpertIndexLevel.RAW)
3596:         val getUrl = "${esProperties.baseUrl}/$rawIndex/_doc/${profile.orcidId}"
3597:         val rawDoc = try {
3598:             val response = restTemplate.exchange(getUrl, HttpMethod.GET, HttpEntity(null, esHeaders()),
3599:                 com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode::class.java).body
3600:             val source = response?.path("_source")
3601:             if (source != null && !source.isMissingNode) {
3602:                 objectMapper.convertValue(source, Map::class.java) as? Map<String, Any?>
3603:             } else null
3604:         } catch (e: Exception) {
3605:             log.warn("Failed to read raw document for promotion: {}", e.message)
3606:             null
3607:         }         ?: return false
3608: 
3609:         val now = LocalDateTime.now().format(dateFormatter)
3610:         val candidateDoc = rawDoc.toMutableMap().apply {
3611:             put("candidateValidatedAt", now)
3612:             put("updatedAt", now)
3613:             val existingTags = (get("tags") as? List<*>)?.filterIsInstance<String>() ?: emptyList()
3614:             put("tags", (existingTags + "auto_promoted").distinct())
3615:         }
3616:         val putUrl = "${esProperties.baseUrl}/$candidateIndex/_doc/${profile.orcidId}" +
3617:             if (DiscoveryIdentity.isDiscovery(profile)) "?op_type=create" else ""
3618:         return try {
3619:             restTemplate.exchange(putUrl, HttpMethod.PUT, HttpEntity(candidateDoc, esHeaders()),
3620:                 com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode::class.java)
3621:             true
3622:         } catch (e: Exception) {
3623:             log.warn("Failed to promote raw expert {} with email to CANDIDATE: {}", profile.orcidId, e.message)
3624:             false
3625:         }
3626:     }
3627: 
3628:     private fun backfillRawEmailsAndPromote(limit: Int = 100) {
3629:         var attemptedCount = 0
3630:         var promotedCount = 0
3631:         expertSearchService.scrollExperts(ExpertIndexLevel.RAW) { batch, batchNumber, totalHits ->
3632:             if (progressStore.isCancelled("EXPERT_DISCOVERY")) return@scrollExperts false
3633:             if (attemptedCount >= limit) return@scrollExperts false
3634: 
3635:             for (profile in batch) {
3636:                 if (DiscoveryIdentity.isDiscovery(profile)) continue
3637:                 if (attemptedCount >= limit) break
3638:                 if (progressStore.isCancelled("EXPERT_DISCOVERY")) break
3639: 
3640:                 if (profile.email.isNullOrBlank()) {
3641:                     val tempProfile = profile.copy(email = "temp@weibo.com")
3642:                     if (eligibilityService.evaluateEligibility(tempProfile).eligible) {
3643:                         val orcidId = profile.orcidId
3644:                         if (!orcidId.startsWith(EMAIL_PRIMARY_KEY_PREFIX) && orcidId.isNotBlank()) {
3645:                             attemptedCount++
3646:                             val emails = tryGetEmailFromOrcid(orcidId)
3647:                             if (progressStore.isCancelled("EXPERT_DISCOVERY")) break
3648:                             if (emails.isNotEmpty()) {
3649:                                 val validEmail = emails.firstOrNull { emailValidationService.validate(it).valid }
3650:                                 if (validEmail != null && !progressStore.isCancelled("EXPERT_DISCOVERY")) {
3651:                                     if (updateRawDocumentEmail(orcidId, validEmail)) {
3652:                                         if (progressStore.isCancelled("EXPERT_DISCOVERY")) break
3653:                                         if (promoteRawToCandidateWithEmail(profile.copy(email = validEmail))) {
3654:                                             promotedCount++
3655:                                             log.info("Successfully backfilled email {} for ORCID {} and promoted to CANDIDATE", validEmail, orcidId)
3656:                                         }
3657:                                     }
3658:                                 }
3659:                             }
3660:                         }
3661:                     }
3662:                 }
3663:             }
3664:             log.info("RAW email backfill batch {}: attempted={}, promoted={}, totalHits={}", batchNumber, attemptedCount, promotedCount, totalHits)
3665:             attemptedCount < limit
3666:         }
3667:     }
3668: }
3669: 
3670: // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
3671: // c2（I-1/I-2/I-4）：持久化队列的对外契约类型
3672: // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
3673: 
3674: /** I-2：负载版本。未知版本**不得消费**（形成可观测失败，绝不按旧/新语义猜测）。 */
3675: const val QUEUED_PAYLOAD_VERSION_V1: Int = 1
3676: 
3677: /** I-2/I-5：不可作为正常工作消费的负载原因（形成轻量可观测 FAILED 诊断，绝不静默丢弃）。 */
3678: const val QUEUE_PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE: String = "PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE"
3679: 
3680: /** I-2：无可靠身份（例如没有 ORCID 的 ORCID 记录）。 */
3681: const val QUEUE_UNIDENTIFIABLE: String = "UNIDENTIFIABLE"
3682: 
3683: /** I-6：来源名不在本次启用集合内（拒绝把任意 source 字符串当数据源）。 */
3684: const val QUEUE_SOURCE_NOT_ENABLED: String = "SOURCE_NOT_ENABLED"
3685: 
3686: /** I-1/I-6：来源级取数失败（下一次尝试重放同一页，游标不推进）。 */
3687: const val QUEUE_SEARCH_FAILED: String = "SEARCH_FAILED"
3688: 
3689: /** I-2：负载版本未知。 */
3690: const val QUEUE_UNKNOWN_PAYLOAD_VERSION: String = "UNKNOWN_PAYLOAD_VERSION"
3691: 
3692: /** I-4：抽取结果无法反序列化。 */
3693: const val QUEUE_EXTRACTION_UNREADABLE: String = "EXTRACTION_UNREADABLE"
3694: 
3695: /** I-2：元数据负载无法反序列化。 */
3696: const val QUEUE_PAYLOAD_UNREADABLE: String = "PAYLOAD_UNREADABLE"
3697: 
3698: /** I-5：抽取结果超过 `extraction-max-bytes`。 */
3699: const val QUEUE_EXTRACTION_TOO_LARGE: String = "EXTRACTION_TOO_LARGE"
3700: 
3701: private val HEX_DIGITS = "0123456789abcdef".toCharArray()
3702: 
3703: /**
3704:  * I-2：一条队列工作的**完整信封**（身份 + 版本 + 序列化负载 + 字节数 + 可见性）。
3705:  *
3706:  * [payloadJson] 就是 `payload_version=1` 的序列化契约（PAPER = `PaperMetadata`，RECORD = ORCID 记录）；
3707:  * [itemKey] 是队列唯一键的第二段（`UNIQUE(stream_id, item_key)`），[identityQuality] 说明它是怎么来的。
3708:  */
3709: data class QueuedItemEnvelope(
3710:     val sourceName: String,
3711:     val itemKey: String,
3712:     val identityQuality: String,
3713:     /** `PAPER` / `RECORD`（[QueueItemUnit]）。 */
3714:     val unit: String,
3715:     val payloadVersion: Int,
3716:     val payloadJson: String,
3717:     val payloadBytes: Long,
3718:     /** I-6：可公开下载（有可重取地址或不需要下载）→ 消费者在来源内优先处理。 */
3719:     val publiclyDownloadable: Boolean,
3720:     /** I-2/I-5：非空即本条不能作为正常工作消费，形成轻量 FAILED 诊断。 */
3721:     val rejectReason: String? = null
3722: )
3723: 
3724: /**
3725:  * I-1/I-6：一次**单页**采集的结果。
3726:  *
3727:  * - [nextCursor] 是下一页要用的游标；[exhausted] 只在把来源翻到底时为 true；
3728:  * - [deferredUntil]/[deferredReason]：额度延期（不是失败，也不是穷尽）；
3729:  * - [errorReason]：来源级取数失败（调用方按来源安排下次尝试，游标保持进入值）；
3730:  * - [windowLimit]：CORE 分片触达供应商 offset 窗口（已切下一分片，未覆盖尾部只记录）。
3731:  */
3732: data class QueuedSourcePage(
3733:     val sourceName: String,
3734:     val unit: String,
3735:     val items: List<QueuedItemEnvelope>,
3736:     val nextCursor: String?,
3737:     val exhausted: Boolean,
3738:     val deferredUntil: Instant? = null,
3739:     val deferredReason: String? = null,
3740:     val errorReason: String? = null,
3741:     val windowLimit: Boolean = false
3742: )
3743: 
3744: /**
3745:  * I-4/I-6：一条工作的抽取结果。
3746:  *
3747:  * - [Extracted] 的 `extractionJson` **须通过身份规则版本门禁**：后续尝试直接消费、不再下载（I-4）；
3748:  * - [HostBusy] 只延期、绝不消耗 attempts（I-6）；
3749:  * - [Failed] 是适配器抛出的真实异常：可重试的受 5 次上限与退避约束，不可重试的直接 FAILED；
3750:  * - [TooLarge] 形成 FAILED/EXTRACTION_TOO_LARGE 诊断，绝不写截断专家。
3751:  */
3752: sealed class QueuedItemExtraction {
3753:     data class Extracted(
3754:         val extractionJson: String,
3755:         val emailsEmpty: Boolean,
3756:         val httpRequests: Int,
3757:         val contentObtained: Boolean,
3758:         val failureReason: String?
3759:     ) : QueuedItemExtraction()
3760: 
3761:     object HostBusy : QueuedItemExtraction()
3762: 
3763:     data class Failed(val reason: String, val retryable: Boolean) : QueuedItemExtraction()
3764: 
3765:     object TooLarge : QueuedItemExtraction()
3766: }
3767: 
3768: /**
3769:  * I-4：消费已保存抽取结果的结果。
3770:  *
3771:  * [succeeded] 为 true 只表示「必要写入全部成功，或明确判定没有可收录邮箱」——
3772:  * 后者可以 `SUCCEEDED` 但新增 0（原始 0 与「写失败」必须可区分）。
3773:  */
3774: data class QueuedItemConsumption(
3775:     val indexedExperts: Int = 0,
3776:     val duplicateExperts: Int = 0,
3777:     val promoted: Int = 0,
3778:     val emailsValid: Int = 0,
3779:     val rawWriteFailed: Boolean = false,
3780:     val enqueueFailed: Boolean = false,
3781:     val dedupFailed: Boolean = false,
3782:     /**
3783:      * 01（I-3）：重复发现的同身份补证部分/整体失败 —— 仅内存返回值，不落库。
3784:      * 为 true 时 [succeeded] 必为 false，队列必须按可重试错误保留该 job。
3785:      */
3786:     val identityRepairFailed: Boolean = false,
3787:     /**
3788:      * 05（I-2/I-3）：本页/本次消费有可重试的人物登记失败（STALE/FAILED）。为 true 时 [succeeded]
3789:      * 必为 false，队列必须按可重试错误保留该 job（不无限重试，超限转 FAILED）。
3790:      */
3791:     val registrationFailed: Boolean = false,
3792:     /** 05（I-3）：单位相互独立的登记计数（新增人物 / 追加邮箱 / 需复核 / 范围冲突）。 */
3793:     val personCreated: Int = 0,
3794:     val personAttached: Int = 0,
3795:     val personNeedsReview: Int = 0,
3796:     val personScopeConflict: Int = 0,
3797:     val failureReasons: Map<String, Int> = emptyMap(),
3798:     /** 不可恢复的消费失败（未知版本 / 负载不可读）—— job 直接 FAILED 并给出原因。 */
3799:     val unrecoverableReason: String? = null
3800: ) {
3801:     val succeeded: Boolean
3802:         get() = unrecoverableReason == null && !rawWriteFailed && !enqueueFailed && !dedupFailed &&
3803:             !identityRepairFailed && !registrationFailed
3804: }
3805: 
3806: /**
3807:  * I-4（c2）：`consumeOutcome` 的显式结果 —— 队列据它决定 job 终态；旧流程忽略返回值，
3808:  * 统计语义逐字不变（改动的只是「吞掉的写失败」现在有返回值可观察）。
3809:  */
3810: data class ConsumeOutcomeResult(
3811:     val extractionFailed: Boolean = false,
3812:     val fulltextAttempted: Boolean = false,
3813:     val fulltextObtained: Boolean = false,
3814:     val noEligibleEmail: Boolean = false,
3815:     val emailsValid: Int = 0,
3816:     val indexedExperts: Int = 0,
3817:     val duplicateExperts: Int = 0,
3818:     val promoted: Int = 0,
3819:     val rawWriteFailed: Boolean = false,
3820:     val enqueueFailed: Boolean = false,
3821:     val dedupFailed: Boolean = false,
3822:     /** 01（I-3）：本页/本次消费有补证部分/整体失败；仅内存返回值，不落库。 */
3823:     val identityRepairFailed: Boolean = false,
3824:     /**
3825:      * 05（I-2/I-3）：本页/本次消费有可重试的人物登记失败；仅内存返回值，不落库。
3826:      * 为 true 时消费入口必须保留可重试错误、不推进当前页。
3827:      */
3828:     val registrationFailed: Boolean = false,
3829:     /** 05（I-3）：单位相互独立的登记计数（新增人物 / 追加邮箱 / 需复核 / 范围冲突）。 */
3830:     val personCreated: Int = 0,
3831:     val personAttached: Int = 0,
3832:     val personNeedsReview: Int = 0,
3833:     val personScopeConflict: Int = 0,
3834:     val failureReasons: Map<String, Int> = emptyMap()
3835: )
3836: 
3837: /**
3838:  * I-1: 单来源一次运行的结果。[resumeCursor] 是可以安全续跑的游标（首请求失败/部分页/取消/预算停止
3839:  * 时保持进入该页的值）；[exhausted] 只在把来源翻到底时为 true；[stopReason] 见 [DiscoveryStopReason]。
3840:  *
3841:  * c4 的 CORE/ORCID 分页继续使用本类型，异常分支只允许产出 failed/deferred 结果，绝不产出
3842:  * `exhausted = true`。新增的 [retry] 默认 null，CORE/ORCID 与「不进入恢复」的路径逐字兼容。
3843:  */
3844: data class SourceRunOutcome(
3845:     val resumeCursor: String?,
3846:     val exhausted: Boolean,
3847:     val stopReason: String,
3848:     /** I-1: 非空表示本来源已安排一次有限延迟恢复，调用方必须回访同一页入口（而不是记终止失败）。 */
3849:     val retry: SourceRetryPending? = null
3850: )
3851: 
3852: /**
3853:  * I-1/I-3：短尝试用尽后的待恢复信息，同时也是下一次回访的完整入口上下文。
3854:  *
3855:  * - [reason] 只允许脱敏码（`REMOTE_TLS_HANDSHAKE`/`TIMEOUT`/`NETWORK_IO`/`HTTP_5xx`），不含 URL 或密钥；
3856:  * - [resumeCursor] 是本轮内存中的确切页入口（null 是合法的「第一页」）；
3857:  * - [processedBaseline] 是页面入口停止时已处理（且已提交）的数量，回访时从这里起步 ——
3858:  *   既不重新获得完整额度，也不会把已计过的论文再加一遍。
3859:  */
3860: data class SourceRetryPending(
3861:     val round: Int,
3862:     val maxRounds: Int,
3863:     val nextRetryAt: Instant,
3864:     val reason: String,
3865:     val resumeCursor: String?,
3866:     val processedBaseline: Int
3867: )
3868: 
3869: /** I-1: 单来源运行的停止原因，与主方案「每种约束各自给原因」对齐。 */
3870: object DiscoveryStopReason {
3871:     const val EXHAUSTED = "EXHAUSTED"
3872:     const val SEARCH_FAILED = "SEARCH_FAILED"
3873:     const val BUDGET_DEFERRED = "BUDGET_DEFERRED"
3874:     const val CIRCUIT_BREAKER = "CIRCUIT_BREAKER"
3875:     const val CANCELLED = "CANCELLED"
3876:     const val DEDUP_INCOMPLETE = "DEDUP_INCOMPLETE"
3877:     const val GLOBAL_PAPER_LIMIT = "GLOBAL_PAPER_LIMIT"
3878:     const val GLOBAL_AUTHOR_LIMIT = "GLOBAL_AUTHOR_LIMIT"
3879:     const val SOURCE_LIMIT = "SOURCE_LIMIT"
3880:     const val PAGE_PARTIAL = "PAGE_PARTIAL"
3881:     const val PAGE_CONSUMED = "PAGE_CONSUMED"
3882:     const val EMPTY_PAGE = "EMPTY_PAGE"
3883:     const val RAW_WRITE_INCOMPLETE = "RAW_WRITE_INCOMPLETE"
3884: 
3885:     /**
3886:      * I-1（08）：页内 RAW 已落库但补全任务入队未完成（数据库错误）。与 [RAW_WRITE_INCOMPLETE] 一样
3887:      * 保留进入该页的游标以便重放补建，但原因单独给：两者是不同的持久化缺口。
3888:      */
3889:     const val ENQUEUE_INCOMPLETE = "ENQUEUE_INCOMPLETE"
3890: 
3891:     /**
3892:      * 01（I-3）：页内重复发现补证部分/整体失败。与 [RAW_WRITE_INCOMPLETE]/[ENQUEUE_INCOMPLETE]
3893:      * 一样保留进入该页的游标以便重放：补证未完成的页不推进、不记成功。
3894:      */
3895:     const val IDENTITY_REPAIR_INCOMPLETE = "IDENTITY_REPAIR_INCOMPLETE"
3896: 
3897:     /**
3898:      * 05（I-3）：页内存在可重试的人物登记失败（STALE/FAILED）。与 [IDENTITY_REPAIR_INCOMPLETE]
3899:      * 一样保留进入该页的游标以便重放：登记未完成的页不推进、不记成功。
3900:      */
3901:     const val PERSON_REGISTRATION_INCOMPLETE = "PERSON_REGISTRATION_INCOMPLETE"
3902: 
3903:     /**
3904:      * I-4: 分片触达供应商分页窗口（CORE offset 9000 / ORCID start 9999）。这是「该分片停止」，
3905:      * 既不是搜索失败也不是穷尽：游标切到下一分片，未覆盖尾部只在日志与 failureReasons 里记录。
3906:      */
3907:     const val WINDOW_LIMIT = "WINDOW_LIMIT"
3908: 
3909:     /**
3910:      * I-3（09）：单次发现的运行级时间预算到点。与额度延期/熔断一样保留进入该页的检查点，
3911:      * 不把未消费的半页当成已消费，也绝不置 `exhausted`。
3912:      */
3913:     const val TIME_BUDGET = "TIME_BUDGET"
3914: 
3915:     /**
3916:      * I-4：来源正在等待有限延迟恢复 —— 这是**运行中**状态，不是终态错误：不新增 task_execution 状态，
3917:      * 等待期间 `sourceFailureCount=0`、`pendingWork=true`、`exhausted=false`。真正用尽所有尝试才会记
3918:      * 一次 [SEARCH_FAILED]；恢复成功则按实际停止原因覆盖本值。
3919:      */
3920:     const val RETRY_WAIT = "RETRY_WAIT"
3921: }
3922: 
3923: /**
3924:  * 人工补全 scope（08 追加 [DISCOVERY_PENDING]）：
3925:  * - [DEFAULT] / [INSTITUTION_TYPE_BACKFILL] / [LAST_PUBLICATION_YEAR_BACKFILL]：既有历史回填口径，语义不变；
3926:  * - [DISCOVERY_PENDING]：显式重试自动补全队列里到期/待重试的任务（PENDING、到点的 RETRY_WAIT、
3927:  *   租约已过期的 RUNNING），与 worker 走同一批次核心。
3928:  */
3929: enum class EnrichmentScope { DEFAULT, INSTITUTION_TYPE_BACKFILL, LAST_PUBLICATION_YEAR_BACKFILL, DISCOVERY_PENDING }
3930: 
3931: /** I-1：无 ORCID 的专家主键前缀 —— 它不是 ORCID，绝不能作为 `filter=orcid:` 的值传给 OpenAlex。 */
3932: private const val EMAIL_PRIMARY_KEY_PREFIX = "EMAIL-"
3933: 
3934: /** I-1：OpenAlex 的 `filter=...|...` 每批最多 100 个不同身份。 */
3935: private const val MAX_ENRICHMENT_IDENTITIES_PER_BATCH = 100
3936: 
3937: private const val OPENALEX_MAX_PAGE_ATTEMPTS = 3
3938: private val OPENALEX_RETRYABLE_HTTP_STATUS = setOf(500, 502, 503, 504)
3939: 
3940: /** I-1/I-7：短等待与延迟恢复等待的检查粒度（≤100ms，取消/超时必须在下一片内被观察到）。 */
3941: private const val RETRY_WAIT_SLICE_MS = 100L
3942: 
3943: /**
3944:  * I-5：重试事件（安排等待 / 回访开始 / 恢复结束）用的非批次日志号。
3945:  * `batchOnly=true` 只聚合 `batchNumber > 0` 的日志，因此既有按批聚合规则逐字不变。
3946:  */
3947: private const val RETRY_EVENT_BATCH_NUMBER = 0
3948: 
3949: /** I-5：运行中详情里 summaryText 的占位终态（不是 task_execution 状态）。 */
3950: private const val SUMMARY_STATUS_RUNNING = "RUNNING"
3951: 
3952: /** I-1（08）：补全入队的失败原因码（与 [DiscoveryStopReason.ENQUEUE_INCOMPLETE] 配对）。 */
3953: private const val ENRICHMENT_ENQUEUE_FAILED = "ENRICHMENT_ENQUEUE_FAILED"
3954: 
3955: /** 04（I-1）：准入接缝未装配（仅历史单测/未接线）—— 不伪造结论、不晋升，按此原因计入过滤。 */
3956: private const val ADMISSION_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE = "ADMISSION_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE"
3957: 
3958: /** I-4（08）：补全任务的任务类型（worker 与人工入口共用同一把互斥锁）。 */
3959: const val EXPERT_ENRICHMENT_TASK_TYPE = "EXPERT_ENRICHMENT"
3960: 
3961: /** I-2：单层学术字段写入结果。 */
3962: enum class LayerUpdateStatus {
3963:     /** 文档存在且局部 `_update` 成功。 */
3964:     UPDATED,
3965:     /** HEAD 404：该层本来就没有这份文档，按计划跳过（绝不创建）。 */
3966:     ABSENT,
3967:     /** HEAD 非 404 失败或 `_update` 失败：该层可重试。 */
3968:     FAILED
3969: }
3970: 
3971: /**
3972:  * I-2：三层局部更新的逐层结果。c7 直接把本类型存进 `result_json`，三个字段名即持久化契约。
3973:  * 判定一律用方法，避免序列化出派生键。
3974:  */
3975: data class LayerUpdateResult(
3976:     val raw: LayerUpdateStatus,
3977:     val candidate: LayerUpdateStatus,
3978:     val application: LayerUpdateStatus
3979: ) {
3980:     /** 至少有一层真实写成功（RAW-only 补全同样算成功）。 */
3981:     fun updatedAnyLayer(): Boolean =
3982:         raw == LayerUpdateStatus.UPDATED ||
3983:             candidate == LayerUpdateStatus.UPDATED ||
3984:             application == LayerUpdateStatus.UPDATED
3985: 
3986:     /** 存在「该层本来存在但没写成功」的层：整体只能算部分完成，可按层重试。 */
3987:     fun hasFailedLayer(): Boolean =
3988:         raw == LayerUpdateStatus.FAILED ||
3989:             candidate == LayerUpdateStatus.FAILED ||
3990:             application == LayerUpdateStatus.FAILED
3991: }
3992: 
3993: /**
3994:  * 定向补全的逐人结果（跨子计划契约：变体名与语义固定，c7 依此持久化任务状态）。
3995:  * 结果按真实 `esDocId` 为键；`NoId` 与 `NotFound` 都不得伪造成 `Success`。
3996:  */
3997: sealed class ProfileEnrichmentOutcome {
3998:     /** 学术事实已按真实 `_id` 局部写入全部现存层。 */
3999:     data class Success(val layers: LayerUpdateResult, val promoted: Boolean = false) : ProfileEnrichmentOutcome()
4000: 
4001:     /**
4002:      * 基础事实已拿到，但仍有未完成部分：
4003:      * [layers].[LayerUpdateResult.hasFailedLayer] 表示某现存层没写成功（可按层重试）；
4004:      * [recentWorksFailed] 表示开关控制的最近论文/专利标题子请求失败（可单独重试，空结果不算失败）。
4005:      */
4006:     data class Partial(val layers: LayerUpdateResult, val recentWorksFailed: Boolean) : ProfileEnrichmentOutcome()
4007: 
4008:     /** OpenAlex 日额度延期：[resetAt] 是额度实际重置时刻；未发请求，重试不消耗故障尝试次数。 */
4009:     data class Deferred(val resetAt: Instant) : ProfileEnrichmentOutcome()
4010: 
4011:     /** OpenAlex 明确查无此身份（有 A ID/ORCID 但作者不存在）。 */
4012:     object NotFound : ProfileEnrichmentOutcome()
4013: 
4014:     /** 无可靠身份（既无 A ID 也无有效 ORCID）：绝不发作者查询。 */
4015:     object NoId : ProfileEnrichmentOutcome()
4016: 
4017:     /**
4018:      * I-3：同一 ORCID 在 OpenAlex 完整响应里对应多个不同作者 ID，本次无法唯一选择作者。
4019:      * 语义只表示「本次没有唯一身份」：不写任何学术事实、不触发再资格核验、不请求最近论文/标题，
4020:      * 也不清空既有字段；队列侧按未匹配处理（复用 `UNMATCHED` + `AUTHOR_IDENTITY_AMBIGUOUS`）。
4021:      */
4022:     object AmbiguousIdentity : ProfileEnrichmentOutcome()
4023: 
4024:     /** 可重试的失败。[rateLimited] = true 是供应商限流（旧人工入口按 WAIT/ABORT 语义退避重试）。 */
4025:     data class RetryableError(
4026:         val retryAfterMs: Long? = null,
4027:         val rateLimited: Boolean = false
4028:     ) : ProfileEnrichmentOutcome()
4029: }
4030: 
4031: /**
4032:  * I-4（08）：单个来源在本批次补全里的计数。四个结果桶与 07 的任务终态同口径
4033:  * （成功 / 待重试 / 未匹配 / 失败），`enqueued` 是本批为该来源领取处理的任务数。
4034:  * 这是进度 details 与 `/enrich/stats` 的展示口径，不是任务生命周期的唯一事实（那是任务表本身）。
4035:  */
4036: data class AutoEnrichmentSourceCounts(
4037:     val enqueued: Int = 0,
4038:     val succeeded: Int = 0,
4039:     val pending: Int = 0,
4040:     val unmatched: Int = 0,
4041:     val failed: Int = 0
4042: )
4043: 
4044: /**
4045:  * I-2/I-4（08）：一批补全的结果 —— 领取了多少、逐源成功/待补/未匹配/失败多少，以及额度延期与取消。
4046:  * worker 用它写自己的 EXPERT_ENRICHMENT 任务记录（逐源计数放在既有 details/result_summary JSON 里），
4047:  * `/enrich/stats` 用它展示最近一批；发现成功数与本结果完全无关（不叠加）。
4048:  */
4049: data class AutoEnrichmentBatchResult(
4050:     /** 本批实际交给补全核心的任务数（= 各来源 `enqueued` 之和）。 */
4051:     val claimed: Int = 0,
4052:     val succeeded: Int = 0,
4053:     /** 到点待重试（含额度延期、限流、可重试故障）的任务数。 */
4054:     val pending: Int = 0,
4055:     val unmatched: Int = 0,
4056:     val failed: Int = 0,
4057:     /** 完成时租约已失效/行不再是 RUNNING 的任务数（不写任何列）。 */
4058:     val claimLost: Int = 0,
4059:     /** 达到 06 定向复评条件（成功且 RAW-only）的专家数与其候选晋升数。 */
4060:     val revalidated: Int = 0,
4061:     val promoted: Int = 0,
4062:     val bySource: Map<String, AutoEnrichmentSourceCounts> = emptyMap(),
4063:     /** OpenAlex 日额度延期：本批没有继续重试，其余来源的发现/补全不受影响。 */
4064:     val budgetDeferred: Boolean = false,
4065:     val deferredUntil: String? = null,
4066:     val cancelled: Boolean = false
4067: ) : TaskExecutionSummaryProvider {
4068:     override val taskSuccessCount: Int get() = succeeded
4069: 
4070:     /** 未匹配（无可靠身份/查无作者）与故障失败都算本批未完成的任务。 */
4071:     override val taskFailureCount: Int get() = failed + unmatched
4072: 
4073:     override val taskFinalStatus: String?
4074:         get() = when {
4075:             cancelled -> "CANCELLED"
4076:             budgetDeferred || pending > 0 -> "PARTIAL_SUCCESS"
4077:             else -> null
4078:         }
4079: 
4080:     /** I-4：逐源入队/成功/待补/未匹配/失败 —— 只进既有 details/result_summary JSON，不加列也不改前端。 */
4081:     fun toDetails(): Map<String, Any> {
4082:         val details = LinkedHashMap<String, Any>()
4083:         details["claimed"] = claimed
4084:         details["succeeded"] = succeeded
4085:         details["pending"] = pending
4086:         details["unmatched"] = unmatched
4087:         details["failed"] = failed
4088:         details["revalidated"] = revalidated
4089:         details["promoted"] = promoted
4090:         details["budgetDeferred"] = budgetDeferred
4091:         if (claimLost > 0) details["claimLost"] = claimLost
4092:         deferredUntil?.let { details["deferredUntil"] = it }
4093:         details["bySource"] = bySource.mapValues { (_, counts) ->
4094:             mapOf(
4095:                 "enqueued" to counts.enqueued,
4096:                 "succeeded" to counts.succeeded,
4097:                 "pending" to counts.pending,
4098:                 "unmatched" to counts.unmatched,
4099:                 "failed" to counts.failed
4100:             )
4101:         }
4102:         return details
4103:     }
4104: }
4105: 
4106: data class EnrichmentStats(
4107:     val pending: Long,
4108:     val enrichedLast30d: Long,
4109:     val total: Long,
4110:     val institutionTypePending: Long,
4111:     val lastPublicationYearPending: Long,
4112:     /**
4113:      * I-4（08）：最近一批自动/待补补全的逐源计数（进程内观测值，重启即空）；
4114:      * 历史任务的详情始终是当时快照，不会被后续补全改写。
4115:      */
4116:     val autoEnrichment: AutoEnrichmentBatchResult? = null
4117: )
4118: 
4119: data class EnrichmentResult(
4120:     val enriched: Int,
4121:     val failed: Int,
4122:     val failureReasons: Map<String, Int> = emptyMap(),
4123:     val wasCancelled: Boolean = false,
4124:     val circuitBreakerTripped: Boolean = false,
4125:     /** I-5：OpenAlex 日额度延期导致本轮提前停止 —— 不是失败，剩余工作可续跑。 */
4126:     val budgetDeferred: Boolean = false
4127: ) : TaskExecutionSummaryProvider {
4128:     override val taskSuccessCount: Int get() = enriched
4129:     override val taskFailureCount: Int get() = failed
4130:     override val taskFinalStatus: String?
4131:         get() = when {
4132:             wasCancelled -> "CANCELLED"
4133:             circuitBreakerTripped -> "FAILED"
4134:             budgetDeferred -> "PARTIAL_SUCCESS"
4135:             else -> null
4136:         }
4137: }

### src/main/kotlin/com/weibo/talentintroduction/discovery/service/DiscoveryVerificationRegistrationService.kt
1: package com.weibo.talentintroduction.discovery.service
2: 
3: import com.fasterxml.jackson.module.kotlin.jacksonObjectMapper
4: import com.weibo.talentintroduction.config.DiscoveryVerificationProperties
5: import com.weibo.talentintroduction.discovery.domain.*
6: import com.weibo.talentintroduction.discovery.repository.DiscoveryReviewRepository
7: import com.weibo.talentintroduction.discovery.repository.DiscoveryVerificationRepository
8: import com.weibo.talentintroduction.expert.domain.DiscoveryIdentity
9: import com.weibo.talentintroduction.expert.domain.ExpertIndexLevel
10: import com.weibo.talentintroduction.expert.domain.ExpertProfile
11: import com.weibo.talentintroduction.expert.service.ExpertIndexWriterService
