15: import org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionTemplate
16: import org.springframework.web.server.ResponseStatusException
17: import java.time.Clock
18: 
19: /** Passed explicitly across executor boundaries; never inferred from a task progress slot. */
20: data class DiscoveryVerificationExecution(val executionId: Long?, val run: VerificationRun? = null) {
21:     init {
22:         require(executionId == null || executionId > 0)
23:         require(run == null || executionId == run.executionId)
24:     }
25: }
26: 
27: data class DiscoveryRegistration(val item: VerificationItem? = null, val protected: Boolean = false)
28: class DiscoveryRegistrationException(cause: Exception) : IllegalStateException("VERIFICATION_REGISTRATION_FAILED", cause)
29: 
30: @Service
31: class DiscoveryVerificationRegistrationService(
32:     private val properties: DiscoveryVerificationProperties,
33:     private val repository: DiscoveryVerificationRepository,
34:     private val writer: ExpertIndexWriterService,
35:     private val policy: DiscoveryAdmissionPolicy,
36:     private val admissions: DiscoveryReviewRepository,
37:     private val jdbc: JdbcTemplate,
38:     private val transactions: TransactionTemplate,
39:     private val clock: Clock = Clock.systemUTC()
40: ) : RegistrationRecovery {
41:     private val mapper = jacksonObjectMapper().findAndRegisterModules()
42: 
43:     fun resolveOptions(criteria: PaperSearchCriteria): PaperSearchCriteria {
44:         if (criteria.apolloAssist == true && (!properties.enabled || properties.apolloApiKey.isBlank())) {
45:             throw ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE, "Apollo verification is not ready")
46:         }
47:         return criteria.copy(apolloAssist = criteria.apolloAssist ?: (properties.enabled && properties.apolloApiKey.isNotBlank() && properties.apolloDefault))
48:     }
49: 
50:     fun begin(executionId: Long?, criteria: PaperSearchCriteria, historical: Boolean = false): DiscoveryVerificationExecution {
51:         require(executionId == null || executionId > 0) { "A real positive execution ID is required" }
52:         val existing = executionId?.let { repository.findRun(it) }
53:         if (existing != null) return DiscoveryVerificationExecution(executionId, existing)
54:         if (!properties.enabled) return DiscoveryVerificationExecution(executionId)
55:         require(executionId != null) { "Verification requires a real positive execution ID" }
56:         check(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM task_execution WHERE id=?", Long::class.java, executionId) == 1L) {
57:             "Verification requires an existing task execution"
58:         }
59:         // Old persisted JSON is OFF, never reinterpreted through today's default.
60:         val apollo = if (historical && criteria.apolloAssist == null) false else requireNotNull(resolveOptions(criteria).apolloAssist)
61:         val settings = mapper.writeValueAsString(linkedMapOf(
62:             "mode" to properties.mode.name, "policyVersion" to properties.mode.policyVersion,
63:             "requestsPerMinute" to properties.requestsPerMinute, "requestsPerDay" to properties.requestsPerDay,
64:             "maxRequestsPerItem" to properties.maxRequestsPerItem, "maxPagesPerQuery" to properties.maxPagesPerQuery,
65:             "maxTransientAttempts" to properties.maxTransientAttempts, "leaseSeconds" to properties.leaseSeconds,
66:             "httpConnectTimeout" to properties.httpConnectTimeout.toString(), "httpReadTimeout" to properties.httpReadTimeout.toString()
67:         ))
68:         return DiscoveryVerificationExecution(executionId, repository.createRun(executionId, properties.mode, apollo, settings, clock.instant()))
69:     }
70: 
71:     fun closeInput(context: DiscoveryVerificationExecution) {
72:         context.run?.let { repository.closeInput(it.executionId, clock.instant()) }
73:     }
74: 
75:     fun reserve(context: DiscoveryVerificationExecution, docId: String, profile: ExpertProfile,
76:                 sourceName: String, sourceItemKey: String): DiscoveryRegistration {
77:         try {
78:             val run = context.run ?: run {
79:                 check(!properties.enabled) { "Verification execution context is missing" }
80:                 return DiscoveryRegistration()
81:             }
82:             // Match toIndexMap's RAW identity convention; real ORCID remains in externalIds/provenance.
83:             val rawProfile = profile.copy(esDocId = docId, orcidId = docId)
84:             val snapshot = DiscoveryVerificationAdmissionGate.snapshot(docId, rawProfile, policy.evaluate(docId, rawProfile).configSnapshot)
85:             // Read every business protection before the first RAW write; read failures propagate.
86:             val layers = ExpertIndexLevel.values().associateWith { writer.readDiscoveryDocument(it, docId) }
87:             val admission = admissions.findAdmission(docId)
88:             val email = DiscoveryIdentity.normalizedEmail(profile.email)
89:             val contacted = jdbc.queryForObject("""SELECT (EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM expert_contact WHERE LOWER(TRIM(expert_email))=? OR orcid_id=?)
90:                 OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM expert_email_alias WHERE normalized_email=?))""", Boolean::class.java,
91:                 email, profile.orcidId.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }, email) == true
92:             if (profile.dataSource.orEmpty().contains("SBIR", true) || profile.emailSource.orEmpty().contains("SBIR", true) ||
93:                 contacted || layers.values.any { it != null } ||
94:                 admission?.let { it.identityHash == snapshot.identityHash && (it.decisionEnum.manual || it.decision in setOf("LEGACY_APPROVED", "AUTO_PASSED")) } == true) {
95:                 return DiscoveryRegistration(protected = true)
96:             }
97:             val saved = mapper.writeValueAsString(linkedMapOf("docId" to docId, "identityHash" to snapshot.identityHash,
98:                 "factFingerprint" to snapshot.factFingerprint, "profile" to rawProfile, "admissionRevision" to admission?.revision,
99:                 "sourceName" to sourceName, "sourceItemKey" to sourceItemKey, "constructedAt" to clock.instant().toString()))
100:             val item = repository.reserveItem(VerificationReservation(run.executionId, docId, snapshot.identityHash,
101:                 run.policyVersion, sourceName, sourceItemKey, saved, snapshot.factFingerprint), clock.instant())
102:             check(item.sourceName == sourceName && item.sourceItemKey == sourceItemKey) { "Reserved source differs" }
103:             return DiscoveryRegistration(item)
104:         } catch (e: Exception) { throw DiscoveryRegistrationException(e) }
105:     }
106: 
107:     fun activate(registration: DiscoveryRegistration) {
108:         val item = registration.item ?: return
109:         try {
110:             transactions.executeWithoutResult {
111:                 // run -> admission -> item. No external reads while holding these locks.
112:                 jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT execution_id FROM discovery_verification_run WHERE execution_id=? FOR UPDATE", Long::class.java, item.ownerExecutionId)
113:                 if (item.state == VerificationState.RESERVED) {
114:                     admissions.initializeAdmission(item.expertDocId, item.identityHash, "NEEDS_REVIEW",
115:                         item.policyVersion, java.time.LocalDateTime.ofInstant(clock.instant(), java.time.ZoneOffset.UTC))
116:                 }
117:                 val count = jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM discovery_verification_item WHERE owner_execution_id=? AND activated_at IS NOT NULL FOR UPDATE", Long::class.java, item.ownerExecutionId)!!
118:                 repository.activateItem(item.id, (count / 100 + 1).toInt(), clock.instant())
119:             }
120:         } catch (e: Exception) { throw DiscoveryRegistrationException(e) }
121:     }
122: 
123:     /** Only an existing reservation with the exact RAW identity and source may be recovered. */
124:     fun recoverDuplicate(docId: String, sourceName: String, sourceItemKey: String) {
125:         try {
126:             repository.findItemsByDocIds(listOf(docId)).filter {
127:                 it.state == VerificationState.RESERVED && it.sourceName == sourceName && it.sourceItemKey == sourceItemKey
128:             }.forEach { recover(it) }
129:         } catch (e: Exception) { throw DiscoveryRegistrationException(e) }
130:     }
131: 
132:     private fun recover(item: VerificationItem): Boolean {
133:         val raw = writer.readDiscoveryDocument(ExpertIndexLevel.RAW, item.expertDocId) ?: run {
134:             // A closed input alone is not terminal: queued source retries may still write RAW.
135:             // A finished synchronous execution has no source job, so its finished_at is the
136:             // terminal proof instead. Never infer completion from the progress slot.
137:             if (repository.findRun(item.ownerExecutionId)?.inputClosed == true) {
138:                 val statuses = jdbc.query("""SELECT j.status FROM discovery_paper_job j
139:                     JOIN discovery_collection_stream s ON s.id=j.stream_id WHERE s.source=? AND j.item_key=?""",
140:                     { rs, _ -> rs.getString(1) }, item.sourceName, item.sourceItemKey)
141:                 val synchronousFinished = statuses.isEmpty() && jdbc.queryForObject(
142:                     "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM task_execution WHERE id=? AND finished_at IS NOT NULL",
143:                     Long::class.java, item.ownerExecutionId) == 1L
144:                 if (synchronousFinished || (statuses.isNotEmpty() && statuses.all { it in setOf("FAILED", "SUCCEEDED") })) {
145:                     repository.failReserved(item.id, "[\"REGISTRATION_RAW_MISSING\"]", clock.instant())
146:                 }
147:             }
148:             return false
149:         }
150:         val profile = writer.discoveryProfile(item.expertDocId, raw.source)
151:         val identity = DiscoveryReviewIdentity.hash(item.expertDocId, profile.email, profile.givenNames, profile.familyNames)
152:         if (identity != item.identityHash) {
153:             repository.failReserved(item.id, "[\"REGISTRATION_IDENTITY_CHANGED\"]", clock.instant())
154:             return false
155:         }
156:         activate(DiscoveryRegistration(item))
157:         return true
158:     }
159: 
160:     override fun recoverReserved(limit: Int): Int = repository.listReserved(limit).count { recover(it) }
161: }

22: data class VerificationAdmissionEvidence(
23:     val evidence: List<VerificationEvidence>,
24:     val search: ApolloSearchEvidence? = null,
25:     val schemaVersion: Int = 1
26: ) {
27:     init { require(schemaVersion == 1) }
28: }
29: 
30: @Service
31: class DiscoveryVerificationAdmissionGate(
32:     private val repository: DiscoveryVerificationRepository,
33:     private val rules: DiscoveryVerificationRules
34: ) {
35:     fun findContext(docId: String, profile: ExpertProfile): VerificationAdmissionContext =
36:         findContexts(mapOf(docId to profile)).getValue(docId)
37: 
38:     fun findContexts(keys: Map<String, ExpertProfile>): Map<String, VerificationAdmissionContext> {
39:         require(keys.size <= 500)
40:         val items = repository.findItemsByDocIds(keys.keys).groupBy { it.expertDocId }
41:         return keys.mapValues { (docId, profile) -> VerificationAdmissionContext(docId, profile, items[docId].orEmpty()) }
42:     }
43: 
44:     fun evaluateManaged(baseAutomatic: AutomaticAdmissionResult, context: VerificationAdmissionContext): AutomaticAdmissionResult {
45:         if (!context.managed || baseAutomatic.status == DiscoveryAdmissionStatus.LEGACY_APPROVED) return baseAutomatic
46:         val blockers = baseAutomatic.blockingReasons.filterNot { it.code in REPLACED_CODES }.toMutableList()
47:         fun result(codes: List<String>): AutomaticAdmissionResult {
48:             blockers += codes.map { AdmissionReason(it, it, "verification", null, "有效身份与资格证据", "discovery_verification_item") }
49:             return baseAutomatic.copy(
50:                 status = if (blockers.isEmpty()) DiscoveryAdmissionStatus.AUTO_PASSED else DiscoveryAdmissionStatus.NEEDS_REVIEW,
51:                 blockingReasons = blockers.distinctBy { it.code }, policyVersion = POLICY_VERSION
52:             )
53:         }
54:         val snapshot = snapshot(context.docId, context.profile, baseAutomatic.configSnapshot)
55:         val item = context.items.filter { it.policyVersion == VerificationMode.ENFORCE.policyVersion && it.identityHash == snapshot.identityHash }
56:             .maxByOrNull { it.id } ?: return result(listOf("IDENTITY_CHANGED"))
57:         if (item.factFingerprint != snapshot.factFingerprint) return result(listOf("FACT_CHANGED"))
58:         if (item.state != VerificationState.COMPLETED) return result(listOf("VERIFICATION_PENDING"))
59:         val decision = try {
60:             val frozen = mapper.readValue<VerificationSnapshot>(item.snapshotJson)
61:             require(frozen.identityHash == item.identityHash && frozen.factFingerprint == item.factFingerprint)
62:             require(snapshot(context.docId, frozen.profile, baseAutomatic.configSnapshot).factFingerprint == item.factFingerprint)
63:             val payload = mapper.readValue<VerificationAdmissionEvidence>(requireNotNull(item.evidenceJson))
64:             rules.evaluate(snapshot, payload.evidence, payload.search).also {
65:                 require(it.outcome == item.outcome && it.qualification == item.qualification && it.route == item.route)
66:                 require(it.matchedPersonId == item.apolloPersonId)
67:             }
68:         } catch (_: Exception) {
69:             return result(listOf("VERIFICATION_EVIDENCE_INVALID"))
70:         }
71:         return result(if (decision.outcome in ADMITTED_OUTCOMES) emptyList() else decision.reasonCodes.ifEmpty { listOf("VERIFICATION_PENDING") })
72:     }
73: 
74:     companion object {
75:         const val POLICY_VERSION = "APV_20261018_V1"
76:         val REPLACED_CODES = setOf("NO_DOCTORAL_DEGREE", "AGE_EXCEEDED")
77:         private val ADMITTED_OUTCOMES = setOf(VerificationOutcome.DIRECT_CLEAR, VerificationOutcome.APOLLO_CLEAR, VerificationOutcome.APOLLO_UNCLEAR)
78:         private val mapper = jacksonObjectMapper().findAndRegisterModules()
79:             .disable(DeserializationFeature.FAIL_ON_UNKNOWN_PROPERTIES)
80:             .enable(SerializationFeature.ORDER_MAP_ENTRIES_BY_KEYS)
81: 
82:         /** Shared with registration/commit: acquired provider facts never enter this RAW fingerprint. */
83:         fun snapshot(docId: String, profile: ExpertProfile, config: AdmissionConfigSnapshot): VerificationSnapshot {
84:             val identity = DiscoveryReviewIdentity.hash(docId, profile.email, profile.givenNames, profile.familyNames)
85:             val external = profile.externalIds?.let { mapper.readTree(it) }
86:             val facts = sortedMapOf<String, Any?>(
87:                 "identityHash" to identity,
88:                 "externalIds" to sortedMapOf("orcid" to external?.get("orcid"), "openAlexAuthorId" to external?.get("openAlexAuthorId")),
89:                 "orcidId" to profile.orcidId, "institution" to profile.institution, "employment" to profile.employment,
90:                 "degree" to profile.degree, "age" to profile.age,
91:                 "identityVerification" to profile.identityVerification, "institutionEvidence" to profile.institutionEvidence,
92:                 "config" to config
93:             )
94:             return VerificationSnapshot(identity, com.weibo.talentintroduction.expert.domain.DiscoveryIdentity.hash(mapper.writeValueAsString(facts)), profile)
95:         }
96:     }
97: }

### src/main/kotlin/com/weibo/talentintroduction/discovery/service/DiscoveryVerificationCommitService.kt
1: package com.weibo.talentintroduction.discovery.service
2: 
3: import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.DeserializationFeature
4: import com.fasterxml.jackson.module.kotlin.jacksonObjectMapper
5: import com.weibo.talentintroduction.discovery.domain.*
6: import com.weibo.talentintroduction.discovery.repository.DiscoveryReviewRepository
7: import com.weibo.talentintroduction.discovery.repository.DiscoveryVerificationRepository
8: import com.weibo.talentintroduction.expert.domain.DiscoveryIdentity
9: import com.weibo.talentintroduction.expert.domain.ExpertIndexLevel
10: import com.weibo.talentintroduction.expert.service.ExpertIndexWriterService
11: import org.springframework.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate
12: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service
13: import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional
14: import java.time.Clock
15: 
16: /** All network reads finish in prepare/project, outside commit's SQL transaction. */
17: data class VerificationCommitInput(val snapshot: VerificationSnapshot?, val admission: ExpertDiscoveryAdmission?, val protection: String?)
18: 
19: @Service
20: class DiscoveryVerificationCommitService(
21:     private val repository: DiscoveryVerificationRepository,
22:     private val admissions: DiscoveryReviewRepository,
23:     private val review: DiscoveryReviewService,
24:     private val policy: DiscoveryAdmissionPolicy,
25:     private val writer: ExpertIndexWriterService,
26:     private val jdbc: JdbcTemplate,
27:     private val clock: Clock = Clock.systemUTC()
28: ) {
29:     private val mapper = jacksonObjectMapper().findAndRegisterModules().disable(DeserializationFeature.FAIL_ON_UNKNOWN_PROPERTIES)
30: 
31:     fun prepare(item: VerificationItem): VerificationCommitInput {
32:         val raw = writer.readDiscoveryDocument(ExpertIndexLevel.RAW, item.expertDocId)
33:         // A failed layer read must propagate, not become absence of protection.
34:         writer.readDiscoveryDocument(ExpertIndexLevel.CANDIDATE, item.expertDocId)
35:         val application = writer.readDiscoveryDocument(ExpertIndexLevel.APPLICATION, item.expertDocId)
36:         val profile = raw?.let { writer.discoveryProfile(item.expertDocId, it.source) }
37:         val snapshot = profile?.let { DiscoveryVerificationAdmissionGate.snapshot(item.expertDocId, it, policy.evaluate(item.expertDocId, it).configSnapshot) }
38:         val protection = when {
39:             application != null -> "APPLICATION_PRESENT"
40:             profile == null -> null
41:             profile.dataSource.orEmpty().contains("SBIR", true) || profile.emailSource.orEmpty().contains("SBIR", true) -> "SBIR_PROTECTED"
42:             contacted(snapshot!!) -> "CONTACT_PROTECTED"
43:             else -> null
44:         }
45:         return VerificationCommitInput(snapshot, admissions.findAdmission(item.expertDocId), protection)
46:     }
47: 
48:     @Transactional(isolation = org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Isolation.READ_COMMITTED)
49:     fun commit(lease: VerificationItem, proposed: VerificationResult, input: VerificationCommitInput): VerificationItem? {
50:         val now = clock.instant()
51:         val item = repository.lockForCommit(lease.id)
52:         if (item.state != VerificationState.RUNNING || item.leaseToken != lease.leaseToken || item.generation != lease.generation ||
53:             item.leaseUntil?.let { it > now } != true) return null
54:         val current = admissions.findAdmission(item.expertDocId)
55:         val changed = when {
56:             input.snapshot == null -> "SOURCE_MISSING"
57:             input.snapshot.identityHash != item.identityHash -> "IDENTITY_CHANGED"
58:             input.snapshot.factFingerprint != item.factFingerprint -> "FACT_CHANGED"
59:             else -> null
60:         }
61:         val protection = input.protection ?: when {
62:             current?.identityHash == item.identityHash && (current.decisionEnum.manual || current.decision == "LEGACY_APPROVED") -> "REVIEW_PROTECTED"
63:             current?.identityHash == item.identityHash && current.decision == "AUTO_PASSED" && current.policyVersion != DiscoveryVerificationAdmissionGate.POLICY_VERSION -> "LEGACY_AUTO_PROTECTED"
64:             input.snapshot != null && contacted(input.snapshot) -> "CONTACT_PROTECTED"
65:             else -> null
66:         }
67:         fun withheld(code: String, protected: Boolean = false) = proposed.copy(
68:             outcome = if (protected) VerificationOutcome.PROTECTED else VerificationOutcome.PENDING,
69:             qualification = if (protected) VerificationQualification.NOT_EVALUATED else VerificationQualification.UNKNOWN,
70:             route = VerificationRoute.NONE, reasonsJson = mapper.writeValueAsString(listOf(code)),
71:             admissionRevision = null, projectionState = VerificationProjection.NOT_REQUIRED)
72:         var result = when {
73:             changed != null -> withheld(changed)
74:             protection != null -> withheld(protection, true)
75:             current != null && current.identityHash != item.identityHash -> withheld("IDENTITY_CHANGED")
76:             current?.revision != input.admission?.revision || current?.identityHash != input.admission?.identityHash -> withheld("ADMISSION_CHANGED")
77:             else -> proposed
78:         }
79:         var automatic: AutomaticAdmissionResult? = null
80:         val admitted = result.outcome in ADMITTED
81:         if (admitted) {
82:             // Give the existing policy/gate the exact proposed completed row, never a second qualification implementation.
83:             val staged = item.copy(state = result.state, outcome = result.outcome, qualification = result.qualification,
84:                 route = result.route, evidenceJson = result.evidenceJson, reasonsJson = result.reasonsJson,
85:                 apolloPersonId = result.apolloPersonId, personMatchedAt = result.personMatchedAt,
86:                 completedAt = now, leaseToken = null, leaseUntil = null, policyVersion = VerificationMode.ENFORCE.policyVersion)
87:             automatic = policy.evaluate(item.expertDocId, input.snapshot!!.profile,
88:                 VerificationAdmissionContext(item.expertDocId, input.snapshot.profile, listOf(staged)))
89:             if (automatic.status != DiscoveryAdmissionStatus.AUTO_PASSED) {
90:                 result = withheld("HARD_BLOCKED").copy(reasonsJson = mapper.writeValueAsString(automatic.blockingReasons.map { it.code }))
91:             }
92:         }
93:         check(repository.saveEvidenceAndOutcome(item.id, lease.leaseToken!!, lease.generation, result, now))
94:         if (item.policyVersion == VerificationMode.ENFORCE.policyVersion && result.outcome in ADMITTED) {
95:             // Evidence is already visible in this transaction. The shared CAS path must confirm a real row.
96:             val persisted = review.recordAutomaticPersisted(item.expertDocId, input.snapshot!!.profile, requireNotNull(automatic))
97:             repository.attachAdmission(item.id, persisted.revision, now)
98:         }
99:         return repository.findItem(item.id)
100:     }
101: 
102:     /** Projection retries never redo qualification or call providers. Historical admission events stay intact. */
103:     fun project(item: VerificationItem) {
104:         val revision = item.admissionRevision ?: return
105:         if (item.projectionState !in setOf(VerificationProjection.PENDING, VerificationProjection.ERROR) || item.projectionError == "SUPERSEDED_BY_REVIEW") return
106:         fun current(): Boolean = admissions.findAdmission(item.expertDocId)?.let {
107:             it.revision == revision && it.identityHash == item.identityHash && it.decision == "AUTO_PASSED"
108:         } == true
109:         fun mark(state: VerificationProjection, code: String? = null) = repository.markProjection(item.id, revision, state, code, clock.instant())
110:         try {
111:             if (!current()) { mark(VerificationProjection.ERROR, "SUPERSEDED_BY_REVIEW"); return }
112:             val raw = writer.readDiscoveryDocument(ExpertIndexLevel.RAW, item.expertDocId)
113:             if (raw == null) { mark(VerificationProjection.ERROR, "SOURCE_MISSING"); return }
114:             val profile = writer.discoveryProfile(item.expertDocId, raw.source)
115:             val snapshot = DiscoveryVerificationAdmissionGate.snapshot(item.expertDocId, profile, policy.evaluate(item.expertDocId, profile).configSnapshot)
116:             if (snapshot.identityHash != item.identityHash || snapshot.factFingerprint != item.factFingerprint || !current()) {
117:                 mark(VerificationProjection.ERROR, "SUPERSEDED_BY_REVIEW"); return
118:             }
119:             val sync = review.projectApprovedCandidate(item.expertDocId, raw.source, "AUTO_PASSED")
120:             if (!current()) { mark(VerificationProjection.ERROR, "SUPERSEDED_BY_REVIEW"); return }
121:             val state = when (sync.result) {
122:                 "PROJECTED" -> VerificationProjection.PROJECTED
123:                 "ALREADY_PRESENT" -> VerificationProjection.ALREADY_PRESENT
124:                 "APPLICATION_PRESENT" -> VerificationProjection.APPLICATION_PRESENT
125:                 else -> VerificationProjection.ERROR
126:             }
127:             mark(state, if (state == VerificationProjection.ERROR) sync.errorCode ?: sync.result else null)
128:         } catch (_: Exception) { mark(VerificationProjection.ERROR, "CANDIDATE_SYNC_FAILED") }
129:     }
130: 
131:     private fun contacted(snapshot: VerificationSnapshot): Boolean {
132:         val profile = snapshot.profile
133:         val email = DiscoveryIdentity.normalizedEmail(profile.email)
134:         // RAW's primary orcidId is the ES document key, not the bound external identity.
135:         val orcid = profile.externalIds?.let { runCatching { mapper.readTree(it) }.getOrNull() }
136:             ?.path("orcid")?.takeIf { it.isTextual }?.asText()?.let(::normalizeOrcid)
137:         return jdbc.queryForObject("""SELECT (EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM expert_contact WHERE LOWER(TRIM(expert_email))=? OR orcid_id=?)
138:             OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM expert_email_alias WHERE normalized_email=?))""", Boolean::class.java,
139:             email, orcid, email) == true
140:     }
141: 
142:     companion object {
143:         private val ADMITTED = setOf(VerificationOutcome.DIRECT_CLEAR, VerificationOutcome.APOLLO_CLEAR, VerificationOutcome.APOLLO_UNCLEAR)
144:     }
145: }

### src/main/kotlin/com/weibo/talentintroduction/discovery/repository/DiscoveryVerificationRepository.kt
1: package com.weibo.talentintroduction.discovery.repository
2: 
3: import com.weibo.talentintroduction.discovery.domain.*
4: import org.springframework.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate
5: import org.springframework.jdbc.core.ConnectionCallback
6: import org.springframework.jdbc.core.RowMapper
7: import org.springframework.stereotype.Repository
8: import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional
9: import org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronization
10: import org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronizationManager
11: import java.sql.ResultSet
12: import java.time.Instant
13: import java.time.LocalDate
14: import java.time.ZoneOffset
15: import java.util.UUID
16: 
17: /** Database-only seam. Call public transactional methods through the Spring bean.
18:  * Lock order is run -> admission (caller, when needed) -> item -> attempt.
19:  * No HTTP/ES work may be performed while these locks are held.
20:  */
21: @Repository
22: class DiscoveryVerificationRepository(private val jdbc: JdbcTemplate) {
23:     /** Reporting queries deliberately have no locks, writes, or recovery side effects. */
24:     fun reportingCollectionStatuses(ids: List<Long>): Map<Long, String> {
25:         require(ids.isNotEmpty() && ids.size <= 50 && ids.all { it > 0 })
26:         return jdbc.query("SELECT id,status FROM task_execution WHERE id IN (${ids.joinToString(",") { "?" }})",
27:             RowMapper { rs, _ -> rs.getLong("id") to rs.getString("status") }, *ids.toTypedArray()).toMap()
28:     }
29: 
30:     fun reportingRuns(ids: List<Long>): Map<Long, VerificationRun> {
31:         require(ids.isNotEmpty() && ids.size <= 50 && ids.all { it > 0 })
32:         return jdbc.query("SELECT * FROM discovery_verification_run WHERE execution_id IN (${ids.joinToString(",") { "?" }})",
33:             runMapper, *ids.toTypedArray()).associateBy { it.executionId }
34:     }
35: 
36:     fun reportingCounts(filter: VerificationReportFilter, batches: Boolean = false, executionIds: List<Long>? = null): Map<Long, VerificationCounts> {
37:         require(executionIds == null || executionIds.isNotEmpty() && executionIds.size <= 50 && executionIds.all { it > 0 })
38:         val (where, args) = if (executionIds == null) reportingWhere(filter) else
39:             "i.owner_execution_id IN (${executionIds.joinToString(",") { "?" }})" to executionIds
40:         fun sum(condition: String, alias: String) = "COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN $condition THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),0) AS $alias"
41:         val admitted = "i.activated_at IS NOT NULL AND r.mode='ENFORCE' AND i.admission_revision IS NOT NULL"
42:         val active = "i.activated_at IS NOT NULL"
43:         val columns = listOf(
44:             sum(active, "profiles"),
45:             sum("$admitted AND i.outcome IN ('DIRECT_CLEAR','APOLLO_CLEAR','APOLLO_UNCLEAR')", "autoAdmitted"),
46:             sum("$admitted AND i.outcome='DIRECT_CLEAR'", "directAdmitted"),
47:             sum("$admitted AND i.outcome IN ('APOLLO_CLEAR','APOLLO_UNCLEAR')", "apolloAdmitted"),
48:             sum("$active AND i.outcome IN ('DIRECT_CLEAR','APOLLO_CLEAR')", "clear"),
49:             sum("$active AND i.outcome='APOLLO_UNCLEAR'", "unclear"),
50:             sum("$active AND i.outcome='PENDING'", "pending"),
51:             sum("$active AND i.state='ERROR'", "errors"),
52:             sum("$active AND i.state IN ('QUEUED','RUNNING','RETRY_WAIT')", "queued"),
53:             sum("$active AND i.outcome='PROTECTED'", "protected"),
54:             sum("$active AND i.projection_state='PENDING'", "projectionPending"),
55:             sum("$active AND i.projection_state='ERROR'", "projectionErrors"),
56:             sum("$active AND i.apollo_entered_at IS NOT NULL", "apolloEntered"),
57:             sum("$active AND i.search_completed_at IS NOT NULL", "apolloSearched"),
58:             sum("$active AND i.candidate_found_at IS NOT NULL", "apolloCandidates"),
59:             sum("$active AND i.person_matched_at IS NOT NULL", "apolloMatched"),
60:             """COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN $active THEN
61:                 (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM discovery_verification_attempt a WHERE a.item_id=i.id AND a.provider='APOLLO')
62:                 ELSE 0 END),0) AS apolloHttpAttempts""",
63:             sum("i.activated_at IS NULL AND i.state='ERROR'", "registrationErrors")
64:         ).joinToString(",")
65:         val group = when { batches -> "i.batch_number"; executionIds != null -> "i.owner_execution_id"; else -> "0" }
66:         val grouping = if (batches || executionIds != null) "GROUP BY $group ORDER BY $group" else ""
67:         return jdbc.query("""SELECT $group AS batch,$columns FROM discovery_verification_item i
68:             JOIN discovery_verification_run r ON r.execution_id=i.owner_execution_id WHERE $where
69:             ${if (batches) "AND i.activated_at IS NOT NULL" else ""} $grouping""",
70:             RowMapper { rs, _ -> rs.getLong("batch") to VerificationCounts(
71:                 rs.getLong("profiles"), rs.getLong("autoAdmitted"), rs.getLong("directAdmitted"),
72:                 rs.getLong("apolloAdmitted"), rs.getLong("clear"), rs.getLong("unclear"), rs.getLong("pending"),
73:                 rs.getLong("errors"), rs.getLong("queued"), rs.getLong("protected"), rs.getLong("projectionPending"),
74:                 rs.getLong("projectionErrors"), rs.getLong("apolloEntered"), rs.getLong("apolloSearched"),
75:                 rs.getLong("apolloCandidates"), rs.getLong("apolloMatched"), rs.getLong("apolloHttpAttempts"),
76:                 rs.getLong("registrationErrors"))
77:             }, *args.toTypedArray()).toMap()
78:     }
79: 
80:     fun reportingSuggestions(filter: VerificationReportFilter, batches: Boolean = false,
81:         executionIds: List<Long>? = null): Map<Long, Triple<Long, Long, Long>> {
82:         require(executionIds == null || executionIds.isNotEmpty() && executionIds.size <= 50 && executionIds.all { it > 0 })
83:         val (where, args) = if (executionIds == null) reportingWhere(filter) else
84:             "i.owner_execution_id IN (${executionIds.joinToString(",") { "?" }})" to executionIds
85:         val group = when { batches -> "i.batch_number"; executionIds != null -> "i.owner_execution_id"; else -> "0" }
86:         return jdbc.query("""SELECT $group AS bucket,
87:             COALESCE(SUM(i.outcome IN ('DIRECT_CLEAR','APOLLO_CLEAR','APOLLO_UNCLEAR')),0) AS admitted,
88:             COALESCE(SUM(i.outcome='DIRECT_CLEAR'),0) AS direct,
89:             COALESCE(SUM(i.outcome IN ('APOLLO_CLEAR','APOLLO_UNCLEAR')),0) AS apollo
90:             FROM discovery_verification_item i JOIN discovery_verification_run r ON r.execution_id=i.owner_execution_id
91:             WHERE $where AND i.activated_at IS NOT NULL AND r.mode='AUDIT'
92:             ${if (batches || executionIds != null) "GROUP BY $group ORDER BY $group" else ""}""",
93:             RowMapper { rs, _ -> rs.getLong("bucket") to Triple(rs.getLong("admitted"), rs.getLong("direct"), rs.getLong("apollo")) },
94:             *args.toTypedArray()).toMap()
95:     }
96: 
97:     /** Hash the complete reason set: grouping TEXT directly can truncate comparisons at MySQL max_sort_length. */
98:     fun reportingReasons(filter: VerificationReportFilter): List<Pair<String, Long>> {
99:         val (where, args) = reportingWhere(filter)
100:         return jdbc.query("""SELECT MAX(i.reasons_json) AS reasons_json,COUNT(*) AS n FROM discovery_verification_item i
101:             WHERE $where AND i.activated_at IS NOT NULL AND i.reasons_json IS NOT NULL GROUP BY SHA2(i.reasons_json,256)""",
102:             RowMapper { rs, _ -> rs.getString("reasons_json") to rs.getLong("n") }, *args.toTypedArray())
103:     }
104: 
105:     fun reportingBatchTimes(executionId: Long): Map<Int, Instant> =
106:         jdbc.query("""SELECT batch_number,MAX(updated_at) AS updated_at FROM discovery_verification_item
107:             WHERE owner_execution_id=? AND activated_at IS NOT NULL GROUP BY batch_number ORDER BY batch_number""",
108:             RowMapper { rs, _ -> rs.getInt("batch_number") to rs.time("updated_at")!! }, executionId).toMap()
109: 
110:     fun reportingBatchReasons(executionId: Long): List<Triple<Int, String, Long>> =
111:         jdbc.query("""SELECT batch_number,MAX(reasons_json) AS reasons_json,COUNT(*) AS n
112:             FROM discovery_verification_item WHERE owner_execution_id=? AND activated_at IS NOT NULL
113:             AND reasons_json IS NOT NULL GROUP BY batch_number,SHA2(reasons_json,256) ORDER BY batch_number""",
114:             RowMapper { rs, _ -> Triple(rs.getInt("batch_number"), rs.getString("reasons_json"), rs.getLong("n")) }, executionId)
115: 
116:     fun reportingRecordTotal(filter: VerificationReportFilter): Long {
117:         val (where, args) = reportingWhere(filter)
118:         return jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM discovery_verification_item i WHERE $where AND i.activated_at IS NOT NULL",
119:             Long::class.java, *args.toTypedArray())!!
120:     }
121: 
122:     fun reportingRecords(filter: VerificationReportFilter, page: Int, size: Int): List<VerificationItem> {
123:         require(page >= 0 && size in 1..100)
124:         val (where, args) = reportingWhere(filter)
125:         // Prevent ORDER BY id/LIMIT from choosing an unrelated-owner PRIMARY scan on large runs.
126:         return jdbc.query("""SELECT i.* FROM discovery_verification_item i FORCE INDEX (idx_dvi_run_batch) WHERE $where AND i.activated_at IS NOT NULL
127:             ORDER BY i.id LIMIT ? OFFSET ?""", itemMapper, *(args + listOf(size, page.toLong() * size)).toTypedArray())
128:     }
129: 
130:     private fun reportingWhere(filter: VerificationReportFilter): Pair<String, List<Any>> {
131:         val clauses = mutableListOf("i.owner_execution_id=?")
132:         val args = mutableListOf<Any>(filter.executionId)
133:         when (filter.route) {
134:             "APOLLO" -> clauses += "i.apollo_entered_at IS NOT NULL"
135:             "DIRECT" -> { clauses += "i.route=?"; args += filter.route }
136:         }
137:         when (filter.result) {
138:             "CLEAR" -> clauses += "i.outcome IN ('DIRECT_CLEAR','APOLLO_CLEAR')"
139:             "UNCLEAR" -> clauses += "i.outcome='APOLLO_UNCLEAR'"
140:             "PENDING" -> clauses += "i.outcome='PENDING'"
141:             "ERROR" -> clauses += "i.state='ERROR'"
142:             "QUEUED" -> clauses += "i.state IN ('QUEUED','RUNNING','RETRY_WAIT')"
143:             "PROTECTED" -> clauses += "i.outcome='PROTECTED'"
144:         }
145:         filter.batchNumber?.let { clauses += "i.batch_number=?"; args += it }
146:         if (filter.q.isNotEmpty()) {
147:             // Use ! as SQL escape, escaping literal !, %, _, and backslash explicitly.
148:             val q = "%" + filter.q.replace("!", "!!").replace("\\", "!\\").replace("%", "!%").replace("_", "!_") + "%"
149:             clauses += """(i.expert_doc_id LIKE ? ESCAPE '!' OR
150:                 CONCAT_WS(' ',JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(i.snapshot_json,'$.profile.givenNames')),
151:                     JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(i.snapshot_json,'$.profile.familyNames'))) LIKE ? ESCAPE '!' OR
152:                 JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(i.snapshot_json,'$.profile.email')) LIKE ? ESCAPE '!' OR
153:                 JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(i.snapshot_json,'$.profile.institution')) LIKE ? ESCAPE '!')"""
154:             repeat(4) { args += q }
155:         }
156:         return clauses.joinToString(" AND ") to args
157:     }
158: 
159:     @Transactional
160:     fun createRun(executionId: Long, mode: VerificationMode, apolloRequested: Boolean, settingsJson: String, now: Instant): VerificationRun {
161:         require(executionId > 0 && settingsJson.isNotBlank())
162:         jdbc.update("INSERT IGNORE INTO discovery_verification_run (execution_id,mode,policy_version,apollo_requested,settings_json,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
163:             executionId, mode.name, mode.policyVersion, apolloRequested, settingsJson, now.verificationUtc(), now.verificationUtc())
164:         val run = lockRun(executionId)
165:         require(run.mode == mode && run.apolloRequested == apolloRequested && run.settingsJson == settingsJson) { "Run snapshot is immutable" }
166:         return run
167:     }
168: 
169:     @Transactional
170:     fun reserveItem(input: VerificationReservation, now: Instant): VerificationItem {
171:         // Read duplicate identity without locks first: never lock two runs in one transaction.
172:         val existing = identity(input)
173:         if (existing != null) return checkedIdentity(existing, input)
174:         val run = lockRun(input.ownerExecutionId)
175:         require(run.policyVersion == input.policyVersion && !run.inputClosed)
176:         val inserted = jdbc.update("""INSERT IGNORE INTO discovery_verification_item
177:             (owner_execution_id,expert_doc_id,identity_hash,policy_version,source_name,source_item_key,batch_number,snapshot_json,fact_fingerprint,created_at,updated_at)
178:             VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,0,?,?,?,?)""", input.ownerExecutionId, input.expertDocId, input.identityHash,
179:             input.policyVersion, input.sourceName, input.sourceItemKey, input.snapshotJson, input.factFingerprint,
180:             now.verificationUtc(), now.verificationUtc())
181:         val item = jdbc.query("SELECT * FROM discovery_verification_item WHERE expert_doc_id=? AND identity_hash=? AND policy_version=? FOR UPDATE", itemMapper,
182:             input.expertDocId, input.identityHash, input.policyVersion).single()
183:         checkedIdentity(item, input)
184:         if (inserted == 1) bump(input.ownerExecutionId, now)
185:         return item
186:     }
187: 
188:     @Transactional
189:     fun activateItem(itemId: Long, batchNumber: Int, now: Instant): VerificationItem {
190:         require(batchNumber > 0)
191:         val item = lockItem(itemId)
192:         if (item.state == VerificationState.RESERVED) {
193:             jdbc.update("UPDATE discovery_verification_item SET state='QUEUED',batch_number=?,activated_at=?,updated_at=? WHERE id=? AND state='RESERVED'",
194:                 batchNumber, now.verificationUtc(), now.verificationUtc(), itemId)
195:             bump(item.ownerExecutionId, now)
196:         }
197:         return findItem(itemId)!!
198:     }
199: 
200:     @Transactional
201:     fun closeInput(executionId: Long, now: Instant): Boolean {
202:         val run = lockRun(executionId)
203:         if (run.inputClosed) return false
204:         jdbc.update("UPDATE discovery_verification_run SET input_closed=1,revision=revision+1,updated_at=? WHERE execution_id=?", now.verificationUtc(), executionId)
205:         return true
206:     }
207: 
208:     fun findRun(executionId: Long): VerificationRun? {
209:         require(executionId > 0)
210:         return jdbc.query("SELECT * FROM discovery_verification_run WHERE execution_id=?", runMapper, executionId).singleOrNull()
211:     }
212:     fun findItem(itemId: Long): VerificationItem? {
213:         require(itemId > 0)
214:         return jdbc.query("SELECT * FROM discovery_verification_item WHERE id=?", itemMapper, itemId).singleOrNull()
215:     }
216:     fun findManagedContexts(docIds: Collection<String>): Map<String, VerificationContext> {
217:         require(docIds.size <= 500)
218:         docIds.forEach { verificationText(it, 128) }
219:         if (docIds.isEmpty()) return emptyMap()
220:         val result = docIds.associateWith { VerificationContext.UNMANAGED }.toMutableMap()
221:         jdbc.query("SELECT DISTINCT expert_doc_id,policy_version FROM discovery_verification_item WHERE expert_doc_id IN (${docIds.joinToString(",") { "?" }})",
222:             RowMapper { rs, _ -> rs.getString(1) to rs.getString(2) }, *docIds.toTypedArray()).forEach { (doc, policy) ->
223:             val context = when (policy) {
224:                 VerificationMode.ENFORCE.policyVersion -> VerificationContext.MANAGED_ENFORCE
225:                 VerificationMode.AUDIT.policyVersion -> VerificationContext.MANAGED_AUDIT
226:                 else -> throw IllegalStateException("Unknown verification policy")
227:             }
228:             if (result[doc] != VerificationContext.MANAGED_ENFORCE) result[doc] = context
229:         }
230:         // Database failures propagate, never become UNMANAGED.
231:         return result
232:     }
233: 
234:     /** One evidence read for a bounded batch of real ES document IDs, including old identities. */
235:     fun findItemsByDocIds(docIds: Collection<String>): List<VerificationItem> {
236:         require(docIds.size <= 500)
237:         docIds.forEach { verificationText(it, 128) }
238:         if (docIds.isEmpty()) return emptyList()
239:         return jdbc.query(
240:             "SELECT * FROM discovery_verification_item WHERE expert_doc_id IN (${docIds.joinToString(",") { "?" }}) ORDER BY id",
241:             itemMapper, *docIds.toTypedArray()
242:         )
243:     }
244: 
245:     @Transactional
246:     fun claimDue(now: Instant, leaseSeconds: Long): VerificationItem? {
247:         require(leaseSeconds > 0)
248:         val candidates = jdbc.query("""SELECT * FROM discovery_verification_item WHERE
249:             state='QUEUED' OR (state='RETRY_WAIT' AND next_attempt_at<=?) OR (state='RUNNING' AND lease_until<=?) ORDER BY id LIMIT 100""",
250:             itemMapper, now.verificationUtc(), now.verificationUtc())
251:         // One run per transaction, even when all of its candidates lost a race.
252:         val owner = candidates.firstOrNull()?.ownerExecutionId ?: return null
253:         lockRun(owner)
254:         for (candidate in candidates.filter { it.ownerExecutionId == owner }) {
255:             val item = lockedItem(candidate.id)
256:             if (!(item.state == VerificationState.QUEUED ||
257:                     item.state == VerificationState.RETRY_WAIT && item.nextAttemptAt?.let { it <= now } == true ||
258:                     item.state == VerificationState.RUNNING && item.leaseUntil?.let { it <= now } == true)) continue
259:             jdbc.update("UPDATE discovery_verification_item SET state='RUNNING',lease_token=?,generation=generation+1,lease_until=?,next_attempt_at=NULL,updated_at=? WHERE id=?",
260:                 UUID.randomUUID().toString(), now.plusSeconds(leaseSeconds).verificationUtc(), now.verificationUtc(), item.id)
261:             bump(owner, now)
262:             return findItem(item.id)
263:         }
264:         return null
265:     }
266: 
267:     @Transactional
268:     fun renewLease(itemId: Long, token: String, generation: Long, now: Instant, leaseSeconds: Long): Boolean {
269:         require(leaseSeconds > 0)
270:         val item = lockItem(itemId)
271:         if (!owns(item, token, generation, now)) return false
272:         jdbc.update("UPDATE discovery_verification_item SET lease_until=?,updated_at=? WHERE id=? AND lease_token=? AND generation=? AND state='RUNNING'",
273:             now.plusSeconds(leaseSeconds).verificationUtc(), now.verificationUtc(), itemId, token, generation)
274:         bump(item.ownerExecutionId, now)
275:         return true
276:     }
277: 
278:     @Transactional
279:     fun reserveAttemptWithBudget(itemId: Long, token: String, generation: Long, request: VerificationAttemptRequest,
280:                                  requestsPerMinute: Int, requestsPerDay: Int, maxRequestsPerItem: Int, now: Instant): VerificationAttemptReservation {
281:         require(requestsPerMinute > 0 && requestsPerDay > 0 && maxRequestsPerItem > 0)
282:         val item = lockItem(itemId)
283:         if (!owns(item, token, generation, now)) return VerificationAttemptReservation(deferred = VerificationBudgetReason.LEASE_LOST)
284:         // Lock is retained through COMMIT, not merely through INSERT. Same bound JDBC connection.
285:         if (request.provider == VerificationProvider.APOLLO) {
286:             val acquired = jdbc.execute(ConnectionCallback<Boolean> { connection ->
287:                 connection.prepareStatement("SELECT GET_LOCK('discovery_verification_apollo_budget',1)").use { statement ->
288:                     statement.executeQuery().use { rs -> rs.next(); rs.getInt(1) == 1 }
289:                 }.also { acquired ->
290:                     if (acquired) TransactionSynchronizationManager.registerSynchronization(object : TransactionSynchronization {
291:                         override fun afterCompletion(status: Int) {
292:                             connection.prepareStatement("SELECT RELEASE_LOCK('discovery_verification_apollo_budget')").use { it.execute() }
293:                         }
294:                     })
295:                 }
296:             }) == true
297:             if (!acquired) return VerificationAttemptReservation(deferred = VerificationBudgetReason.LOCK_BUSY)
298:         }
299:         val duplicate = jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM discovery_verification_attempt WHERE request_key=? FOR UPDATE", Long::class.java, request.requestKey)!!
300:         if (duplicate > 0) return VerificationAttemptReservation(deferred = VerificationBudgetReason.DUPLICATE_REQUEST)
301:         if (item.requestCount >= maxRequestsPerItem) return VerificationAttemptReservation(deferred = VerificationBudgetReason.ITEM_LIMIT)
302:         if (request.provider == VerificationProvider.APOLLO) {
303:             // Current reads are required even inside an outer REPEATABLE_READ transaction:
304:             // acquiring a named lock does not refresh an earlier MVCC snapshot.
305:             val start = LocalDate.ofInstant(now, ZoneOffset.UTC).atStartOfDay()
306:             val day = jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM discovery_verification_attempt WHERE provider='APOLLO' AND requested_at>=? AND requested_at<? FOR UPDATE",
307:                 Long::class.java, start, start.plusDays(1))!!
308:             if (day >= requestsPerDay) return VerificationAttemptReservation(deferred = VerificationBudgetReason.DAY_LIMIT)
309:             val minute = jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM discovery_verification_attempt WHERE provider='APOLLO' AND requested_at>? AND requested_at<=? FOR UPDATE",
310:                 Long::class.java, now.minusSeconds(60).verificationUtc(), now.verificationUtc())!!
311:             if (minute >= requestsPerMinute) return VerificationAttemptReservation(deferred = VerificationBudgetReason.MINUTE_LIMIT)
312:         }
313:         jdbc.update("""INSERT INTO discovery_verification_attempt (item_id,request_key,provider,query_kind,page_number,attempt_number,requested_at,result_code,request_json)
314:             VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,'DISPATCH_RESERVED',?)""", itemId, request.requestKey, request.provider.name, request.queryKind.name,
315:             request.pageNumber, request.attemptNumber, now.verificationUtc(), request.requestJson)
316:         val id = jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT id FROM discovery_verification_attempt WHERE request_key=?", Long::class.java, request.requestKey)!!
317:         jdbc.update("UPDATE discovery_verification_item SET request_count=request_count+1,updated_at=? WHERE id=?", now.verificationUtc(), itemId)
318:         bump(item.ownerExecutionId, now)
319:         return VerificationAttemptReservation(attemptId = id)
320:     }
321: 
322:     @Transactional
323:     fun completeAttempt(itemId: Long, token: String, generation: Long, attemptId: Long, httpStatus: Int?, resultCode: String,
324:                         responseJson: String?, responseSha256: String?, now: Instant): Boolean {
325:         verificationText(resultCode, 64)
326:         require(httpStatus == null || httpStatus in 100..599)
327:         responseSha256?.let { verificationHash(it) }
328:         val item = lockItem(itemId)
329:         if (!owns(item, token, generation, now)) return false
330:         val changed = jdbc.update("""UPDATE discovery_verification_attempt SET completed_at=?,http_status=?,result_code=?,response_json=?,response_sha256=?
331:             WHERE id=? AND item_id=? AND completed_at IS NULL""", now.verificationUtc(), httpStatus, resultCode, responseJson, responseSha256, attemptId, itemId)
332:         if (changed == 1) bump(item.ownerExecutionId, now)
333:         return changed == 1
334:     }
335: 
336:     @Transactional
337:     fun saveEvidenceAndOutcome(itemId: Long, token: String, generation: Long, result: VerificationResult, now: Instant): Boolean {
338:         val item = lockItem(itemId)
339:         if (!owns(item, token, generation, now)) return false
340:         if (result.admissionRevision != null) require(item.policyVersion == VerificationMode.ENFORCE.policyVersion)
341:         val changed = jdbc.update("""UPDATE discovery_verification_item SET state=?,outcome=?,qualification=?,route=?,evidence_json=?,reasons_json=?,
342:             apollo_entered_at=COALESCE(apollo_entered_at,?),search_completed_at=COALESCE(search_completed_at,?),candidate_found_at=COALESCE(candidate_found_at,?),
343:             person_matched_at=COALESCE(person_matched_at,?),apollo_person_id=?,admission_revision=?,projection_state=?,completed_at=?,updated_at=?,lease_token=NULL,lease_until=NULL
344:             WHERE id=? AND lease_token=? AND generation=? AND state='RUNNING'""", result.state.name, result.outcome?.name, result.qualification.name,
345:             result.route.name, result.evidenceJson, result.reasonsJson, result.apolloEnteredAt?.verificationUtc(), result.searchCompletedAt?.verificationUtc(),
346:             result.candidateFoundAt?.verificationUtc(), result.personMatchedAt?.verificationUtc(), result.apolloPersonId, result.admissionRevision,
347:             result.projectionState.name, now.verificationUtc(), now.verificationUtc(), itemId, token, generation)
348:         if (changed == 1) bump(item.ownerExecutionId, now)
349:         return changed == 1
350:     }
351: 
352:     @Transactional
353:     fun saveFailure(itemId: Long, token: String, generation: Long, result: VerificationResult,
354:         transientAttempts: Int, now: Instant): Boolean {
355:         require(result.state == VerificationState.ERROR)
356:         val item = lockItem(itemId)
357:         if (!owns(item, token, generation, now)) return false
358:         require(transientAttempts >= item.transientAttempts)
359:         jdbc.update("UPDATE discovery_verification_item SET transient_attempts=? WHERE id=? AND lease_token=? AND generation=? AND state='RUNNING'",
360:             transientAttempts, itemId, token, generation)
361:         check(saveEvidenceAndOutcome(itemId, token, generation, result, now))
362:         return true
363:     }
364: 
365:     @Transactional
366:     fun markProjection(itemId: Long, admissionRevision: Long, projection: VerificationProjection, error: String?, now: Instant): Boolean {
367:         require(admissionRevision >= 0)
368:         require(error == null || error.length <= 128)
369:         require(projection != VerificationProjection.NOT_REQUIRED)
370:         val item = lockItem(itemId)
371:         if (item.admissionRevision != admissionRevision || item.state != VerificationState.COMPLETED) return false
372:         if (item.projectionState in setOf(VerificationProjection.PROJECTED, VerificationProjection.ALREADY_PRESENT, VerificationProjection.APPLICATION_PRESENT)) return false
373:         jdbc.update("UPDATE discovery_verification_item SET projection_state=?,projection_error=?,updated_at=? WHERE id=?", projection.name, error, now.verificationUtc(), itemId)
374:         bump(item.ownerExecutionId, now)
375:         return true
376:     }
377: 
378:     @Transactional
379:     fun reschedule(itemId: Long, token: String, generation: Long, nextAttemptAt: Instant, transientAttempts: Int,
380:                    evidenceJson: String, reasonsJson: String, now: Instant): Boolean {
381:         require(nextAttemptAt >= now && transientAttempts >= 0)
382:         val item = lockItem(itemId)
383:         if (!owns(item, token, generation, now)) return false
384:         require(transientAttempts >= item.transientAttempts)
385:         jdbc.update("""UPDATE discovery_verification_item SET state='RETRY_WAIT',next_attempt_at=?,transient_attempts=?,evidence_json=?,reasons_json=?,
386:             lease_token=NULL,lease_until=NULL,updated_at=? WHERE id=? AND lease_token=? AND generation=? AND state='RUNNING'""",
387:             nextAttemptAt.verificationUtc(), transientAttempts, evidenceJson, reasonsJson, now.verificationUtc(), itemId, token, generation)
388:         bump(item.ownerExecutionId, now)
389:         return true
390:     }
391: 
392:     fun listReserved(limit: Int): List<VerificationItem> {
393:         require(limit in 1..500)
394:         return jdbc.query("SELECT * FROM discovery_verification_item WHERE state='RESERVED' ORDER BY id LIMIT ?", itemMapper, limit)
395:     }
396: 
397:     /** Recovery may terminate an unactivated registration only after its owner's input is closed. */
398:     @Transactional
399:     fun failReserved(itemId: Long, reasonsJson: String, now: Instant): Boolean {
400:         val item = lockItem(itemId)
401:         if (item.state != VerificationState.RESERVED || !findRun(item.ownerExecutionId)!!.inputClosed) return false
402:         jdbc.update("UPDATE discovery_verification_item SET state='ERROR',reasons_json=?,completed_at=?,updated_at=? WHERE id=?", reasonsJson, now.verificationUtc(), now.verificationUtc(), itemId)
403:         bump(item.ownerExecutionId, now)
404:         return true
405:     }
406: 
407: 
408:     /** Called by the separate commit bean: no ES/HTTP while the fixed SQL lock order is held. */
409:     @Transactional(propagation = org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Propagation.MANDATORY)
410:     fun lockForCommit(itemId: Long): VerificationItem {
411:         val item = findItem(itemId) ?: error("Verification item not found")
412:         lockRun(item.ownerExecutionId)
413:         jdbc.query("SELECT expert_doc_id FROM expert_discovery_admission WHERE expert_doc_id=? FOR UPDATE",
414:             RowMapper { rs, _ -> rs.getString(1) }, item.expertDocId)
415:         return lockedItem(itemId)
416:     }
417: 
418:     @Transactional
419:     fun saveProgress(itemId: Long, token: String, generation: Long, evidenceJson: String,
420:         enteredAt: Instant?, searchedAt: Instant?, candidatesAt: Instant?, now: Instant): Boolean {
421:         val item = lockItem(itemId)
422:         if (!owns(item, token, generation, now)) return false
423:         jdbc.update("""UPDATE discovery_verification_item SET evidence_json=?,apollo_entered_at=COALESCE(apollo_entered_at,?),
424:             search_completed_at=COALESCE(search_completed_at,?),candidate_found_at=COALESCE(candidate_found_at,?),updated_at=?
425:             WHERE id=? AND lease_token=? AND generation=? AND state='RUNNING'""",
426:             evidenceJson, enteredAt?.verificationUtc(), searchedAt?.verificationUtc(), candidatesAt?.verificationUtc(),
427:             now.verificationUtc(), itemId, token, generation)
428:         bump(item.ownerExecutionId, now)
429:         return true
430:     }
431: 
432:     /** Completion and cursor are one commit, acknowledged before any subsequent wire request. */
433:     @Transactional
434:     fun completeAttemptAndProgress(itemId: Long, token: String, generation: Long, attemptId: Long,
435:         httpStatus: Int?, resultCode: String, responseJson: String?, responseSha256: String?,
436:         evidenceJson: String, enteredAt: Instant?, searchedAt: Instant?, candidatesAt: Instant?, now: Instant): Boolean {
437:         if (!completeAttempt(itemId, token, generation, attemptId, httpStatus, resultCode, responseJson, responseSha256, now)) return false
438:         check(saveProgress(itemId, token, generation, evidenceJson, enteredAt, searchedAt, candidatesAt, now))
439:         return true
440:     }
441: 
442:     @Transactional(propagation = org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Propagation.MANDATORY)
443:     fun attachAdmission(itemId: Long, revision: Long, now: Instant) {
444:         require(revision >= 0)
445:         check(jdbc.update("""UPDATE discovery_verification_item SET admission_revision=?,projection_state='PENDING',updated_at=?
446:             WHERE id=? AND state='COMPLETED' AND admission_revision IS NULL
447:             AND policy_version=? AND outcome IN ('DIRECT_CLEAR','APOLLO_CLEAR','APOLLO_UNCLEAR')""",
448:             revision, now.verificationUtc(), itemId, VerificationMode.ENFORCE.policyVersion) == 1)
449:         bump(findItem(itemId)!!.ownerExecutionId, now)
450:     }
451: 
452:     fun listProjectionDue(limit: Int): List<VerificationItem> {
453:         require(limit in 1..100)
454:         return jdbc.query("""SELECT * FROM discovery_verification_item WHERE state='COMPLETED' AND admission_revision IS NOT NULL
455:             AND projection_state IN ('PENDING','ERROR') AND (projection_error IS NULL OR projection_error<>'SUPERSEDED_BY_REVIEW')
456:             ORDER BY updated_at,id LIMIT ?""", itemMapper, limit)
457:     }
458: 
459:     fun findAttempts(itemId: Long): List<VerificationAttempt> = jdbc.query(
460:         "SELECT * FROM discovery_verification_attempt WHERE item_id=? ORDER BY id",
461:         RowMapper { rs, _ -> VerificationAttempt(rs.getLong("id"), rs.getLong("item_id"), rs.getString("request_key"),
462:             VerificationProvider.valueOf(rs.getString("provider")), VerificationQueryKind.valueOf(rs.getString("query_kind")),
463:             rs.getInt("page_number"), rs.getInt("attempt_number"), rs.time("requested_at")!!, rs.time("completed_at"),
464:             rs.getInt("http_status").let { if (rs.wasNull()) null else it }, rs.getString("result_code"),
465:             rs.getString("request_json"), rs.getString("response_json"), rs.getString("response_sha256")) }, itemId)
466:     private fun identity(input: VerificationReservation) = jdbc.query("SELECT * FROM discovery_verification_item WHERE expert_doc_id=? AND identity_hash=? AND policy_version=?",
467:         itemMapper, input.expertDocId, input.identityHash, input.policyVersion).singleOrNull()
468:     private fun checkedIdentity(item: VerificationItem, input: VerificationReservation): VerificationItem {
469:         check(item.expertDocId == input.expertDocId && item.identityHash == input.identityHash && item.policyVersion == input.policyVersion) { "Identity collision" }
470:         return item
471:     }
472:     private fun lockRun(id: Long): VerificationRun {
473:         require(id > 0)
474:         return jdbc.query("SELECT * FROM discovery_verification_run WHERE execution_id=? FOR UPDATE", runMapper, id).singleOrNull()
475:             ?: throw IllegalStateException("Verification run not found")
476:     }
477:     private fun lockItem(id: Long): VerificationItem {
478:         val item = findItem(id) ?: throw IllegalArgumentException("Verification item not found")
479:         lockRun(item.ownerExecutionId)
480:         return lockedItem(id)
481:     }
482:     private fun lockedItem(id: Long) = jdbc.query("SELECT * FROM discovery_verification_item WHERE id=? FOR UPDATE", itemMapper, id).single()
483:     private fun owns(item: VerificationItem, token: String, generation: Long, now: Instant): Boolean =
484:         item.state == VerificationState.RUNNING && item.leaseToken == token && item.generation == generation && item.leaseUntil?.let { it > now } == true
485:     private fun bump(owner: Long, now: Instant) {
486:         jdbc.update("UPDATE discovery_verification_run SET revision=revision+1,updated_at=? WHERE execution_id=?", now.verificationUtc(), owner)
487:     }
488: 
489:     companion object {
490:         private fun ResultSet.time(name: String): Instant? = getTimestamp(name)?.toLocalDateTime()?.verificationInstant()
491:         private fun ResultSet.nullLong(name: String): Long? = getLong(name).let { if (wasNull()) null else it }
492:         private val runMapper = RowMapper { rs: ResultSet, _: Int -> VerificationRun(rs.getLong("execution_id"), VerificationMode.valueOf(rs.getString("mode")),
493:             rs.getString("policy_version"), rs.getBoolean("apollo_requested"), rs.getString("settings_json"), rs.getBoolean("input_closed"), rs.getLong("revision"), rs.time("created_at")!!, rs.time("updated_at")!!) }
494:         private val itemMapper = RowMapper { rs: ResultSet, _: Int -> VerificationItem(
495:             rs.getLong("id"), rs.getLong("owner_execution_id"), rs.getString("expert_doc_id"), rs.getString("identity_hash"), rs.getString("policy_version"),
496:             rs.getString("source_name"), rs.getString("source_item_key"), rs.getInt("batch_number"), rs.getString("snapshot_json"), rs.getString("fact_fingerprint"),
497:             VerificationState.valueOf(rs.getString("state")), rs.getString("outcome")?.let(VerificationOutcome::valueOf), VerificationQualification.valueOf(rs.getString("qualification")),
498:             VerificationRoute.valueOf(rs.getString("route")), rs.time("apollo_entered_at"), rs.time("search_completed_at"), rs.time("candidate_found_at"), rs.time("person_matched_at"),
499:             rs.getString("apollo_person_id"), rs.getString("evidence_json"), rs.getString("reasons_json"), rs.getInt("request_count"), rs.getInt("transient_attempts"), rs.getLong("generation"),
500:             rs.getString("lease_token"), rs.time("lease_until"), rs.time("next_attempt_at"), rs.nullLong("admission_revision"), VerificationProjection.valueOf(rs.getString("projection_state")),
501:             rs.getString("projection_error"), rs.time("activated_at"), rs.time("completed_at"), rs.time("created_at")!!, rs.time("updated_at")!!) }
502:     }
503: }
504: 
505: data class VerificationReportFilter(
506:     val executionId: Long, val route: String = "ALL", val result: String = "ALL",
507:     val q: String = "", val batchNumber: Int? = null
508: ) {
509:     init {
510:         require(executionId > 0)
511:         require(route in setOf("ALL", "DIRECT", "APOLLO"))
512:         require(result in setOf("ALL", "CLEAR", "UNCLEAR", "PENDING", "ERROR", "QUEUED", "PROTECTED"))
513:         require(q.length <= 100 && (batchNumber == null || batchNumber > 0))
514:     }
515: }

### src/main/kotlin/com/weibo/talentintroduction/expert/domain/DiscoveryIdentity.kt
1: package com.weibo.talentintroduction.expert.domain
2: 
3: import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode
4: import com.fasterxml.jackson.module.kotlin.jacksonObjectMapper
5: import com.weibo.talentintroduction.discovery.domain.AdmissionReason
6: import com.weibo.talentintroduction.discovery.domain.AdmissionReasonCodes
7: import java.security.MessageDigest
8: import java.util.Locale
