10: /** A proof is bound to the identity it verified, never to a mutable business document ID. */
11: data class IdentityVerification(
12:     val status: String = "UNRESOLVED",
13:     val version: Int = 0,
14:     val email: String? = null,
15:     val givenNames: String? = null,
16:     val familyNames: String? = null,
17:     val source: String? = null,
18:     val evidenceHash: String? = null,
19:     val orcid: String? = null,
20:     val openAlexAuthorId: String? = null
21: )
22: 
23: object DiscoveryIdentity {
24:     const val VERSION = 20260925
25:     /** Cached extractor output compatibility; independent of persisted proof versions. */
26:     const val EXTRACTION_VERSION = 20261004
27:     private val mapper = jacksonObjectMapper()
28:     private val sources = listOf("PAPER_FULLTEXT", "ORCID_PUBLIC")
29:     fun normalizedEmail(email: String?) = email.orEmpty().trim().lowercase(Locale.ROOT)
30:     fun hash(text: String): String = MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256").digest(text.toByteArray(Charsets.UTF_8))
31:         .joinToString("") { "%02x".format(it) }
32:     fun isDiscovery(profile: ExpertProfile) = profile.identityVerification != null ||
33:         profile.emailSource in sources || profile.tags.orEmpty().contains("discovered")
34:     fun validEvidence(evidence: String?): Boolean = evidence != null &&
35:         evidence.matches(Regex("(?:JATS_SHA256|ORCID_RECORD_SHA256|SOURCE_SHA256):[0-9a-f]{64}"))
36:     fun verified(email: String, given: String?, family: String?, evidence: String, orcid: String?, authorId: String?) =
37:         IdentityVerification("VERIFIED", VERSION, normalizedEmail(email), given, family,
38:             evidence.substringBefore(':'), evidence.substringAfter(':'), orcid, authorId)
39: 
40:     fun isDiscoveryMap(source: Map<String, Any?>): Boolean = source["identityVerification"] != null ||
41:         source["emailSource"] in sources || (source["tags"] as? Collection<*>)?.contains("discovered") == true
42: 
43:     fun isDiscoverySource(source: JsonNode): Boolean =
44:         (!source.path("identityVerification").isMissingNode && !source.path("identityVerification").isNull) ||
45:             source.path("emailSource").asText(null) in sources ||
46:             source.path("tags").takeIf { it.isArray }?.any { it.asText() == "discovered" } == true
47: 
48:     /** Source-verified identity; a legacy outreach approval cannot authorize academic author binding. */
49:     fun allowed(profile: ExpertProfile): Boolean {
50:         if (!isDiscovery(profile)) return true
51:         val proof = profile.identityVerification ?: return false
52:         return proof.status == "VERIFIED" && proof.version == VERSION &&
53:             normalizedEmail(proof.email).isNotEmpty() && normalizedEmail(proof.email) == normalizedEmail(profile.email) &&
54:             !proof.givenNames.isNullOrBlank() && !proof.familyNames.isNullOrBlank() &&
55:             proof.givenNames == profile.givenNames && proof.familyNames == profile.familyNames &&
56:             (validEvidence("${proof.source}:${proof.evidenceHash}") ||
57:                 (proof.source == "REVIEWED_SOURCE_SHA256" && proof.evidenceHash.orEmpty().matches(Regex("[0-9a-f]{64}"))))
58:     }
59:     fun read(node: JsonNode): IdentityVerification? {
60:         if (!node.isObject) return null
61:         return try { mapper.treeToValue(node, IdentityVerification::class.java) } catch (_: Exception) {
62:             IdentityVerification() // malformed presence must remain a discovery identity, never a legacy bypass
63:         }
64:     }
65:     fun allowedSource(source: JsonNode): Boolean = allowed(ExpertProfile(
66:         orcidId = source.path("orcidId").asText(""), email = source.path("email").asText(null),
67:         givenNames = source.path("givenNames").asText(null), familyNames = source.path("familyNames").asText(null),
68:         country = null, keyword = null, employment = null, emailSource = source.path("emailSource").asText(null),
69:         tags = source.path("tags").takeIf { it.isArray }?.map { it.asText() },
70:         identityVerification = read(source.path("identityVerification"))
71:     ))
72:     fun allowedMap(source: Map<String, Any?>): Boolean = allowedSource(mapper.valueToTree(source))
73: 
74:     /** Explicit, finite historical approval. Automatic discovery never issues this receipt. */
75:     const val LEGACY_APPROVAL_SOURCE = "LEGACY_USER_APPROVED_20261002"
76: 
77:     fun legacyApprovalDigest(profile: ExpertProfile): String = hash(mapper.writeValueAsString(listOf(
78:         LEGACY_APPROVAL_SOURCE, profile.orcidId, normalizedEmail(profile.email),
79:         profile.givenNames, profile.familyNames, profile.institution, profile.country, profile.institutionType
80:     )))
81: 
82:     fun legacyOutreachApproved(profile: ExpertProfile): Boolean {
83:         val proof = profile.identityVerification ?: return false
84:         return proof.status == "LEGACY_APPROVED" && proof.version == VERSION &&
85:             proof.source == LEGACY_APPROVAL_SOURCE && normalizedEmail(profile.email).isNotEmpty() &&
86:             proof.email == normalizedEmail(profile.email) &&
87:             proof.givenNames == profile.givenNames && proof.familyNames == profile.familyNames &&
88:             proof.evidenceHash == legacyApprovalDigest(profile)
89:     }
90: 
91:     // ── 02（I-1/I-2）：机构来源证据 token ──────────────────────────────────────
92:     // 一个字段把「已存身份 + 来源种类 + 显示机构」绑成可重算的校验值：签发与验签共用下面
93:     // 同一份 NUL 分隔、固定顺序的输入；机构/国家/类型或身份 ID 一变，旧 token 立即失效。
94: 
95:     /**
96:      * 证据 token 的来源种类词表；取值与 01 的内部 `institutionSource` 逐字一致。
97:      * `ORCID` 不来自 `institutionSource`（ORCID 路径没有该字段），只能由 01b 的「唯一机构」判定结果驱动。
98:      */
99:     const val EVIDENCE_SOURCE_JATS = "JATS"
100:     const val EVIDENCE_SOURCE_OPENALEX = "OPENALEX"
101:     const val EVIDENCE_SOURCE_ORCID = "ORCID"
102:     private val EVIDENCE_SOURCES = setOf(EVIDENCE_SOURCE_JATS, EVIDENCE_SOURCE_OPENALEX, EVIDENCE_SOURCE_ORCID)
103: 
104:     /** 无 ORCID 的专家主键前缀（与发现写入侧同值）：它不是真实 ORCID，不参与「主键 = ORCID 值」检查。 */
105:     private const val EMAIL_KEY_PREFIX = "EMAIL-"
106: 
107:     /**
108:      * 唯一签发函数（02）：返回 null 表示**不写这个键**（绝不写 `false`/`UNVERIFIED`）。
109:      * 只有身份凭证成立、来源身份与 `externalIds`/主键无冲突、该来源必需的来源 ID 齐备、机构非空时才产出
110:      * `来源种类:<64位小写SHA256>`。输入顺序即 token 版本：见 [tokenDigestInput]。
111:      */
112:     fun institutionEvidence(profile: ExpertProfile, source: String): String? {
113:         if (source !in EVIDENCE_SOURCES) return null
114:         if (!allowed(profile)) return null
115:         val proof = profile.identityVerification ?: return null
116:         val ids = parsedExternalIds(profile.externalIds)
117:         if (!consistentIdentityIds(profile, proof, ids)) return null
118:         val institution = profile.institution?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() } ?: return null
119:         // JATS 必须有 externalIds.pmcId；OPENALEX 必须有同作者 openAlexAuthorId（且 doi/pmcId 至少一项）；
120:         // ORCID 必须有 externalIds.orcid。
121:         val sourceAuthorId = when (source) {
122:             EVIDENCE_SOURCE_OPENALEX -> ids["openAlexAuthorId"]?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() } ?: return null
123:             EVIDENCE_SOURCE_ORCID -> ids["orcid"]?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() } ?: return null
124:             else -> ids["orcid"].orEmpty()
125:         }
126:         if (source == EVIDENCE_SOURCE_JATS && ids["pmcId"].isNullOrBlank()) return null
127:         if (source == EVIDENCE_SOURCE_OPENALEX && ids["doi"].isNullOrBlank() && ids["pmcId"].isNullOrBlank()) return null
128:         return "$source:${hash(tokenDigestInput(source, profile, proof, ids, sourceAuthorId, institution).joinToString("\u0000"))}"
129:     }
130: 
131:     /**
132:      * 唯一验签函数（02）：**只读已存字段重算**（来源种类取自 token 前缀，不依赖任何本次审计备份）。
133:      * 旧文档没有该键 → false；机构/国家/类型或身份 ID 与签发时不一致 → false。
134:      */
135:     fun validInstitutionEvidence(profile: ExpertProfile): Boolean {
136:         val token = profile.institutionEvidence ?: return false
137:         val source = token.substringBefore(':')
138:         if (source !in EVIDENCE_SOURCES) return false
139:         return token == institutionEvidence(profile, source)
140:     }
141: 
142:     /** token 的固定输入顺序（NUL 分隔）：来源种类、凭证 source/evidenceHash、规范邮箱、姓名、来源作者 ID、论文/ORCID ID、机构、国家、机构类型。 */
143:     private fun tokenDigestInput(
144:         source: String,
145:         profile: ExpertProfile,
146:         proof: IdentityVerification,
147:         ids: Map<String, String>,
148:         sourceAuthorId: String,
149:         institution: String
150:     ): List<String> = listOf(
151:         source,
152:         proof.source.orEmpty(),
153:         proof.evidenceHash.orEmpty(),
154:         normalizedEmail(profile.email),
155:         "${profile.givenNames.orEmpty()} ${profile.familyNames.orEmpty()}",
156:         sourceAuthorId,
157:         sourcePaperOrOrcidId(ids, source),
158:         institution,
159:         profile.country.orEmpty(),
160:         profile.institutionType.orEmpty()
161:     )
162: 
163:     /** `已存 externalIds 中论文 ID 或 ORCID ID`：论文来源取论文 ID（pmcId→doi→pmid），ORCID 来源取 ORCID ID。 */
164:     private fun sourcePaperOrOrcidId(ids: Map<String, String>, source: String): String = when {
165:         source == EVIDENCE_SOURCE_ORCID -> ids["orcid"].orEmpty()
166:         !ids["pmcId"].isNullOrBlank() -> ids["pmcId"]!!
167:         !ids["doi"].isNullOrBlank() -> ids["doi"]!!
168:         else -> ids["pmid"].orEmpty()
169:     }
170: 
171:     /**
172:      * I-2 的签发前置：`identityVerification` 中非空的 ORCID/OpenAlex 作者 ID 与 `externalIds` 对应 ID 不得冲突；
173:      * 真实 ORCID 主键（非 `EMAIL-*`）不得与其 ORCID 值冲突（线上 1,256 条反例即此形状）。任一侧缺值不算冲突。
174:      */
175:     private fun consistentIdentityIds(
176:         profile: ExpertProfile,
177:         proof: IdentityVerification,
178:         ids: Map<String, String>
179:     ): Boolean {
180:         val orcidPrimaryKey = profile.orcidId.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() && !it.startsWith(EMAIL_KEY_PREFIX) }
181:         return !conflictingId(proof.orcid, ids["orcid"]) &&
182:             !conflictingId(proof.openAlexAuthorId, ids["openAlexAuthorId"]) &&
183:             !conflictingId(orcidPrimaryKey, ids["orcid"]) &&
184:             !conflictingId(orcidPrimaryKey, proof.orcid)
185:     }
186: 
187:     private fun conflictingId(stored: String?, incoming: String?): Boolean {
188:         val left = canonicalId(stored) ?: return false
189:         val right = canonicalId(incoming) ?: return false
190:         return !left.equals(right, ignoreCase = true)
191:     }
192: 
193:     /** 只做形状归一（去 URL 前缀 / trim），不改写已存值的语义。 */
194:     private fun canonicalId(raw: String?): String? = raw?.trim()
195:         ?.removePrefix("https://orcid.org/")?.removePrefix("http://orcid.org/")
196:         ?.removePrefix("https://openalex.org/")?.removePrefix("http://openalex.org/")
197:         ?.trim()?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }
198: 
199:     private fun parsedExternalIds(externalIds: String?): Map<String, String> {
200:         if (externalIds.isNullOrBlank()) return emptyMap()
201:         return try {
202:             val node = mapper.readTree(externalIds)
203:             if (!node.isObject) emptyMap()
204:             else node.fields().asSequence()
205:                 .filter { it.value.isTextual && it.value.asText().isNotBlank() }
206:                 .associate { it.key to it.value.asText() }
207:         } catch (_: Exception) {
208:             emptyMap()
209:         }
210:     }
211: 
212:     // ── 01（I-1/I-2/I-3）：准入解释 ────────────────────────────────────────────
213:     //
214:     // 只读解释：逐项列出与布尔判定**完全相同**的失败条件，绝不签发、改写或升级任何凭证。
215:     // 一致性契约（真值表，见 DiscoveryIdentityTest）：
216:     //   explainIdentity(p).isEmpty()            == allowed(p)
217:     //   explainInstitutionEvidence(p).isEmpty() == validInstitutionEvidence(p)
218:     // 解释不依赖调用范围：是否需要机构来源凭证由调用方（发送门禁/准入 policy）决定。
219: 
220:     private const val IDENTITY_STATUS_LOCATION = "DiscoveryIdentity.allowed()"
221:     private const val EVIDENCE_LOCATION = "DiscoveryIdentity.validInstitutionEvidence()"
222:     private const val EVIDENCE_EXPECTED = "JATS_SHA256|ORCID_RECORD_SHA256|SOURCE_SHA256:<64位小写SHA256>"
223: 
224:     /**
225:      * 身份凭证失败原因；空列表 ⟺ [allowed] 为 true。
226:      * 非发现档案（[isDiscovery] 为 false）按 [allowed] 的原语义直接放行，不产生原因。
227:      */
228:     fun explainIdentity(profile: ExpertProfile): List<AdmissionReason> {
229:         if (!isDiscovery(profile)) return emptyList()
230:         val proof = profile.identityVerification ?: return listOf(AdmissionReason(
231:             AdmissionReasonCodes.IDENTITY_MISSING, "身份凭证缺失（没有 VERIFIED 身份对象）",
232:             "identityVerification", null, "VERIFIED 身份凭证", "profile.identityVerification"))
233:         val reasons = mutableListOf<AdmissionReason>()
234:         if (proof.status != "VERIFIED") reasons += AdmissionReason(
235:             AdmissionReasonCodes.IDENTITY_STATUS_UNVERIFIED, "身份凭证未通过来源验证",
236:             "identityVerification.status", proof.status.ifBlank { null }, "VERIFIED", IDENTITY_STATUS_LOCATION)
237:         if (proof.version != VERSION) reasons += AdmissionReason(
238:             AdmissionReasonCodes.IDENTITY_VERSION_UNSUPPORTED, "身份凭证版本不受支持",
239:             "identityVerification.version", proof.version.toString(), VERSION.toString(), IDENTITY_STATUS_LOCATION)
240:         val proofEmail = normalizedEmail(proof.email)
241:         if (proofEmail.isEmpty()) reasons += AdmissionReason(
242:             AdmissionReasonCodes.IDENTITY_FIELDS_MISMATCH, "身份凭证未记录邮箱",
243:             "identityVerification.email", null, "与档案邮箱一致的规范邮箱", IDENTITY_STATUS_LOCATION)
244:         else if (proofEmail != normalizedEmail(profile.email)) reasons += AdmissionReason(
245:             AdmissionReasonCodes.IDENTITY_FIELDS_MISMATCH, "身份凭证邮箱与档案不一致",
246:             "identityVerification.email", proof.email, profile.email, IDENTITY_STATUS_LOCATION)
247:         if (proof.givenNames.isNullOrBlank()) reasons += AdmissionReason(
248:             AdmissionReasonCodes.IDENTITY_FIELDS_MISMATCH, "身份凭证未记录名字",
249:             "identityVerification.givenNames", null, "与档案 givenNames 一致的非空名字", IDENTITY_STATUS_LOCATION)
250:         else if (proof.givenNames != profile.givenNames) reasons += AdmissionReason(
251:             AdmissionReasonCodes.IDENTITY_FIELDS_MISMATCH, "身份凭证名字与档案不一致",
252:             "identityVerification.givenNames", proof.givenNames, profile.givenNames, IDENTITY_STATUS_LOCATION)
253:         if (proof.familyNames.isNullOrBlank()) reasons += AdmissionReason(
254:             AdmissionReasonCodes.IDENTITY_FIELDS_MISMATCH, "身份凭证未记录姓氏",
255:             "identityVerification.familyNames", null, "与档案 familyNames 一致的非空姓氏", IDENTITY_STATUS_LOCATION)
256:         else if (proof.familyNames != profile.familyNames) reasons += AdmissionReason(
257:             AdmissionReasonCodes.IDENTITY_FIELDS_MISMATCH, "身份凭证姓氏与档案不一致",
258:             "identityVerification.familyNames", proof.familyNames, profile.familyNames, IDENTITY_STATUS_LOCATION)
259:         // 与 allowed() 相同的两种可接受摘要形态；REVIEWED_SOURCE_SHA256 只影响此处判定，不改来源识别。
260:         if (!validEvidence("${proof.source}:${proof.evidenceHash}") &&
261:             !(proof.source == "REVIEWED_SOURCE_SHA256" && proof.evidenceHash.orEmpty().matches(Regex("[0-9a-f]{64}")))) {
262:             reasons += AdmissionReason(
263:                 AdmissionReasonCodes.IDENTITY_SOURCE_INVALID, "身份来源摘要格式无效",
264:                 "identityVerification.source / identityVerification.evidenceHash",
265:                 "${proof.source}:${proof.evidenceHash}", EVIDENCE_EXPECTED, IDENTITY_STATUS_LOCATION)
266:         }
267:         return reasons
268:     }
269: 
270:     /**
271:      * 机构来源凭证失败原因；空列表 ⟺ [validInstitutionEvidence] 为 true。
272:      *
273:      * - 凭证缺失 ⇒ [AdmissionReasonCodes.INSTITUTION_EVIDENCE_MISSING]：只陈述「当前档案没有该键」，
274:      *   并列出当前输入中可核实的来源 ID 冲突，**不**声称还原当时未生成的历史原因。
275:      * - 凭证存在但来源种类不在词表内，或与当前身份/机构/国家字段重算结果不一致 ⇒
276:      *   [AdmissionReasonCodes.INSTITUTION_EVIDENCE_INVALID]，并按凭证来源种类补列
277:      *   [AdmissionReasonCodes.SOURCE_ID_MISSING] / [AdmissionReasonCodes.SOURCE_ID_CONFLICT]。
278:      */
279:     fun explainInstitutionEvidence(profile: ExpertProfile): List<AdmissionReason> {
280:         val token = profile.institutionEvidence
281:         val source = token?.substringBefore(':')?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }
282:         if (source != null && source in EVIDENCE_SOURCES && token == institutionEvidence(profile, source)) return emptyList()
283:         val reasons = mutableListOf<AdmissionReason>()
284:         when {
285:             token.isNullOrBlank() -> reasons += AdmissionReason(
286:                 AdmissionReasonCodes.INSTITUTION_EVIDENCE_MISSING, "机构来源凭证缺失（当前档案没有该键）",
287:                 "institutionEvidence", null, "来源种类:<64位小写SHA256>", "profile.institutionEvidence")
288:             source == null || source !in EVIDENCE_SOURCES -> reasons += AdmissionReason(
289:                 AdmissionReasonCodes.INSTITUTION_EVIDENCE_INVALID, "机构来源凭证的来源种类不受支持",
290:                 "institutionEvidence", source, EVIDENCE_SOURCES.joinToString("|"), EVIDENCE_LOCATION)
291:             else -> reasons += AdmissionReason(
292:                 AdmissionReasonCodes.INSTITUTION_EVIDENCE_INVALID, "机构来源凭证与当前身份/机构字段不一致",
293:                 "institutionEvidence", source, "由当前已存字段重算出的同一来源凭证", EVIDENCE_LOCATION)
294:         }
295:         reasons += sourceIdConflictReasons(profile)
296:         if (source != null && source in EVIDENCE_SOURCES) reasons += missingSourceIdReasons(profile, source)
297:         return reasons
298:     }
299: 
300:     /** 与 `consistentIdentityIds` 同一组配对、同一归一化、同一冲突判据；只用于解释，不参与签发。 */
301:     private fun sourceIdConflictReasons(profile: ExpertProfile): List<AdmissionReason> {
302:         val proof = profile.identityVerification ?: return emptyList()
303:         val ids = parsedExternalIds(profile.externalIds)
304:         val primaryOrcid = profile.orcidId.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() && !it.startsWith(EMAIL_KEY_PREFIX) }
305:         val reasons = mutableListOf<AdmissionReason>()
306:         fun check(leftLabel: String, left: String?, rightLabel: String, right: String?) {
307:             val leftId = canonicalId(left) ?: return
308:             val rightId = canonicalId(right) ?: return
309:             if (leftId.equals(rightId, ignoreCase = true)) return
310:             reasons += AdmissionReason(
311:                 AdmissionReasonCodes.SOURCE_ID_CONFLICT, "来源 ID 冲突（身份凭证与档案不一致）",
312:                 "externalIds", "$leftLabel=$leftId / $rightLabel=$rightId", "两侧 ID 归一后一致", EVIDENCE_LOCATION)
313:         }
314:         check("identityVerification.orcid", proof.orcid, "externalIds.orcid", ids["orcid"])
315:         check("identityVerification.openAlexAuthorId", proof.openAlexAuthorId,
316:             "externalIds.openAlexAuthorId", ids["openAlexAuthorId"])
317:         check("orcidId", primaryOrcid, "externalIds.orcid", ids["orcid"])
318:         check("orcidId", primaryOrcid, "identityVerification.orcid", proof.orcid)
319:         return reasons
320:     }
321: 
322:     /** [source] 凭证签发必需的来源 ID 缺项（与 [institutionEvidence] 的必需 ID 判据逐条对应）。 */
323:     private fun missingSourceIdReasons(profile: ExpertProfile, source: String): List<AdmissionReason> {
324:         val ids = parsedExternalIds(profile.externalIds)
325:         fun missing(label: String, field: String, expected: String) = AdmissionReason(
326:             AdmissionReasonCodes.SOURCE_ID_MISSING, label, field, null, expected, "profile.externalIds")
327:         return when (source) {
328:             EVIDENCE_SOURCE_OPENALEX -> buildList {
329:                 if (ids["openAlexAuthorId"].isNullOrBlank())
330:                     add(missing("OPENALEX 凭证缺少作者 ID", "externalIds.openAlexAuthorId", "非空 openAlexAuthorId"))
331:                 if (ids["doi"].isNullOrBlank() && ids["pmcId"].isNullOrBlank())
332:                     add(missing("OPENALEX 凭证缺少论文 ID", "externalIds.doi / externalIds.pmcId", "doi 或 pmcId 至少一项"))
333:             }
334:             EVIDENCE_SOURCE_ORCID -> listOfNotNull(
335:                 if (ids["orcid"].isNullOrBlank())
336:                     missing("ORCID 凭证缺少 ORCID ID", "externalIds.orcid", "非空 orcid") else null)
337:             else -> listOfNotNull(
338:                 if (ids["pmcId"].isNullOrBlank())
339:                     missing("JATS 凭证缺少论文 ID", "externalIds.pmcId", "非空 pmcId") else null)
340:         }
341:     }
342: 
343: }

### src/main/kotlin/com/weibo/talentintroduction/expert/domain/CountryContinentMapping.kt
1: package com.weibo.talentintroduction.expert.domain
2: 
3: import java.util.Locale
4: 
5: object CountryContinentMapping {
6:     const val REGION_CHINA = "China"
7:     const val REGION_ASIA_JK = "Asia (Japan & Korea)"
8:     const val REGION_ASIA_OTHER = "Asia (Other)"
9:     const val REGION_EUROPE = "Europe"

249: 
250:     private val REGION_TO_KEYS: Map<String, Set<String>> =
251:         MAPPING.entries.groupBy({ it.value }, { it.key })
252:             .mapValues { (_, keys) -> keys.toSet() }
253: 
254:     fun toRegion(countryOrNationality: String?): String {
255:         val normalized = countryOrNationality
256:             ?.lowercase(Locale.ROOT)
257:             ?.trim()
258:             .orEmpty()
259:         if (normalized.isBlank()) return REGION_OTHER
260:         return MAPPING[normalized] ?: REGION_OTHER
261:     }
262: 
263:     fun countriesForRegion(region: String): Set<String> {
264:         if (region == REGION_OTHER) return emptySet()
265:         val keys = REGION_TO_KEYS[region] ?: return emptySet()
266:         return keys.flatMap { key -> esTermVariants(key) }.toSet()
267:     }
268: 
269:     fun allKnownEsTermValues(): Set<String> =
270:         MAPPING.keys.flatMap { esTermVariants(it) }.toSet()
271: 
272:     fun allRegions(): List<String> = REGION_ORDER
273: 
274:     private fun esTermVariants(key: String): List<String> {
275:         val variants = linkedSetOf(key)
276:         if (key.length == 2) {
277:             variants.add(key.uppercase(Locale.ROOT))
278:         }
279:         if (key.contains(' ')) {
280:             variants.add(
281:                 key.split(' ').joinToString(" ") { word ->
282:                     word.replaceFirstChar { ch ->
283:                         if (ch.isLowerCase()) ch.titlecase(Locale.ROOT) else ch.toString()
284:                     }
285:                 }
286:             )
287:         } else if (key.length > 2) {
288:             variants.add(key.replaceFirstChar { ch ->
289:                 if (ch.isLowerCase()) ch.titlecase(Locale.ROOT) else ch.toString()
290:             })
291:         }
292:         return variants.toList()
293:     }
294: }

31: @Service
32: class ExpertIndexWriterService(
33:     private val restTemplate: RestTemplate,
34:     private val properties: ElasticsearchProperties,
35:     private val expertIndexService: ExpertIndexService,
36:     private val objectMapper: ObjectMapper,
37:     private val expertPromotionAuditService: ExpertPromotionAuditService,
38:     private val expertContactRepository: ExpertContactRepository
39: ) {
40:     private val log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(ExpertIndexWriterService::class.java)
41:     private val dateFormatter = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss")
42: 
43:     companion object {
44:         /** 分类 bulk 单批上限（I2-2/Task 1）；实际批次大小由 backfill request.batchSize 控制。 */
45:         const val CLASSIFICATION_BULK_BATCH_CAP = 1000
46: 
47:         /** 失败样本保留上限：统计全部失败，但样本最多保留 100 条（I2-4）。 */
48:         const val CLASSIFICATION_FAILURE_SAMPLE_CAP = 100
49: 
50:         /** 04（I-2）：允许投影到候选的准入结论（人工批准、历史认可、自动通过）。 */
51:         val ADMITTED_DECISIONS = setOf("AUTO_PASSED", "MANUAL_APPROVED", "LEGACY_APPROVED")
52: 
53:         private const val DISCOVERY_TAG = "discovered"
54: 
55:         /** I-2：规范邮箱全量命中的单次查询上限；补证全集必须来自这里，不是去重时的 `size=1`。 */
56:         const val EMAIL_HIT_LIMIT = 100

59:     fun markApplicationClosed(contact: ExpertContact) {
60:         if (!contact.applicationIndexed) return
61:         val applicationIndex = expertIndexService.indexName(ExpertIndexLevel.APPLICATION)
62:         val orcid = ExpertIdNormalizer.normalize(contact.orcidId)
63:         val now = LocalDateTime.now().format(dateFormatter)
64:         try {
65:             val updateDoc = mapOf(
66:                 "doc" to mapOf(
67:                     "applicationStatus" to "CLOSED",
68:                     "currentConversationStatus" to contact.currentStatus,
69:                     "updatedAt" to now
70:                 )
71:             )
72:             val updateUrl = "${properties.baseUrl}/$applicationIndex/_update/$orcid"
73:             restTemplate.exchange(
74:                 updateUrl,
75:                 HttpMethod.POST,
76:                 HttpEntity(updateDoc, headers()),
77:                 JsonNode::class.java
78:             )
79:         } catch (e: Exception) {
80:             log.warn("Failed to mark application closed for contact {} (orcid={})", contact.id, orcid, e)
81:         }
82:     }
83: 
84:     fun syncOperatorStatus(orcidId: String, operatorStatus: String): SingleSyncResult {
85:         // IP-3: operatorStatus must reach all three layers; follow the
86:         // ExpertDiscoveryService.updateExpertAcademicFields pattern — skip layers whose
87:         // document does not exist (HEAD check), then _update by doc id.
88:         val normalizedOrcidId = ExpertIdNormalizer.normalize(orcidId)
89:         val now = LocalDateTime.now().format(dateFormatter)
90:         var updated = 0L
91:         for (level in listOf(ExpertIndexLevel.RAW, ExpertIndexLevel.CANDIDATE, ExpertIndexLevel.APPLICATION)) {
92:             if (!documentExistsInIndex(level, normalizedOrcidId)) continue
93:             try {
94:                 val index = expertIndexService.indexName(level)
95:                 val body: Map<String, Any> = if (operatorStatus == "NOT_CONTACTED") {
96:                     mapOf(
97:                         "script" to mapOf(
98:                             "source" to "if (ctx._source.containsKey('operatorStatus')) { ctx._source.remove('operatorStatus'); ctx._source.updatedAt = params.updatedAt; }",
99:                             "params" to mapOf("updatedAt" to now)
100:                         )
101:                     )
102:                 } else {
103:                     mapOf(
104:                         "doc" to mapOf(
105:                             "operatorStatus" to operatorStatus,
106:                             "updatedAt" to now
107:                         )
108:                     )
109:                 }
110:                 val updateUrl = "${properties.baseUrl}/$index/_update/$normalizedOrcidId"
111:                 val resp = restTemplate.exchange(
112:                     updateUrl, HttpMethod.POST,
113:                     HttpEntity(body, headers()),
114:                     JsonNode::class.java
115:                 ).body
116:                 val result = resp?.path("result")?.asText("")
117:                 if (result == "updated" || result == "noop") {
118:                     updated++
119:                 }
120:             } catch (e: Exception) {
121:                 log.warn("Failed to sync operatorStatus for orcid={} in index {}: {}", normalizedOrcidId, level, e.message, e)
122:                 return SingleSyncResult(matched = updated, ok = false, error = e.message)
123:             }
124:         }
125:         if (updated == 0L) {
126:             log.warn("syncOperatorStatus matched 0 docs across raw/candidate/application for orcid={}", normalizedOrcidId)
127:         }
128:         return SingleSyncResult(matched = updated, ok = true)
129:     }
130: 
131:     fun syncOperatorStatusBatch(updates: List<Pair<String, String>>): BulkSyncResult {
132:         // IP-3: sync operatorStatus on all three layers (RAW/CANDIDATE/APPLICATION);
133:         // per layer resolve orcidId → _id and bulk-update, aggregating results.
134:         val overallResult = BulkSyncResult()
135:         if (updates.isEmpty()) return overallResult
136:         val now = LocalDateTime.now().format(dateFormatter)
137:         for (level in listOf(ExpertIndexLevel.RAW, ExpertIndexLevel.CANDIDATE, ExpertIndexLevel.APPLICATION)) {
138:             val index = expertIndexService.indexName(level)
139:             val batches = updates.chunked(500)
140:             for (batch in batches) {
141:                 try {
142:                     // Resolve orcidId → _id mapping via terms query
143:                     val orcidIds = batch.map { ExpertIdNormalizer.normalize(it.first) }.distinct()
144:                     val idMapping = resolveOrcidToDocIds(index, orcidIds)
145: 
146:                     val bulkBody = batch.joinToString(separator = "\n", postfix = "\n") { (orcidId, operatorStatus) ->
147:                         val normalizedOrcidId = ExpertIdNormalizer.normalize(orcidId)
148:                         val docId = idMapping[normalizedOrcidId] ?: return@joinToString ""
149:                         val meta = mapOf("update" to mapOf("_id" to docId, "_index" to index))
150:                         val data = if (operatorStatus == "NOT_CONTACTED") {
151:                             mapOf(
152:                                 "script" to mapOf(
153:                                     "source" to "if (ctx._source.containsKey('operatorStatus')) { ctx._source.remove('operatorStatus'); ctx._source.updatedAt = params.updatedAt; }",
154:                                     "params" to mapOf("updatedAt" to now)
155:                                 )
156:                             )
157:                         } else {
158:                             mapOf(
159:                                 "doc" to mapOf(
160:                                     "operatorStatus" to operatorStatus,
161:                                     "updatedAt" to now
162:                                 ),
163:                                 "doc_as_upsert" to false
164:                             )
165:                         }
166:                         "${objectMapper.writeValueAsString(meta)}\n${objectMapper.writeValueAsString(data)}"
167:                     }
168: 
169:                     // Count orcidIds not found in this layer as skipped
170:                     for ((orcidId, _) in batch) {
171:                         if (!idMapping.containsKey(ExpertIdNormalizer.normalize(orcidId))) {
172:                             overallResult.total++
173:                             overallResult.skipped++
174:                         }
175:                     }
176: 
177:                     if (bulkBody.isBlank()) {
178:                         log.debug("No _id mappings found for batch of {} orcidIds in index {}", batch.size, index)
179:                         continue
180:                     }
181: 
182:                     val bulkUrl = "${properties.baseUrl}/_bulk"
183:                     val bulkHeaders = HttpHeaders().apply {
184:                         contentType = MediaType.valueOf("application/x-ndjson")
185:                         set(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, basicAuthHeader())
186:                     }
187:                     val responseNode = restTemplate.exchange(
188:                         bulkUrl, HttpMethod.POST,
189:                         HttpEntity(bulkBody, bulkHeaders),
190:                         JsonNode::class.java
191:                     ).body
192:                     if (responseNode != null) {
193:                         val items = responseNode.path("items")
194:                         if (items.isArray) {
195:                             for (item in items) {
196:                                 val updateNode = item.path("update")
197:                                 val status = updateNode.path("status").asInt(200)
198:                                 val docId = updateNode.path("_id").asText("")
199:                                 overallResult.total++
200:                                 if (status in 200..299) {
201:                                     overallResult.success++
202:                                 } else if (status == 404) {
203:                                     overallResult.skipped++
204:                                 } else {
205:                                     overallResult.failure++
206:                                     val errReason = updateNode.path("error").path("reason").asText("Unknown error")
207:                                     overallResult.errors.add("docId=$docId error: $errReason")
208:                                 }
209:                             }
210:                         } else {
211:                             overallResult.total += batch.size
212:                             overallResult.failure += batch.size
213:                             overallResult.errors.add("Bulk response items path is not an array")
214:                         }
215:                     } else {
216:                         overallResult.total += batch.size
217:                         overallResult.failure += batch.size
218:                         overallResult.errors.add("Empty bulk response from ES")
219:                     }
220:                 } catch (e: Exception) {
221:                     log.warn("Failed to batch sync operatorStatus for index {}: {}", index, e.message, e)
222:                     overallResult.total += batch.size
223:                     overallResult.failure += batch.size
224:                     overallResult.errors.add("Bulk request failed: ${e.message}")
225:                 }
226:             }
227:         }
228:         return overallResult
229:     }
230: 
231:     /**
232:      * 分类批量局部更新的唯一写入口（I2-2，M-4）。
233:      *
234:      * 以调用方传入的 [level] 解析唯一目标 index（不跨三层循环，不做 ORCID→docId 二次查询）；
235:      * 每个 item 按 [ClassificationBulkItem.esDocId] 更新原索引 `_id`，NDJSON data 逐字结构为
236:      * `{"doc":{"expertClassification":{...}},"doc_as_upsert":false}` —— 只写分类对象，
237:      * 不写根级 `updatedAt`、不自动创建缺失文档（`doc_as_upsert=false`）、不使用 `_update_by_query`。
238:      *
239:      * 返回每项 updated/noop/failure 状态；统计全部失败但只保留最多 100 条失败样本。
240:      * 整批请求异常（ES 不可达 / bulk 被拒）时本批全部计 failure 并停止后续批次，
241:      * 通过 [ClassificationBulkResult.wholesaleError] 暴露，由调用方决定是否中止整个任务。
242:      */
243:     fun bulkUpdateExpertClassifications(
244:         level: ExpertIndexLevel,
245:         updates: List<ClassificationBulkItem>
246:     ): ClassificationBulkResult {
247:         val index = expertIndexService.indexName(level)
248:         val results = mutableListOf<ClassificationBulkItemResult>()
249:         val failureSamples = mutableListOf<String>()
250:         var anyMapperError = false
251:         var wholesaleError: String? = null
252: 
253:         for (batch in updates.chunked(CLASSIFICATION_BULK_BATCH_CAP)) {
254:             val bulkBody = batch.joinToString(separator = "\n", postfix = "\n") { item ->
255:                 val meta = mapOf("update" to mapOf("_id" to item.esDocId, "_index" to index))
256:                 val data = mapOf(
257:                     "doc" to mapOf("expertClassification" to classificationNode(item.classification)),
258:                     "doc_as_upsert" to false
259:                 )
260:                 "${objectMapper.writeValueAsString(meta)}\n${objectMapper.writeValueAsString(data)}"
261:             }
262:             try {
263:                 val bulkUrl = "${properties.baseUrl}/_bulk"
264:                 val bulkHeaders = HttpHeaders().apply {
265:                     contentType = MediaType.valueOf("application/x-ndjson")
266:                     set(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, basicAuthHeader())
267:                 }
268:                 val responseNode = restTemplate.exchange(
269:                     bulkUrl, HttpMethod.POST,
270:                     HttpEntity(bulkBody, bulkHeaders),
271:                     JsonNode::class.java
272:                 ).body
273:                 if (responseNode == null) {
274:                     batch.forEach { item ->
275:                         recordClassificationFailure(results, failureSamples, item.esDocId, "Empty bulk response from ES")
276:                     }
277:                     wholesaleError = "Empty bulk response from ES for index $index"
278:                     break
279:                 }
280:                 val items = responseNode.path("items")
281:                 if (!items.isArray) {
282:                     batch.forEach { item ->
283:                         recordClassificationFailure(results, failureSamples, item.esDocId, "Bulk response items path is not an array")
284:                     }
285:                     wholesaleError = "Bulk response items path is not an array for index $index"
286:                     break
287:                 }
288:                 for ((item, itemNode) in batch.zip(items)) {
289:                     val updateNode = itemNode.path("update")
290:                     val status = updateNode.path("status").asInt(200)
291:                     val resultField = updateNode.path("result").asText("")
292:                     when {
293:                         status in 200..299 && resultField == "noop" ->
294:                             results += ClassificationBulkItemResult(item.esDocId, ClassificationBulkItemStatus.NOOP)
295: 
296:                         status in 200..299 ->
297:                             results += ClassificationBulkItemResult(item.esDocId, ClassificationBulkItemStatus.UPDATED)
298: 
299:                         else -> {
300:                             val errorType = updateNode.path("error").path("type").asText("")
301:                             val errReason = updateNode.path("error").path("reason").asText("Unknown error")
302:                             if (errorType == "mapper_parsing_exception") {
303:                                 anyMapperError = true
304:                             }
305:                             recordClassificationFailure(results, failureSamples, item.esDocId, errReason)
306:                         }
307:                     }
308:                 }
309:             } catch (e: Exception) {
310:                 log.warn("Failed to bulk update expert classifications for index {}: {}", index, e.message, e)
311:                 batch.forEach { item ->
312:                     recordClassificationFailure(results, failureSamples, item.esDocId, "Bulk request failed: ${e.message}")
313:                 }
314:                 wholesaleError = "Bulk request failed for index $index: ${e.message}"
315:                 break
316:             }
317:         }
318: 
319:         val failures = results.count { it.status == ClassificationBulkItemStatus.FAILED }
320:         return ClassificationBulkResult(
321:             items = results,
322:             failureSamples = failureSamples,
323:             allFailedWithMapperError = results.isNotEmpty() && failures == results.size && anyMapperError,
324:             wholesaleError = wholesaleError
325:         )
326:     }
327: 
328:     /**
329:      * 前置 mapping 检查（I2-2 立即 FAILED 前提）：确认 [level] 索引已声明
330:      * `expertClassification.type`（keyword）。缺失/异常 → false，调用方应在扫描前停止。
331:      */
332:     fun checkExpertClassificationMapping(level: ExpertIndexLevel): Boolean {
333:         val index = expertIndexService.indexName(level)
334:         val url = "${properties.baseUrl}/$index/_mapping"
335:         return try {
336:             val response = restTemplate.exchange(
337:                 url, HttpMethod.GET,
338:                 HttpEntity(null, headers()),
339:                 JsonNode::class.java
340:             ).body ?: return false
341:             var found = false
342:             response.fields().forEachRemaining { (_, indexNode) ->
343:                 val type = indexNode.path("mappings")
344:                     .path("properties")
345:                     .path("expertClassification")
346:                     .path("properties")
347:                     .path("type")
348:                     .path("type")
349:                     .asText()
350:                 if (type == "keyword") {
351:                     found = true
352:                 }
353:             }
354:             found
355:         } catch (e: Exception) {
356:             log.warn("Failed to check expertClassification mapping for index {}", index, e)
357:             false
358:         }
359:     }
360: 
361:     fun classificationNode(classification: ExpertClassification): JsonNode =
362:         objectMapper.createObjectNode().apply {
363:             put("type", classification.type.name)
364:             put("productionScore", classification.productionScore)
365:             put("researchScore", classification.researchScore)
366:             putArray("positiveEvidence").apply { classification.positiveEvidence.forEach { add(it) } }
367:             putArray("negativeEvidence").apply { classification.negativeEvidence.forEach { add(it) } }
368:             put("version", classification.version)
369:             put("sourceFingerprint", classification.sourceFingerprint)
370:             put("classifiedAt", classification.classifiedAt.format(dateFormatter))
371:         }
372: 
373:     private fun recordClassificationFailure(
374:         results: MutableList<ClassificationBulkItemResult>,
375:         failureSamples: MutableList<String>,
376:         esDocId: String,
377:         reason: String
378:     ) {
379:         results += ClassificationBulkItemResult(esDocId, ClassificationBulkItemStatus.FAILED, reason)
380:         if (failureSamples.size < CLASSIFICATION_FAILURE_SAMPLE_CAP) {
381:             failureSamples.add("docId=$esDocId error: $reason")
382:         }
383:     }
384: 
385:     private fun resolveOrcidToDocIds(index: String, orcidIds: List<String>): Map<String, String> {
386:         if (orcidIds.isEmpty()) return emptyMap()
387:         val searchBody = mapOf(
388:             "size" to orcidIds.size,
389:             "_source" to listOf("orcidId"),
390:             "query" to mapOf("terms" to mapOf("orcidId" to orcidIds))
391:         )
392:         return try {
393:             val resp = restTemplate.exchange(
394:                 "${properties.baseUrl}/$index/_search",
395:                 HttpMethod.POST,
396:                 HttpEntity(searchBody, headers()),
397:                 JsonNode::class.java
398:             ).body
399:             val hits = resp?.path("hits")?.path("hits") ?: return emptyMap()
400:             val result = mutableMapOf<String, String>()
401:             for (hit in hits) {
402:                 val hitOrcid = hit.path("_source").path("orcidId").asText(null) ?: continue
403:                 val docId = hit.path("_id").asText(null) ?: continue
404:                 result[hitOrcid] = docId
405:             }
406:             result
407:         } catch (e: Exception) {
408:             log.warn("Failed to resolve orcidId → _id mapping: {}", e.message)
409:             emptyMap()
410:         }
411:     }
412: 
413:     fun promoteToApplication(
414:         orcid: String,
415:         contact: ExpertContact,
416:         firstReplyAt: Instant,
417:         sourceInboundId: Long? = null,
418:         triggeredBy: String = TriggeredBy.SYSTEM,
419:         operatorName: String? = null
420:     ): Boolean {
421:         val audit = expertPromotionAuditService.create(contact, orcid, sourceInboundId, triggeredBy, operatorName)
422:         val normalizedOrcid = ExpertIdNormalizer.normalize(orcid)
423:         val candidateIndex = expertIndexService.indexName(ExpertIndexLevel.CANDIDATE)
424:         val applicationIndex = expertIndexService.indexName(ExpertIndexLevel.APPLICATION)
425: 
426:         val getUrl = "${properties.baseUrl}/$candidateIndex/_doc/$normalizedOrcid"
427:         val candidateResponse = try {
428:             restTemplate.exchange(
429:                 getUrl,
430:                 HttpMethod.GET,
431:                 HttpEntity(null, headers()),
432:                 JsonNode::class.java
433:             ).body
434:         } catch (e: Exception) {
435:             expertPromotionAuditService.markFailed(audit, e.message ?: "Failed to read candidate index")
436:             return false
437:         }
438: 
439:         val source = candidateResponse?.path("_source") ?: run {
440:             expertPromotionAuditService.markFailed(audit, "Candidate index document has no _source")
441:             return false
442:         }
443: 
444:         val now = LocalDateTime.now().format(dateFormatter)
445:         val firstReplyStr = firstReplyAt
446:             .let { LocalDateTime.ofInstant(it, ZoneId.systemDefault()).format(dateFormatter) }
447: 
448:         val doc = objectMapper.createObjectNode().apply {
449:             source.fields().forEachRemaining { (key, value) ->
450:                 val copy: JsonNode = value.deepCopy()
451:                 replace(key, copy)
452:             }
453:             put("currentConversationStatus", contact.currentStatus)
454:             put("applicationStatus", "ACTIVE")
455:             put("autoReplyEnabled", contact.autoReplyEnabled)
456:             put("firstReplyAt", firstReplyStr)
457:             put("lastReplyAt", firstReplyStr)
458:             put("expertContactId", contact.id ?: -1)
459:             put("campaignId", contact.campaignId)
460:             put("promotionSource", "INBOUND_REPLY")
461:             put("applicationPromotedAt", now)
462:             put("updatedAt", now)
463:         }
464: 
465:         val putUrl = "${properties.baseUrl}/$applicationIndex/_doc/$normalizedOrcid" +
466:             if (source.path("identityVerification").isObject) "?op_type=create" else ""
467:         return try {
468:             restTemplate.exchange(
469:                 putUrl,
470:                 HttpMethod.PUT,
471:                 HttpEntity(toStringMap(doc), headers()),
472:                 JsonNode::class.java
473:             )
474:             val removedFromCandidate = removeFromCandidateIndex(normalizedOrcid)
475:             if (!removedFromCandidate) {
476:                 expertPromotionAuditService.markFailed(audit, "Failed to remove candidate index document after application promotion")
477:                 return false
478:             }
479:             expertPromotionAuditService.markSuccess(audit)
480:             true
481:         } catch (e: Exception) {
482:             expertPromotionAuditService.markFailed(audit, e.message ?: "Failed to write application index")
483:             throw e
484:         }
485:     }
486: 
487:     fun retryFailedPromotion(promotionId: Long): ExpertApplicationPromotion {
488:         val promotion = expertPromotionAuditService.requireById(promotionId)
489:         require(promotion.promotionStatus == "FAILED") { "Only FAILED promotions can be retried" }
490:         val contact = expertContactRepository.findById(promotion.expertContactId)
491:             .orElseThrow { error("Expert contact not found: ${promotion.expertContactId}") }
492:         val firstReplyAt = contact.firstReplyAt ?: LocalDateTime.now()
493:         promoteToApplication(
494:             orcid = promotion.orcidId,
495:             contact = contact,
496:             firstReplyAt = firstReplyAt.toInstant(ZoneId.systemDefault().rules.getOffset(firstReplyAt)),
497:             sourceInboundId = promotion.sourceInboundId,
498:             triggeredBy = promotion.triggeredBy,
499:             operatorName = promotion.operatorName
500:         )
501:         return expertPromotionAuditService
502:             .findLatestByContactAndStatus(promotion.expertContactId, "SUCCESS")
503:             ?: expertPromotionAuditService
504:                 .findLatestByContactAndStatus(promotion.expertContactId, "FAILED")
505:             ?: promotion
506:     }

508:     fun syncApplicationStatus(contact: ExpertContact, intent: String? = null) {
509:         val applicationIndex = expertIndexService.indexName(ExpertIndexLevel.APPLICATION)
510:         val orcid = ExpertIdNormalizer.normalize(contact.orcidId)
511:         val now = LocalDateTime.now().format(dateFormatter)
512: 
513:         try {
514:             val updates = mutableMapOf(
515:                 "currentConversationStatus" to contact.currentStatus,
516:                 "autoReplyEnabled" to contact.autoReplyEnabled,
517:                 "updatedAt" to now
518:             )
519:             if (intent != null) {
520:                 updates["latestInboundIntent"] = intent
521:             }
522:             if (contact.lastReplyAt != null) {
523:                 updates["lastReplyAt"] = contact.lastReplyAt.format(dateFormatter)
524:             }
525: 
526:             val updateDoc = mapOf("doc" to updates)
527: 
528:             val updateUrl = "${properties.baseUrl}/$applicationIndex/_update/$orcid"
529:             restTemplate.exchange(
530:                 updateUrl,
531:                 HttpMethod.POST,
532:                 HttpEntity(updateDoc, headers()),
533:                 JsonNode::class.java
534:             )
535:         } catch (e: Exception) {
536:             log.warn("Failed to sync application status for contact {} (orcid={})", contact.id, orcid, e)
537:         }
538:     }

540:     fun promoteToCandidate(orcid: String, contact: ExpertContact): Boolean {
541:         val normalizedOrcid = ExpertIdNormalizer.normalize(orcid)
542:         val rawIndex = expertIndexService.indexName(ExpertIndexLevel.RAW)
543:         val candidateIndex = expertIndexService.indexName(ExpertIndexLevel.CANDIDATE)
544: 
545:         val getUrl = "${properties.baseUrl}/$rawIndex/_doc/$normalizedOrcid"
546:         val rawResponse = try {
547:             restTemplate.exchange(
548:                 getUrl,
549:                 HttpMethod.GET,
550:                 HttpEntity(null, headers()),
551:                 JsonNode::class.java
552:             ).body
553:         } catch (e: Exception) {
554:             log.warn("Failed to get raw expert profile from {} for orcid={}", rawIndex, orcid, e)
555:             return false
556:         }
557: 
558:         val source = rawResponse?.path("_source") ?: return false
559:         val now = LocalDateTime.now().format(dateFormatter)
560: 
561:         val doc = objectMapper.createObjectNode().apply {
562:             source.fields().forEachRemaining { (key, value) ->
563:                 val copy: JsonNode = value.deepCopy()
564:                 replace(key, copy)
565:             }
566:             put("candidateValidatedAt", now)
567:             put("updatedAt", now)
568:         }
569: 
570:         val putUrl = "${properties.baseUrl}/$candidateIndex/_doc/$normalizedOrcid" +
571:             if (source.path("identityVerification").isObject) "?op_type=create" else ""
572:         try {
573:             restTemplate.exchange(
574:                 putUrl,
575:                 HttpMethod.PUT,
576:                 HttpEntity(toStringMap(doc), headers()),
577:                 JsonNode::class.java
578:             )
579:             return true
580:         } catch (e: Exception) {
581:             log.warn("Failed to PUT candidate profile to {} for orcid={}", candidateIndex, orcid, e)
582:             return false
583:         }
584:     }
585: 
586:     fun removeFromCandidateIndex(esDocId: String): Boolean {
587:         val candidateIndex = expertIndexService.indexName(ExpertIndexLevel.CANDIDATE)
588:         val deleteUrl = "${properties.baseUrl}/$candidateIndex/_doc/$esDocId"
589:         return try {
590:             restTemplate.exchange(
591:                 deleteUrl,
592:                 HttpMethod.DELETE,
593:                 HttpEntity(null, headers()),
594:                 JsonNode::class.java
595:             )
596:             true
597:         } catch (e: HttpClientErrorException) {
598:             if (e.statusCode == HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND) {
599:                 log.warn("Candidate doc not found for esDocId={}, DELETE returned 404", esDocId)
600:                 false
601:             } else {
602:                 log.warn("Failed to remove esDocId={} from candidate index (HTTP {}): {}", esDocId, e.statusCode, e.message)
603:                 false
604:             }
605:         } catch (e: Exception) {
606:             log.warn("Failed to remove esDocId={} from candidate index", esDocId, e)
607:             false
608:         }
609:     }

615:             if (DiscoveryIdentity.isDiscoveryMap(profile)) "?op_type=create" else ""
616:         return try {
617:             restTemplate.exchange(
618:                 putUrl,
619:                 HttpMethod.PUT,
620:                 HttpEntity(profile, headers()),
621:                 JsonNode::class.java
622:             )
623:             true
624:         } catch (e: Exception) {
625:             log.warn("Failed to index orcid={} to raw index", orcid, e)
626:             false
627:         }
628:     }

630:     fun documentExistsInIndex(indexLevel: ExpertIndexLevel, esDocId: String): Boolean {
631:         val index = expertIndexService.indexName(indexLevel)

633:         return try {

635:                 headUrl,
636:                 HttpMethod.HEAD,
637:                 HttpEntity(null, headers()),
638:                 Void::class.java
639:             )
640:             true
641:         } catch (e: HttpClientErrorException) {
642:             if (e.statusCode == HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND) false else throw e
643:         }

646:     fun addTag(docId: String, tag: String, level: ExpertIndexLevel): Boolean {
647:         val index = expertIndexService.indexName(level)
648:         val script = mapOf(
649:             "script" to mapOf(
650:                 "source" to "if (ctx._source.tags == null) ctx._source.tags = []; if (!ctx._source.tags.contains(params.tag)) ctx._source.tags.add(params.tag)",
651:                 "params" to mapOf("tag" to tag)
652:             )
653:         )
654:         val url = "${properties.baseUrl}/$index/_update/$docId"
655:         return try {
656:             restTemplate.exchange(url, HttpMethod.POST, HttpEntity(script, headers()), JsonNode::class.java)
657:             true
658:         } catch (e: Exception) {
659:             log.warn("Failed to add tag '{}' to {} in {}: {}", tag, docId, level, e.message)
660:             false
661:         }
662:     }
663: 
664:     fun removeTag(docId: String, tag: String, level: ExpertIndexLevel): Boolean {
665:         val index = expertIndexService.indexName(level)
666:         val script = mapOf(
667:             "script" to mapOf(
668:                 "source" to "if (ctx._source.tags != null) ctx._source.tags.removeIf(t -> t == params.tag)",
669:                 "params" to mapOf("tag" to tag)
670:             )
671:         )
672:         val url = "${properties.baseUrl}/$index/_update/$docId"
673:         return try {
674:             restTemplate.exchange(url, HttpMethod.POST, HttpEntity(script, headers()), JsonNode::class.java)
675:             true
676:         } catch (e: Exception) {
677:             log.warn("Failed to remove tag '{}' from {} in {}: {}", tag, docId, level, e.message)
678:             false
679:         }
680:     }

682:     fun readRawDocument(docId: String): Map<String, Any?>? {
683:         val rawIndex = expertIndexService.indexName(ExpertIndexLevel.RAW)
684:         val getUrl = "${properties.baseUrl}/$rawIndex/_doc/$docId"
685:         return try {
686:             val response = restTemplate.exchange(
687:                 getUrl,
688:                 HttpMethod.GET,
689:                 HttpEntity(null, headers()),
690:                 JsonNode::class.java
691:             ).body
692:             val source = response?.path("_source")
693:             if (source == null || source.isMissingNode) null
694:             else toStringMap(source)
695:         } catch (e: Exception) {
696:             log.warn("Failed to read raw document for esDocId={}", docId, e)
697:             null
698:         }
699:     }
700: 
701:     data class DiscoverySnapshot(val source: Map<String, Any?>, val seqNo: Long, val primaryTerm: Long)
702: 
703:     /** Unlike readRawDocument, a transport failure is never mistaken for missing RAW. */
704:     fun readDiscoveryDocument(level: ExpertIndexLevel, docId: String): DiscoverySnapshot? {
705:         val index = expertIndexService.indexName(level)
706:         val response = try {
707:             restTemplate.exchange(
708:                 "${properties.baseUrl}/$index/_doc/$docId", HttpMethod.GET,
709:                 HttpEntity(null, headers()), JsonNode::class.java
710:             ).body ?: error("Empty ES GET response for $level/$docId")
711:         } catch (e: HttpClientErrorException) {
712:             if (e.statusCode == HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND) return null
713:             throw e
714:         }
715:         val source = response.path("_source")
716:         require(source.isObject && response.path("_seq_no").isIntegralNumber &&
717:             response.path("_primary_term").isIntegralNumber) { "Incomplete ES snapshot for $level/$docId" }
718:         return DiscoverySnapshot(toStringMap(source), response.path("_seq_no").asLong(), response.path("_primary_term").asLong())
719:     }
720: 
721:     /**
722:      * I-2：按规范邮箱查询三层**全量**命中（不是去重用的 `size=1`）。补证前必须据此确认
723:      * 「该邮箱只对应唯一业务 `_id`」：原 `size=1` 去重结果只能用于发现重复，不能作为修复全集。
724:      *
725:      * 传输/解析失败**抛出**，绝不返回空表 —— 调用方必须能区分「没有命中」与「查询失败」。
726:      */
727:     fun findDiscoveryHitsByEmail(email: String): List<DiscoveryEmailHit> {
728:         val levels = ExpertIndexLevel.values()
729:         val indices = levels.joinToString(",") { expertIndexService.indexName(it) }
730:         val levelByIndex = levels.associateBy { expertIndexService.indexName(it) }
731:         val query = mapOf(
732:             "query" to mapOf("term" to mapOf("email" to DiscoveryIdentity.normalizedEmail(email))),
733:             "size" to EMAIL_HIT_LIMIT,
734:             "_source" to listOf(
735:                 "email", "givenNames", "familyNames", "identityVerification",
736:                 "externalIds", "emailSource", "tags", "orcidId"
737:             )
738:         )
739:         val response = restTemplate.exchange(
740:             "${properties.baseUrl}/$indices/_search?ignore_unavailable=true",
741:             HttpMethod.POST, HttpEntity(query, headers()), JsonNode::class.java
742:         ).body ?: error("Empty ES _search response for email lookup")
743:         // R-4（V-28）：唯一性用途的搜索必须确认**正常完整结束** —— timeout / failed shard / 缺必要结构
744:         // 一律不可用，绝不从一个可见 hit 推断「唯一 doc」后写 proof。
745:         if (response.isNull || response.isMissingNode) {
746:             throw IllegalStateException("ES email lookup returned no usable body")
747:         }
748:         if (response.path("timed_out").asBoolean(false)) {
749:             throw IllegalStateException("ES email lookup timed out")
750:         }
751:         val failedShards = response.path("_shards").path("failed").asInt(0)
752:         if (failedShards > 0) {
753:             throw IllegalStateException("ES email lookup had $failedShards failed shard(s)")
754:         }
755:         val hits = response.path("hits").path("hits")
756:         require(hits.isArray) { "Malformed ES _search response for email lookup" }
757:         return hits.map { hit ->
758:             val id = hit.path("_id").asText().takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }
759:                 ?: error("ES email lookup hit without _id")
760:             val level = levelByIndex[hit.path("_index").asText()]
761:                 ?: error("ES email lookup hit with unknown _index")
762:             val source = hit.path("_source")
763:             require(source.isObject) { "ES email lookup hit without _source" }
764:             DiscoveryEmailHit(id, level, toStringMap(source))
765:         }
766:     }
767: 
768:     private fun sameDiscoveryIdentity(raw: Map<String, Any?>, candidate: Map<String, Any?>): Boolean =
769:         listOf("orcidId", "email", "givenNames", "familyNames", "externalIds", "identityVerification")
770:             .all { raw[it] == candidate[it] }
771:     fun discoveryProfile(docId: String, source: Map<String, Any?>): com.weibo.talentintroduction.expert.domain.ExpertProfile {
772:         fun text(key: String) = source[key] as? String
773:         fun number(key: String) = (source[key] as? Number)?.toInt()
774:         fun strings(key: String) = (source[key] as? List<*>)?.filterIsInstance<String>()
775:         return com.weibo.talentintroduction.expert.domain.ExpertProfile(
776:             esDocId = docId, orcidId = text("orcidId").orEmpty(), email = text("email"),
777:             givenNames = text("givenNames"), familyNames = text("familyNames"), country = text("country"),
778:             keyword = text("keyword"), employment = text("employment"), age = number("age"),
779:             degree = text("degree"), nationality = text("nationality"), hIndex = number("hIndex"),
780:             citationCount = number("citationCount"), lastPublicationYear = number("lastPublicationYear"),
781:             researchFields = text("researchFields"), disciplineCategory = text("disciplineCategory"),
782:             institution = text("institution"), emailSource = text("emailSource"),
783:             emailVerifiedLevel = number("emailVerifiedLevel"), dataSource = text("dataSource"),
784:             externalIds = source["externalIds"]?.let { objectMapper.writeValueAsString(it) },
785:             worksCount = number("worksCount"), tags = strings("tags"), updatedAt = text("updatedAt"),
786:             recentWorkTitles = strings("recentWorkTitles"),
787:             patentTitles = strings("patentTitles"), enrichedAt = text("enrichedAt"),
788:             enrichmentSource = text("enrichmentSource"), institutionType = text("institutionType"),
789:             identityVerification = source["identityVerification"]?.let {
790:                 DiscoveryIdentity.read(objectMapper.valueToTree(it))
791:             }, researchFieldIds = strings("researchFieldIds"),
792:             // 02（I-1/I-3）：完整 profile 读取必须带上发送门禁依赖的证据与资格键；
793:             // 旧文档没有这两个键 → null（不是合格），不在本方法做任何 ES 写。
794:             institutionEvidence = text("institutionEvidence"), filterResult = text("filterResult")
795:         )
796:     }
797: 
798: 
799:     /**
800:      * CAS both writes; an unchanged RAW identity and factual snapshot is a prerequisite to removing
801:      * a candidate replica. Missing RAW or ambiguous GET/UPDATE/DELETE is retryable, never success.
802:      */
803:     fun reconcileDiscoveryCandidate(
804:         docId: String, snapshot: DiscoverySnapshot, classification: ExpertClassification,
805:         reasons: List<String>, preserveApplication: Boolean = false
806:     ): Boolean {
807:         val rawIndex = expertIndexService.indexName(ExpertIndexLevel.RAW)
808:         val candidateIndex = expertIndexService.indexName(ExpertIndexLevel.CANDIDATE)
809:         val current = readDiscoveryDocument(ExpertIndexLevel.RAW, docId) ?: return false
810:         if (current != snapshot || !DiscoveryIdentity.isDiscoveryMap(current.source) ||
811:             !DiscoveryIdentity.allowedMap(current.source)) return false
812:         val candidate = if (preserveApplication) null else readDiscoveryDocument(ExpertIndexLevel.CANDIDATE, docId)
813:         if (candidate != null && !sameDiscoveryIdentity(current.source, candidate.source)) return false
814:         val qualification = mapOf(
815:             "filterResult" to if (reasons.isEmpty()) "PASSED" else "REJECTED",
816:             "filterRejectReason" to reasons.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }?.joinToString("; "),
817:             "expertClassification" to classificationNode(classification)
818:         )
819:         val update = restTemplate.exchange(
820:             "${properties.baseUrl}/$rawIndex/_update/$docId?if_seq_no=${snapshot.seqNo}&if_primary_term=${snapshot.primaryTerm}",
821:             HttpMethod.POST, HttpEntity(mapOf("doc" to qualification), headers()), JsonNode::class.java
822:         ).body ?: return false
823:         if (update.path("result").asText() == "noop") return false
824:         val stableRaw = readDiscoveryDocument(ExpertIndexLevel.RAW, docId) ?: return false
825:         if (stableRaw.seqNo != update.path("_seq_no").asLong(-1) ||
826:             stableRaw.primaryTerm != update.path("_primary_term").asLong(-1) ||
827:             !sameDiscoveryIdentity(snapshot.source, stableRaw.source)) return false
828:         if (preserveApplication) return true
829:         if (reasons.isEmpty()) {
830:             if (candidate != null) return true // never overwrite operator-owned candidate fields
831:             val candidateDoc = stableRaw.source.toMutableMap().apply {
832:                 put("candidateValidatedAt", LocalDateTime.now().format(dateFormatter))
833:                 put("tags", ((this["tags"] as? List<*>)?.filterIsInstance<String>().orEmpty() + "auto_promoted").distinct())
834:             }
835:             return writeCandidateDocument(docId, candidateDoc)
836:         }
837:         if (candidate == null) return true
838:         return try {
839:             restTemplate.exchange(
840:                 "${properties.baseUrl}/$candidateIndex/_doc/$docId?if_seq_no=${candidate.seqNo}&if_primary_term=${candidate.primaryTerm}",
841:                 HttpMethod.DELETE, HttpEntity(null, headers()), JsonNode::class.java
842:             )
843:             true
844:         } catch (e: HttpClientErrorException) {
845:             if (e.statusCode == HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND) true else throw e
846:         }
847:     }
848: 
849:     /**
850:      * 04（I-2）：RAW-only 准入通过后的候选投影结果。区分「真的创建」「已存在/已进有效层（不覆盖）」
851:      * 「身份冲突（同一 `_id` 却是别人）」「未准入」「源缺失」「写入失败」；调用方据此把失败单独记
852:      * `CANDIDATE_SYNC_FAILED`，绝不把失败当成功。
853:      */
854:     enum class DiscoveryCandidateProjection {
855:         PROJECTED,
856:         ALREADY_PRESENT,
857:         APPLICATION_PRESENT,
858:         IDENTITY_CONFLICT,
859:         NOT_ADMITTED,
860:         SOURCE_MISSING,
861:         WRITE_FAILED
862:     }
863: 
864:     /**
865:      * 04（I-2）：人工批准与自动晋升共用的发现候选投影接缝。
866:      *
867:      * 以**真实 `_id`**（[docId]）和调用方手里的 RAW 源快照 [source] 执行 create：
868:      * 有效层已存在时不反向复制候选；候选已存在时不覆盖任何运营字段；`op_type=create` 的 409
869:      * 只有复读身份一致才算「已存在」，否则身份冲突。**绝不**写 `filterResult`/`identityVerification`/
870:      * 机构/国家来伪装自动通过 —— 正文逐字来自 RAW 源。
871:      */
872:     fun projectDiscoveryCandidate(
873:         docId: String,
874:         source: Map<String, Any?>,
875:         decision: String
876:     ): DiscoveryCandidateProjection {
877:         if (docId.isBlank()) return DiscoveryCandidateProjection.SOURCE_MISSING
878:         if (decision !in ADMITTED_DECISIONS) return DiscoveryCandidateProjection.NOT_ADMITTED
879:         if (source.isEmpty()) return DiscoveryCandidateProjection.SOURCE_MISSING
880: 
881:         val alreadyApplied = try {
882:             documentExistsInIndex(ExpertIndexLevel.APPLICATION, docId)
883:         } catch (e: Exception) {
884:             log.warn("Failed to check APPLICATION existence for esDocId={} during projection", docId, e)
885:             return DiscoveryCandidateProjection.WRITE_FAILED
886:         }
887:         if (alreadyApplied) return DiscoveryCandidateProjection.APPLICATION_PRESENT
888: 
889:         val candidate = try {
890:             readDiscoveryDocument(ExpertIndexLevel.CANDIDATE, docId)
891:         } catch (e: Exception) {
892:             log.warn("Failed to read CANDIDATE for esDocId={} during projection", docId, e)
893:             return DiscoveryCandidateProjection.WRITE_FAILED
894:         }
895:         if (candidate != null) {
896:             return if (sameDiscoveryIdentity(source, candidate.source)) {
897:                 DiscoveryCandidateProjection.ALREADY_PRESENT
898:             } else {
899:                 DiscoveryCandidateProjection.IDENTITY_CONFLICT
900:             }
901:         }
902: 
903:         val now = LocalDateTime.now().format(dateFormatter)
904:         val candidateDoc = source.toMutableMap().apply {
905:             put("candidateValidatedAt", now)
906:             put("updatedAt", now)
907:             val existingTags = (get("tags") as? List<*>)?.filterIsInstance<String>().orEmpty()
908:             put("tags", (existingTags + DISCOVERY_TAG).distinct())
909:         }
910:         val candidateIndex = expertIndexService.indexName(ExpertIndexLevel.CANDIDATE)
911:         val putUrl = "${properties.baseUrl}/$candidateIndex/_doc/$docId?op_type=create"
912:         return try {
913:             restTemplate.exchange(putUrl, HttpMethod.PUT, HttpEntity(candidateDoc, headers()), JsonNode::class.java)
914:             DiscoveryCandidateProjection.PROJECTED
915:         } catch (e: HttpClientErrorException) {
916:             if (e.statusCode == HttpStatus.CONFLICT) {
917:                 val raced = try {
918:                     readDiscoveryDocument(ExpertIndexLevel.CANDIDATE, docId)
919:                 } catch (_: Exception) {
920:                     null
921:                 }
922:                 if (raced != null && sameDiscoveryIdentity(source, raced.source)) {
923:                     DiscoveryCandidateProjection.ALREADY_PRESENT
924:                 } else {
925:                     DiscoveryCandidateProjection.IDENTITY_CONFLICT
926:                 }
927:             } else {
928:                 log.warn("Failed to project candidate for esDocId={} (HTTP {})", docId, e.statusCode)
929:                 DiscoveryCandidateProjection.WRITE_FAILED
930:             }
931:         } catch (e: Exception) {
932:             log.warn("Failed to project candidate for esDocId={}", docId, e)
933:             DiscoveryCandidateProjection.WRITE_FAILED
934:         }
935:     }
936: 
937:     fun writeCandidateDocument(docId: String, doc: Map<String, Any?>): Boolean {
938:         val candidateIndex = expertIndexService.indexName(ExpertIndexLevel.CANDIDATE)
939:         val putUrl = "${properties.baseUrl}/$candidateIndex/_doc/$docId" +
940:             if (doc["identityVerification"] != null) "?op_type=create" else ""
941:         return try {
942:             restTemplate.exchange(
943:                 putUrl,
944:                 HttpMethod.PUT,
945:                 HttpEntity(doc, headers()),
946:                 JsonNode::class.java
947:             )
948:             true
949:         } catch (e: Exception) {
950:             log.warn("Failed to write candidate document for esDocId={}", docId, e)
951:             false
952:         }
953:     }
954: 
955:     /**
956:      * I-2/I-3：只写 `identityVerification` 的条件补丁 —— 带 `if_seq_no/if_primary_term`，
957:      * 其他字段（运营状态、学术事实、姓名、邮箱、作者编号）逐字不动；缺层由调用方跳过，绝不创建。
958:      *
959:      * 409（版本被并发改动）单独回报，调用方**不得**用旧快照盲目重试（不自动覆盖另一人的并发改动）。
960:      */
961:     fun writeIdentityProofIfVersionMatches(
962:         level: ExpertIndexLevel,
963:         docId: String,
964:         seqNo: Long,
965:         primaryTerm: Long,
966:         proof: IdentityVerification
967:     ): IdentityProofWriteResult {
968:         val index = expertIndexService.indexName(level)
969:         val url = "${properties.baseUrl}/$index/_update/$docId?if_seq_no=$seqNo&if_primary_term=$primaryTerm"
970:         val body = mapOf("doc" to mapOf("identityVerification" to proof))
971:         return try {
972:             val response = restTemplate.exchange(
973:                 url, HttpMethod.POST, HttpEntity(body, headers()), JsonNode::class.java
974:             ).body ?: return IdentityProofWriteResult.FAILED
975:             if (response.path("result").asText() == "updated") IdentityProofWriteResult.UPDATED
976:             else IdentityProofWriteResult.FAILED
977:         } catch (e: HttpClientErrorException) {
978:             when (e.statusCode) {
979:                 HttpStatus.CONFLICT -> IdentityProofWriteResult.VERSION_CONFLICT
980:                 HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND -> IdentityProofWriteResult.MISSING
981:                 else -> {
982:                     log.warn("Failed to write identity proof for {} in {} (HTTP {})", docId, level, e.statusCode)
983:                     IdentityProofWriteResult.FAILED
984:                 }
985:             }
986:         } catch (e: Exception) {
987:             log.warn("Failed to write identity proof for {} in {}", docId, level, e)
988:             IdentityProofWriteResult.FAILED
989:         }
990:     }
991: 
992:     /**
993:      * 02（I-2）：受限「填空姓名 + proof」条件补丁 —— 只补调用方确认**为空**的姓名字段
994:      * （`null` = 不写该字段，已填姓名逐字不覆盖），并写入身份凭证；带 `if_seq_no/if_primary_term`，
995:      * 邮箱/作者编号/学术/运营字段一律不动。缺层（404）与 409 语义与
996:      * [writeIdentityProofIfVersionMatches] 一致：绝不创建缺层，也绝不用旧快照盲目重试。
997:      */
998:     fun writeDiscoveryNameAndProofIfVersionMatches(
999:         level: ExpertIndexLevel,
1000:         docId: String,
1001:         seqNo: Long,
1002:         primaryTerm: Long,
1003:         givenNames: String?,
1004:         familyNames: String?,
1005:         proof: IdentityVerification
1006:     ): IdentityProofWriteResult {
1007:         val doc = linkedMapOf<String, Any?>("identityVerification" to proof)
1008:         givenNames?.let { doc["givenNames"] = it }
1009:         familyNames?.let { doc["familyNames"] = it }
1010:         val index = expertIndexService.indexName(level)
1011:         val url = "${properties.baseUrl}/$index/_update/$docId?if_seq_no=$seqNo&if_primary_term=$primaryTerm"
1012:         return try {
1013:             val response = restTemplate.exchange(
1014:                 url, HttpMethod.POST, HttpEntity(mapOf("doc" to doc), headers()), JsonNode::class.java
1015:             ).body ?: return IdentityProofWriteResult.FAILED
1016:             if (response.path("result").asText() == "updated") IdentityProofWriteResult.UPDATED
1017:             else IdentityProofWriteResult.FAILED
1018:         } catch (e: HttpClientErrorException) {
1019:             when (e.statusCode) {
1020:                 HttpStatus.CONFLICT -> IdentityProofWriteResult.VERSION_CONFLICT
1021:                 HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND -> IdentityProofWriteResult.MISSING
1022:                 else -> {
1023:                     log.warn("Failed to write name/proof for {} in {} (HTTP {})", docId, level, e.statusCode)
1024:                     IdentityProofWriteResult.FAILED
1025:                 }
1026:             }
1027:         } catch (e: Exception) {
1028:             log.warn("Failed to write name/proof for {} in {}", docId, level, e)
1029:             IdentityProofWriteResult.FAILED
1030:         }
1031:     }
1032: 
1033:     fun demoteToRaw(orcid: String, contact: ExpertContact): Boolean {
1034:         val normalizedOrcid = ExpertIdNormalizer.normalize(orcid)
1035:         val candidateIndex = expertIndexService.indexName(ExpertIndexLevel.CANDIDATE)
1036:         val applicationIndex = expertIndexService.indexName(ExpertIndexLevel.APPLICATION)
1037: 
1038:         val deleteBody = mapOf("query" to mapOf("term" to mapOf("orcidId" to normalizedOrcid)))
1039:         val deleteCandidateUrl = "${properties.baseUrl}/$candidateIndex/_delete_by_query"
1040:         val deleteApplicationUrl = "${properties.baseUrl}/$applicationIndex/_delete_by_query"
1041: 
1042:         var deleted = 0L
1043:         try {
1044:             val resp = restTemplate.exchange(
1045:                 deleteCandidateUrl, HttpMethod.POST,
1046:                 HttpEntity(deleteBody, headers()),
1047:                 JsonNode::class.java
1048:             ).body
1049:             val candidateDeleted = resp?.path("deleted")?.asLong(0) ?: 0
1050:             deleted += candidateDeleted
1051:             if (candidateDeleted > 0) {
1052:                 log.info("Demoted orcid={}: {} docs deleted from candidate index", orcid, candidateDeleted)
1053:             }
1054:         } catch (e: Exception) {
1055:             log.warn("Failed to delete orcid={} from candidate index via _delete_by_query", orcid, e)
1056:         }
1057: 
1058:         try {
1059:             val resp = restTemplate.exchange(
1060:                 deleteApplicationUrl, HttpMethod.POST,
1061:                 HttpEntity(deleteBody, headers()),
1062:                 JsonNode::class.java
1063:             ).body
1064:             val appDeleted = resp?.path("deleted")?.asLong(0) ?: 0
1065:             deleted += appDeleted
1066:             if (appDeleted > 0) {
1067:                 log.info("Demoted orcid={}: {} docs deleted from application index", orcid, appDeleted)
1068:             }
1069:         } catch (e: Exception) {
1070:             log.warn("Failed to delete orcid={} from application index via _delete_by_query", orcid, e)
1071:         }
1072: 
1073:         return deleted >= 1
1074:     }
1075: 
1076:     private fun toStringMap(node: JsonNode): Map<String, Any?> {
1077:         val result = linkedMapOf<String, Any?>()
1078:         node.fields().forEachRemaining { (key, value) ->
1079:             result[key] = toAny(value)
1080:         }
1081:         return result
1082:     }
1083: 
1084:     private fun toAny(node: JsonNode): Any? = when {
1085:         node.isNull -> null
1086:         node.isBoolean -> node.asBoolean()
1087:         node.isInt -> node.asInt()
1088:         node.isLong -> node.asLong()
1089:         node.isDouble -> node.asDouble()
1090:         node.isTextual -> node.asText()
1091:         node.isArray -> node.map { toAny(it) }
1092:         node.isObject -> toStringMap(node)
1093:         else -> node.asText()
1094:     }
1095: 
1096:     private fun headers(): HttpHeaders =
1097:         HttpHeaders().apply {
1098:             contentType = MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON
1099:             set(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, basicAuthHeader())
1100:         }
1101: 
1102:     private fun basicAuthHeader(): String {
1103:         val raw = "${properties.username}:${properties.password}"
1104:         val encoded = Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(raw.toByteArray(StandardCharsets.UTF_8))
1105:         return "Basic $encoded"
1106:     }
1107: 
1108: }

1110: /** I-2：规范邮箱全量命中里的一条 —— 真实业务 `_id` 及所在层与源快照。 */

1112:     val docId: String,
1113:     val level: ExpertIndexLevel,
1114:     val source: Map<String, Any?>
1115: )

1117: /** I-2/I-3：按版本条件仅写 proof 的结果；[VERSION_CONFLICT] 绝不允许用旧快照盲目重试。 */
1118: sealed class IdentityProofWriteResult {
1119:     object UPDATED : IdentityProofWriteResult()
1120:     /** 目标层已不存在（404）—— 视为缺层，绝不创建。 */
1121:     object MISSING : IdentityProofWriteResult()
1122:     object VERSION_CONFLICT : IdentityProofWriteResult()
1123:     object FAILED : IdentityProofWriteResult()
1124: }

1127:     val matched: Long,
1128:     val ok: Boolean,
1129:     val error: String? = null
1130: )
1131: 
1132: data class BulkSyncResult(
1133:     var total: Int = 0,
1134:     var success: Int = 0,
1135:     var failure: Int = 0,
1136:     var skipped: Int = 0,
1137:     val errors: MutableList<String> = mutableListOf()
1138: ) : TaskExecutionSummaryProvider {
1139:     override val taskSuccessCount: Int get() = success
1140:     override val taskFailureCount: Int get() = failure
1141:     override val taskFinalStatus: String?
1142:         get() = when {
1143:             failure > 0 && success > 0 -> "PARTIAL_SUCCESS"
1144:             failure > 0 -> "FAILED"
1145:             else -> "SUCCESS"
1146:         }
1147: }
