1149: /** 分类 bulk 更新单项（I2-2）：只含 esDocId + 分类对象。 */
1150: data class ClassificationBulkItem(
1151:     val esDocId: String,
1152:     val classification: ExpertClassification
1153: )
1154: 
1155: enum class ClassificationBulkItemStatus { UPDATED, NOOP, FAILED }
1156: 
1157: /** 分类 bulk 单项结果（I2-4）：保留每项 updated/noop/failure 状态。 */
1158: data class ClassificationBulkItemResult(
1159:     val esDocId: String,
1160:     val status: ClassificationBulkItemStatus,
1161:     val error: String? = null
1162: )
1163: 
1164: /**
1165:  * 分类 bulk 汇总结果（I2-4/I2-6）。
1166:  * 统计全部失败（[failure]），样本 [failureSamples] 最多 100 条；
1167:  * [allFailedWithMapperError] 供调用方在首批全失败且均为 mapper_parsing_exception 时立即 FAILED；
1168:  * [wholesaleError] 非空表示整批请求异常（ES 不可达/被拒），调用方应停止整个扫描任务。
1169:  */
1170: data class ClassificationBulkResult(
1171:     val items: List<ClassificationBulkItemResult>,
1172:     val failureSamples: List<String>,
1173:     val allFailedWithMapperError: Boolean,
1174:     val wholesaleError: String? = null
1175: ) {
1176:     val updated: Int get() = items.count { it.status == ClassificationBulkItemStatus.UPDATED }
1177:     val noop: Int get() = items.count { it.status == ClassificationBulkItemStatus.NOOP }
1178:     val failure: Int get() = items.count { it.status == ClassificationBulkItemStatus.FAILED }
1179: }

### src/main/kotlin/com/weibo/talentintroduction/expert/service/ExpertSearchService.kt
1: package com.weibo.talentintroduction.expert.service

3: import com.weibo.talentintroduction.expert.domain.DiscoveryIdentity
4: import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode
5: import com.weibo.talentintroduction.config.ElasticsearchProperties
6: import com.weibo.talentintroduction.expert.domain.CountryContinentMapping

9: import com.weibo.talentintroduction.expert.domain.ExpertProfile

28: @Service
29: class ExpertSearchService(
30:     private val restTemplate: RestTemplate,
31:     private val properties: ElasticsearchProperties,
32:     private val expertIndexService: ExpertIndexService
33: ) {
34:     private val log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(ExpertSearchService::class.java)
35:     companion object {
36:         val ALLOWED_HAS_FIELDS = setOf("employment", "degree", "institution", "researchFields", "patentTitles", "recentWorkTitles")
37: 
38:         /**
39:          * R-10：单次 `_search` 读页上限（与各 `require(size in 1..MAX_SEARCH_PAGE_SIZE)` 同值）。
40:          * 需要读取更长前缀的调用方必须按该上限分页，**不能**把 `from + size` 当作单页 size。
41:          */
42:         const val MAX_SEARCH_PAGE_SIZE = 1000
43: 
44:         private val CLASSIFIED_AT_TIME_FORMATTER = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss")
45:         private val CLASSIFIED_AT_DATE_FORMATTER = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyy-MM-dd")
46: 
47:         /**
48:          * Fields whose ES mapping type is `keyword`, so an empty-string term can
49:          * reliably exclude documents holding blank values. `text` fields are not
50:          * listed here: `term ""` against them matches tokenized content and may
51:          * wrongly exclude documents that have values (I-12).
52:          */
53:         val BLANK_EXCLUDABLE_FIELDS = setOf(
54:             "researchFields", "recentWorkTitles", "patentTitles", "degree", "country"
55:         )
56: 
57:         /**
58:          * Existence filter for a hasField-style query. For `keyword` fields the
59:          * `exists` check is combined with `must_not term ""` so blank values do
60:          * not count as present; for `text` fields a bare `exists` is used (I-9).
61:          */
62:         fun fieldPresenceFilter(field: String): Map<String, Any> =
63:             if (field in BLANK_EXCLUDABLE_FIELDS) {
64:                 mapOf(
65:                     "bool" to mapOf(
66:                         "must" to listOf(mapOf("exists" to mapOf("field" to field))),
67:                         "must_not" to listOf(mapOf("term" to mapOf(field to "")))
68:                     )
69:                 )
70:             } else {
71:                 mapOf("exists" to mapOf("field" to field))
72:             }
73: 
74:         /**
75:          * I4a-2: 门禁字段之间是 AND —— 每个字段产出一个独立 filter，由调用方平铺进
76:          * bool.filter。空集合返回空列表（I4a-1）。
77:          * 调用方必须已把字段裁剪到 [ALLOWED_HAS_FIELDS] 之内（I4a-3）；此处仍保留
78:          * require 作为兜底，越界即 fail-fast，不静默忽略。
79:          */
80:         fun fieldPresenceFilters(fields: List<String>): List<Map<String, Any>> =
81:             fields.distinct().map {
82:                 require(it in ALLOWED_HAS_FIELDS) { "Invalid gate ES field: $it" }
83:                 fieldPresenceFilter(it)
84:             }
85: 
86:         val ALLOWED_DISCIPLINES = setOf("STEM", "HUMANITIES", "UNCLASSIFIED")
87: 
88:         fun disciplineFilter(discipline: String): Map<String, Any> {
89:             require(discipline in ALLOWED_DISCIPLINES) { "Invalid discipline: $discipline" }
90:             return when (discipline) {
91:                 "UNCLASSIFIED" -> mapOf(
92:                     "bool" to mapOf(
93:                         "must_not" to listOf(mapOf("exists" to mapOf("field" to "disciplineCategory")))
94:                     )
95:                 )
96:                 else -> mapOf("term" to mapOf("disciplineCategory" to discipline))
97:             }
98:         }
99: 
100:         /**
101:          * I1-1: 研发类型筛选取值白名单 —— 唯一权威声明处。
102:          * 从 [ExpertType] 枚举派生（禁止手写六值名单，M-2）；`UNCLASSIFIED` 是字面量，
103:          * 语义为 `expertClassification.type` 字段**不存在**（见 [expertTypePredicate]）。
104:          */
105:         val ALLOWED_EXPERT_TYPES: Set<String> =
106:             ExpertType.values().map { it.name }.toSet() + "UNCLASSIFIED"
107: 
108:         /**
109:          * I1-2/I1-3: N 个研发类型取 OR，产出**单个** bool.should + minimum_should_match:1
110:          * 的 filter 项；空集合返回 null（I1-2，调用方不得追加，禁止产出
111:          * `should: [] + minimum_should_match: 1`）。逐字照 [operatorStatusesFilter] 的
112:          * map/trim/filter/distinct 结构。
113:          */
114:         fun expertTypesFilter(types: List<String>): Map<String, Any>? {
115:             val values = types.map { it.trim() }.filter { it.isNotEmpty() }.distinct()
116:             if (values.isEmpty()) return null
117:             values.forEach { require(it in ALLOWED_EXPERT_TYPES) { "Invalid expert type: $it" } }
118:             return mapOf(
119:                 "bool" to mapOf(
120:                     "should" to values.map { expertTypePredicate(it) },
121:                     "minimum_should_match" to 1
122:                 )
123:             )
124:         }
125: 
126:         /**
127:          * I4-2: 发信目标的 fail-closed 表达 —— 类型集合为空时追加它，命中恒为 0。
128:          * 只允许用在 INTRODUCTION 的发信目标查询上；列表页等"空 = 不限"的调用点禁止使用。
129:          */
130:         val MATCH_NONE_FILTER: Map<String, Any> = mapOf(
131:             "bool" to mapOf("must_not" to listOf(mapOf("match_all" to emptyMap<String, Any>())))
132:         )
133: 
134:         /**
135:          * I1-1: 单个研发类型的**纯谓词** —— 只判定类型本身，不得在分支内混入
136:          * `exists email` 之类的 AND 语义条件，否则其余 should 分支会绕过它
137:          * （K-batch-multi-value-filter-seams）。`UNCLASSIFIED` = `expertClassification.type`
138:          * 字段不存在（must_not exists），不是某个字符串 term。
139:          */
140:         private fun expertTypePredicate(type: String): Map<String, Any> =
141:             if (type == "UNCLASSIFIED") {
142:                 mapOf(
143:                     "bool" to mapOf(
144:                         "must_not" to listOf(mapOf("exists" to mapOf("field" to "expertClassification.type")))
145:                     )
146:                 )
147:             } else {
148:                 mapOf("term" to mapOf("expertClassification.type" to type))
149:             }
150: 
151:         fun regionFilter(region: String): Map<String, Any> {
152:             if (region == CountryContinentMapping.REGION_OTHER) {
153:                 val knownValues = CountryContinentMapping.allKnownEsTermValues().toList()
154:                 return mapOf(
155:                     "bool" to mapOf(
156:                         "should" to listOf(
157:                             mapOf(
158:                                 "bool" to mapOf(
159:                                     "must" to listOf(mapOf("exists" to mapOf("field" to "country"))),
160:                                     "must_not" to listOf(mapOf("terms" to mapOf("country" to knownValues)))
161:                                 )
162:                             ),
163:                             mapOf(
164:                                 "bool" to mapOf(
165:                                     "must" to listOf(mapOf("exists" to mapOf("field" to "nationality"))),
166:                                     "must_not" to listOf(mapOf("terms" to mapOf("nationality" to knownValues)))
167:                                 )
168:                             )
169:                         ),
170:                         "minimum_should_match" to 1
171:                     )
172:                 )
173:             }
174: 
175:             val countryValues = CountryContinentMapping.countriesForRegion(region).toList()
176:             return mapOf(
177:                 "bool" to mapOf(
178:                     "should" to listOf(
179:                         mapOf("terms" to mapOf("country" to countryValues)),
180:                         mapOf("terms" to mapOf("nationality" to countryValues))
181:                     ),
182:                     "minimum_should_match" to 1
183:                 )
184:             )
185:         }
186: 
187:         /** 多选地区：并集（should + minimum_should_match 1）。空列表返回 null 表示不限制。 */
188:         fun regionsFilter(regions: List<String>): Map<String, Any>? {
189:             if (regions.isEmpty()) return null
190:             regions.forEach { require(it in CountryContinentMapping.allRegions()) { "Invalid region: $it" } }
191:             if (regions.size == 1) return regionFilter(regions.first())
192:             return mapOf(
193:                 "bool" to mapOf(
194:                     "should" to regions.map { regionFilter(it) },
195:                     "minimum_should_match" to 1
196:                 )
197:             )
198:         }
199: 
200:         /**
201:          * I2a-3: N 个邮箱域取 OR，产出**单个** filter 项；空集合返回 null（I2a-2，
202:          * 调用方不得追加）。照 [regionsFilter] 的 should + minimum_should_match 范式。
203:          */
204:         fun emailDomainsFilter(emailDomains: List<String>): Map<String, Any>? {
205:             val domains = emailDomains.map { it.trim() }.filter { it.isNotEmpty() }.distinct()
206:             if (domains.isEmpty()) return null
207:             return mapOf(
208:                 "bool" to mapOf(
209:                     "should" to domains.map {
210:                         mapOf("wildcard" to mapOf("email" to mapOf("value" to "*@$it")))
211:                     },
212:                     "minimum_should_match" to 1
213:                 )
214:             )
215:         }
216: 
217:         /**
218:          * 多域版 [notContactedWithEmailFilters]。**旧单值重载保持原样不动**（N2a-2）——
219:          * 专家列表等路径仍在用它。
220:          */
221:         fun notContactedWithEmailDomainsFilters(
222:             emailDomains: List<String> = emptyList(),
223:             discipline: String? = null
224:         ): List<Map<String, Any>> {
225:             val filters = mutableListOf<Map<String, Any>>(
226:                 mapOf("exists" to mapOf("field" to "email")),
227:                 mapOf("bool" to mapOf(
228:                     "must_not" to listOf(
229:                         mapOf("exists" to mapOf("field" to "operatorStatus")),
230:                         mapOf("term" to mapOf("operatorStatus" to "EMAIL_INVALID"))
231:                     )
232:                 ))
233:             )
234:             emailDomainsFilter(emailDomains)?.let { filters.add(it) }
235:             if (!discipline.isNullOrBlank()) {
236:                 filters.add(disciplineFilter(discipline))
237:             }
238:             return filters
239:         }
240: 
241:         fun notContactedWithEmailFilters(
242:             emailDomain: String? = null,
243:             discipline: String? = null
244:         ): List<Map<String, Any>> {
245:             val filters = mutableListOf<Map<String, Any>>(
246:                 mapOf("exists" to mapOf("field" to "email")),
247:                 mapOf("bool" to mapOf(
248:                     "must_not" to listOf(
249:                         mapOf("exists" to mapOf("field" to "operatorStatus")),
250:                         mapOf("term" to mapOf("operatorStatus" to "EMAIL_INVALID"))
251:                     )
252:                 ))
253:             )
254:             if (!emailDomain.isNullOrBlank()) {
255:                 filters.add(mapOf("wildcard" to mapOf("email" to mapOf("value" to "*@$emailDomain"))))
256:             }
257:             if (!discipline.isNullOrBlank()) {
258:                 filters.add(disciplineFilter(discipline))
259:             }
260:             return filters
261:         }
262: 
263:         /**
264:          * I-3: NOT_CONTACTED 语义唯一 —— 复用 [notContactedWithEmailFilters] 的 must_not exists
265:          * 表达（= ES 文档无 operatorStatus 字段），其余状态走 term。两处活体旁路
266:          * （buildEsFiltersForLevel / matchesExpert）共用此实现，
267:          * 禁止在别处另写 `term operatorStatus=NOT_CONTACTED`。
268:          */
269:         fun operatorStatusFilter(status: String): List<Map<String, Any>> {
270:             if (status == "NOT_CONTACTED") {
271:                 return notContactedWithEmailFilters(null)
272:             }
273:             return listOf(mapOf("term" to mapOf("operatorStatus" to status)))
274:         }
275: 
276:         /**
277:          * I3a-2: 单个状态的**纯谓词** —— 只判定状态本身，不夹带 exists email /
278:          * EMAIL_INVALID 排除等 AND 语义条件，因此可安全放进 bool.should 分支。
279:          *
280:          * NOT_CONTACTED = ES 文档无 operatorStatus 字段（I3a-1）。与
281:          * [notContactedWithEmailFilters] 的 `must_not [exists, term EMAIL_INVALID]` 逻辑等价：
282:          * `term operatorStatus=EMAIL_INVALID` 蕴含 `exists operatorStatus`，
283:          * 故 NOT(exists) AND NOT(term) ≡ NOT(exists)。
284:          */
285:         fun operatorStatusPredicate(status: String): Map<String, Any> =
286:             if (status == "NOT_CONTACTED") {
287:                 mapOf("bool" to mapOf(
288:                     "must_not" to listOf(mapOf("exists" to mapOf("field" to "operatorStatus")))
289:                 ))
290:             } else {
291:                 mapOf("term" to mapOf("operatorStatus" to status))
292:             }
293: 
294:         /**
295:          * I3a-3: N 个状态取 OR，产出**单个** filter 项；空集合返回 null（调用方不得追加）。
296:          * 照 [regionsFilter] / [emailDomainsFilter] 的 should + minimum_should_match 范式。
297:          */
298:         fun operatorStatusesFilter(statuses: List<String>): Map<String, Any>? {
299:             val values = statuses.map { it.trim() }.filter { it.isNotEmpty() }.distinct()
300:             if (values.isEmpty()) return null
301:             return mapOf(
302:                 "bool" to mapOf(
303:                     "should" to values.map { operatorStatusPredicate(it) },
304:                     "minimum_should_match" to 1
305:                 )
306:             )
307:         }
308:     }
309: 
310:     fun searchExperts(
311:         size: Int,
312:         level: ExpertIndexLevel,
313:         tag: String? = null,
314:         sortBy: String? = null,
315:         from: Int = 0,
316:         operatorStatus: String? = null,
317:         emailDomain: String? = null,
318:         region: String? = null,
319:         hIndexMin: Int? = null,
320:         citationCountMin: Int? = null,
321:         recentYears: Int? = null,
322:         hasField: List<String>? = null,
323:         discipline: String? = null,
324:         expertTypes: List<String> = emptyList()
325:     ): ExpertSearchResult {
326:         require(size in 1..1000) { "size must be between 1 and 1000" }
327:         require(from >= 0) { "from must be >= 0" }
328: 
329:         val filters = buildExpertFilters(
330:             tag, operatorStatus, emailDomain, region, hIndexMin, citationCountMin, recentYears, hasField, discipline, expertTypes
331:         )
332: 
333:         val query = if (filters.isEmpty()) {
334:             mapOf("match_all" to emptyMap<String, Any>())
335:         } else {
336:             mapOf("bool" to mapOf("filter" to filters))
337:         }
338: 
339:         val sort = if (sortBy == "updatedAt") {
340:             listOf(dateSort("updatedAt"))
341:         } else {
342:             sortFields(level)
343:         }
344: 
345:         val requestBody = mapOf(
346:             "from" to from,
347:             "size" to size,
348:             // ES 默认 track_total_hits=10000，超过 1 万条时 total 固定为 10000（relation=gte）；
349:             // 这里需要精确总数用于前端分页
350:             "track_total_hits" to true,
351:             "_source" to sourceFields(),
352:             "query" to query,
353:             "sort" to sort
354:         )
355: 
356:         val response = restTemplate.exchange(
357:             "${properties.baseUrl}/${expertIndexService.indexName(level)}/_search",
358:             HttpMethod.POST,
359:             HttpEntity(requestBody, headers()),
360:             JsonNode::class.java
361:         ).body ?: return ExpertSearchResult(emptyList(), 0L)
362: 
363:         val totalHits = response.path("hits").path("total").path("value").asLong(0L)
364:         val experts = response.path("hits")
365:             .path("hits")
366:             .map { hit -> toExpertProfile(hit) }
367:         return ExpertSearchResult(experts = experts, totalHits = totalHits)
368:     }
369: 
370:     fun searchExpertsWithEmail(
371:         size: Int,
372:         level: ExpertIndexLevel = ExpertIndexLevel.CANDIDATE
373:     ): ExpertSearchResult {
374:         require(size in 1..1000) { "size must be between 1 and 1000" }
375: 
376:         val requestBody = mapOf(
377:             "size" to size,
378:             "_source" to sourceFields(),
379:             "query" to mapOf(
380:                 "bool" to mapOf(
381:                     "filter" to listOf(
382:                         mapOf("exists" to mapOf("field" to "email"))
383:                     )
384:                 )
385:             ),
386:             "sort" to sortFields(level)
387:         )
388: 
389:         val response = restTemplate.exchange(
390:             "${properties.baseUrl}/${expertIndexService.indexName(level)}/_search",
391:             HttpMethod.POST,
392:             HttpEntity(requestBody, headers()),
393:             JsonNode::class.java
394:         ).body ?: return ExpertSearchResult(emptyList(), 0L)
395: 
396:         val totalHits = response.path("hits").path("total").path("value").asLong(0L)
397:         val experts = response.path("hits")
398:             .path("hits")
399:             .map { hit -> toExpertProfile(hit) }
400:         return ExpertSearchResult(experts = experts, totalHits = totalHits)
401:     }
402: 
403:     fun scrollExperts(
404:         level: ExpertIndexLevel,
405:         batchSize: Int = 500,
406:         handler: (List<ExpertProfile>) -> Boolean
407:     ) {
408:         scrollExperts(level, batchSize) { batch, _, _ -> handler(batch) }
409:     }
410: 
411:     fun scrollExperts(
412:         level: ExpertIndexLevel,
413:         batchSize: Int = 500,
414:         handler: (batch: List<ExpertProfile>, batchNumber: Int, totalHits: Long) -> Boolean
415:     ) {
416:         val index = expertIndexService.indexName(level)
417:         var scrollId: String? = null
418: 
419:         try {
420:             val initialUrl = "${properties.baseUrl}/$index/_search?scroll=5m"
421:             val requestBody = mapOf(
422:                 "size" to batchSize,
423:                 "_source" to sourceFields(),
424:                 "query" to mapOf("match_all" to emptyMap<String, Any>()),
425:                 "sort" to listOf(mapOf("_doc" to "asc"))
426:             )
427:             var response = restTemplate.exchange(
428:                 initialUrl,
429:                 HttpMethod.POST,
430:                 HttpEntity(requestBody, headers()),
431:                 JsonNode::class.java
432:             ).body ?: return
433: 
434:             scrollId = response.path("_scroll_id").asText()
435:             val totalHits = response.path("hits").path("total").path("value").asLong(0)
436:             var batchNumber = 0
437: 
438:             do {
439:                 val hits = response.path("hits").path("hits")
440:                 if (hits.isEmpty) break
441: 
442:                 batchNumber++
443:                 val experts = hits.map { hit -> toExpertProfile(hit) }
444:                 val shouldContinue = handler(experts, batchNumber, totalHits)
445:                 if (!shouldContinue) break
446:                 if (hits.size() < batchSize) break
447: 
448:                 response = restTemplate.exchange(
449:                     "${properties.baseUrl}/_search/scroll",
450:                     HttpMethod.POST,
451:                     HttpEntity(mapOf("scroll" to "5m", "scroll_id" to scrollId), headers()),
452:                     JsonNode::class.java
453:                 ).body ?: break
454: 
455:                 scrollId = response.path("_scroll_id").asText()
456:             } while (true)
457:         } finally {
458:             if (scrollId != null) {
459:                 try {
460:                     restTemplate.exchange(
461:                         "${properties.baseUrl}/_search/scroll",
462:                         HttpMethod.DELETE,
463:                         HttpEntity(mapOf("scroll_id" to scrollId), headers()),
464:                         JsonNode::class.java
465:                     )
466:                 } catch (_: Exception) {}
467:             }
468:         }
469:     }
470: 
471:     private fun toExpertProfile(hit: JsonNode): ExpertProfile {
472:         val source = hit.path("_source").takeUnless(JsonNode::isMissingNode) ?: hit
473:         val esDocId = hit.path("_id").asText(null)
474:         return ExpertProfile(
475:             esDocId = esDocId,
476:             orcidId = source.nullableText("orcidId")
477:                 ?: source.nullableText("orcid")
478:                 ?: source.nullableText("id")
479:                 ?: "",
480:             email = source.nullableText("email"),
481:             givenNames = source.nullableText("givenNames"),
482:             familyNames = source.nullableText("familyNames"),
483:             country = source.nullableText("country"),
484:             keyword = source.nullableText("keyword"),
485:             employment = source.nullableText("employment"),
486:             age = source.path("age").let { if (it.isInt) it.asInt() else null },
487:             degree = source.nullableText("degree"),
488:             nationality = source.nullableText("nationality"),
489:             hIndex = source.path("hIndex").let { if (it.isInt) it.asInt() else null },
490:             citationCount = source.path("citationCount").let { if (it.isInt) it.asInt() else null },
491:             lastPublicationYear = source.path("lastPublicationYear").let { if (it.isInt) it.asInt() else null },
492:             researchFields = source.nullableText("researchFields"),
493:             disciplineCategory = source.nullableText("disciplineCategory"),
494:             institution = source.nullableText("institution"),
495:             institutionType = source.nullableText("institutionType")?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() },
496:             emailSource = source.nullableText("emailSource"),
497:             emailVerifiedLevel = source.path("emailVerifiedLevel").let { if (it.isInt) it.asInt() else null },
498:             dataSource = source.nullableText("dataSource"),
499:             externalIds = source.path("externalIds").let { if (it.isObject) it.toString() else null },
500:             worksCount = source.path("worksCount").let { if (it.isInt) it.asInt() else null },
501:             tags = source.path("tags").takeIf { it.isArray }
502:                 ?.map { it.asText() }
503:                 ?.filter { it.isNotBlank() },
504:             updatedAt = source.nullableText("updatedAt"),
505:             operatorStatus = source.nullableText("operatorStatus"),
506:             recentWorkTitles = source.path("recentWorkTitles").takeIf { it.isArray }
507:                 ?.map { it.asText() }?.filter { it.isNotBlank() },
508:             patentTitles = source.path("patentTitles").takeIf { it.isArray }
509:                 ?.map { it.asText() }?.filter { it.isNotBlank() },
510:             enrichedAt = source.nullableText("enrichedAt"),
511:             enrichmentSource = source.nullableText("enrichmentSource"),
512:             expertClassification = parseExpertClassification(source.path("expertClassification")),
513:             identityVerification = DiscoveryIdentity.read(source.path("identityVerification")),
514:             researchFieldIds = stringArrayOrNull(source, "researchFieldIds"),
515:             // 02（I-3）：发送模型必须读到证据与资格；旧文档没有这两个键 → null，不默认合格。
516:             institutionEvidence = source.nullableText("institutionEvidence"),
517:             filterResult = source.nullableText("filterResult")
518:         )
519:     }
520: 
521:     /**
522:      * 显式解析 ES 中的 `expertClassification` 对象（I1-5）。缺失/null → null；
523:      * 未知 `type` 记录 warn 并将整个对象视为 null（fail closed，绝不悄悄映射为 UNKNOWN）。
524:      * `sendable` 不读自 ES：领域 getter 恒由 type 派生，ES 中不可信的 sendable 值无法覆盖。
525:      */
526:     private fun parseExpertClassification(node: JsonNode): ExpertClassification? {
527:         if (node.isMissingNode || node.isNull) return null
528:         val typeName = node.nullableText("type") ?: return null
529:         val type = try {
530:             ExpertType.valueOf(typeName)
531:         } catch (e: IllegalArgumentException) {
532:             log.warn(
533:                 "Unknown expertClassification type '{}'; treating whole classification as null (fail closed)",
534:                 typeName
535:             )
536:             return null
537:         }
538:         val productionScore = node.path("productionScore").takeIf { it.isIntegralNumber }?.asInt() ?: return null
539:         val researchScore = node.path("researchScore").takeIf { it.isIntegralNumber }?.asInt() ?: return null
540:         val positiveEvidence = stringArrayOrNull(node, "positiveEvidence") ?: return null
541:         val negativeEvidence = stringArrayOrNull(node, "negativeEvidence") ?: return null
542:         val version = node.nullableText("version") ?: return null
543:         val sourceFingerprint = node.nullableText("sourceFingerprint") ?: return null
544:         val classifiedAt = parseClassifiedAt(node.path("classifiedAt")) ?: return null
545:         return ExpertClassification(
546:             type = type,
547:             productionScore = productionScore,
548:             researchScore = researchScore,
549:             positiveEvidence = positiveEvidence,
550:             negativeEvidence = negativeEvidence,
551:             version = version,
552:             sourceFingerprint = sourceFingerprint,
553:             classifiedAt = classifiedAt
554:         )
555:     }
556: 
557:     private fun stringArrayOrNull(node: JsonNode, field: String): List<String>? {
558:         val array = node.path(field)
559:         if (array.isMissingNode || array.isNull) return null
560:         if (!array.isArray) return null
561:         if (!array.all(JsonNode::isTextual)) return null
562:         return array.map { it.asText() }
563:     }
564: 
565:     /** 支持 mapping 声明的三种格式：`yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss`、`yyyy-MM-dd`、epoch_millis。 */
566:     private fun parseClassifiedAt(node: JsonNode): LocalDateTime? {
567:         if (node.isMissingNode || node.isNull) return null
568:         if (node.isIntegralNumber) {
569:             return try {
570:                 LocalDateTime.ofInstant(Instant.ofEpochMilli(node.asLong()), ZoneId.systemDefault())
571:             } catch (e: DateTimeException) {
572:                 null
573:             }
574:         }
575:         if (!node.isTextual) return null
576:         val raw = node.asText()
577:         return try {
578:             LocalDateTime.parse(raw, CLASSIFIED_AT_TIME_FORMATTER)
579:         } catch (e: DateTimeParseException) {
580:             try {
581:                 LocalDate.parse(raw, CLASSIFIED_AT_DATE_FORMATTER).atStartOfDay()
582:             } catch (e2: DateTimeParseException) {
583:                 null
584:             }
585:         }
586:     }
587: 
588:     private fun JsonNode.nullableText(field: String): String? {
589:         val node = path(field)
590:         return if (node.isMissingNode || node.isNull) null else node.asText()
591:     }
592: 
593:     private fun sourceFields(): List<String> =
594:         listOf(
595:             "orcidId", "orcid", "id",
596:             "email", "givenNames", "familyNames",
597:             "country", "keyword", "employment",
598:             "age", "degree", "nationality",
599:             "hIndex", "citationCount", "lastPublicationYear",
600:             "researchFields", "researchFieldIds", "disciplineCategory", "institution", "institutionType",
601:             "filterResult", "institutionEvidence",
602:             "emailSource", "emailVerifiedLevel",
603:             "dataSource", "externalIds", "worksCount", "identityVerification",
604:             "tags",
605:             "updatedAt",
606:             "operatorStatus",
607:             "recentWorkTitles", "patentTitles", "enrichedAt", "enrichmentSource",
608:             "expertClassification"
609:         )
610: 
611:     private fun sortFields(level: ExpertIndexLevel): List<Map<String, Any>> =
612:         when (level) {
613:             ExpertIndexLevel.CANDIDATE ->
614:                 listOf(dateSort("candidateValidatedAt"))
615: 
616:             ExpertIndexLevel.APPLICATION ->
617:                 listOf(dateSort("applicationPromotedAt"), dateSort("lastReplyAt"))
618: 
619:             ExpertIndexLevel.RAW ->
620:                 listOf(mapOf("_doc" to mapOf("order" to "asc")))
621:         }
622: 
623:     private fun dateSort(field: String): Map<String, Any> =
624:         mapOf(
625:             field to mapOf(
626:                 "order" to "desc",
627:                 "missing" to "_last",
628:                 "unmapped_type" to "date"
629:             )
630:         )
631: 
632:     private fun headers(): HttpHeaders =
633:         HttpHeaders().apply {
634:             contentType = MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON
635:             set(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, basicAuthHeader())
636:         }
637: 
638:     private fun basicAuthHeader(): String {
639:         val raw = "${properties.username}:${properties.password}"
640:         val encoded = Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(raw.toByteArray(StandardCharsets.UTF_8))
641:         return "Basic $encoded"
642:     }
643: 
644:     fun searchByOrcidIds(
645:         orcidIds: List<String>,
646:         level: ExpertIndexLevel = ExpertIndexLevel.CANDIDATE
647:     ): List<ExpertProfile> {
648:         if (orcidIds.isEmpty()) return emptyList()
649:         val index = expertIndexService.indexName(level)
650:         val requestBody = mapOf(
651:             "size" to orcidIds.size,
652:             "_source" to sourceFields(),
653:             "query" to mapOf(
654:                 "terms" to mapOf(
655:                     "orcidId" to orcidIds
656:                 )
657:             )
658:         )
659:         val response = restTemplate.exchange(
660:             "${properties.baseUrl}/$index/_search",
661:             HttpMethod.POST,
662:             HttpEntity(requestBody, headers()),
663:             JsonNode::class.java
664:         ).body ?: return emptyList()
665: 
666:         return response.path("hits")
667:             .path("hits")
668:             .map { hit -> toExpertProfile(hit) }
669:     }
670: 
671:     fun searchExpertsFiltered(
672:         level: ExpertIndexLevel,
673:         filters: List<Map<String, Any>>,
674:         from: Int,
675:         size: Int
676:     ): List<ExpertProfile> {
677:         require(size in 1..1000) { "size must be between 1 and 1000" }
678:         require(from >= 0) { "from must be >= 0" }
679: 
680:         val query = if (filters.isEmpty()) {
681:             mapOf("match_all" to emptyMap<String, Any>())
682:         } else {
683:             mapOf("bool" to mapOf("filter" to filters))
684:         }
685: 
686:         val requestBody = mapOf(
687:             "from" to from,
688:             "size" to size,
689:             "_source" to sourceFields(),
690:             "query" to query,
691:             "sort" to sortFields(level)
692:         )
693: 
694:         val response = restTemplate.exchange(
695:             "${properties.baseUrl}/${expertIndexService.indexName(level)}/_search",
696:             HttpMethod.POST,
697:             HttpEntity(requestBody, headers()),
698:             JsonNode::class.java
699:         ).body ?: return emptyList()
700: 
701:         return response.path("hits")
702:             .path("hits")
703:             .map { hit -> toExpertProfile(hit) }
704:     }
705: 
706:     fun countExperts(
707:         level: ExpertIndexLevel,
708:         filters: List<Map<String, Any>> = emptyList()
709:     ): Long = countExpertsResponse(level, filters)?.path("count")?.asLong(0L) ?: 0L
710: 
711:     /**
712:      * R-8（**人物查询专用**调用边界）：与 [countExperts] 完全同一请求，但只在**完整**响应上
713:      * 发布精确计数 —— 缺响应体/超时/失败分片一律 [ExpertSearchUnavailableException]（明确查询不可用），
714:      * 绝不把不完整响应退成合法 0 或某个精确数（`count=123,failed=1` 不得返回 123）。
715:      *
716:      * 既有 [countExperts] 的非人物调用方（发现面板、批量首发预估）保持原响应/错误合同不变。
717:      */
718:     fun countExpertsComplete(
719:         level: ExpertIndexLevel,
720:         filters: List<Map<String, Any>> = emptyList()
721:     ): Long {
722:         val body = requireCompleteResponse(countExpertsResponse(level, filters))
723:         // 缺/畸形 count 同样不表示 0：一律明确查询不可用。
724:         if (!body.path("count").isNumber) {
725:             throw ExpertSearchUnavailableException("ES 计数响应缺少 count 字段，查询结果不可用")
726:         }
727:         return body.path("count").asLong(0L)
728:     }
729: 
730:     private fun countExpertsResponse(
731:         level: ExpertIndexLevel,
732:         filters: List<Map<String, Any>>
733:     ): JsonNode? {
734:         val query = if (filters.isEmpty()) {
735:             mapOf("match_all" to emptyMap<String, Any>())
736:         } else {
737:             mapOf("bool" to mapOf("filter" to filters))
738:         }
739:         val requestBody = mapOf("query" to query)
740:         val index = expertIndexService.indexName(level)
741:         return restTemplate.exchange(
742:             "${properties.baseUrl}/$index/_count",
743:             HttpMethod.POST,
744:             HttpEntity(requestBody, headers()),
745:             JsonNode::class.java
746:         ).body
747:     }
748: 
749:     fun scrollExpertsFiltered(
750:         level: ExpertIndexLevel,
751:         filters: List<Map<String, Any>>,
752:         batchSize: Int = 500,
753:         handler: (List<ExpertProfile>) -> Boolean
754:     ) {
755:         val index = expertIndexService.indexName(level)
756:         var scrollId: String? = null
757: 
758:         try {
759:             val initialUrl = "${properties.baseUrl}/$index/_search?scroll=5m"
760:             val query = if (filters.isEmpty()) {
761:                 mapOf("match_all" to emptyMap<String, Any>())
762:             } else {
763:                 mapOf("bool" to mapOf("filter" to filters))
764:             }
765:             val requestBody = mapOf(
766:                 "size" to batchSize,
767:                 "_source" to sourceFields(),
768:                 "query" to query,
769:                 "sort" to listOf(mapOf("_doc" to "asc"))
770:             )
771:             var response = restTemplate.exchange(
772:                 initialUrl,
773:                 HttpMethod.POST,
774:                 HttpEntity(requestBody, headers()),
775:                 JsonNode::class.java
776:             ).body ?: return
777: 
778:             scrollId = response.path("_scroll_id").asText()
779:             val totalHits = response.path("hits").path("total").path("value").asLong(0)
780:             var batchNumber = 0
781: 
782:             do {
783:                 val hits = response.path("hits").path("hits")
784:                 if (hits.isEmpty) break
785: 
786:                 batchNumber++
787:                 val experts = hits.map { hit -> toExpertProfile(hit) }
788:                 val shouldContinue = handler(experts)
789:                 if (!shouldContinue) break
790:                 if (hits.size() < batchSize) break
791: 
792:                 response = restTemplate.exchange(
793:                     "${properties.baseUrl}/_search/scroll",
794:                     HttpMethod.POST,
795:                     HttpEntity(mapOf("scroll" to "5m", "scroll_id" to scrollId), headers()),
796:                     JsonNode::class.java
797:                 ).body ?: break
798: 
799:                 scrollId = response.path("_scroll_id").asText()
800:             } while (true)
801:         } finally {
802:             if (scrollId != null) {
803:                 try {
804:                     restTemplate.exchange(
805:                         "${properties.baseUrl}/_search/scroll",
806:                         HttpMethod.DELETE,
807:                         HttpEntity(mapOf("scroll_id" to scrollId), headers()),
808:                         JsonNode::class.java
809:                     )
810:                 } catch (_: Exception) {}
811:             }
812:         }
813:     }
814: 
815:     fun searchAfterExpertsFiltered(
816:         level: ExpertIndexLevel,
817:         filters: List<Map<String, Any>>,
818:         batchSize: Int = 500,
819:         handler: (List<ExpertProfile>) -> Boolean
820:     ) {
821:         val index = expertIndexService.indexName(level)
822:         var searchAfter: String? = null
823: 
824:         while (true) {
825:             val query = if (filters.isEmpty()) {
826:                 mapOf("match_all" to emptyMap<String, Any>())
827:             } else {
828:                 mapOf("bool" to mapOf("filter" to filters))
829:             }
830:             val requestBody = mutableMapOf<String, Any>(
831:                 "size" to batchSize,
832:                 "_source" to sourceFields(),
833:                 "query" to query,
834:                 "sort" to listOf(mapOf("orcidId" to "asc"))
835:             )
836:             if (searchAfter != null) {
837:                 requestBody["search_after"] = listOf(searchAfter)
838:             }
839: 
840:             val response = restTemplate.exchange(
841:                 "${properties.baseUrl}/$index/_search",
842:                 HttpMethod.POST,
843:                 HttpEntity(requestBody, headers()),
844:                 JsonNode::class.java
845:             ).body ?: break
846: 
847:             val hits = response.path("hits").path("hits")
848:             if (hits.isEmpty) break
849: 
850:             val experts = hits.map { hit -> toExpertProfile(hit) }
851:             if (!handler(experts)) break
852:             if (hits.size() < batchSize) break
853: 
854:             searchAfter = hits[hits.size() - 1].path("sort").get(0).asText()
855:         }
856:     }
857: 
858:     fun findByOrcidId(orcidId: String, level: ExpertIndexLevel): ExpertProfile? {
859:         require(orcidId.isNotBlank()) { "orcidId must not be blank" }
860: 
861:         val requestBody = mapOf(
862:             "size" to 1,
863:             "_source" to sourceFields(),
864:             "query" to mapOf("term" to mapOf("orcidId" to orcidId))
865:         )
866: 
867:         val response = restTemplate.exchange(
868:             "${properties.baseUrl}/${expertIndexService.indexName(level)}/_search",
869:             HttpMethod.POST,
870:             HttpEntity(requestBody, headers()),
871:             JsonNode::class.java
872:         ).body ?: return null
873: 
874:         val hits = response.path("hits").path("hits")
875:         if (!hits.isArray || hits.isEmpty) {
876:             return null
877:         }
878:         return toExpertProfile(hits[0])
879:     }
880: 
881:     /**
882:      * R-3（V-5）：人物调用链的**严格批读边界** —— 响应不完整/缺必要结构即抛
883:      * [ExpertSearchUnavailableException]，绝不把读取失败伪装成「合法缺档」；`found=false` 仍是合法缺档。
884:      * 既有 [findByDocumentIds] 的非人物调用合同保持不变。
885:      */
886:     fun findByDocumentIdsStrict(level: ExpertIndexLevel, ids: List<String>): List<ExpertProfile> {
887:         val documentIds = ids.filter { it.isNotBlank() }.distinct()
888:         if (documentIds.isEmpty()) return emptyList()
889: 
890:         val index = expertIndexService.indexName(level)
891:         val requestBody = mapOf(
892:             "docs" to documentIds.map { mapOf("_index" to index, "_id" to it, "_source" to sourceFields()) }
893:         )
894:         val response = restTemplate.exchange(
895:             "${properties.baseUrl}/_mget",
896:             HttpMethod.POST,
897:             HttpEntity(requestBody, headers()),
898:             JsonNode::class.java
899:         ).body
900:         requireCompleteResponse(response)
901:         val docs = response!!.path("docs")
902:         if (!docs.isArray) {
903:             throw ExpertSearchUnavailableException("ES _mget 响应缺少 docs 数组，批量读取不可用")
904:         }
905:         // R-2（V-5/V-13）：逐项完整性 —— 条目 error、缺 found、重复/错 _id、found=true 却缺 _source
906:         // 或响应漏掉请求中的 id 一律明确不可用；只有 found=false 才允许当作合法不存在。
907:         val expectedIds = documentIds.toSet()
908:         val seen = mutableSetOf<String>()
909:         val profiles = mutableListOf<ExpertProfile>()
910:         for (item in docs) {
911:             if (item.has("error")) {
912:                 throw ExpertSearchUnavailableException("ES _mget 条目返回错误，批量读取不可用")
913:             }
914:             if (!item.has("found")) {
915:                 throw ExpertSearchUnavailableException("ES _mget 条目缺少 found 字段，批量读取不可用")
916:             }
917:             val id = item.path("_id").asText()
918:             if (id.isBlank() || id !in expectedIds || !seen.add(id)) {
919:                 throw ExpertSearchUnavailableException("ES _mget 条目 _id 缺失/重复/不在请求集内，批量读取不可用")
920:             }
921:             if (item.path("found").asBoolean(false)) {
922:                 if (!item.path("_source").isObject) {
923:                     throw ExpertSearchUnavailableException("ES _mget found=true 条目缺少 _source，批量读取不可用")
924:                 }
925:                 profiles += toExpertProfile(item)
926:             }
927:         }
928:         if (seen != expectedIds) {
929:             throw ExpertSearchUnavailableException("ES _mget 响应缺少请求中的条目，批量读取不可用")
930:         }
931:         return profiles
932:     }
933: 
934:     /**
935:      * I-1/I-3：按真实 `_id` 批量读取（`_mget`），结果复用 [toExpertProfile]，因此与列表/详情读到同一批字段。
936:      * `orcidId` 只是文档里的一个字段：搜索式 `findByOrcidId` 不能保证命中真实文档，定位一律以 `_id` 为准。
937:      * 未命中的 id（`found=false`）不产出条目，也不补空对象；入参为空时不发请求。
938:      */
939:     fun findByDocumentIds(level: ExpertIndexLevel, ids: List<String>): List<ExpertProfile> {
940:         val documentIds = ids.filter { it.isNotBlank() }.distinct()
941:         if (documentIds.isEmpty()) return emptyList()
942: 
943:         val index = expertIndexService.indexName(level)
944:         val requestBody = mapOf(
945:             "docs" to documentIds.map { mapOf("_index" to index, "_id" to it, "_source" to sourceFields()) }
946:         )
947: 
948:         val response = restTemplate.exchange(
949:             "${properties.baseUrl}/_mget",
950:             HttpMethod.POST,
951:             HttpEntity(requestBody, headers()),
952:             JsonNode::class.java
953:         ).body ?: return emptyList()
954: 
955:         return response.path("docs")
956:             .filter { it.path("found").asBoolean(false) }
957:             .map { toExpertProfile(it) }
958:     }
959: 
960:     fun countByFieldPresence(
961:         level: ExpertIndexLevel,
962:         fields: List<String>,
963:         mode: FieldPresenceMode
964:     ): Long {
965:         if (fields.isEmpty()) {
966:             return countExperts(level, emptyList())
967:         }
968:         return countExperts(level, buildFieldPresenceFilters(fields, mode))
969:     }
970: 
971:     fun findRandomByFieldPresence(
972:         level: ExpertIndexLevel,
973:         fields: List<String>,
974:         mode: FieldPresenceMode
975:     ): ExpertProfile? {
976:         val innerQuery = buildFieldPresenceQuery(fields, mode)
977:         val requestBody = mapOf(
978:             "size" to 20,
979:             "_source" to sourceFields(),
980:             "query" to mapOf(
981:                 "function_score" to mapOf(
982:                     "query" to innerQuery,
983:                     "random_score" to emptyMap<String, Any>()
984:                 )
985:             )
986:         )
987: 
988:         val response = restTemplate.exchange(
989:             "${properties.baseUrl}/${expertIndexService.indexName(level)}/_search",
990:             HttpMethod.POST,
991:             HttpEntity(requestBody, headers()),
992:             JsonNode::class.java
993:         ).body ?: return null
994: 
995:         val experts = response.path("hits")
996:             .path("hits")
997:             .map { hit -> toExpertProfile(hit) }
998:         if (experts.isEmpty()) {
999:             return null
1000:         }
1001:         if (mode == FieldPresenceMode.SATISFY_ALL && fields.isNotEmpty()) {
1002:             val candidates = experts.filter { profile ->
1003:                 fields.all { field -> profile.hasNonBlankEsField(field) }
1004:             }
1005:             return candidates.randomOrNull()
1006:         }
1007:         return experts.random()
1008:     }
1009: 
1010:     private fun buildFieldPresenceQuery(fields: List<String>, mode: FieldPresenceMode): Map<String, Any> =
1011:         if (fields.isEmpty()) {
1012:             mapOf("match_all" to emptyMap<String, Any>())
1013:         } else if (mode == FieldPresenceMode.MISSING_ANY) {
1014:             val bool = buildFieldPresenceFilters(fields, mode).single()["bool"] as Map<String, Any>
1015:             mapOf("bool" to bool)
1016:         } else {
1017:             mapOf("bool" to mapOf("filter" to buildFieldPresenceFilters(fields, mode)))
1018:         }
1019: 
1020:     private fun buildFieldPresenceFilters(fields: List<String>, mode: FieldPresenceMode): List<Map<String, Any>> =
1021:         when (mode) {
1022:             FieldPresenceMode.SATISFY_ALL -> fields.map { field ->
1023:                 fieldPresenceFilter(field)
1024:             }
1025:             FieldPresenceMode.MISSING_ANY -> listOf(
1026:                 mapOf(
1027:                     "bool" to mapOf(
1028:                         "should" to fields.map { field ->
1029:                             mapOf(
1030:                                 "bool" to mapOf(
1031:                                     "must_not" to listOf(mapOf("exists" to mapOf("field" to field)))
1032:                                 )
1033:                             )
1034:                         },
1035:                         "minimum_should_match" to 1
1036:                     )
1037:                 )
1038:             )
1039:         }
1040: 
1041:     private fun ExpertProfile.hasNonBlankEsField(esField: String): Boolean =
1042:         when (esField) {
1043:             "familyNames" -> !familyNames.isNullOrBlank()
1044:             "institution" -> !institution.isNullOrBlank()
1045:             "keyword" -> !keyword.isNullOrBlank()
1046:             "employment" -> !employment.isNullOrBlank()
1047:             "researchFields" -> !researchFields.isNullOrBlank()
1048:             "country" -> !country.isNullOrBlank()
1049:             "degree" -> !degree.isNullOrBlank()
1050:             "hIndex" -> hIndex != null
1051:             "worksCount" -> worksCount != null
1052:             "lastPublicationYear" -> lastPublicationYear != null
1053:             "recentWorkTitles" -> !recentWorkTitles.isNullOrEmpty()
1054:             "patentTitles" -> !patentTitles.isNullOrEmpty()
1055:             else -> true
1056:         }
1057: 
1058:     fun aggregateTags(
1059:         level: ExpertIndexLevel,
1060:         operatorStatus: String? = null,
1061:         emailDomain: String? = null,
1062:         region: String? = null
1063:     ): List<TagCount> {
1064:         val filters = buildExpertFilters(tag = null, operatorStatus, emailDomain, region)
1065:         val query = if (filters.isEmpty()) {
1066:             mapOf("match_all" to emptyMap<String, Any>())
1067:         } else {
1068:             mapOf("bool" to mapOf("filter" to filters))
1069:         }
1070: 
1071:         val requestBody = mapOf(
1072:             "size" to 0,
1073:             "query" to query,
1074:             "aggs" to mapOf(
1075:                 "tags" to mapOf(
1076:                     "terms" to mapOf(
1077:                         "field" to "tags",
1078:                         "size" to 100
1079:                     )
1080:                 )
1081:             )
1082:         )
1083: 
1084:         val response = restTemplate.exchange(
1085:             "${properties.baseUrl}/${expertIndexService.indexName(level)}/_search",
1086:             HttpMethod.POST,
1087:             HttpEntity(requestBody, headers()),
1088:             JsonNode::class.java
1089:         ).body ?: return emptyList()
1090: 
1091:         val buckets = response.path("aggregations")
1092:             .path("tags")
1093:             .path("buckets")
1094: 
1095:         return buckets.map { bucket ->
1096:             TagCount(
1097:                 tag = bucket.path("key").asText(),
1098:                 count = bucket.path("doc_count").asLong()
1099:             )
1100:         }
1101:     }
1102: 
1103:     fun aggregateRegions(
1104:         level: ExpertIndexLevel,
1105:         tag: String? = null,
1106:         operatorStatus: String? = null,
1107:         emailDomain: String? = null
1108:     ): List<RegionCount> {
1109:         val filters = buildExpertFilters(tag, operatorStatus, emailDomain, region = null)
1110:         val query = if (filters.isEmpty()) {
1111:             mapOf("match_all" to emptyMap<String, Any>())
1112:         } else {
1113:             mapOf("bool" to mapOf("filter" to filters))
1114:         }
1115: 
1116:         val requestBody = mapOf(
1117:             "size" to 0,
1118:             "query" to query,
1119:             "aggs" to mapOf(
1120:                 "countries" to mapOf(
1121:                     "terms" to mapOf(
1122:                         "field" to "country",
1123:                         "size" to 500
1124:                     )
1125:                 )
1126:             )
1127:         )
1128: 
1129:         val response = restTemplate.exchange(
1130:             "${properties.baseUrl}/${expertIndexService.indexName(level)}/_search",
1131:             HttpMethod.POST,
1132:             HttpEntity(requestBody, headers()),
1133:             JsonNode::class.java
1134:         ).body ?: return emptyRegionCounts()
1135: 
1136:         val buckets = response.path("aggregations")
1137:             .path("countries")
1138:             .path("buckets")
1139: 
1140:         val regionCounts = mutableMapOf<String, Long>()
1141:         buckets.forEach { bucket ->
1142:             val key = bucket.path("key").asText()
1143:             val count = bucket.path("doc_count").asLong()
1144:             val mappedRegion = CountryContinentMapping.toRegion(key)
1145:             regionCounts[mappedRegion] = regionCounts.getOrDefault(mappedRegion, 0L) + count
1146:         }
1147: 
1148:         return CountryContinentMapping.allRegions().map { regionName ->
1149:             RegionCount(region = regionName, count = regionCounts.getOrDefault(regionName, 0L))
1150:         }
1151:     }
1152: 
1153:     private fun emptyRegionCounts(): List<RegionCount> =
1154:         CountryContinentMapping.allRegions().map { RegionCount(it, 0L) }
1155: 
1156:     private fun buildExpertFilters(
1157:         tag: String?,
1158:         operatorStatus: String?,
1159:         emailDomain: String?,
1160:         region: String?,
1161:         hIndexMin: Int? = null,
1162:         citationCountMin: Int? = null,
1163:         recentYears: Int? = null,
1164:         hasField: List<String>? = null,
1165:         discipline: String? = null,
1166:         expertTypes: List<String> = emptyList()
1167:     ): MutableList<Map<String, Any>> {
1168:         val filters = mutableListOf<Map<String, Any>>()
1169: 
1170:         if (!tag.isNullOrBlank()) {
1171:             filters.add(mapOf("term" to mapOf("tags" to tag)))
1172:         }
1173: 
1174:         if (!operatorStatus.isNullOrBlank()) {
1175:             when (operatorStatus) {
1176:                 "NOT_CONTACTED" -> {
1177:                     filters.addAll(notContactedWithEmailFilters(null))
1178:                 }
1179:                 else -> {
1180:                     filters.add(mapOf("term" to mapOf("operatorStatus" to operatorStatus)))
1181:                 }
1182:             }
1183:         }
1184: 
1185:         if (!emailDomain.isNullOrBlank()) {
1186:             filters.add(mapOf("wildcard" to mapOf("email" to mapOf("value" to "*@$emailDomain"))))
1187:         }
1188: 
1189:         if (!region.isNullOrBlank()) {
1190:             filters.add(regionFilter(region))
1191:         }
1192: 
1193:         hIndexMin?.let { filters.add(mapOf("range" to mapOf("hIndex" to mapOf("gte" to it)))) }
1194:         citationCountMin?.let { filters.add(mapOf("range" to mapOf("citationCount" to mapOf("gte" to it)))) }
1195:         recentYears?.let {
1196:             val cutoff = java.time.Year.now().value - it
1197:             filters.add(mapOf("range" to mapOf("lastPublicationYear" to mapOf("gte" to cutoff))))
1198:         }
1199:         hasField?.forEach { field ->
1200:             require(field in ALLOWED_HAS_FIELDS) { "Invalid hasField: $field" }
1201:             filters.add(fieldPresenceFilter(field))
1202:         }
1203: 
1204:         if (!discipline.isNullOrBlank()) {
1205:             filters.add(disciplineFilter(discipline))
1206:         }
1207: 
1208:         expertTypesFilter(expertTypes)?.let { filters.add(it) }
1209: 
1210:         return filters
1211:     }
1212: 
1213:     fun aggregateEmailDomains(
1214:         level: ExpertIndexLevel,
1215:         tag: String? = null,
1216:         operatorStatus: String? = null,
1217:         region: String? = null
1218:     ): List<EmailDomainCount> {
1219:         val filters = buildExpertFilters(tag, operatorStatus, emailDomain = null, region)
1220:         filters.add(mapOf("exists" to mapOf("field" to "email")))
1221:         val requestBody = mapOf(
1222:             "size" to 0,
1223:             "query" to mapOf("bool" to mapOf("filter" to filters)),
1224:             "aggs" to mapOf(
1225:                 "email_domains" to mapOf(
1226:                     "terms" to mapOf(
1227:                         "field" to "email",
1228:                         "size" to 10000,
1229:                         "script" to mapOf(
1230:                             "source" to "doc['email'].value.substring(doc['email'].value.indexOf('@') + 1)"
1231:                         )
1232:                     )
1233:                 )
1234:             )
1235:         )
1236: 
1237:         val response = restTemplate.exchange(
1238:             "${properties.baseUrl}/${expertIndexService.indexName(level)}/_search",
1239:             HttpMethod.POST,
1240:             HttpEntity(requestBody, headers()),
1241:             JsonNode::class.java
1242:         ).body ?: return emptyList()
1243: 
1244:         val buckets = response.path("aggregations")
1245:             .path("email_domains")
1246:             .path("buckets")
1247: 
1248:         return buckets.map { bucket ->
1249:             EmailDomainCount(
1250:                 domain = bucket.path("key").asText(),
1251:                 count = bucket.path("doc_count").asLong()
1252:             )
1253:         }
1254:     }
1255:     /**
1256:      * I2-4: 旧首发链路专用 —— filter 只有两项：exists email 与研发类型集合。
1257:      * 不得追加任何其他条件（主计划 M-1：唯一收口点）。
1258:      * 调用方保证 expertTypes 非空（I2-2），故这里不处理空集合。
1259:      * 03（I-3）：支持 `from` 显式分页起点，默认 0 —— 既有调用行为逐字不变；旧首发据此在被过滤页后继续取页。
1260:      */
1261:     fun searchExpertsByTypesWithEmail(
1262:         size: Int,
1263:         level: ExpertIndexLevel = ExpertIndexLevel.CANDIDATE,
1264:         expertTypes: List<String>,
1265:         from: Int = 0
1266:     ): ExpertSearchResult {
1267:         require(size in 1..1000) { "size must be between 1 and 1000" }
1268:         require(from >= 0) { "from must be >= 0" }
1269:         val typesFilter = expertTypesFilter(expertTypes)
1270:             ?: throw IllegalArgumentException("expertTypes must not be empty")
1271: 
1272:         val requestBody = mapOf(
1273:             "from" to from,
1274:             "size" to size,
1275:             "_source" to sourceFields(),
1276:             "query" to mapOf(
1277:                 "bool" to mapOf(
1278:                     "filter" to listOf(
1279:                         mapOf("exists" to mapOf("field" to "email")),
1280:                         typesFilter
1281:                     )
1282:                 )
1283:             ),
1284:             "sort" to sortFields(level)
1285:         )
1286: 
1287:         val response = restTemplate.exchange(
1288:             "${properties.baseUrl}/${expertIndexService.indexName(level)}/_search",
1289:             HttpMethod.POST,
1290:             HttpEntity(requestBody, headers()),
1291:             JsonNode::class.java
1292:         ).body ?: return ExpertSearchResult(emptyList(), 0L)
1293: 
1294:         val totalHits = response.path("hits").path("total").path("value").asLong(0L)
1295:         val experts = response.path("hits")
1296:             .path("hits")
1297:             .map { hit -> toExpertProfile(hit) }
1298:         return ExpertSearchResult(experts = experts, totalHits = totalHits)
1299:     }
1300: 
1301:     // ── 06：人物口径查询的分区读取（只读；不改变任何既有低层方法的行为/单位）────────────
1302: 
1303:     /**
1304:      * 06：复用原筛选构造的唯一公开入口。`DiscoveryPersonQueryService` 用它把一次混合查询
1305:      * 拆成 discovered / 其他两个分区，保证「整套条件由同一成员满足」沿用同一套 filter，
1306:      * 不在别处重写筛选语义。
1307:      */
1308:     fun expertFilters(
1309:         tag: String?,
1310:         operatorStatus: String?,
1311:         emailDomain: String?,
1312:         region: String?,
1313:         hIndexMin: Int? = null,
1314:         citationCountMin: Int? = null,
1315:         recentYears: Int? = null,
1316:         hasField: List<String>? = null,
1317:         discipline: String? = null,
1318:         expertTypes: List<String> = emptyList()
1319:     ): List<Map<String, Any>> =
1320:         buildExpertFilters(
1321:             tag, operatorStatus, emailDomain, region,
1322:             hIndexMin, citationCountMin, recentYears, hasField, discipline, expertTypes
1323:         )
1324: 
1325:     /**
1326:      * 06：按显示排序读取前缀，并保留 ES 排序键 [ExpertHitWithSort.sort]，供跨分区归并使用。
1327:      * 既有 [searchExpertsFiltered] 的返回/行为不变。
1328:      */
1329:     fun searchExpertsWithSort(
1330:         level: ExpertIndexLevel,
1331:         filters: List<Map<String, Any>>,
1332:         from: Int,
1333:         size: Int,
1334:         sortBy: String? = null
1335:     ): List<ExpertHitWithSort> {
1336:         require(size in 1..1000) { "size must be between 1 and 1000" }
1337:         require(from >= 0) { "from must be >= 0" }
1338: 
1339:         val query = if (filters.isEmpty()) {
1340:             mapOf("match_all" to emptyMap<String, Any>())
1341:         } else {
1342:             mapOf("bool" to mapOf("filter" to filters))
1343:         }
1344:         val requestBody = mapOf(
1345:             "from" to from,
1346:             "size" to size,
1347:             "_source" to sourceFields(),
1348:             "query" to query,
1349:             "sort" to displaySort(level, sortBy)
1350:         )
1351:         val response = restTemplate.exchange(
1352:             "${properties.baseUrl}/${expertIndexService.indexName(level)}/_search",
1353:             HttpMethod.POST,
1354:             HttpEntity(requestBody, headers()),
1355:             JsonNode::class.java
1356:         ).body
1357:         // R-10/V-13：超时/失败分片/缺 body/缺 hits 结构都不是「完整结果」，绝不返回空页冒充精确结果。
1358:         val body = requireCompleteHitsResponse(response)
1359: 
1360:         return body.path("hits").path("hits").map { hit ->
1361:             ExpertHitWithSort(toExpertProfile(hit), sortTokens(hit))
1362:         }
1363:     }
1364: 
1365:     /**
1366:      * 06：按显示排序流式读取**完整**匹配集，并保留 ES 排序键；scroll 在 finally 关闭。
1367:      * 既有 [scrollExpertsFiltered]（`_doc` 序、无排序键）保持不动。
1368:      */
1369:     fun scrollExpertsFilteredWithSort(
1370:         level: ExpertIndexLevel,
1371:         filters: List<Map<String, Any>>,
1372:         batchSize: Int = 500,
1373:         sortBy: String? = null,
1374:         handler: (List<ExpertHitWithSort>) -> Boolean
1375:     ) {
1376:         val index = expertIndexService.indexName(level)
1377:         var scrollId: String? = null
1378:         try {
1379:             val query = if (filters.isEmpty()) {
1380:                 mapOf("match_all" to emptyMap<String, Any>())
1381:             } else {
1382:                 mapOf("bool" to mapOf("filter" to filters))
1383:             }
1384:             val requestBody = mapOf(
1385:                 "size" to batchSize,
1386:                 "_source" to sourceFields(),
1387:                 "query" to query,
1388:                 "sort" to displaySort(level, sortBy)
1389:             )
1390:             val initial = restTemplate.exchange(
1391:                 "${properties.baseUrl}/$index/_search?scroll=5m",
1392:                 HttpMethod.POST,
1393:                 HttpEntity(requestBody, headers()),
1394:                 JsonNode::class.java
1395:             ).body
1396:             // R-10：先记住 scroll_id，任何完整性失败都要在 finally 里关闭游标。
1397:             scrollId = initial?.path("_scroll_id")?.asText()?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() } ?: scrollId
1398:             var response = requireCompleteHitsResponse(initial)
1399:             do {
1400:                 val hits = response.path("hits").path("hits")
1401:                 if (hits.isEmpty) break
1402: 
1403:                 val batch = hits.map { hit -> ExpertHitWithSort(toExpertProfile(hit), sortTokens(hit)) }
1404:                 if (!handler(batch)) break
1405:                 if (hits.size() < batchSize) break
1406: 
1407:                 val next = restTemplate.exchange(
1408:                     "${properties.baseUrl}/_search/scroll",
1409:                     HttpMethod.POST,
1410:                     HttpEntity(mapOf("scroll" to "5m", "scroll_id" to scrollId), headers()),
1411:                     JsonNode::class.java
1412:                 ).body
1413:                 val nextScrollId = next?.path("_scroll_id")?.asText()?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }
1414:                 if (nextScrollId != null) scrollId = nextScrollId
1415:                 // R-10/V-13：缺 body / 超时 / 失败分片 / 缺 hits 结构 → 明确不可用；缺 scroll_id 且仍有下一页 → 遍历无法完整继续。
1416:                 val validated = requireCompleteHitsResponse(next)
1417:                 if (nextScrollId == null && validated.path("hits").path("hits").size() >= batchSize) {
1418:                     throw ExpertSearchUnavailableException(
1419:                         "ES scroll 未返回 scroll_id，遍历无法完整继续，查询结果不可用"
1420:                     )
1421:                 }
1422:                 response = validated
1423:             } while (true)
1424:         } finally {
1425:             if (scrollId != null) {
1426:                 try {
1427:                     restTemplate.exchange(
1428:                         "${properties.baseUrl}/_search/scroll",
1429:                         HttpMethod.DELETE,
1430:                         HttpEntity(mapOf("scroll_id" to scrollId), headers()),
1431:                         JsonNode::class.java
1432:                     )
1433:                 } catch (_: Exception) {}
1434:             }
1435:         }
1436:     }
1437: 
1438:     /**
1439:      * 06：在给定原始筛选上做一次 terms 聚合（只读）。`script` 为空时按 keyword 字段聚合；
1440:      * 非空时用于邮箱域名等派生键。既有 [aggregateTags]/[aggregateRegions]/[aggregateEmailDomains]
1441:      * 保持不变；本方法只服务人物口径的「其他」分区桶计数。
1442:      */
1443:     fun aggregateTerms(
1444:         level: ExpertIndexLevel,
1445:         filters: List<Map<String, Any>>,
1446:         field: String,
1447:         size: Int,
1448:         script: Map<String, Any>? = null
1449:     ): List<Pair<String, Long>> {
1450:         val query = if (filters.isEmpty()) {
1451:             mapOf("match_all" to emptyMap<String, Any>())
1452:         } else {
1453:             mapOf("bool" to mapOf("filter" to filters))
1454:         }
1455:         val terms = mutableMapOf<String, Any>("field" to field, "size" to size)
1456:         if (script != null) terms["script"] = script
1457:         val requestBody = mapOf(
1458:             "size" to 0,
1459:             "query" to query,
1460:             "aggs" to mapOf("bucket" to mapOf("terms" to terms))
1461:         )
1462:         val response = restTemplate.exchange(
1463:             "${properties.baseUrl}/${expertIndexService.indexName(level)}/_search",
1464:             HttpMethod.POST,
1465:             HttpEntity(requestBody, headers()),
1466:             JsonNode::class.java
1467:         ).body
1468:         // R-10：聚合桶同样只在完整响应上发布；超时/失败分片/缺 body 全部明确失败。
1469:         val body = requireCompleteResponse(response)
1470: 
1471:         // R-5（V-13）：人物查询消费的聚合必须带必要桶结构；缺 aggregations/bucket/buckets 或桶缺
1472:         // key/doc_count 一律明确不可用，绝不退成「合法空桶/零人」。合法空数组仍返回空。
1473:         val buckets = body.path("aggregations").path("bucket").path("buckets")
1474:         if (!buckets.isArray) {
1475:             throw ExpertSearchUnavailableException("ES 聚合响应缺少必要的 buckets 结构，聚合结果不可用")
1476:         }
1477:         return buckets.map { bucket ->
1478:             if (!bucket.has("key") || !bucket.has("doc_count")) {
1479:                 throw ExpertSearchUnavailableException("ES 聚合桶缺少 key/doc_count，聚合结果不可用")
1480:             }
1481:             bucket.path("key").asText() to bucket.path("doc_count").asLong()
1482:         }
1483:     }
1484: 
1485:     /**
1486:      * R-10：ES 响应完整性闸门。`timed_out`、`_shards.failed > 0`、缺 body/null 都表示
1487:      * 本次遍历不完整 —— 一律抛 [ExpertSearchUnavailableException]（可重试的明确「查询不可用」），
1488:      * 绝不把部分结果当成精确结果发布。`_shards` 缺失按 0 处理（既有测试替身与 `_count`
1489:      * 型响应不带该字段）。
1490:      */
1491:     private fun requireCompleteResponse(response: JsonNode?): JsonNode {
1492:         if (response == null || response.isNull || response.isMissingNode) {
1493:             throw ExpertSearchUnavailableException("ES 查询未返回响应体，查询结果不可用")
1494:         }
1495:         if (response.path("timed_out").asBoolean(false)) {
1496:             throw ExpertSearchUnavailableException("ES 查询超时（timed_out=true），查询结果不可用")
1497:         }
1498:         val failedShards = response.path("_shards").path("failed").asInt(0)
1499:         if (failedShards > 0) {
1500:             throw ExpertSearchUnavailableException(
1501:                 "ES 分片失败（_shards.failed=$failedShards），查询结果不可用"
1502:             )
1503:         }
1504:         return response
1505:     }
1506: 
1507:     /**
1508:      * R-10/V-13：搜索/滚动响应除完整性外，还须带**结构完整**的 `hits` —— `hits` 为对象且
1509:      * `hits.hits` 为数组。缺 `hits`、缺 `hits.hits` 或类型不对都不表示「0 条」：一律明确
1510:      * [ExpertSearchUnavailableException]，绝不把缺失结构读成空页，也不让滚动当成完整遍历结束。
1511:      */
1512:     private fun requireCompleteHitsResponse(response: JsonNode?): JsonNode {
1513:         val body = requireCompleteResponse(response)
1514:         val hits = body.path("hits")
1515:         if (!hits.isObject || !hits.path("hits").isArray) {
1516:             throw ExpertSearchUnavailableException("ES 响应缺少完整的 hits 结构，查询结果不可用")
1517:         }
1518:         return body
1519:     }
1520: 
1521:     private fun displaySort(level: ExpertIndexLevel, sortBy: String?): List<Map<String, Any>> =
1522:         if (sortBy == "updatedAt") listOf(dateSort("updatedAt")) else sortFields(level)
1523: 
1524:     private fun sortTokens(hit: JsonNode): List<Any?> {
1525:         val sort = hit.path("sort")
1526:         if (!sort.isArray) return emptyList()
1527:         return sort.map { node ->
1528:             when {
1529:                 node.isIntegralNumber -> node.asLong()
1530:                 node.isNumber -> node.asDouble()
1531:                 node.isNull -> null
1532:                 else -> node.asText()
1533:             }
1534:         }
1535:     }
1536: }
1537: 
1538: /** 06：命中文档及其 ES 排序键（跨分区归并与人物代表成员选择的只读投影）。 */
1539: data class ExpertHitWithSort(val profile: ExpertProfile, val sort: List<Any?>)
1540: 
1541: /**
1542:  * R-10：ES 查询结果不完整（超时/失败分片/缺 body/遍历无法继续）时的明确「查询不可用」失败。
1543:  * 继承 [IllegalStateException]，与既有全局异常处理（`DiscoveryPersonQueryUnavailableException`）
1544:  * 同口径：绝不据此返回部分数或近似数。调用方（人物只读查询等）直接向上传播。
1545:  */
1546: class ExpertSearchUnavailableException(message: String) : IllegalStateException(message)
1547:

1549:     val experts: List<ExpertProfile>,
1550:     val totalHits: Long
1551: )
1552:

1554:     val domain: String,
1555:     val count: Long
1556: )
1557: 
1558: data class RegionCount(
1559:     val region: String,
1560:     val count: Long
1561: )
1562: 
1563: data class TagCount(
1564:     val tag: String,
1565:     val count: Long
1566: )
1567: 
1568: enum class FieldPresenceMode {
1569:     SATISFY_ALL,
1570:     MISSING_ANY
1571: }

23:     val maxTransientAttempts: Int = 3,

30: ) {

36:         require(httpConnectTimeout > Duration.ZERO && httpConnectTimeout <= Duration.ofMinutes(1))
37:         require(httpReadTimeout > Duration.ZERO && httpReadTimeout <= Duration.ofMinutes(5))

39:     }
